āraucaiyyāti  anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji’’nti vattukāmau ‘‘tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ, suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhaṃ, animittaṃ vimaukkhaṃ, appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhaṃ, suñ‍ñataṃ samādhiṃ, animittaṃ samādhiṃ, appaṇihitaṃ samādhiṃ, suñ‍ñataṃ samāpattiṃ, animittaṃ samāpattiṃ, appaṇihitaṃ samāpattiṃ, tissau vij‍jā, cattārau satipaṭṭhānai, cattārau sammappadhānai, cattārau iddhipādai, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgai, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattaṃ arahattaphalaṃ (syā॰) samāpaj‍jiṃ…pai॰… rāgau mai cattau, dausau mai cattau, mauhau mai cattau, vantau, muttau, pahīnau; paṭinissaṭṭhau, ukkhaiṭitau, samukkhaiṭitau; rāgā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ, dausā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ, mauhā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇa’’nti bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pācittiyassa, na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

āraucaiyyāti  anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji’’nti vattukāmau…pai॰… ‘‘mauhā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇa’’nti bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pācittiyassa, na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji’’nti vattukāmau – ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji’’nti bhaṇantassa  paṭivijānantassa āpatti pācittiyassa, na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

mūlaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|

āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘mauhā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇa’’nti vattukāmau – ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji’’nti bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pācittiyassa, na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘mauhā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇa’’nti vattukāmau – ‘‘dausā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇa’’nti bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pācittiyassa, na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘paṭhamañ‍ca jhānaṃ dutiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ tatiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ catutthañ‍ca jhānaṃ  …pai॰… dausā ca mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇa’’nti vattukāmau – ‘‘mauhā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇa’’nti bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pācittiyassa, na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘dutiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ tatiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ catutthañ‍ca jhānaṃ…pai॰… mauhā ca mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇa’’nti vattukāmau – ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji’’nti bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pācittiyassa, na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

75. āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘yau tai vihārai vasi sau bhikkhu paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji, samāpaj‍jati, samāpan‍nau; sau bhikkhu  paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhī, vasī; taina bhikkhunā paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikata’’nti bhaṇantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘yau tai vihārai vasi sau bhikkhu dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji, samāpaj‍jati, samāpan‍nau; sau bhikkhu catutthassa jhānassa lābhī, vasī; taina bhikkhunā catutthaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikata’’nti bhaṇantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

āraucaiyyāti  anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘yau tai vihārai vasi sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhaṃ…pai॰… animittaṃ vimaukkhaṃ appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ samādhiṃ animittaṃ samādhiṃ appaṇihitaṃ samādhiṃ samāpaj‍ji, samāpaj‍jati, samāpan‍nau; sau bhikkhu appaṇihitassa samādhissa lābhī, vasī; taina bhikkhunā appaṇihitau samādhi sacchikatau’’ti bhaṇantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘yau tai vihārai vasi sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñataṃ samāpattiṃ…pai॰… animittaṃ samāpattiṃ appaṇihitaṃ samāpattiṃ samāpaj‍ji, samāpaj‍jati, samāpan‍nau; appaṇihitāya samāpattiyā lābhī, vasī; taina bhikkhunā appaṇihitā samāpatti sacchikatā’’ti bhaṇantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘yau tai vihārai vasi sau bhikkhu tissau vij‍jā  …pai॰… cattārau satipaṭṭhānai, cattārau sammappadhānai, cattārau iddhipādai, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujhaṅgai, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattaṃ arahattaphalaṃ (syā॰) samāpaj‍ji…pai॰… samāpaj‍jati, samāpan‍nau…pai॰… tassa bhikkhunau rāgau cattau, dausau cattau, mauhau  cattau, vantau, muttau, pahīnau, paṭinissaṭṭhau, ukkhaiṭitau, samukkhaiṭitau; tassa bhikkhunau rāgā cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ, dausā cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ, mauhā cittaṃ vinīvaraṇa’’nti bhaṇantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

āraucaiyyāti  anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘yau tai vihārai vasi sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji, samāpaj‍jati, samāpan‍nau; sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthassa jhānassa lābhī, vasī; taina bhikkhunā suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikata’’nti bhaṇantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘yau tai cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍ji, yau tai piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍ji, yau tai saināsanaṃ paribhuñ‍ji, yau tai gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍ji sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji, samāpaj‍jati, samāpan‍nau; sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthassa jhānassa lābhī, vasī; taina bhikkhunā suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikata’’nti bhaṇantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

76. āraucaiyyāti  anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘yaina tai vihārau paribhuttau…pai॰… yaina tai cīvaraṃ paribhuttaṃ, yaina tai piṇḍapātau paribhuttau, yaina tai saināsanaṃ paribhuttaṃ, yaina tai gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārau paribhuttau sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji, samāpaj‍jati, samāpan‍nau; sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthassa jhānassa lābhī, vasī; taina bhikkhunā suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikata’’nti bhaṇantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

āraucaiyyāti anupasampan‍nassa – ‘‘yaṃ tvaṃ āgamma vihāraṃ adāsi…pai॰… cīvaraṃ adāsi, piṇḍapātaṃ adāsi, saināsanaṃ adāsi, gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ  adāsi sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji, samāpaj‍jati, samāpan‍nau; sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthassa jhānassa lābhī, vasī; taina bhikkhunā suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikata’’nti bhaṇantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

77. anāpatti  upasampan‍nassa, bhūtaṃ āraucaiti, ādikammikassāti|

bhūtāraucanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. duṭṭhul‍lāraucanasikkhāpadaṃ

78. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau chabbaggiyaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍanakatau hauti| sau sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā saṅghaṃ tassā āpattiyā parivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau tassā āpattiyā parivāsaṃ adāsi| taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñatarassa pūgassa saṅghabhattaṃ hauti| sau parivasantau bhattaggai āsanapariyantai nisīdi| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tai upāsakai aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aisau, āvusau, āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tumhākaṃ sambhāvitau kulūpakau; yainaiva hatthaina saddhādaiyyaṃ bhuñ‍jati tainaiva hatthaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaisi| sau sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ āpattiṃ  āpaj‍jitvā saṅghaṃ tassā āpattiyā parivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau tassā āpattiyā parivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivasantau āsanapariyantai nisin‍nau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhussa duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ anupasampan‍nassa āraucaissantī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ anupasampan‍nassa āraucaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhussa duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ anupasampan‍nassa āraucaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

79. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ anupasampan‍nassa āraucaiyya, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā bhikkhusammatiyā (syā॰), pācittiya’’nti|

80. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhussāti añ‍ñassa bhikkhussa|

duṭṭhul‍lā  nāma āpatti – cattāri ca pārājikāni, tairasa ca saṅghādisaisā|

anupasampan‍nau nāma bhikkhuñ‍ca bhikkhuniñ‍ca ṭhapaitvā avasaisau anupasampan‍nau nāma|

āraucaiyyāti āraucaiyya itthiyā vā purisassa vā gahaṭṭhassa vā pabbajitassa vā|

añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyāti ṭhapaitvā bhikkhusammutiṃ|

atthi bhikkhusammuti āpattipariyantā, na kulapariyantā| atthi bhikkhusammuti kulapariyantā, na āpattipariyantā, atthi bhikkhusammuti āpattipariyantā ca kulapariyantā ca, atthi bhikkhusammuti naiva āpattipariyantā na kulapariyantā|

āpattipariyantā nāma āpattiyau pariggahitāyau haunti – ‘‘aittakāhi āpattīhi āraucaitabbau’’ti|

kulapariyantā  nāma kulāni pariggahitāni haunti – ‘‘aittakaisu kulaisu āraucaitabbau’’ti| āpattipariyantā ca kulapariyantā ca nāma āpattiyau  ca pariggahitāyau haunti, kulāni ca pariggahitāni haunti – ‘‘aittakāhi āpattīhi aittakaisu kulaisu āraucaitabbau’’ti| naiva āpattipariyantā na kulapariyantā nāma āpattiyau ca apariggahitāyau haunti, kulāni ca apariggahitāni haunti – ‘‘aittakāhi āpattīhi aittakaisu kulaisu āraucaitabbau’’ti|

81. āpattipariyantai yā āpattiyau pariggahitāyau haunti, tā āpattiyau ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñāhi āpattīhi āraucaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

kulapariyantai yāni kulāni pariggahitāni haunti,  tāni kulāni ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñaisu kulaisu āraucaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

āpattipariyantai ca kulapariyantai ca yā āpattiyau pariggahitāyau haunti, tā āpattiyau  ṭhapaitvā yāni kulāni pariggahitāni haunti, tāni kulāni ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñaisu kulaisu āraucaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

naiva āpattipariyantai na kulapariyantai, anāpatti|

82. duṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā duṭṭhul‍lāpattisañ‍ñī anupasampan‍nassa āraucaiti, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

duṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā vaimatikau anupasampan‍nassa āraucaiti, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

duṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā aduṭṭhul‍lāpattisañ‍ñī anupasampan‍nassa āraucaiti, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aduṭṭhul‍laṃ  āpattiṃ āraucaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anupasampan‍nassa duṭṭhul‍laṃ vā aduṭṭhul‍laṃ vā ajjhācāraṃ āraucaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

aduṭṭhul‍lāya  āpattiyā duṭṭhul‍lāpattisañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

aduṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

aduṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā aduṭṭhul‍lāpattisañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

83. anāpatti vatthuṃ āraucaiti nau āpattiṃ, āpattiṃ āraucaiti nau vatthuṃ, bhikkhusammutiyā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

duṭṭhul‍lāraucanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pathavīkhaṇanasikkhāpadaṃ

84. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai| taina khau pana samayaina āḷavakā āḷavikā (syā॰) bhikkhū navakammaṃ karauntā pathaviṃ khaṇantipi khaṇāpaintipi| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā pathaviṃ khaṇissantipi khaṇāpaissantipi! aikindriyaṃ samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jīvaṃ vihaiṭhaintī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āḷavakā bhikkhū pathaviṃ khaṇissantipi khaṇāpaissantipī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, pathaviṃ khaṇathapi khaṇāpaithapī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, pathaviṃ khaṇissathapi khaṇāpaissathapi! jīvasañ‍ñinau hi, maughapurisā,  manussā pathaviyā| naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

85. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu pathaviṃ khaṇaiyya vā khaṇāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

86. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

pathavī nāma dvai pathaviyau  – jātā ca pathavī ajātā ca pathavī|

jātā  nāma pathavī – suddhapaṃsu suddhamattikā appapāsāṇā appasakkharā appakaṭhalā appamarumbā appavālikā, yaibhuyyainapaṃsukā, yaibhuyyainamattikā| adaḍḍhāpi vuc‍cati jātā pathavī| yaupi paṃsupuñ‍jau vā mattikāpuñ‍jau vā atiraikacātumāsaṃ auvaṭṭhau, ayampi vuc‍cati jātā pathavī|

ajātā nāma pathavī – suddhapāsāṇā suddhasakkharā suddhakaṭhalā suddhamarumbā suddhavālikā appapaṃsukā appamattikā, yaibhuyyainapāsāṇā, yaibhuyyainasakkharā, yaibhuyyainakaṭhalā, yaibhuyyainamarumbā, yaibhuyyainavālikā| daḍḍhāpi vuc‍cati ajātā pathavī| yaupi paṃsupuñ‍jau vā mattikāpuñ‍jau vā aumakacātumāsaṃ auvaṭṭhau, ayampi vuc‍cati ajātā pathavī|

khaṇaiyyāti  sayaṃ khaṇati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

khaṇāpaiyyāti añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| sakiṃ āṇattau bahukampi khaṇati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

87. pathaviyā pathavisañ‍ñī khaṇati vā khaṇāpaiti vā, bhindati vā bhaidāpaiti vā, dahati vā dahāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

pathaviyā vaimatikau khaṇati vā khaṇāpaiti vā, bhindati vā bhaidāpaiti vā, dahati vā dahāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

pathaviyā apathavisañ‍ñī khaṇati vā khaṇāpaiti vā, bhindati vā bhaidāpaiti vā, dahati  vā dahāpaiti vā, anāpatti|

apathaviyā pathavisañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apathaviyā vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apathaviyā apathavisañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

88. anāpatti – ‘‘imaṃ jāna, imaṃ daihi, imaṃ āhara, iminā atthau, imaṃ kappiyaṃ karauhī’’ti bhaṇati, asañ‍cic‍ca, asatiyā, ajānantassa, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

pathavīkhaṇanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

musāvādavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

musā  aumasapaisuñ‍ñaṃ, padasaiyyāya vai duvai|

añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñunā bhūtā, duṭṭhul‍lāpatti khaṇanāti||

2. bhūtagāmavaggau

1. bhūtagāmasikkhāpadaṃ

89. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai| taina khau pana samayaina āḷavakā bhikkhū navakammaṃ karauntā rukkhaṃ chindantipi chaidāpaintipi| añ‍ñataraupi āḷavakau bhikkhu rukkhaṃ chindati| tasmiṃ rukkhai adhivatthā daivatā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā, bhantai, attanau bhavanaṃ kattukāmau mayhaṃ bhavanaṃ chindī’’ti| sau bhikkhu anādiyantau chindi yaiva, tassā ca daivatāya dārakassa bāhuṃ ākauṭaisi| atha khau tassā daivatāya aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃn‍nūnāhaṃ imaṃ bhikkhuṃ idhaiva jīvitā vauraupaiyya’’nti| atha khau tassā daivatāya aitadahausi – ‘‘na khau maitaṃ patirūpaṃ yāhaṃ imaṃ bhikkhuṃ idhaiva jīvitā vauraupaiyyaṃ| yan‍nūnāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaiyya’’nti| atha khau sā daivatā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘sādhu sādhu daivatai! sādhu khau tvaṃ, daivatai, taṃ bhikkhuṃ jīvitā na vauraupaisi| sacaj‍ja tvaṃ, daivatai, taṃ bhikkhuṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyyāsi, bahuñ‍ca tvaṃ, daivatai, apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavaiyyāsi| gaccha tvaṃ, daivatai, amukasmiṃ aukāsai rukkhau vivittau tasmiṃ upagacchā’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā rukkhaṃ chindissantipi chaidāpaissantipi aikindriyaṃ  samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jīvaṃ vihaiṭhaissantī’’ti!

assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āḷavakā bhikkhū rukkhaṃ chindissantipi chaidāpaissantipī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, rukkhaṃ chindathāpi chaidāpaithāpī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, rukkhaṃ chindissathāpi, chaidāpaissathāpi! jīvasañ‍ñinau hi, maughapurisā, manussā rukkhasmiṃ,  naitaṃ maughapurisā appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

90. ‘‘bhūtagāmapātabyatāya pācittiya’’nti|

91. bhūtagāmau  nāma pañ‍ca bījajātāni – mūlabījaṃ, khandhabījaṃ, phaḷubījaṃ, aggabījaṃ, bījabījamaiva bījabījañ‍caiva (itipi) pañ‍camaṃ|

mūlabījaṃ  nāma – haliddi, siṅgivairaṃ, vacā, vacattaṃ, ativisā, kaṭukarauhiṇī, usīraṃ, bhaddamūttakaṃ, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi mūlai jāyanti, mūlai sañ‍jāyanti, aitaṃ mūlabījaṃ nāma|

khandhabījaṃ nāma – assatthau, nigraudhau, pilakkhau, udumbarau, kacchakau, kapitthanau, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi khandhai jāyanti, khandhai sañ‍jāyanti, aitaṃ khandhabījaṃ nāma|

phaḷubījaṃ nāma – ucchu, vaiḷu, naḷau, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi pabbai jāyanti, pabbai  sañ‍jāyanti, aitaṃ phaḷubījaṃ nāma|

aggabījaṃ nāma – aj‍jukaṃ, phaṇij‍jakaṃ, hirivairaṃ, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi aggai jāyanti, aggai sañ‍jāyanti, aitaṃ aggabījaṃ nāma|

bījabījaṃ nāma – pubbaṇṇaṃ, aparaṇṇaṃ, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi bījai jāyanti, bījai sañ‍jāyanti, aitaṃ bījabījaṃ nāma|

92. bījai bījasañ‍ñī chindati vā chaidāpaiti vā, bhindati vā bhaidāpaiti vā, pacati vā pacāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| bījai vaimatikau chindati vā chaidāpaiti vā, bhindati vā bhaidāpaiti vā, pacati vā pacāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bījai abījasañ‍ñī chindati vā chaidāpaiti vā, bhindati vā bhaidāpaiti vā, pacati vā pacāpaiti vā, anāpatti| abījai bījasañ‍ñī āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| abījai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| abījai abījasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

93. anāpatti – ‘‘imaṃ jāna, imaṃ daihi, imaṃ āhara, iminā atthau, imaṃ kappiyaṃ karauhī’’ti bhaṇati, asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

bhūtagāmasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. añ‍ñavādakasikkhāpadaṃ

94. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā chan‍nau anācāraṃ ācaritvā saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati – ‘‘kau āpan‍nau, kiṃ āpan‍nau, kismiṃ āpan‍nau, kathaṃ āpan‍nau, kaṃ bhaṇatha, kiṃ bhaṇathā’’ti? yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā chan‍nau saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarissati – kau āpan‍nau, kiṃ āpan‍nau, kismiṃ āpan‍nau, kathaṃ āpan‍nau, kaṃ bhaṇatha, kiṃ bhaṇathā’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, chan‍na, saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarasi – kau āpan‍nau, kiṃ āpan‍nau, kismiṃ āpan‍nau, kathaṃ āpan‍nau, kaṃ bhaṇatha, kiṃ bhaṇathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarissasi – kau āpan‍nau, kiṃ āpan‍nau, kismiṃ āpan‍nau, kathaṃ āpan‍nau, kaṃ bhaṇatha, kiṃ bhaṇathāti! naitaṃ,  maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau añ‍ñavādakaṃ raupaitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, raupaitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

95. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau añ‍ñavādakaṃ raupaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati| saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau añ‍ñavādakaṃ raupaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati chan‍nassa bhikkhunau añ‍ñavādakassa raupanā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘raupitaṃ saṅghaina chan‍nassa bhikkhunau añ‍ñavādakaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘añ‍ñavādakai pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

96. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā chan‍nau saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau ‘‘añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarantau – ‘‘āpattiṃ āpaj‍jissāmī’’ti tuṇhībhūtau saṅghaṃ vihaisaiti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā chan‍nau saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau tuṇhībhūtau saṅghaṃ vihaisaissatī’’ti…pai॰…  sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, chan‍na, saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau tuṇhībhūtau saṅghaṃ vihaisaisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau tuṇhībhūtau saṅghaṃ vihaisaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau vihaisakaṃ raupaitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, raupaitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

97. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau tuṇhībhūtau saṅghaṃ vihaisaiti| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau vihaisakaṃ raupaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau tuṇhībhūtau saṅghaṃ vihaisaiti| saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau vihaisakaṃ raupaiti| yassāyasmātau khamati chan‍nassa bhikkhunau vihaisakassa raupanā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘raupitaṃ saṅghaina chan‍nassa bhikkhunau vihaisakaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰…  aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

98. ‘‘añ‍ñavādakai  vihaisakai pācittiya’’nti|

99. añ‍ñavādakau  nāma saṅghamajjhai vatthusmiṃ vā āpattiyā vā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau taṃ na kathaitukāmau taṃ na ugghāṭaitukāmau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati – ‘‘kau āpan‍nau, kiṃ āpan‍nau, kismiṃ āpan‍nau, kathaṃ āpan‍nau, kaṃ bhaṇatha, kiṃ bhaṇathā’’ti| aisau añ‍ñavādakau nāma|

vihaisakau nāma saṅghamajjhai vatthusmiṃ vā āpattiyā vā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau taṃ na kathaitukāmau taṃ na ugghāṭaitukāmau tuṇhībhūtau saṅghaṃ vihaisaiti| aisau vihaisakau nāma|

100. āraupitai añ‍ñavādakai saṅghamajjhai vatthusmiṃ vā āpattiyā vā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau taṃ na kathaitukāmau taṃ na ugghāṭaitukāmau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati – ‘‘kau āpan‍nau, kiṃ āpan‍nau, kismiṃ āpan‍nau, kathaṃ āpan‍nau, kaṃ bhaṇatha, kiṃ bhaṇathā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āraupitai vihaisakai saṅghamajjhai vatthusmiṃ vā āpattiyā vā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau taṃ na kathaitukāmau taṃ na ugghāṭaitukāmau tuṇhībhūtau saṅghaṃ vihaisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| raupitai añ‍ñavādakai saṅghamajjhai vatthusmiṃ vā āpattiyā vā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau taṃ na kathaitukāmau taṃ na ugghāṭaitukāmau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati – ‘‘kau āpan‍nau, kiṃ āpan‍nau, kismiṃ āpan‍nau, kathaṃ āpan‍nau, kaṃ bhaṇatha, kiṃ bhaṇathā’’ti, āpatti pācittiyassa| raupitai vihaisakai saṅghamajjhai vatthusmiṃ vā āpattiyā vā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau taṃ na kathaitukāmau taṃ na ugghāṭaitukāmau tuṇhībhūtau saṅghaṃ vihaisaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

101. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī añ‍ñavādakai vihaisakai, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikau añ‍ñavādakai vihaisakai, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī añ‍ñavādakai vihaisakai, āpatti pācittiyassa| adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

102. anāpatti  ajānantau pucchati, gilānau vā na kathaiti; ‘‘saṅghassa bhaṇḍanaṃ vā  kalahau vā viggahau vā vivādau vā bhavissatī’’ti na kathaiti; ‘‘saṅghabhaidau vā saṅgharāji vā bhavissatī’’ti na kathaiti; ‘‘adhammaina vā vaggaina vā nakammārahassa vā kammaṃ karissatī’’ti na kathaiti; ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

añ‍ñavādakasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. ujjhāpanakasikkhāpadaṃ

103. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau saṅghassa saināsanañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaiti bhattāni ca uddisati| taina khau pana samayaina maittiyabhūmajakā maittiyabhūmmajakā (sī॰ syā॰) bhikkhū navakā caiva haunti appapuñ‍ñā ca| yāni saṅghassa lāmakāni saināsanāni tāni taisaṃ pāpuṇanti lāmakāni ca bhattāni| tai āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ bhikkhū ujjhāpainti – ‘‘chandāya dabbau mal‍laputtau saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti, chandāya ca bhattāni uddisatī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ bhikkhū ujjhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ bhikkhū ujjhāpaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ bhikkhū ujjhāpaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘ujjhāpanakai pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

104. taina  khau pana samayaina maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā ujjhāpanakaṃ paṭikkhitta’’nti, ‘‘aittāvatā bhikkhū saussantī’’ti vihaisissantīti (itipi) bhikkhūnaṃ sāmantā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ khiyyanti – ‘‘chandāya dabbau mal‍laputtau saināsanaṃ  pañ‍ñapaiti, chandāya ca bhattāni uddisatī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ khiyyissantī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ khiyyathā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi  buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ khiyyissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

105. ‘‘ujjhāpanakai khiyyanakai pācittiya’’nti|

106. ujjhāpanakaṃ nāma upasampan‍naṃ saṅghaina sammataṃ saināsanapañ‍ñāpakaṃ vā bhattuddaisakaṃ vā yāgubhājakaṃ vā phalabhājakaṃ vā khaj‍jabhājakaṃ vā appamattakavissaj‍jakaṃ vā avaṇṇaṃ kattukāmau, ayasaṃ kattukāmau, maṅkukattukāmau, upasampan‍naṃ ujjhāpaiti vā khiyyati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī ujjhāpanakai khiyyanakai āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikau ujjhāpanakai khiyyanakai āpatti  pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī ujjhāpanakai khiyyanakai āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍naṃ ujjhāpaiti vā khiyyati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| upasampan‍naṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ saināsanapañ‍ñāpakaṃ vā bhattuddaisakaṃ vā yāgubhājakaṃ vā phalabhājakaṃ vā khaj‍jabhājakaṃ vā appamattakavissaj‍jakaṃ vā avaṇṇaṃ kattukāmau, ayasaṃ kattukāmau, maṅkukattukāmau, upasampan‍naṃ vā anupasampan‍naṃ vā ujjhāpaiti vā khiyyati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍naṃ saṅghaina sammataṃ vā asammataṃ vā saināsanapañ‍ñāpakaṃ vā bhattuddaisakaṃ vā yāgubhājakaṃ vā phalabhājakaṃ vā khaj‍jabhājakaṃ vā appamattakavissaj‍jakaṃ vā avaṇṇaṃ kattukāmau, ayasaṃ kattukāmau, maṅkukattukāmau, upasampan‍naṃ vā anupasampan‍naṃ vā ujjhāpaiti vā khiyyati  vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

107. anāpatti pakatiyā chandā dausā mauhā bhayā karauntaṃ ujjhāpaiti vā khiyyati vā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

ujjhāpanakasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamasaināsanasikkhāpadaṃ

108. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū haimantikai kālai ajjhaukāsai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā kāyaṃ autāpaintā kālai āraucitai taṃ pak‍kamantā naiva uddhariṃsu na uddharāpaisuṃ, anāpucchā pak‍kamiṃsu| saināsanaṃ auvaṭṭhaṃ hauti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathaṃ hi nāma bhikkhū ajjhaukāsai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā taṃ pak‍kamantā naiva uddharissanti na uddharāpaissanti, anāpucchā pak‍kamissanti, saināsanaṃ auvaṭṭha’’nti! atha khau tai bhikkhū tai anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū ajjhaukāsai…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

109. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu saṅghikaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā bhisiṃ vā kaucchaṃ vā ajjhaukāsai  santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

110. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ajjhaukāsai vasitvā kālassaiva saināsanaṃ abhiharanti| addasā khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū kālassaiva saināsanaṃ abhiharantai| disvāna aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha māsai avassikasaṅkaitai vasituṃ avassikasaṃkaitai (itipi) maṇḍapai  vā rukkhamūlai vā yattha kākā vā kulalā vā na ūhadanti tattha saināsanaṃ nikkhipitu’’nti|

111. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

saṅghikaṃ nāma saṅghassa din‍naṃ hauti paric‍cattaṃ|

mañ‍cau  nāma cattārau mañ‍cā – masārakau, bundikābaddhau, kuḷīrapādakau, āhac‍capādakau|

pīṭhaṃ  nāma cattāri pīṭhāni – masārakaṃ, bundikābaddhaṃ, kuḷīrapādakaṃ, āhac‍capādakaṃ|

bhisi nāma pañ‍ca bhisiyau – uṇṇabhisi, cauḷabhiti, vākabhisi, tiṇabhisi, paṇṇabhisi|

kaucchaṃ nāma – vākamayaṃ vā usīramayaṃ vā muñ‍jamayaṃ vā pabbajamayaṃ babbajamayaṃ (sī॰) vā antau saṃvaiṭhaitvā baddhaṃ hauti|

santharitvāti sayaṃ santharitvā|

santharāpaitvāti añ‍ñaṃ santharāpaitvā| anupasampan‍naṃ santharāpaiti, tassa  palibaudhau| upasampan‍naṃ santharāpaiti, santhārakassa santhatassa (itipi) palibaudhau|

taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyyāti na sayaṃ uddharaiyya|

na uddharāpaiyyāti na añ‍ñaṃ uddharāpaiyya|

anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyyāti bhikkhuṃ vā sāmaṇairaṃ vā ārāmikaṃ vā anāpucchā majjhimassa purisassa laiḍḍupātaṃ atik‍kamantassa āpatti pācittiyassa|

112. saṅghikai saṅghikasañ‍ñī ajjhaukāsai santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya na uddharāpaiyya anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṅghikai vaimatikau…pai॰… saṅghikai puggalikasañ‍ñī ajjhaukāsai santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, āpatti pācittiyassa|

cimilikaṃ vā uttarattharaṇaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā taṭṭikaṃ vā cammakhaṇḍaṃ vā pādapuñchaniṃ vā  phalakapīṭhaṃ vā ajjhaukāsai santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai saṅghikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai puggalikasañ‍ñī añ‍ñassa puggalikai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| attanau puggalikai anāpatti|

113. anāpatti  uddharitvā gacchati, uddharāpaitvā gacchati, āpucchaṃ gacchati, autāpaintau gacchati, kainaci palibuddhaṃ hauti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

paṭhamasaināsanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyasaināsanasikkhāpadaṃ

114. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū sahāyakā haunti| tai vasantāpi aikatauva vasanti, pak‍kamantāpi aikatauva pak‍kamanti| tai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ saṅghikai vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā taṃ pak‍kamantā naiva uddhariṃsu na uddharāpaisuṃ, anāpucchā pak‍kamiṃsu| saināsanaṃ upacikāhi khāyitaṃ hauti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū saṅghikai vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā taṃ pak‍kamantā naiva uddharissanti na uddharāpaissanti, anāpucchā pak‍kamissanti, saināsanaṃ upacikāhi khāyita’’nti! atha khau tai bhikkhū sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū saṅghikai vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā taṃ pak‍kamantā naiva uddhariṃsu na uddharāpaisuṃ, anāpucchā pak‍kamiṃsu, saināsanaṃ upacikāhi khāyita’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā saṅghikai vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā taṃ pak‍kamantā naiva uddharissanti na uddharāpaissanti, anāpucchā pak‍kamissanti, saināsanaṃ upacikāhi khāyitaṃ! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

115. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu saṅghikai vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

116. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

saṅghikau nāma vihārau saṅghassa din‍nau hauti paric‍cattau|

saiyyaṃ nāma bhisi, cimilikā uttarattharaṇaṃ, bhūmattharaṇaṃ, taṭṭikā, cammakhaṇḍau, nisīdanaṃ, pac‍cattharaṇaṃ, tiṇasanthārau, paṇṇasanthārau|

santharitvāti sayaṃ santharitvā|

santharāpaitvāti añ‍ñaṃ santharāpaitvā|

taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyyāti na sayaṃ uddharaiyya|

na uddharāpaiyyāti na añ‍ñaṃ uddharāpaiyya|

anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyyāti bhikkhuṃ vā sāmaṇairaṃ vā ārāmikaṃ  vā anāpucchā parikkhittassa ārāmassa parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kamantassa āpatti pācittiyassa| aparikkhittassa ārāmassa upacāraṃ atik‍kamantassa āpatti pācittiyassa| saṅghikai saṅghikasañ‍ñī saiyyaṃ santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṅghikai vaimatikau saiyyaṃ santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṅghikai puggalikasañ‍ñī saiyyaṃ santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā  vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, āpatti pācittiyassa|

117. vihārassa upacārai vā upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ vā maṇḍapai vā rukkhamūlai vā saiyyaṃ santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā vihārai vā vihārassūpacārai vā upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ vā maṇḍapai vā rukkhamūlai vā santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā taṃ pak‍kamantau naiva uddharaiyya na uddharāpaiyya, anāpucchaṃ vā gacchaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

puggalikai  saṅghikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai puggalikasañ‍ñī añ‍ñassa puggalikai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| attanau puggalikai anāpatti|

118. anāpatti  uddharitvā gacchati, uddharāpaitvā gacchati, āpucchaṃ gacchati, kainaci palibuddhaṃ hauti, sāpaikkhau gantvā tattha ṭhitau āpucchati, kainaci palibuddhau hauti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dutiyasaināsanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. anupakhaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

119. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū varasaiyyāyau palibundhainti, thairā bhikkhū vuṭṭhāpainti aittha tai iti kammapadaṃ ūnaṃ viya dissati| atha khau chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau mayaṃ upāyaina idhaiva vassaṃ vasaiyyāmā’’ti? atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū thairai bhikkhū anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappainti – yassa sambādhau bhavissati sau pak‍kamissatīti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū thairai bhikkhū anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ …pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, thairai bhikkhū anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, thairai bhikkhū anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

120. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu saṅghikai vihārai jānaṃ pubbupagataṃ bhikkhuṃ anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaiyya – yassa sambādhau bhavissati sau pak‍kamissatī’ti, aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiya’’nti|

121. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ 2.0251 atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

saṅghikau  nāma vihārau saṅghassa din‍nau hauti paric‍cattau|

jānāti  nāma vuḍḍhauti jānāti, gilānauti jānāti, saṅghaina din‍nauti jānāti|

anupakhaj‍jāti anupavisitvā|

saiyyaṃ kappaiyyāti mañ‍cassa vā pīṭhassa vā pavisantassa vā nikkhamantassa vā upacārai saiyyaṃ santharati vā santharāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| abhinisīdati vā abhinipaj‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñanti na añ‍ñau kauci pac‍cayau hauti anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaituṃ|

122. saṅghikai saṅghikasañ‍ñī anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṅghikai vaimatikau anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṅghikai puggalikasañ‍ñī anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

mañ‍cassa vā pīṭhassa vā pavisantassa vā nikkhamantassa vā upacāraṃ ṭhapaitvā saiyyaṃ santharati vā santharāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| abhinisīdati vā abhinipaj‍jati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| vihārassa upacārai vā upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ vā maṇḍapai vā rukkhamūlai vā ajjhaukāsai vā saiyyaṃ santharati vā santharāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| abhinisīdati vā abhinippaj‍jati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai saṅghikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai puggalikasañ‍ñī añ‍ñassa puggalikai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| attanau puggalikai anāpatti|

123. anāpatti gilānau pavisati, sītaina vā uṇhaina vā pīḷitau pavisati, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

anupakhaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. nik‍kaḍḍhanasikkhāpadaṃ

124. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū añ‍ñataraṃ pac‍cantimaṃ mahāvihāraṃ paṭisaṅkharaunti – ‘‘idha mayaṃ vassaṃ vasissāmā’’ti| addasaṃsu khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū vihāraṃ paṭisaṅkharauntai| disvāna aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘imai, āvusau, sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū vihāraṃ paṭisaṅkharaunti| handa nai vuṭṭhāpaissāmā’’ti! aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘āgamaithāvusau, yāva paṭisaṅkharaunti; paṭisaṅkhatai vuṭṭhāpaissāmā’’ti|

atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘uṭṭhaithāvusau, amhākaṃ vihārau pāpuṇātī’’ti| ‘‘nanu, āvusau, paṭikac‍caiva paṭigac‍caiva (sī॰) ācikkhitabbaṃ, mayañ‍cañ‍ñaṃ paṭisaṅkharaiyyāmā’’ti| ‘‘nanu, āvusau, saṅghikau vihārau’’ti? ‘‘āmāvusau, saṅghikau vihārau’’ti| ‘‘uṭṭhaithāvusau, amhākaṃ vihārau pāpuṇātī’’ti| ‘‘mahal‍lakau, āvusau, vihārau| tumhaipi vasatha, mayampi vasissāmā’’ti| ‘‘uṭṭhaithāvusau, amhākaṃ vihārau pāpuṇātī’’ti kupitā anattamanā gīvāyaṃ gahaitvā nik‍kaḍḍhanti| tai nik‍kaḍḍhīyamānā raudanti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tumhai, āvusau, raudathā’’ti? ‘‘imai, āvusau, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā amhai saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhantī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā bhikkhū saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, kupitā anattamanā bhikkhū saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhathā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, kupitā anattamanā bhikkhū saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhissatha? naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

125. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ kupitau anattamanau saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhaiyya vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

126. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunti  añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ|

kupitau  anattamanauti anabhiraddhau āhatacittau khilajātau|

saṅghikau nāma vihārau saṅghassa din‍nau hauti paric‍cattau|

nik‍kaḍḍhaiyyāti gabbhai gahaitvā pamukhaṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati, āpatti pācittiyassa| pamukhai gahaitvā bahi nik‍kaḍḍhati, āpatti pācittiyassa| aikaina payaugaina bahukaipi dvārai atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiyyāti añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa duk‍kaṭassa (katthaci)| sakiṃ āṇattau bahukaipi dvārai atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

127. saṅghikai  saṅghikasañ‍ñī kupitau anattamanau nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṅghikai vaimatikau kupitau anattamanau nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṅghikai puggalikasañ‍ñī kupitau anattamanau nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

tassa parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| vihārassa upacārā vā upaṭṭhānasālāya vā maṇḍapā vā rukkhamūlā vā ajjhaukāsā vā nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tassa parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍naṃ vihārā vā vihārassa upacārā vā upaṭṭhānasālāya vā maṇḍapā vā rukkhamūlā vā ajjhaukāsā vā nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tassa parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

puggalikai saṅghikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai puggalikasañ‍ñī añ‍ñassa puggalikai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| attanau puggalikai anāpatti|

128. anāpatti alaj‍jiṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, tassa parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati  vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, ummattakaṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, tassa parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā  nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, bhaṇḍanakārakaṃ kalahakārakaṃ vivādakārakaṃ bhassakārakaṃ saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakaṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti  vā, tassa parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, antaivāsikaṃ vā saddhivihārikaṃ vā na sammā vattantaṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, tassa parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

nik‍kaḍḍhanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. vaihāsakuṭisikkhāpadaṃ

129. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina  dvai bhikkhū saṅghikai vihārai uparivaihāsakuṭiyā viharanti?| aikau haiṭṭhā viharati viharanti, aikau haiṭṭhā (?), aikau upari| uparimau bhikkhu āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ sahasā abhinisīdi| mañ‍capādau nippatitvā patitvā (syā॰) haiṭṭhimassa bhikkhunau matthakai avatthāsi| sau bhikkhu vissaramakāsi| bhikkhū upadhāvitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, āvusau, vissaramakāsī’’ti? atha khau sau bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu saṅghikai vihārai uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ sahasā abhinisīdissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, saṅghikai vihārai uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ sahasā abhinisīdasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, saṅghikai vihārai uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ sahasā abhinisīdissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

130. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu saṅghikai vihārai uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ  vā abhinisīdaiyya vā abhinipaj‍jaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

131. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

saṅghikau nāma vihārau saṅghassa din‍nau hauti paric‍cattau|

vaihāsakuṭi nāma majjhimassa purisassa asīsaghaṭṭā|

āhac‍capādakau nāma mañ‍cau aṅgai vijjhitvā ṭhitau hauti| āhac‍capādakaṃ nāma pīṭhaṃ aṅgai vijjhitvā ṭhitaṃ hauti|

abhinisīdaiyyāti tasmiṃ abhinisīdati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

abhinipaj‍jaiyyāti tasmiṃ abhinipaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

132. saṅghikai saṅghikasañ‍ñī uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā abhinisīdati vā abhinipaj‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṅghikai vaimatikau…pai॰… saṅghikai puggalikasañ‍ñī uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā abhinisīdati vā abhinipaj‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

puggalikai saṅghikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalikai puggalikasañ‍ñī añ‍ñassa puggalikai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| attanau puggalikai, anāpatti|

133. anāpatti – avaihāsakuṭiyā sīsaghaṭṭāya haiṭṭhā aparibhaugaṃ  hauti, padarasañ‍citaṃ hauti, paṭāṇi din‍nā hauti, tasmiṃ ṭhitau gaṇhati vā laggaiti vā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

vaihāsakuṭisikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. mahal‍lakavihārasikkhāpadaṃ

134. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau chan‍nassa upaṭṭhākau mahāmattau āyasmatau chan‍nassa vihāraṃ kārāpaiti| atha khau āyasmā chan‍nau katapariyausitaṃ vihāraṃ punappunaṃ chādāpaiti, punappunaṃ  laipāpaiti| atibhāritau vihārau paripati| atha khau āyasmā chan‍nau tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca saṃkaḍḍhantau añ‍ñatarassa brāhmaṇassa yavakhaittaṃ dūsaisi| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā amhākaṃ yavakhaittaṃ dūsaissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa brāhmaṇassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā chan‍nau katapariyausitaṃ vihāraṃ punappunaṃ chādāpaissati, punappunaṃ laipāpaissati, atibhāritau vihārau paripatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, chan‍na, katapariyausitaṃ vihāraṃ punappunaṃ chādāpaisi, punappunaṃ laipāpaisi, atibhāritau vihārau paripatī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, katapariyausitaṃ vihāraṃ punappunaṃ chādāpaissasi, punappunaṃ laipāpaissasi, atibhāritau vihārau paripati! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

135. ‘‘mahal‍lakaṃ pana bhikkhunā vihāraṃ kārayamānaina yāvadvārakausā aggaḷaṭṭhapanāya ālaukasandhiparikammāya dvatticchadanassa pariyāya appaharitai ṭhitaina adhiṭṭhātabbaṃ| tatau cai uttariṃ appaharitaipi ṭhitau adhiṭṭhahaiyya pācittiya’’nti|

136. mahal‍lakau nāma vihārau sassāmikau vuc‍cati|

vihārau nāma ul‍littau vā hauti avalittau vā ul‍littāvalittau vā|

kārayamānaināti karauntau vā kārāpaintau vā|

yāva dvārakausāti piṭṭhasaṅghāṭassa pīṭhasaṃghāṭassa (itipi), piṭṭhisaṅghāṭassa (syā॰) samantā hatthapāsā|

aggaḷaṭṭhapanāyāti  dvāraṭṭhapanāya|

ālaukasandhiparikammāyāti vātapānaparikammāya saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭikaṃ|

dvatticchadanassa  pariyāyaṃ appaharitai ṭhitaina adhiṭṭhātabbanti – haritaṃ nāma pubbaṇṇaṃ aparaṇṇaṃ| sacai haritai ṭhitau adhiṭṭhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| maggaina chādaintassa dvai maggai adhiṭṭhahitvā tatiyaṃ maggaṃ āṇāpaitvā pak‍kamitabbaṃ| pariyāyaina chādaintassa dvai pariyāyai adhiṭṭhahitvā tatiyaṃ pariyāyaṃ āṇāpaitvā pak‍kamitabbaṃ|

137. tatau cai uttari appaharitaipi ṭhitau adhiṭṭhahaiyyāti iṭṭhakāya 2.0262 chādaintassa iṭṭhakiṭṭhakāya āpatti pācittiyassa| silāya chādaintassa silāya silāya āpatti pācittiyassa| sudhāya chādaintassa piṇḍai piṇḍai āpatti pācittiyassa| tiṇaina chādaintassa karaḷai karaḷai āpatti pācittiyassa| paṇṇaina chādaintassa paṇṇai paṇṇai āpatti pācittiyassa|

atiraikadvattipariyāyai atiraikasañ‍ñī adhiṭṭhāti, āpatti pācittiyassa| atiraikadvattipariyāyai vaimatikau adhiṭṭhāti, āpatti pācittiyassa| atiraikadvattipariyāyai ūnakasañ‍ñī adhiṭṭhāti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

ūnakadvattipariyāyai atiraikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakadvattipariyāyai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakadvattipariyāyai ūnakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

138. anāpatti dvattipariyāyai, ūnakadvattipariyāyai, laiṇai, guhāya, tiṇakuṭikāya, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, vāsāgāraṃ ṭhapaitvā sabbattha anāpatti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

mahal‍lakavihārasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. sappāṇakasikkhāpadaṃ

139. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai| taina khau pana samayaina āḷavakā bhikkhū navakammaṃ karauntā jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇampi mattikampi siñ‍cantipi siñ‍cāpaintipi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āḷavakā  bhikkhū jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇampi mattikampi siñ‍cissantipi siñ‍cāpaissantipī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āḷavakai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇampi mattikampi siñ‍cathapi siñ‍cāpaithapī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇampi mattikampi siñ‍cissathapi siñ‍cāpaissathapi! naitaṃ,  maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

140. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇaṃ vā mattikaṃ vā siñ‍caiyya vā siñ‍cāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

141. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

jānāti nāma jānaṃ nāma (syā॰) sāmaṃ vā jānāti añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti|

siñ‍caiyyāti sayaṃ siñ‍cati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

siñ‍cāpaiyyāti  añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa (katthaci)| sakiṃ āṇattau bahukampi siñ‍cati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

142. sappāṇakai sappāṇakasañ‍ñī tiṇaṃ vā mattikaṃ vā siñ‍cati vā siñ‍cāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| sappāṇakai vaimatikau tiṇaṃ vā mattikaṃ vā siñ‍cati vā siñ‍cāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sappāṇakai appāṇakasañ‍ñī tiṇaṃ vā mattikaṃ vā siñ‍cati  vā siñ‍cāpaiti vā, anāpatti| appāṇakai sappāṇakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| appāṇakai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| appāṇakai appāṇakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

143. anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sappāṇakasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

bhūtagāmavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

bhūtaṃ  añ‍ñāya ujjhāyaṃ, pak‍kamantaina tai duvai|

pubbai nik‍kaḍḍhanāhac‍ca, dvāraṃ sappāṇakaina cāti||

3. auvādavaggau

1. auvādasikkhāpadaṃ

144. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantā lābhinau haunti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| atha khau chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘aitarahi khau, āvusau, thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantā lābhinau haunti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| handāvusau, mayampi bhikkhuniyau auvadāmā’’ti| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘amhaipi,  bhaginiyau, upasaṅkamatha; mayampi auvadissāmā’’ti|

atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau yaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunīnaṃ  parittañ‍ñaiva dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā divasaṃ tiracchānakathāya vītināmaitvā uyyaujaisuṃ – ‘‘gacchatha, bhaginiyau’’ti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhikkhuniyau, auvādau iddhau ahausī’’ti? ‘‘kutau, bhantai, auvādau iddhau bhavissati! ayyā chabbaggiyā parittañ‍ñaiva dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā divasaṃ tiracchānakathāya vītināmaitvā uyyaujaisu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā tā bhikkhuniyau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kamiṃsu|

atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīnaṃ  parittañ‍ñaiva dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā divasaṃ tiracchānakathāya vītināmaitvā uyyaujaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhunīnaṃ parittañ‍ñaiva dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā divasaṃ tiracchānakathāya vītināmaitvā uyyaujaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunauvādakaṃ samman‍nituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau| yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

145. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ bhikkhunauvādakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ bhikkhunauvādakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau bhikkhunauvādakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ  bhikkhunauvādakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau bhikkhunauvādakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau  saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā  dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

146. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu asammatau bhikkhuniyau auvadaiyya pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

147. taina khau pana samayaina thairā bhikkhū sammatā bhikkhuniyau auvadantā tathaiva lābhinau haunti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| atha khau chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘aitarahi khau, āvusau, thairā bhikkhū sammatā bhikkhuniyau auvadantā tathaiva  lābhinau haunti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| handāvusau, mayampi nissīmaṃ gantvā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ bhikkhunauvādakaṃ samman‍nitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadāmā’’ti| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nissīmaṃ gantvā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ bhikkhunauvādakaṃ samman‍nitvā bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mayampi, bhaginiyau, sammatā| amhaipi upasaṅkamatha| mayampi auvadissāmā’’ti|

atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau yaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū  bhikkhunīnaṃ parittañ‍ñaiva dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā divasaṃ tiracchānakathāya vītināmaitvā uyyaujaisuṃ – ‘‘gacchatha bhaginiyau’’ti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhikkhuniyau, auvādau iddhau ahausī’’ti? ‘‘kutau, bhantai, auvādau iddhau bhavissati! ayyā chabbaggiyā parittañ‍ñaiva dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā divasaṃ tiracchānakathāya vītināmaitvā uyyaujaisu’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā tā bhikkhuniyau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi…pai॰… atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīnaṃ parittañ‍ñaiva dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā divasaṃ tiracchānakathāya vītināmaitvā uyyaujaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhunīnaṃ parittañ‍ñaiva dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā divasaṃ tiracchānakathāya vītināmaitvā uyyaujaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ bhikkhunauvādakaṃ samman‍nituṃ| sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā  pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasā anupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau; kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau; yaibhuyyaina bhikkhunīnaṃ piyau hauti manāpau; paṭibalau hauti bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ; na khau panaitaṃ pana taṃ (?) bhagavantaṃ uddissa pabbajitāya kāsāyavatthavasanāya garudhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍napubbau hauti; vīsativassau vā hauti atiraikavīsativassau vā – anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaihi aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ bhikkhunauvādakaṃ samman‍nitu’’nti|

148. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

asammatau nāma ñatticatutthaina kammaina asammatau|

bhikkhuniyau nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

auvadaiyyāti aṭṭhahi garudhammaihi auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| añ‍ñaina dhammaina auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aikatauupasampan‍naṃ auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

149. taina  sammataina bhikkhunā parivaiṇaṃ sammaj‍jitvā pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā dutiyaṃ gahaitvā nisīditabbaṃ| bhikkhunīhi tattha gantvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīditabbaṃ| taina bhikkhunā pucchitabbā – ‘‘samaggāttha, bhaginiyau’’ti? sacai ‘‘samaggāmhāyyā’’ti bhaṇanti, ‘‘vattanti, bhaginiyau, aṭṭha garudhammā’’ti? sacai ‘‘vattantāyyā’’ti bhaṇanti, ‘‘aisau, bhaginiyau, auvādau’’ti niyyādaitabbau niyyātaitabbau (itipi)| sacai ‘‘na vattantāyyā’’ti bhaṇanti, ausāraitabbā| ‘‘vassasatūpasampan‍nāya bhikkhuniyā tadahupasampan‍nassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ kātabbaṃ; ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā  yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau| na bhikkhuniyā abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ vasitabbaṃ; ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau| anvaddhamāsaṃ bhikkhuniyā bhikkhusaṅghatau dvai dhammā pac‍cāsīsitabbā pac‍cāsiṃsitabbā (itipi) upausathapucchakañ‍ca auvādupasaṅkamanañ‍ca, ayampi dhammau…pai॰… vassaṃ vuṭṭhāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai tīhi ṭhānaihi pavāraitabbaṃ diṭṭhaina vā sutaina vā parisaṅkāya vā; ayampi dhammau…pai॰… garudhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai pakkhamānattaṃ caritabbaṃ; ayampi  dhammau…pai॰… dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya ubhatausaṅghai upasampadā pariyaisitabbā; ayampi dhammau…pai॰… na bhikkhuniyā kaina ci pariyāyaina bhikkhu ak‍kausitabbau paribhāsitabbau; ayampi dhammau…pai॰… aj‍jataggai auvaṭau bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūsu vacanapathau, anauvaṭau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīsu vacanapathau; ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau’’ti|

sacai ‘‘samaggāmhāyyā’’ti bhaṇantaṃ añ‍ñaṃ dhammaṃ bhaṇati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sacai ‘‘vaggāmhāyyā’’ti bhaṇantaṃ aṭṭha garudhammai bhaṇati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| auvādaṃ aniyyādaitvā añ‍ñaṃ dhammaṃ bhaṇati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

150. adhammakammai  adhammakammasañ‍ñī vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī vaggaṃ bhikkhunīsaṅghaṃ vaimatikau auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai vaimatikau vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaimatikau auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai  dhammakammasañ‍ñī vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaimatikau auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai  adhammakammasañ‍ñī samaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī samaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaimatikau auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī samaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai vaimatikau samaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati…pai॰… vaimatikau auvadati…pai॰… samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī samaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati…pai॰… vaimatikau auvadati…pai॰… samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

151. dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaimatikau auvadati…pai॰… samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

dhammakammai vaimatikau vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati…pai॰… vaimatikau auvadati…pai॰… samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

dhammakammai  dhammakammasañ‍ñī vaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati…pai॰… vaimatikau auvadati…pai॰… samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

dhammakammai  adhammakammasañ‍ñī samaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati…pai॰… vaimatikau auvadati…pai॰… samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

dhammakammai vaimatikau samaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati…pai॰… vaimatikau auvadati…pai॰… samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī samaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī samaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ vaimatikau auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī samaggaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ samaggasañ‍ñī auvadati, anāpatti|

152. anāpatti uddaisaṃ daintau, paripucchaṃ daintau, ‘‘ausāraihi ayyā’’ti vuc‍camānau, ausāraiti, pañhaṃ pucchati, pañhaṃ puṭṭhau kathaiti, añ‍ñassatthāya bhaṇantaṃ bhikkhuniyau suṇanti, sikkhamānāya, sāmaṇairiyā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

auvādasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. atthaṅgatasikkhāpadaṃ

153. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadanti pariyāyaina| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau cūḷapanthakassa pariyāyau hauti bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ| bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na dāni aj‍ja auvādau iddhau bhavissati, tañ‍ñaiva dāni udānaṃ ayyau cūḷapanthakau punappunaṃ bhaṇissatī’’ti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau yaināyasmā cūḷapanthakau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ cūḷapanthakaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tā bhikkhuniyau āyasmā cūḷapanthakau aitadavauca – ‘‘samaggāttha, bhaginiyau’’ti? ‘‘samaggāmhāyyā’’ti| ‘‘vattanti, bhaginiyau, aṭṭha garudhammā’’ti? ‘‘vattantāyyā’’ti| ‘‘aisau, bhaginiyau, auvādau’’ti niyyādaitvā imaṃ udānaṃ punappunaṃ abhāsi –

udā॰ 37 ‘‘adhicaitasau  appamaj‍jatau, muninau maunapathaisu sikkhatau|

saukā na bhavanti tādinau, upasantassa sadā satīmatau’’ti||

bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘nanu avaucumhā – na dāni aj‍ja auvādau iddhau bhavissati, tañ‍ñaiva dāni udānaṃ ayyau cūḷapanthakau punappunaṃ  bhaṇissatī’’ti! assausi khau āyasmā cūḷapanthakau tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau āyasmā cūḷapanthakau vaihāsaṃ abbhuggantvā ākāsai antalikkhai caṅkamatipi tiṭṭhatipi nisīdatipi saiyyampi kappaiti dhūmāyatipi paj‍jalatipi antaradhāyatipi, tañ‍caiva tañ‍ñaiva (itipi) udānaṃ bhaṇati añ‍ñañ‍ca bahuṃ buddhavacanaṃ| bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau, na vata nau itau pubbai auvādau aivaṃ iddhau bhūtapubbau yathā ayyassa cūḷapanthakassā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā cūḷapanthakau tā bhikkhuniyau yāva samandhakārā auvaditvā uyyaujaisi – gacchatha bhaginiyauti|

atha  khau tā bhikkhuniyau nagaradvārai thakitai bahinagarai vasitvā kālassaiva nagaraṃ pavisanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘abrahmacāriniyau imā bhikkhuniyau; ārāmai bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ vasitvā idāni nagaraṃ pavisantī’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ  ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā cūḷapanthakau atthaṅgatai sūriyai bhikkhuniyau auvadissatī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, cūḷapanthaka, atthaṅgatai sūriyai bhikkhuniyau auvadasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, cūḷapanthaka, atthaṅgatai sūriyai bhikkhuniyau auvadissasi! naitaṃ, cūḷapanthaka, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

154. ‘‘sammataupi  cai bhikkhu atthaṅgatai sūriyai bhikkhuniyau auvadaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

155. sammatau nāma ñatticatutthaina kammaina sammatau|

atthaṅgatai sūriyaiti auggatai sūriyai|

bhikkhunī  nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

auvadaiyyāti aṭṭhahi vā garudhammaihi añ‍ñaina vā dhammaina auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

156. atthaṅgatai atthaṅgatasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| atthaṅgatai vaimatikau auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| atthaṅgatai anatthaṅgatasañ‍ñī auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aikatauupasampan‍nāya auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anatthaṅgatai atthaṅgatasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anatthaṅgatai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anatthaṅgatai anatthaṅgatasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

157. anāpatti uddaisaṃ daintau, paripucchaṃ daintau, ‘‘ausāraihi ayyā’’ti vuc‍camānau, ausāraiti, pañhaṃ pucchati, pañhaṃ puṭṭhau kathaiti, añ‍ñassatthāya bhaṇantaṃ bhikkhuniyau suṇanti, sikkhamānāya sāmaṇairiyā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

atthaṅgatasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. bhikkhunupassayasikkhāpadaṃ

158. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau auvadanti| bhikkhuniyau chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aithāyyai, auvādaṃ  gamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘yampi yaṃ hi (ka॰) mayaṃ, ayyai, gacchaiyyāma auvādassa kāraṇā, ayyā chabbaggiyā idhaiva āgantvā amhai auvadantī’’ti| bhikkhuniyau ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadissantī’’ti chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau auvādaṃ na gacchissantīti (sī॰)! atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadissantī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadathā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ  bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

159. taina khau pana samayaina mahāpajāpati gautamī gilānā hauti| thairā bhikkhū yaina mahāpajāpati gautamī tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, gautami, khamanīyaṃ kac‍ci yāpanīya’’nti? ‘‘na mai, ayyā, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ’’| ‘‘iṅghayyā, dhammaṃ daisaithā’’ti| ‘‘na, bhagini, kappati bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau dhammaṃ daisaitu’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na daisaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina mahāpajāpati gautamī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, gautami, khamanīyaṃ kac‍ci yāpanīya’’nti? ‘‘pubbai mai, bhantai, thairā bhikkhū āgantvā dhammaṃ daisainti| taina mai phāsu hauti| idāni pana – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhitta’’nti, kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na daisainti| taina mai na phāsu hautī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā gilānaṃ bhikkhuniṃ auvadituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

160. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadaiyya, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānā hauti bhikkhunī – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

161. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunupassayau  nāma yattha bhikkhuniyau aikarattampi vasanti|

upasaṅkamitvāti tattha gantvā|

bhikkhunī nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

auvadaiyyāti aṭṭhahi garudhammaihi auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñatra samayāti ṭhapaitvā samayaṃ|

gilānā nāma bhikkhunī na sak‍kauti auvādāya vā saṃvāsāya vā gantuṃ|

162. upasampan‍nāya upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā añ‍ñatra samayā auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nāya vaimatikau bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā añ‍ñatra samayā auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā añ‍ñatra samayā auvadati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñaina dhammaina auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aikatauupasampan‍nāya auvadati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

163. anāpatti samayai, uddaisaṃ daintau, paripucchaṃ daintau, ‘‘ausāraihi  ayyā’’ti vuc‍camānau ausāraiti, pañhaṃ pucchati, pañhaṃ puṭṭhau kathaiti, añ‍ñassatthāya bhaṇantaṃ bhikkhuniyau suṇanti, sikkhamānāya sāmaṇairiyā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

bhikkhunupassayasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. āmisasikkhāpadaṃ

164. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantā lābhinau haunti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘na bahukatā thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ; āmisahaitu thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau  auvadantī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū aivaṃ vakkhanti – ‘na bahukatā thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ; āmisahaitu thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantī’’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaitha – ‘na bahukatā thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ; āmisahaitu thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantī’’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, aivaṃ vakkhatha – na bahukatā thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ; āmisahaitu thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantīti! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

165. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘āmisahaitu thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvandatī’ti, pācittiya’’nti|

166. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

āmisahaitūti cīvarahaitu piṇḍapātahaitu saināsanahaitu gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu sak‍kārahaitu garukārahaitu mānanahaitu vandanahaitu pūjanahaitu|

aivaṃ vadaiyyāti upasampan‍naṃ saṅghaina sammataṃ bhikkhunauvādakaṃ avaṇṇaṃ kattukāmau ayasaṃ kattukāmau maṅkukattukāmau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘cīvarahaitu piṇḍapātahaitu saināsanahaitu gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu sak‍kārahaitu garukārahaitu mānanahaitu vandanahaitu pūjanahaitu auvadatī’’ti bhaṇati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

167. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī aivaṃ vadaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikau aivaṃ vadaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī aivaṃ vadaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

upasampan‍naṃ  saṅghaina asammataṃ bhikkhunauvādakaṃ avaṇṇaṃ kattukāmau ayasaṃ kattukāmau maṅkukattukāmau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘cīvarahaitu…pai॰… pūjanahaitu auvadatī’’ti bhaṇati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍naṃ saṅghaina sammataṃ vā asammataṃ vā bhikkhunauvādakaṃ avaṇṇaṃ kattukāmau ayasaṃ kattukāmau maṅkukattukāmau aivaṃ vadaiti  – ‘‘cīvarahaitu piṇḍapātahaitu saināsanahaitu gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu sak‍kārahaitu garukārahaitu mānanahaitu vandanahaitu pūjanahaitu auvadatī’’ti bhaṇati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

168. anāpatti pakatiyā cīvarahaitu piṇḍapātahaitu saināsanahaitu gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu sak‍kārahaitu garukārahaitu mānanahaitu vandanahaitu pūjanahaitu auvadantaṃ bhaṇati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

āmisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. cīvaradānasikkhāpadaṃ

169. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñatarissā visikhāya piṇḍāya carati| añ‍ñatarāpi bhikkhunī tassā visikhāya piṇḍāya carati| atha khau sau bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘gaccha, bhagini, amukasmiṃ aukāsai bhikkhā diyyatī’’ti| sāpi khau aivamāha – ‘‘gacchāyya, amukasmiṃ aukāsai bhikkhā diyyatī’’ti| tai abhiṇhadassanaina sandiṭṭhā ahaisuṃ| taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa cīvaraṃ bhājīyati| atha khau sā bhikkhunī auvādaṃ gantvā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ sau bhikkhu aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ mai, bhagini, cīvarapaṭivīsau paṭiviṃsau (sī॰), paṭivisau (itipi); sādiyissasī’’ti? ‘‘āmāyya, dubbalacīvarāmhī’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu tassā bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ adāsi| saupi khau bhikkhu dubbalacīvarau hauti| bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘karauhi dāni tai, āvusau, cīvara’’nti| atha khau sau bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ dassatī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ adāsī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘ñātikā tai, bhikkhu, añ‍ñātikā’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñātikā, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātakau, maughapurisa, añ‍ñātikāya na jānāti patirūpaṃ vā appatirūpaṃ vā santaṃ vā asantaṃ vā| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ dassasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ dadaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

170. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā bhikkhunīnaṃ pārivattakaṃ pārivaṭṭakaṃ (itipi) cīvaraṃ na dainti| bhikkhuniyau ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā amhākaṃ pārivattakaṃ cīvaraṃ  na dassantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū  tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ ujjhāyantīnaṃ khiyyantīnaṃ vipācaintīnaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ pārivattakaṃ dātuṃ| bhikkhussa, bhikkhuniyā, sikkhamānāya, sāmaṇairassa, sāmaṇairiyā – anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ pārivattakaṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

171. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ dadaiyya, añ‍ñatra pārivattakā, pācittiya’’nti|

172. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

añ‍ñātikā nāma mātitau vā  pititau vā yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā asambaddhā|

bhikkhunī  nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

cīvaraṃ nāma chan‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ vikappanupagaṃ pacchimaṃ vikappanupagapacchimaṃ (sī॰)|

añ‍ñatra pārivattakāti ṭhapaitvā pārivattakaṃ daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

173. añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī cīvaraṃ daiti, añ‍ñatra pārivattakā, āpatti pācittiyassa| añ‍ñātikāya vaimatikau cīvaraṃ daiti, añ‍ñatra pārivattakā, āpatti pācittiyassa| añ‍ñātikāya ñātikasañ‍ñī cīvaraṃ daiti, añ‍ñatra pārivattakā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aikatau upasampan‍nāya cīvaraṃ daiti, añ‍ñatra pārivattakā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya ñātikasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

174. anāpatti ñātikāya, pārivattakaṃ parittaina vā vipulaṃ, vipulaina vā parittaṃ, bhikkhunī vissāsaṃ gaṇhāti, tāvakālikaṃ gaṇhāti, cīvaraṃ ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñaṃ parikkhāraṃ daiti, sikkhamānāya, sāmaṇairiyā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

cīvaradānasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. cīvarasibbanasikkhāpadaṃ

175. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī paṭṭau paṭṭhau (sī॰ syā॰) hauti cīvarakammaṃ kātuṃ| añ‍ñatarā  bhikkhunī yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, ayyau cīvaraṃ sibbatū’’ti| atha khau āyasmā udāyī tassā bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbitvā surattaṃ suparikammakataṃ katvā majjhai paṭibhānacittaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā saṃharitvā nikkhipi| atha khau sā bhikkhunī yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ taṃ, bhantai, cīvara’’nti? ‘‘handa, bhagini, imaṃ cīvaraṃ yathāsaṃhaṭaṃ haritvā nikkhipitvā yadā bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādaṃ āgacchati tadā imaṃ cīvaraṃ pārupitvā bhikkhunisaṅghassa piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau āgacchā’’ti| atha khau sā bhikkhunī taṃ cīvaraṃ yathāsaṃhaṭaṃ haritvā nikkhipitvā yadā bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādaṃ āgacchati tadā taṃ cīvaraṃ pārupitvā bhikkhunisaṅghassa piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau āgacchati| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘yāva chin‍nikā imā bhikkhuniyau dhuttikā ahirikāyau, yatra hi nāma cīvarai paṭibhānacittaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti!

bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kassidaṃ kamma’’nti? ‘‘ayyassa udāyissā’’ti| ‘‘yaipi tai chin‍nakā dhuttakā ahirikā taisampi aivarūpaṃ  na saubhaiyya, kiṃ pana ayyassa udāyissā’’ti! atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā udāyī bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, udāyi, bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘ñātikā tai, udāyi, añ‍ñātikā’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñātikā, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātakau, maughapurisa, añ‍ñātikāya na jānāti patirūpaṃ vā appatirūpaṃ vā pāsādikaṃ vā apāsādikaṃ vā| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

176. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbaiyya vā sibbāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

177. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

añ‍ñātikā nāma mātitau vā pititau vā yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā asambaddhā|

bhikkhunī nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

cīvaraṃ nāma chan‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ|

sibbaiyyāti  sayaṃ sibbati ārāpathai ārāpathai āpatti pācittiyassa|

sibbāpaiyyāti añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| sakiṃ āṇattau bahukampi sibbati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

178. añ‍ñātikāya  añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī cīvaraṃ sibbati vā sibbāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| añ‍ñātikāya vaimatikau cīvaraṃ sibbati vā sibbāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| añ‍ñātikāya ñātikasañ‍ñī cīvaraṃ sibbati vā sibbāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aikatauupasampan‍nāya cīvaraṃ sibbati vā sibbāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya ñātikasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

179. anāpatti ñātikāya, cīvaraṃ ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñaṃ parikkhāraṃ sibbati vā sibbāpaiti vā, sikkhamānāya, sāmaṇairiyā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

cīvarasibbanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. saṃvidhānasikkhāpadaṃ

180. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi  saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘yathaiva mayaṃ sapajāpatikā āhiṇḍāma, aivamaivimai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya āhiṇḍantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jathā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi  nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha|

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya, antamasau gāmantarampi, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|
