181. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū ca bhikkhuniyau  ca sākaitā  sāvatthiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau tai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mayampi ayyaihi saddhiṃ gamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘na, bhaginī, kappati bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jituṃ| tumhai vā paṭhamaṃ gacchatha mayaṃ vā gamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘ayyā, bhantai, aggapurisā| ayyāva paṭhamaṃ gacchantū’’ti| atha khau tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ pacchā gacchantīnaṃ antarāmaggai caurā acchindiṃsu ca dūsaisuñ‍ca| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, satthagamanīyai maggai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

182. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya, antamasau gāmantarampi, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau | satthagamanīyau hauti maggau sāsaṅkasammatau sappaṭibhayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

183. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunī nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

saddhinti  aikatau|

saṃvidhāyāti – ‘‘gacchāma, bhagini, gacchāmāyya; gacchāmāyya, gacchāma, bhagini; aj‍ja vā hiyyau  vā parai vā gacchāmā’’ti saṃvidahati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

antamasau gāmantarampīti kuk‍kuṭasampātai gāmai, gāmantarai gāmantarai āpatti pācittiyassa| agāmakai arañ‍ñai, addhayaujanai addhayaujanai āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñatra samayāti ṭhapaitvā samayaṃ|

satthagamanīyau nāma maggau na sak‍kā hauti vinā satthaina gantuṃ|

sāsaṅkaṃ nāma tasmiṃ maggai yasmiṃ maggai (?) caurānaṃ niviṭṭhaukāsau dissati, bhuttaukāsau dissati, ṭhitaukāsau dissati, nisin‍naukāsau dissati, nipan‍naukāsau dissati|

sappaṭibhayaṃ nāma tasmiṃ maggai cauraihi manussā hatā dissanti, viluttā dissanti, ākauṭitā dissanti, sappaṭibhayaṃ  gantvā appaṭibhayaṃ dassaitvā uyyaujaitabbā – ‘‘gacchatha bhaginiyau’’ti|

184. saṃvidahitai saṃvidahitasañ‍ñī aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, antamasau gāmantarampi, añ‍ñatra samayā, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṃvidahitai vaimatikau aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, antamasau gāmantarampi, añ‍ñatra samayā, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṃvidahitai, asaṃvidahitasañ‍ñī aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, antamasau gāmantarampi, añ‍ñatra samayā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhu saṃvidahati bhikkhunī na saṃvidahati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asaṃvidahitai saṃvidahitasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asaṃvidahitai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asaṃvidahitai asaṃvidahitasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

185. anāpatti  samayai, asaṃvidahitvā gacchati, bhikkhunī saṃvidahati, bhikkhu na saṃvidahati, visaṅkaitaina gacchanti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

saṃvidhānasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. nāvābhiruhanasikkhāpadaṃ

186. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ aikanāvaṃ (syā॰) abhiruhanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘yathaiva mayaṃ sapajāpatikā nāvāya aikanāvāya (syā॰) kīḷāma, aivamaivimai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya nāvāya kīḷantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhissantī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhathā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ aikanāvaṃ (syā॰) abhiruhaiyya, uddhaṃgāminiṃ vā adhaugāminiṃ  vā, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

187. taina  khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū ca bhikkhuniyau ca sākaitā sāvatthiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| antarāmaggai nadī taritabbā uttaritabbā (syā॰) hauti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau tai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mayampi ayyaihi saddhiṃ uttarissāmā’’ti| ‘‘na, bhaginī, kappati bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhituṃ; tumhai vā paṭhamaṃ uttaratha mayaṃ vā uttarissāmā’’ti| ‘‘ayyā, bhantai, aggapurisā| ayyāva paṭhamaṃ uttarantū’’ti| atha khau tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ pacchā uttarantīnaṃ caurā acchindiṃsu ca dūsaisuñ‍ca| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tiriyaṃ taraṇāya bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

188. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhaiyya uddhaṃgāminiṃ vā adhaugāminiṃ vā, añ‍ñatra tiriyaṃ taraṇāya, pācittiya’’nti|

189. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunī nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

saddhinti aikatau|

saṃvidhāyāti ‘‘abhiruhāma, bhagini, abhiruhāmāyya; abhiruhāmāyya, abhiruhāma, bhagini; aj‍ja vā hiyyau vā parai vā abhiruhāmā’’ti saṃvidahati āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhuniyā abhiruḷhai bhikkhu abhiruhati, āpatti pācittiyassa| bhikkhumhi abhiruḷhai bhikkhunī abhiruhati, āpatti pācittiyassa| ubhau vā abhiruhanti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

uddhaṃgāmininti uj‍javanikāya|

adhaugāmininti aujavanikāya|

añ‍ñatra tiriyaṃ taraṇāyāti ṭhapaitvā tiriyaṃ taraṇaṃ|

kuk‍kuṭasampātai gāmai, gāmantarai gāmantarai āpatti pācittiyassa| agāmakai arañ‍ñai, aḍḍhayaujanai aḍḍhayaujanai āpatti pācittiyassa|

190. saṃvidahitai  saṃvidahitasañ‍ñī aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhati uddhaṃgāminiṃ vā adhaugāminiṃ vā, añ‍ñatra tiriyaṃ taraṇāya, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṃvidahitai  vaimatikau aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhati uddhaṃgāminiṃ vā adhaugāminiṃ vā, añ‍ñatra tiriyaṃ taraṇāya, āpatti pācittiyassa| saṃvidahitai asaṃvidahitasañ‍ñī aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhati uddhaṃgāminiṃ vā adhaugāminiṃ vā, añ‍ñatra tiriyaṃ taraṇāya, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhu saṃvidahati, bhikkhunī na saṃvidahati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asaṃvidahitai saṃvidahitasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asaṃvidahitai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asaṃvidahitai, asaṃvidahitasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

191. anāpatti tiriyaṃ taraṇāya, asaṃvidahitvā abhiruhanti, bhikkhunī  saṃvidahati, bhikkhu na saṃvidahati, visaṅkaitaina abhiruhanti, āpadāsu ummattakassa, ādikammissāti|

nāvābhiruhanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paripācitasikkhāpadaṃ

192. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī añ‍ñatarassa kulassa kulūpikā hauti nic‍cabhattikā| taina ca gahapatinā thairā bhikkhū nimantitā haunti| atha khau thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina taṃ kulaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kimidaṃ, gahapati, pahūtaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyatta’’nti? ‘‘thairā mayā, ayyai, nimantitā’’ti| ‘‘kai pana tai, gahapati, thairā’’ti? ‘‘ayyau sāriputtau ayyau mahāmauggal‍lānau ayyau mahākac‍cānau ayyau mahākauṭṭhikau ayyau mahākappinau ayyau mahācundau ayyau anuruddhau ayyau raivatau ayyau upāli ayyau ānandau ayyau rāhulau’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, gahapati, mahānāgai tiṭṭhamānai caiṭakai nimantaisī’’ti?

‘‘kai pana tai, ayyai, mahānāgā’’ti? ‘‘ayyau daivadattau ayyau kaukālikau ayyau kaṭamaudakatissakau kaṭamaurakatissakau (sī॰) katamaurakatissakau (syā॰) ayyau khaṇḍadaiviyā puttau  ayyau samuddadattau’’ti| ayaṃ carahi thul‍lanandāya  bhikkhuniyā antarā kathā vippakatā, atha tai thairā bhikkhū pavisiṃsu| ‘‘sac‍caṃ mahānāgā khau tayā, gahapati, nimantitā’’ti| ‘‘idānaiva khau tvaṃ, ayyai, caiṭakai akāsi; idāni  mahānāgai’’ti| gharatau ca nik‍kaḍḍhi, nic‍cabhattañ‍ca pacchindi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma daivadattau jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, daivadatta, jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissasi| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

193. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu rājagahā pabbajitau ñātikulaṃ agamāsi| manussā – ‘‘cirassampi bhadantau āgatau’’ti sak‍kac‍caṃ bhattaṃ akaṃsu| tassa kulassa kulūpikā bhikkhunī tai manussai aitadavauca – ‘‘daithayyassa, āvusau, bhatta’’nti| atha khau sau bhikkhu – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jitu’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau na paṭiggahaisi| nāsakkhi piṇḍāya carituṃ, chin‍nabhattau ahausi| atha khau sau bhikkhu ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ  kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pubbai gihisamārambhai jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

194. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyya, añ‍ñatra pubbai gihisamārambhā, pācittiya’’nti|

195. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

jānāti  nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti sā vā āraucaiti|

bhikkhunī nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

paripācaiti nāma pubbai adātukāmānaṃ akattukāmānaṃ – ‘‘ayyau bhāṇakau, ayyau bahussutau, ayyau suttantikau, ayyau vinayadharau, ayyau dhammakathikau, daitha ayyassa, karautha ayyassā’’ti aisā paripācaiti nāma|

piṇḍapātau nāma pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaujanānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ bhaujanaṃ|

añ‍ñatra pubbai gihisamārambhāti ṭhapaitvā gihisamārambhaṃ|

gihisamārambhau nāma ñātakā vā haunti pavāritā vā pakatipaṭiyattaṃ vā|

añ‍ñatra pubbai gihisamārambhā bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

196. paripācitai  paripācitasañ‍ñī bhuñ‍jati, añ‍ñatra pubbai gihisamārambhā, āpatti pācittiyassa| paripācitai vaimatikau bhuñ‍jati, añ‍ñatra pubbai gihisamārambhā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paripācitai  aparipācitasañ‍ñī bhuñ‍jati, añ‍ñatra pubbai gihisamārambhā, anāpatti| aikatauupasampan‍nāya paripācitaṃ bhuñ‍jati, añ‍ñatra pubbai gihisamārambhā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aparipācitai paripācitasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aparipācittai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aparipācitai aparipācitasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

197. anāpatti pubbai gihisamārambhai, sikkhamānā paripācaiti, sāmaṇairī paripācaiti, pañ‍ca bhaujanāni ṭhapaitvā sabbattha anāpatti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

paripācitasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. rahaunisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

198. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau udāyissa  purāṇadutiyikā bhikkhunīsu pabbajitā hauti| sā āyasmatau udāyissa santikai abhikkhaṇaṃ āgacchati, āyasmāpi udāyī tassā bhikkhuniyā santikai abhikkhaṇaṃ gacchati| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī tassā bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā udāyī bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, udāyi, bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

199. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

200. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunī nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

saddhinti aikatau|

aikau aikāyāti bhikkhu caiva hauti bhikkhunī ca|

pāci॰ 286, 291; pārā॰ 445,454 rahau  nāma cakkhussa rahau sautassa rahau| cakkhussa rahau nāma na sak‍kā hauti akkhiṃ vā nikhaṇīyamānai bhamukaṃ vā ukkhipīyamānai sīsaṃ vā ukkhipīyamānai passituṃ| sautassa rahau nāma na sak‍kā hauti pakatikathā sautuṃ|

nisaj‍jaṃ  kappaiyyāti bhikkhuniyā nisin‍nāya bhikkhu upanisin‍nau vā hauti upanipan‍nau vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhu nisin‍nai bhikkhunī upanisin‍nā vā hauti upanipan‍nā vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| ubhau vā nisin‍nā haunti ubhau vā nipan‍nā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

201. rahau  rahausañ‍ñī aikau aikāya nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| rahau vaimatikau aikau aikāya nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| rahau arahausañ‍ñī aikau aikāya nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

arahau rahausañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| arahau vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| arahau arahausañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

202. anāpatti yau kauci viñ‍ñū dutiyau hauti, tiṭṭhati na nisīdati, arahaupaikkhau, añ‍ñavihitau nisīdati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

rahaunisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

auvādavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

asammataatthaṅgatū, passayāmisadānaina|

sibbati addhānaṃ nāvaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyya, aikau aikāya tai dasāti||

4. bhaujanavaggau

1. āvasathapiṇḍasikkhāpadaṃ

203. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyā avidūrai añ‍ñatarassa pūgassa āvasathapiṇḍau pañ‍ñattau hauti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pavisitvā piṇḍaṃ alabhamānā āvasathaṃ agamaṃsu| manussā – ‘‘cirassampi bhadantā āgatā’’ti tai sak‍kac‍caṃ parivisiṃsu| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū dutiyampi divasaṃ…pai॰… tatiyampi divasaṃ pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pavisitvā piṇḍaṃ alabhamānā āvasathaṃ gantvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu| atha khau chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ mayaṃ karissāma ārāmaṃ gantvā! hiyyaupi idhaiva āgantabbaṃ bhavissatī’’ti, tatthaiva anuvasitvā anuvasitvā  āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍janti| titthiyā apasak‍kanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma  samaṇā sakyaputtiyā anuvasitvā anuvasitvā āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jissanti! nayimaisañ‍ñaiva āvasathapiṇḍau pañ‍ñattau; sabbaisañ‍ñaiva āvasathapiṇḍau pañ‍ñattau’’ti|

assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū anuvasitvā anuvasitvā āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jissantīti…pai॰…  sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, anuvasitvā anuvasitvā āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, anuvasitvā anuvasitvā āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘aikau āvasathapiṇḍau bhuñ‍jitabbau| tatau cai uttariṃ bhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

204. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā sāriputtau kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ gacchantau yaina añ‍ñatarau āvasathau tainupasaṅkami| manussā – ‘‘cirassampi thairau āgatau’’ti sak‍kac‍caṃ parivisiṃsu| atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhuttāvissa kharau ābādhau uppaj‍ji, nāsakkhi tamhā āvasathā pak‍kamituṃ| atha khau tai manussā dutiyampi divasaṃ āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘bhuñ‍jatha, bhantai’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ anuvasitvā anuvasitvā āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jitu’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau na paṭiggahaisi; chin‍nabhattau ahausi| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi  – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā anuvasitvā anuvasitvā āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

205. ‘‘agilānaina bhikkhunā aikau āvasathapiṇḍau bhuñ‍jitabbau| tatau cai uttari bhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

206. agilānau  nāma sak‍kauti tamhā āvasathā pak‍kamituṃ|

gilānau nāma na sak‍kauti tamhā āvasathā pak‍kamituṃ|

āvasathapiṇḍau  nāma pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaujanānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ bhaujanaṃ – sālāya vā maṇḍapai vā rukkhamūlai vā ajjhaukāsai vā anaudissa yāvadatthau pañ‍ñattau hauti| agilānaina bhikkhunā sakiṃ bhuñ‍jitabbau| tatau cai uttari ‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

207. agilānau agilānasañ‍ñī tatuttari āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| agilānau vaimatikau tatuttari āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| agilānau gilānasañ‍ñī tatuttariṃ āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

gilānau  agilānasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānau vaimatikau āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānau gilānasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

208. anāpatti gilānassa, agilānau sakiṃ bhuñ‍jati, gacchantau, vā āgacchantau vā bhuñ‍jati, sāmikā nimantaitvā bhaujainti, audissa  pañ‍ñattau hauti, na yāvadatthau pañ‍ñattau hauti, pañ‍ca bhaujanāni ṭhapaitvā sabbattha anāpatti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

āvasathapiṇḍasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. gaṇabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

209. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina daivadattau parihīnalābhasak‍kārau saparisau kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissanti! kassa sampan‍naṃ na manāpaṃ, kassa sāduṃ na ruc‍catī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma daivadattau saparisau kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, daivadatta, saparisau kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jasīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, saparisau kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘gaṇabhaujanai pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

210. taina  khau pana samayaina manussā gilānai bhikkhū bhattaina nimantainti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti – ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā gaṇabhaujana’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi  – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā gaṇabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘gaṇabhaujanai, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

211. taina khau pana samayaina manussā cīvaradānasamayai sacīvarabhattaṃ paṭiyādaitvā bhikkhū nimantainti – ‘‘bhaujaitvā cīvaraina acchādaissāmā’’ti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti – ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā gaṇabhaujana’’nti| cīvaraṃ parittaṃ uppaj‍jati| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cīvaradānasamayai gaṇabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘gaṇabhaujanai, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

212. taina khau pana samayaina manussā cīvarakārakai bhikkhū bhattaina nimantainti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti – ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā  gaṇabhaujana’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cīvarakārasamayai gaṇabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘gaṇabhaujanai, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

213. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū manussaihi saddhiṃ addhānaṃ  gacchanti| atha khau  tai bhikkhū tai manussai aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘muhuttaṃ, āvusau, āgamaitha; piṇḍāya carissāmā’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘idhaiva, bhantai, bhuñ‍jathā’’ti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti – ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā gaṇabhaujana’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, addhānagamanasamayai gaṇabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘gaṇabhaujanai, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau, addhānagamanasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

214. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū manussaihi saddhiṃ nāvāya gacchanti| atha khau tai bhikkhū tai manussai aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘muhuttaṃ, āvusau, tīraṃ upanaitha; piṇḍāya carissāmā’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu  – ‘‘idhaiva, bhantai, bhuñ‍jathā’’ti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti – ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā gaṇabhaujana’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, nāvābhiruhanasamayai gaṇabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘gaṇabhaujanai, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau, addhānagamanasamayau, nāvābhiruhanasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

215. taina 0 khau pana samayaina disāsu vassaṃvuṭṭhā bhikkhū rājagahaṃ āgacchanti bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| manussā nānāvairaj‍jakai bhikkhū passitvā bhattaina nimantainti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti – ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā gaṇabhaujana’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamayai gaṇabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘gaṇabhaujanai, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau, addhānagamanasamayau, nāvābhiruhanasamayau, mahāsamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

216. taina  khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa ñātisālauhitau ājīvakaisu pabbajitau hauti| atha khau sau  ājīvakau yaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, mahārāja, sabbapāsaṇḍikabhattaṃ kātu’’nti| ‘‘sacai tvaṃ, bhantai, buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṭhamaṃ bhaujaiyyāsi’’| ‘‘aivaṃ karaiyyāmī’’ti| atha khau sau ājīvakau bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘adhivāsaintu mai bhikkhū svātanāya bhatta’’nti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti – ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā gaṇabhaujana’’nti| atha khau sau ājīvakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sau ājīvakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhavampi gautamau pabbajitau, ahampi pabbajitau; arahati pabbajitau pabbajitassa piṇḍaṃ paṭiggahaituṃ| adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau sau ājīvakau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, samaṇabhattasamayai gaṇabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

217. gaṇabhaujanai , añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau, addhānagamanasamayau, nāvābhiruhanasamayau, mahāsamayau, samaṇabhattasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

218. gaṇabhaujanaṃ  nāma yattha cattārau bhikkhū pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaujanānaṃ añ‍ñataraina bhaujanaina nimantitā bhuñ‍janti| aitaṃ gaṇabhaujanaṃ nāma|

añ‍ñatra samayāti ṭhapaitvā samayaṃ|

gilānasamayau  nāma antamasau pādāpi phalitā phālitā (syā॰ ka॰) haunti| ‘‘gilānasamayau’’ti bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

cīvaradānasamayau nāma anatthatai kathinai vassānassa pacchimau māsau, atthatai kathinai pañ‍camāsā| ‘‘cīvaradānasamayau’’ti bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

cīvarakārasamayau nāma cīvarai kayiramānai| ‘‘cīvarakārasamayau’’ti bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

addhānagamanasamayau  nāma ‘‘addhayaujanaṃ gacchissāmī’’ti bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, gacchantaina bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, gataina bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

nāvābhiruhanasamayau nāma ‘‘nāvaṃ abhiruhissāmī’’ti bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, āruḷhaina bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, auruḷhaina bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

mahāsamayau nāma yattha dvai tayau bhikkhū piṇḍāya caritvā yāpainti, catutthai āgatai na yāpainti| ‘‘mahāsamayau’’ti bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

samaṇabhattasamayau nāma yau kauci paribbājakasamāpan‍nau bhattaṃ karauti| ‘‘samaṇabhattasamayau’’ti bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘añ‍ñatra samayā bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

219. gaṇabhaujanai gaṇabhaujanasañ‍ñī, añ‍ñatra samayā, bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇabhaujanai vaimatikau, añ‍ñatra samayā, bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇabhaujanai nagaṇabhaujanasañ‍ñī, añ‍ñatra samayā, bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

nagaṇabhaujanai  gaṇabhaujanasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nagaṇabhaujanai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nagaṇabhaujanai nagaṇabhaujanasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

220. anāpatti  samayai, dvai tayau aikatau bhuñ‍janti, piṇḍāya caritvā aikatau san‍nipatitvā bhuñ‍janti, nic‍cabhattaṃ, salākabhattaṃ, pakkhikaṃ, upausathikaṃ, pāṭipadikaṃ, pañ‍ca bhaujanāni ṭhapaitvā sabbattha anāpatti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

gaṇabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. paramparabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

221. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina vaisāliyaṃ paṇītānaṃ bhattānaṃ bhattapaṭipāṭi adhiṭṭhitā hauti| atha khau añ‍ñatarassa daliddassa kammakārassa kammakarassa (sī॰) aitadahausi – ‘‘na khau idaṃ aurakaṃ bhavissati yathayimai manussā sak‍kac‍caṃ bhattaṃ karaunti; yaṃnūnāhampi bhattaṃ karaiyya’’nti| atha khau sau daliddau kammakārau yaina kirapatikau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ kirapatikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, ayyaputta, buddhappamukhassa  bhikkhusaṅghassa bhattaṃ kātuṃ| daihi mai vaitana’’nti| saupi khau kirapatikau saddhau hauti pasan‍nau| atha khau sau kirapatikau tassa daliddassa kammakārassa abbhātiraikaṃ atiraikaṃ (syā॰) vaitanaṃ adāsi| atha khau sau daliddau kammakārau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau daliddau kammakārau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| ‘‘mahā khau, āvusau, bhikkhusaṅghau| jānāhī’’ti| ‘‘hautu hautu mai (ka॰) bhantai, mahā bhikkhusaṅghau| bahū mai badarā paṭiyattā badaramissaina paiyyā paripūrissantī’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha  khau sau daliddau kammakārau bhagavatau  adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū – ‘‘daliddaina kira kammakāraina svātanāya buddhappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau nimantitau, badaramissaina paiyyā paripūrissantī’’ti| tai kālassaiva piṇḍāya caritvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu| assausuṃ khau manussā – ‘‘daliddaina kira kammakāraina buddhappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau nimantitau’’ti| tai daliddassa kammakārassa pahūtaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ abhihariṃsu| atha khau sau daliddau kammakārau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti|

atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina tassa daliddassa kammakārassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau sau daliddau kammakārau bhattaggai bhikkhū parivisati| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘thaukaṃ, āvusau, daihi| thaukaṃ, āvusau, daihī’’ti| ‘‘mā khau tumhai, bhantai, ‘ayaṃ daliddau kammakārau’ti thaukaṃ thaukaṃ paṭiggaṇhittha| pahūtaṃ mai khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyattaṃ|

paṭiggaṇhatha, bhantai, yāvadattha’’nti| ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, āvusau, aitaṃkāraṇā thaukaṃ thaukaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| apica, mayaṃ kālassaiva piṇḍāya caritvā bhuñ‍jimhā; taina mayaṃ thaukaṃ thaukaṃ paṭiggaṇhāmā’’ti|

atha khau sau daliddau kammakārau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma  bhadantā mayā nimantitā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jissanti! na cāhaṃ paṭibalau yāvadatthaṃ dātu’’nti? assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa daliddassa kammakārassa  ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū añ‍ñatra nimantitā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū añ‍ñatra nimantitā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā añ‍ñatra nimantitā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘paramparabhaujanai  pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

222. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu piṇḍapātaṃ ādāya yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhuñ‍jāhi, āvusau’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, atthi mai bhattapac‍cāsā’’ti| tassa bhikkhunau piṇḍapātau ussūrai ussūraina (ka॰) āharīyittha| sau bhikkhu na cittarūpaṃ bhuñ‍ji| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā paramparabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘paramparabhaujanai,  añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

223. taina khau pana samayaina manussā cīvaradānasamayai sacīvarabhattaṃ paṭiyādaitvā paṭiyādāpaitvā (itipi) bhikkhū nimantainti – ‘‘bhaujaitvā cīvaraina acchādaissāmā’’ti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti – ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā paramparabhaujana’’nti| cīvaraṃ parittaṃ uppaj‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cīvaradānasamayai paramparabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘paramparabhaujanai, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

224. taina khau pana samayaina manussā cīvarakārakai bhikkhū bhattaina nimantainti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti – ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā paramparabhaujana’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cīvarakārasamayai paramparabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

225. ‘‘paramparabhaujanai,  añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| gilānasamayau, cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

226. atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya  āyasmatā ānandaina pacchāsamaṇaina yaina añ‍ñataraṃ kulaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau tai manussā bhagavatau ca āyasmatau ca ānandassa bhaujanaṃ adaṃsu| āyasmā ānandau kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau na paṭiggaṇhāti| ‘‘gaṇhāhi patigaṇhāhi (sī॰), ānandā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, bhagavā, atthi mai bhattapac‍cāsā’’ti| ‘‘tainahānanda, vikappaitvā gaṇhāhī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vikappaitvā bhattapac‍cāsaṃ vikappaitvā (syā॰) paramparabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, vikappaitabbaṃ – ‘mayhaṃ bhattapac‍cāsaṃ itthan‍nāmassa dammī’’’ti|

227. paramparabhaujanaṃ nāma pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaujanānaṃ añ‍ñataraina bhaujanaina nimantitau, taṃ ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaujanānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jati, aitaṃ paramparabhaujanaṃ nāma|

añ‍ñatra samayāti ṭhapaitvā samayaṃ|

gilānasamayau nāma na sak‍kauti aikāsanai nisin‍nau yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| ‘‘gilānasamayau’’ti bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

cīvaradānasamayau nāma anatthatai kathinai vassānassa pacchimau māsau, atthatai kathinai pañ‍ca māsā| ‘‘cīvaradānasamayau’’ti bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

cīvarakārasamayau nāma cīvarai kayiramānai| ‘‘cīvarakārasamayau’’ti bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘añ‍ñatra samayā bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai  ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

228. paramparabhaujanai  paramparabhaujanasañ‍ñī, añ‍ñatra samayā, bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| paramparabhaujanai vaimatikau, añ‍ñatra samayā, bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| paramparabhaujanai naparamparabhaujanasañ‍ñī, añ‍ñatra samayā, bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

naparamparabhaujanai paramparabhaujanasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| naparamparabhaujanai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| naparamparabhaujanai naparamparabhaujanasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

229. anāpatti samayai, vikappaitvā bhuñ‍jati, dvai tayau nimantanai aikatau bhuñ‍jati, nimantanapaṭipāṭiyā bhuñ‍jati, sakalaina gāmaina nimantitau tasmiṃ gāmai yattha katthaci bhuñ‍jati, sakalaina pūgaina nimantitau tasmiṃ pūgai yattha katthaci bhuñ‍jati, nimantiyamānau bhikkhaṃ gahaissāmīti bhaṇati, nic‍cabhattai, salākabhattai, pakkhikai, upausathikai, pāṭipadikai, pañ‍ca bhaujanāni ṭhapaitvā sabbattha anāpatti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

paramparabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. kāṇamātusikkhāpadaṃ

230. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina  kāṇamātā upāsikā saddhā hauti pasan‍nā| kāṇā gāmakai añ‍ñatarassa purisassa din‍nā hauti| atha khau kāṇā mātugharaṃ agamāsi kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau kāṇāya sāmikau kāṇāya santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘āgacchatu kāṇā, icchāmi kāṇāya āgata’’nti| atha khau kāṇamātā upāsikā ‘‘kismiṃ viya rittahatthaṃ gantu’’nti pūvaṃ pūpaṃ (ṇvādi) paci| pak‍kai pūvai añ‍ñatarau piṇḍacārikau bhikkhu kāṇamātāya upāsikāya nivaisanaṃ pāvisi| atha khau kāṇamātā upāsikā tassa bhikkhunau pūvaṃ dāpaisi| sau nikkhamitvā añ‍ñassa ācikkhi| tassapi pūvaṃ dāpaisi| saupi nikkhamitvā añ‍ñassa ācikkhi| tassapi pūvaṃ  dāpaisi| yathāpaṭiyattaṃ pūvaṃ parikkhayaṃ agamāsi| dutiyampi khau kāṇāya sāmikau kāṇāya santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘āgacchatu kāṇā, icchāmi kāṇāya āgata’’nti| dutiyampi khau kāṇamātā upāsikā ‘‘kismiṃ viya rittahattaṃ gantu’’nti pūvaṃ paci| pak‍kai pūvai añ‍ñatarau piṇḍacārikau bhikkhu kāṇamātāya upāsikāya nivaisanaṃ pāvisi| atha khau kāṇamātā upāsikā tassa bhikkhunau pūvaṃ dāpaisi| sau nikkhamitvā añ‍ñassa ācikkhi| tassapi pūvaṃ  dāpaisi| saupi nikkhamitvā añ‍ñassa ācikkhi| tassapi pūvaṃ dāpaisi| yathāpaṭiyattaṃ pūvaṃ parikkhayaṃ agamāsi| tatiyampi khau kāṇāya sāmikau kāṇāya santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘āgacchatu kāṇā, icchāmi kāṇāya āgataṃ| sacai kāṇā nāgacchissati, ahaṃ añ‍ñaṃ pajāpatiṃ ānaissāmī’’ti| tatiyampi khau kāṇamātā upāsikā kismiṃ viya rittahatthaṃ gantunti pūvaṃ paci| pak‍kai pūvai añ‍ñatarau piṇḍacārikau bhikkhu kāṇamātāya upāsikāya nivaisanaṃ pāvisi| atha khau kāṇamātā upāsikā tassa bhikkhunau pūvaṃ dāpaisi| sau nikkhamitvā añ‍ñassa ācikkhi| tassapi pūvaṃ dāpaisi| saupi nikkhamitvā añ‍ñassa ācikkhi| tassapi  pūvaṃ dāpaisi| yathāpaṭiyattaṃ pūvaṃ parikkhayaṃ agamāsi| atha khau kāṇāya sāmikau añ‍ñaṃ pajāpatiṃ ānaisi|

assausi khau kāṇā – ‘‘taina kira purisaina añ‍ñā pajāpati ānītā’’ti| sā raudantī aṭṭhāsi| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina kāṇamātāya upāsikāya nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau kāṇamātā upāsikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kāṇamātaraṃ upāsikaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kissāyaṃ kāṇā raudatī’’ti? atha khau kāṇamātā upāsikā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau bhagavā kāṇamātaraṃ upāsikaṃ dhammiyā kathāya  sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

231. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau satthau rājagahā paṭiyālaukaṃ gantukāmau hauti| añ‍ñatarau piṇḍacārikau bhikkhu taṃ satthaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| añ‍ñatarau upāsakau tassa bhikkhunau sattuṃ  dāpaisi| sau nikkhamitvā añ‍ñassa ācikkhi| tassapi sattuṃ dāpaisi| sau nikkhamitvā añ‍ñassa ācikkhi| tassapi sattuṃ dāpaisi| yathāpaṭiyattaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ parikkhayaṃ agamāsi| atha khau sau upāsakau tai manussai aitadavauca – ‘‘aj‍jaṇhau, ayyā, āgamaitha, yathāpaṭiyattaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ ayyānaṃ din‍naṃ| pāthaiyyaṃ paṭiyādaissāmī’’ti| ‘‘nāyyau nāyya (syā॰) sak‍kā āgamaituṃ, payātau satthau’’ti agamaṃsu| atha khau tassa upāsakassa pāthaiyyaṃ paṭiyādaitvā pacchā gacchantassa caurā acchindiṃsu| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā na mattaṃ jānitvā paṭiggahaissanti! ayaṃ imaisaṃ datvā pacchā gacchantau cauraihi acchin‍nau’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi  dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya…pai॰… vinayānuggahāya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

232. ‘‘bhikkhuṃ panaiva kulaṃ upagataṃ pūvaihi vā manthaihi vā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyya, ākaṅkhamānaina bhikkhunā dvattipattapūrā paṭiggahaitabbā tatau cai uttari paṭiggaṇhaiyya pācittiyaṃ| dvattipattapūrai  paṭiggahaitvā tatau nīharitvā bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvibhajitabbaṃ| ayaṃ tattha sāmīcī’’ti|

233. bhikkhuṃ panaiva kulaṃ upagatanti kulaṃ nāma cattāri kulāni – khattiyakulaṃ, brāhmaṇakulaṃ, vaissakulaṃ, suddakulaṃ|

upagatanti tattha gataṃ|

pūvaṃ nāma yaṃkiñ‍ci pahaiṇakatthāya paṭiyattaṃ|

manthaṃ nāma yaṃkiñ‍ci pāthaiyyatthāya paṭiyattaṃ|

abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyyāti yāvatakaṃ icchasi tāvatakaṃ gaṇhāhīti|

ākaṅkhamānaināti icchamānaina|

dvattipattapūrā paṭiggahaitabbāti dvaitayau pattapūrā paṭiggahaitabbā|

tatau  cai uttari paṭigaṇhaiyyāti tatuttari paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

dvattipattapūrai paṭiggahaitvā tatau nikkhamantaina bhikkhuṃ passitvā ācikkhitabbaṃ – ‘‘amutra mayā dvattipattapūrā paṭiggahitā, mā khau tattha paṭiggaṇhī’’ti| sacai passitvā na ācikkhati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sacai ācikkhitai paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

tatau  nīharitvā bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvibhajitabbanti paṭik‍kamanaṃ nīharitvā saṃvibhajitabbaṃ|

ayaṃ tattha sāmīcīti ayaṃ tattha anudhammatā|

234. atiraikadvattipattapūrai atiraikasañ‍ñī paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti pācittiyassa| atiraikadvattipattapūrai  vaimatikau paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti pācittiyassa| atiraikadvattipattapūrai ūnakasañ‍ñī paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

ūnakadvattipattapūrai atiraikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakadvattipattapūrai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakadvattipattapūrai ūnakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

235. anāpatti dvattipattapūrai paṭiggaṇhāti, ūnakadvattipattapūrai paṭiggaṇhāti, na pahaiṇakatthāya na pāthaiyyatthāya paṭiyattaṃ dainti, pahaiṇakatthāya vā pāthaiyyatthāya vā paṭiyattasaisakaṃ dainti, gamanai paṭippassaddhai dainti, ñātakānaṃ pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

kāṇamātusikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamapavāraṇāsikkhāpadaṃ

236. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau bhikkhū nimantaitvā bhaujaisi| bhikkhū bhuttāvī pavāritā ñātikulāni  gantvā aikac‍cai bhuñ‍jiṃsu aikac‍cai piṇḍapātaṃ ādāya agamaṃsu| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau paṭivissakai paṭivisakai (yaujanā) aitadavauca – ‘‘bhikkhū mayā ayyā santappitā| aitha, tumhaipi santappaissāmī’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kiṃ tvaṃ, ayyau ayya (syā॰), amhai santappaissasi? yaipi tayā nimantitā taipi amhākaṃ gharāni āgantvā aikac‍cai bhuñ‍jiṃsu aikac‍cai piṇḍapātaṃ ādāya agamaṃsū’’ti!

atha khau sau brāhmaṇau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā amhākaṃ gharai bhuñ‍jitvā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jissanti! na cāhaṃ paṭibalau yāvadatthaṃ dātu’’nti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa brāhmaṇassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū bhuttāvī pavāritā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhuttāvī pavāritā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā bhuttāvī  pavāritā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ  vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhuttāvī pavāritau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

237. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṇītai piṇḍapātai nīharanti| gilānā na cittarūpaṃ bhuñ‍janti| tāni bhikkhū chaṭṭainti| assausi khau bhagavā uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ kākauravasaddaṃ| sutvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau sau, ānanda, uc‍cāsaddau mahāsaddau kākauravasaddau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘bhuñ‍jaiyyuṃ panānanda, bhikkhū gilānātiritta’’nti| ‘‘na bhuñ‍jaiyyuṃ, bhagavā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi bhikkhavai, gilānassa ca agilānassa ca atirittaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, atirittaṃ kātabbaṃ – ‘‘alamaitaṃ sabba’’nti| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

238. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu bhuttāvī pavāritau anatirittaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

239. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhuttāvī nāma pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaujanānaṃ  añ‍ñataraṃ bhaujanaṃ antamasau kusaggainapi bhuttaṃ hauti|

pavāritau nāma asanaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, bhaujanaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, hatthapāsai ṭhitau abhiharati, paṭikkhaipau pañ‍ñāyati|

anatirittaṃ nāma akappiyakataṃ hauti, appaṭiggahitakataṃ hauti, anuc‍cāritakataṃ hauti, ahatthapāsai kataṃ hauti, abhuttāvinā kataṃ hauti, bhuttāvinā pavāritaina āsanā vuṭṭhitaina kataṃ hauti, ‘‘alamaitaṃ sabbanti avuttaṃ hauti, na gilānātirittaṃ hauti’’ – aitaṃ anatirittaṃ nāma|

atirittaṃ nāma kappiyakataṃ hauti, paṭiggahitakataṃ hauti, uc‍cāritakataṃ hauti, hatthapāsai kataṃ hauti, bhuttāvinā kataṃ hauti, bhuttāvinā pavāritaina āsanā avuṭṭhitaina kataṃ hauti, ‘‘alamaitaṃ sabba’’nti vuttaṃ hauti, gilānātirittaṃ hauti – aitaṃ atirittaṃ nāma|

khādanīyaṃ  nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ|

‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

240. anatirittai anatirittasañ‍ñī khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| anatirittai vaimatikau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| anatirittai atirittasañ‍ñī khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

yāmakālikaṃ  sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ āhāratthāya paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| atirittai anatirittasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| atirittai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| atirittai atirittasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

241. anāpatti atirittaṃ kārāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, ‘‘atirittaṃ kārāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, añ‍ñassatthāya harantau gacchati, gilānassa saisakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

paṭhamapavāraṇāsikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyapavāraṇāsikkhāpadaṃ

242. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina dvai bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| aikau bhikkhu anācāraṃ ācarati| dutiyau bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘māvusau, aivarūpamakāsi, naitaṃ kappatī’’ti| sau tasmiṃ upanandhi| atha khau tai bhikkhū sāvatthiṃ agamaṃsu| taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñatarassa pūgassa saṅghabhattaṃ hauti| dutiyau bhikkhu bhuttāvī pavāritau hauti| upanaddhau upanandhau (ka॰) bhikkhu ñātikulaṃ gantvā piṇḍapātaṃ ādāya yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhuñ‍jāhi, āvusau’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, paripuṇṇaumhī’’ti| ‘‘sundarau, āvusau, piṇḍapātau, bhuñ‍jāhī’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu taina bhikkhunā nippīḷiyamānau taṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍ji| upanaddhau bhikkhu  taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tvampi tvaṃ hi (syā॰) nāma, āvusau, maṃ vattabbaṃ mañ‍ñasi yaṃ tvaṃ bhuttāvī pavāritau anatirittaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jasī’’ti| ‘‘nanu, āvusau, ācikkhitabba’’nti| ‘‘nanu, āvusau, pucchitabba’’nti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu  bhikkhuṃ bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittaina bhaujanaina abhihaṭṭhuṃ  pavāraissatī’’ ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhuṃ bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittaina bhaujanaina abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, bhikkhuṃ bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittaina bhaujanaina abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana bhikkhavai imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

243. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittaina khādanīyaina vā bhaujanīyaina vā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyya – ‘handa, bhikkhu, khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’ti, jānaṃ āsādanāpaikkhau, bhuttasmiṃ, pācittiya’’nti|

244. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunti  añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ|

bhuttāvī nāma pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaujanānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ bhaujanaṃ, antamasau kusaggainapi bhuttaṃ hauti|

pavāritau nāma asanaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, bhaujanaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, hatthapāsai ṭhitau abhiharati, paṭikkhaipau pañ‍ñāyati|

anatirittaṃ nāma akappiyakataṃ hauti, appaṭiggahitakataṃ hauti, anuc‍cāritakataṃ hauti, ahatthapāsai kataṃ hauti, abhuttāvinā kataṃ hauti, bhuttāvinā pavāritaina āsanā vuṭṭhitaina kataṃ hauti, ‘‘alamaitaṃ sabba’’nti avuttaṃ hauti, na gilānātirittaṃ hauti – aitaṃ anatirittaṃ nāma|

khādanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ nāma  pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ|

abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyyāti yāvatakaṃ icchasi tāvatakaṃ gaṇhāhīti|

jānāti  nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti, añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti, sau vā āraucaiti|

āsādanāpaikkhauti ‘‘iminā imaṃ caudaissāmi sāraissāmi paṭicaudaissāmi paṭisāraissāmi maṅku karissāmī’’ti abhiharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tassa vacanaina ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhaujanapariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa|

245. pavāritai pavāritasañ‍ñī anatirittaina khādanīyaina vā bhaujanīyaina vā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| pavāritai vaimatikau anatirittaina khādanīyaina vā bhaujanīyaina vā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pavāritai appavāritasañ‍ñī anatirittaina  khādanīyaina vā bhaujanīyaina vā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiti, anāpatti| yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ āhāratthāya abhiharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tassa vacanaina ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| appavāritai pavāritasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| appavāritai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| appavāritai appavāritasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

246. anāpatti atirittaṃ kārāpaitvā daiti, ‘‘atirittaṃ kārāpaitvā  bhuñ‍jāhī’’ti daiti, añ‍ñassatthāya harantau gacchāhīti daiti, gilānassa saisakaṃ daiti, ‘‘yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jā’’ti daiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dutiyapavāraṇāsikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vikālabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

247. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai giraggasamaj‍jau hauti| sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū giraggasamaj‍jaṃ dassanāya agamaṃsu| manussā sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū passitvā nahāpaitvā vilimpaitvā bhaujaitvā khādanīyaṃ  adaṃsu| sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū khādanīyaṃ ādāya ārāmaṃ gantvā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘gaṇhāthāvusau, khādanīyaṃ khādathā’’ti| ‘‘kutau tumhaihi, āvusau, khādanīyaṃ laddha’’nti? sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau, vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jathā’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jissantī’’ti! atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

248. yau  pana bhikkhu vikālai khādanīyaṃ  vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

249. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

vikālau nāma majjhanhikai vītivattai yāva aruṇuggamanā|

khādanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ|

‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

250. vikālai vikālasañ‍ñī khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| vikālai vaimatikau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| vikālai  kālasañ‍ñī khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ āhāratthāya paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kālai vikālasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kālai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kālai kālasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

251. anāpatti yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

vikālabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. san‍nidhikārakasikkhāpadaṃ

252. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau ānandassa upajjhāyau āyasmā bailaṭṭhasīsau bailaṭṭhisīsau (sī॰) vaiḷaṭṭhasīsau (syā॰) arañ‍ñai viharati| sau piṇḍāya caritvā sukkhakuraṃ ārāmaṃ haritvā sukkhāpaitvā nikkhipati| yadā āhāraina atthau hauti, tadā udakaina taimaitvā taimaitvā bhuñ‍jati, ciraina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisati| bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ bailaṭṭhasīsaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, āvusau, ciraina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisasī’’ti? atha khau āyasmā bailaṭṭhasīsau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, āvusau, san‍nidhikārakaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā bailaṭṭhasīsau san‍nidhikārakaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bailaṭṭhasīsa, san‍nidhikārakaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jasīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, bailaṭṭhasīsa, san‍nidhikārakaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jissasi! naitaṃ, bailaṭṭhasīsa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

253. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu san‍nidhikārakaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā  khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

254. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

san‍nidhikārakaṃ nāma aj‍ja paṭiggahitaṃ aparaj‍ju khāditaṃ hauti|

khādanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ|

‘‘khādissāmi  bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

255. san‍nidhikārakai san‍nidhikārakasañ‍ñī khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| san‍nidhikārakai vaimatikau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| san‍nidhikārakai asan‍nidhikārakasañ‍ñī khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ āhāratthāya paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asan‍nidhikārakai san‍nidhikārakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asan‍nidhikārakai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asan‍nidhikārakai asan‍nidhikārakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

256. anāpatti yāvakālikaṃ yāvakālai nidahitvā bhuñ‍jati, yāmakālikaṃ yāmai nidahitvā bhuñ‍jati, sattāhakālikaṃ sattāhaṃ nidahitvā  bhuñ‍jati, yāvajīvikaṃ sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

san‍nidhikārakasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṇītabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

257. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissanti! kassa sampan‍naṃ na manāpaṃ, kassa sāduṃ na ruc‍catī’’ti!! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissantī’’ti …pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā  …pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yāni khau pana tāni paṇītabhaujanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ macchau maṃsaṃ khīraṃ dadhi| yau pana bhikkhu aivarūpāni paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā  bhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

258. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū gilānā haunti| gilānapucchakā bhikkhū gilānai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kac‍cāvusau khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīya’’nti? ‘‘pubbai mayaṃ, āvusau, paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jāma, taina nau phāsu hauti; idāni pana ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhitta’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na viñ‍ñāpaima, taina nau na phāsu hautī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

259. ‘‘yāni  khau pana tāni paṇītabhaujanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ macchau maṃsaṃ khīraṃ dadhi| yau pana bhikkhu aivarūpāni paṇītabhaujanāni agilānau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

260. yāni khau pana tāni paṇītabhaujanānīti sappi nāma gausappi vā ajikāsappi vā mahiṃsasappi vā, yaisaṃ maṃsaṃ kappati taisaṃ sappi|

navanītaṃ nāma taisañ‍ñaiva navanītaṃ|

tailaṃ nāma tilatailaṃ sāsapatailaṃ madhukatailaṃ airaṇḍatailaṃ vasātailaṃ|

madhu  nāma makkhikāmadhu|

phāṇitaṃ nāma ucchumhā nibbattaṃ|

macchau nāma udakau udakacarau (syā॰ ka॰) vuc‍cati|

maṃsaṃ nāma yaisaṃ maṃsaṃ kappati, taisaṃ maṃsaṃ|

khīraṃ nāma gaukhīraṃ vā ajikākhīraṃ vā mahiṃsakhīraṃ vā, yaisaṃ maṃsaṃ kappati, taisaṃ khīraṃ|

dadhi nāma taisañ‍ñaiva dadhi|

yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

aivarūpāni paṇītabhaujanānīti tathārūpāni paṇītabhaujanāni|

agilānau nāma yassa vinā paṇītabhaujanāni phāsu hauti|

gilānau nāma yassa vinā paṇītabhaujanāni na phāsu hauti|

agilānau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, payaugai payaugai payaugai (ka॰) duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina ‘‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

261. agilānau agilānasañ‍ñī paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| agilānau vaimatikau  paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| agilānau gilānasañ‍ñī paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

gilānau  agilānasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānau vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānau gilānasañ‍ñī anāpatti|

262. anāpatti  gilānassa, gilānau hutvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā agilānau bhuñ‍jati, gilānassa saisakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, ñātakānaṃ pavāritānaṃ añ‍ñassatthāya attanau dhanaina, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

paṇītabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. dantapaunasikkhāpadaṃ

263. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sabbapaṃsukūlikau susānai viharati| sau manussaihi diyyamānaṃ na icchati paṭiggahaituṃ, susānaipi rukkhamūlaipi ummāraipi ayyavausāṭitakāni sāmaṃ gahaitvā paribhuñ‍jati bhuñ‍jati (syā॰)| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayaṃ bhikkhu amhākaṃ ayyavausāṭitakāni sāmaṃ gahaitvā paribhuñ‍jissati! thairauyaṃ bhikkhu vaṭharau manussamaṃsaṃ mañ‍ñai khādatī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharasīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

264. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū udakadantapaunai udakadantapauṇai (syā॰ ka॰) kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakadantapaunaṃ sāmaṃ gahaitvā paribhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

265. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharaiyya, añ‍ñatra udakadantapaunā, pācittiya’’nti|

266. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

adin‍naṃ nāma appaṭiggahitakaṃ vuc‍cati|

din‍naṃ nāma kāyaina vā kāyapaṭibaddhaina vā nissaggiyaina vā daintai hatthapāsai ṭhitau kāyaina vā kāyapaṭibaddhaina vā paṭiggaṇhāti, aitaṃ din‍naṃ nāma|

āhārau nāma udakadantapaunaṃ ṭhapaitvā yaṃkiñ‍ci ajjhauharaṇīyaṃ, aisau āhārau nāma|

añ‍ñatra udakadantapaunāti ṭhapaitvā udakadantapaunaṃ|

‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti gaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

267. appaṭiggahitakai appaṭiggahitakasañ‍ñī adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āhāraiti, añ‍ñatra udakadantapaunā, āpatti pācittiyassa| appaṭiggahitakai vaimatikau adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āhāraiti, añ‍ñatra udakadantapaunā, āpatti pācittiyassa| appaṭiggahitakai paṭiggahitakasañ‍ñī adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āhāraiti, añ‍ñatra udakadantapaunā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

paṭiggahitakai appaṭiggahitakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paṭiggahitakai vaimatikau, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa| paṭiggahitakai paṭiggahitakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

268. anāpatti  udakadantapaunai, cattāri mahāvikatāni sati pac‍cayai asati kappiyakārakai sāmaṃ gahaitvā paribhuñ‍jati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dantapaunasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

bhaujanavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

piṇḍau gaṇaṃ paraṃ pūvaṃ, dvai ca vuttā pavāraṇā|

vikālai san‍nidhī khīraṃ, dantapaunaina tai dasāti||

5. acailakavaggau

1. acailakasikkhāpadaṃ

269. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa khādanīyaṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘tainahānanda, vighāsādānaṃ pūvaṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā vighāsādai paṭipāṭiyā nisīdāpaitvā aikaikaṃ pūvaṃ daintau añ‍ñatarissā paribbājikāya aikaṃ mañ‍ñamānau dvai pūvai adāsi| sāmantā paribbājikāyau taṃ paribbājikaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘jārau tai aisau samaṇau’’ti| ‘‘na mai sau samaṇau jārau, aikaṃ mañ‍ñamānau dvai pūvai adāsī’’ti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau aikaikaṃ pūvaṃ daintau tassāyaiva paribbājikāya aikaṃ mañ‍ñamānau dvai pūvai adāsi| sāmantā paribbājikāyau taṃ paribbājikaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘jārau tai aisau samaṇau’’ti| ‘‘na mai sau samaṇau jārau, aikaṃ mañ‍ñamānau dvai pūvai adāsī’’ti| ‘‘jārau na jārau’’ti bhaṇḍiṃsu| añ‍ñataraupi ājīvakau parivaisanaṃ agamāsi| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pahūtaina sappinā audanaṃ madditvā tassa ājīvakassa  mahantaṃ piṇḍaṃ adāsi| atha khau sau ājīvakau taṃ piṇḍaṃ ādāya agamāsi| añ‍ñatarau ājīvakau taṃ ājīvakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kutau  tayā, āvusau, piṇḍau laddhau’’ti? ‘‘tassāvusau,  samaṇassa gautamassa muṇḍagahapatikassa parivaisanāya laddhau’’ti|

assausuṃ khau upāsakā taisaṃ ājīvakānaṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau tai upāsakā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai upāsakā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘imai, bhantai, titthiyā avaṇṇakāmā buddhassa avaṇṇakāmā dhammassa avaṇṇakāmā saṅghassa| sādhu, bhantai, ayyā titthiyānaṃ sahatthā na dadaiyyu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā tai upāsakai dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau tai upāsakā bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya …pai॰… saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, vinayānuggahāya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

270. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu acailakassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā  sahatthā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā dadaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

271. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

acailakau nāma yau kauci paribbājakasamāpan‍nau naggau|

paribbājakau nāma bhikkhuñ‍ca sāmaṇairañ‍ca ṭhapaitvā yau kauci paribbājakasamāpan‍nau|

paribbājikā nāma bhikkhuniñ‍ca sikkhamānañ‍ca sāmaṇairiñ‍ca ṭhapaitvā yā kāci paribbājikasamāpan‍nā|

khādanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – udakadantapaunaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ  nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ|

dadaiyyāti kāyaina vā kāyapaṭibaddhaina vā nissaggiyaina vā daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

272. titthiyai titthiyasañ‍ñī sahatthā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| titthiyai vaimatikau sahatthā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| titthiyai atitthiyasañ‍ñī sahatthā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

udakadantapaunaṃ daiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| atitthiyai titthiyasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| atitthiyai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| atitthiyai atitthiyasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

273. anāpatti dāpaiti na daiti, upanikkhipitvā daiti, bāhirālaipaṃ daiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

acailakasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. uyyaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

274. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau bhātunau saddhivihārikaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihāvusau, gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’’ti| tassa adāpaitvā uyyaujaisi – ‘‘gacchāvusau, na mai tayā saddhiṃ kathā vā nisaj‍jā vā phāsu hauti, aikakassa mai kathā  vā nisaj‍jā vā phāsu hautī’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu upakaṭṭhai kālai nāsakkhi piṇḍāya carituṃ, paṭik‍kamanaipi bhattavissaggaṃ na sambhāvaisi, chin‍nabhattau ahausi| atha khau sau bhikkhu ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau bhikkhuṃ – ‘aihāvusau, gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’ti tassa adāpaitvā  uyyaujaissatī’’ti  …pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, bhikkhuṃ – ‘‘aihāvusau, gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’’ti tassa adāpaitvā uyyaujaisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, bhikkhuṃ – ‘‘aihāvusau, gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’’ti tassa adāpaitvā uyyaujaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

275. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ – ‘‘aihāvusau, gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’ti tassa dāpaitvā vā adāpaitvā vā uyyaujaiyya – ‘gacchāvusau, na mai tayā saddhiṃ kathā vā nisaj‍jā vā phāsu hauti, aikakassa mai kathā vā nisaj‍jā vā phāsu hautī’ti, aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiya’’nti|

276. yau panāti yau, yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunti añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ|

aihāvusau, gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vāti gāmaupi nigamaupi nagarampi, gāmau caiva nigamau ca|

tassa dāpaitvāti yāguṃ vā bhattaṃ vā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā dāpaitvā|

adāpaitvāti na kiñ‍ci dāpaitvā|

uyyaujaiyyāti mātugāmaina saddhiṃ hasitukāmau kīḷitukāmau rahau nisīditukāmau anācāraṃ ācaritukāmau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘gacchāvusau, na mai tayā saddhiṃ kathā vā nisaj‍jā vā phāsu hauti, aikakassa mai kathā vā nisaj‍jā vā phāsu hautī’’ti uyyaujaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dassanūpacāraṃ vā savanūpacāraṃ vā vijahantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| vijahitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñanti na añ‍ñau kauci pac‍cayau hauti uyyaujaituṃ|

277. upasampan‍nai  upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī uyyaujaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau uyyaujaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī uyyaujaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

kalisāsanaṃ āraupaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍naṃ uyyaujaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kalisāsanaṃ āraupaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai  upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

278. anāpatti ‘‘ubhau aikatau na yāpaissāmā’’ti uyyaujaiti, ‘‘mahagghaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ passitvā laubhadhammaṃ uppādaissatī’’ti uyyaujaiti, ‘‘mātugāmaṃ passitvā anabhiratiṃ uppādaissatī’’ti uyyaujaiti, ‘‘gilānassa vā auhiyyakassa vā vihārapālassa vā yāguṃ vā bhattaṃ vā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā nīharā’’ti uyyaujaiti, na anācāraṃ ācaritukāmau, sati karaṇīyai uyyaujaiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

uyyaujanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. sabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ

279. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau sahāyakassa gharaṃ gantvā tassa pajāpatiyā saddhiṃ sayanigharai sayanīgharai (syā॰) nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi| atha khau sau purisau yaināyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau purisau pajāpatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘dadaihāyyassa bhikkha’’nti| atha khau sā itthī āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa bhikkhaṃ adāsi| atha khau sau purisau āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘gacchatha, bhantai, yatau ayyassa bhikkhā din‍nā’’ti| atha khau sā itthī sal‍lakkhaitvā – ‘‘pariyuṭṭhitau ayaṃ purisau’’ti, āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘nisīdatha, bhantai, mā agamitthā’’ti| dutiyampi khau sau purisau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau sau purisau āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘gacchatha, bhantai, yatau ayyassa bhikkhā din‍nā’’ti| tatiyampi khau sā itthī āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nisīdatha, bhantai, mā agamitthā’’ti|

atha khau sau purisau nikkhamitvā bhikkhū ujjhāpaisi – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, ayyau upanandau mayhaṃ pajāpatiyā saddhiṃ sayanigharai nisin‍nau| sau mayā  uyyaujīyamānau na icchati gantuṃ| bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja  nisaj‍jaṃ kappaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

280. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

281. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

sabhaujanaṃ nāma kulaṃ itthī caiva hauti purisau ca, itthī ca purisau ca ubhau anikkhantā haunti, ubhau avītarāgā|

anupakhaj‍jāti anupavisitvā|

nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyyāti mahal‍lakai gharai piṭṭhasaṅghāṭassa piṭṭhisaṅghāṭassa (syā॰) hatthapāsaṃ vijahitvā nisīdati, āpatti pācittiyassa| khuddakai gharai piṭṭhivaṃsaṃ atik‍kamitvā nisīdati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

282. sayanigharai sayanigharasañ‍ñī sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| sayanigharai vaimatikau sabhaujanai kulai  anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti  pācittiyassa| sayanigharai nasayanigharasañ‍ñī  sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

nasayanigharai sayanigharasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nasayanigharai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nasayanigharai nasayanigharasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

283. anāpatti mahal‍lakai gharai piṭṭhasaṅghāṭassa hatthapāsaṃ avijahitvā nisīdati, khuddakai gharai piṭṭhivaṃsaṃ anatik‍kamitvā nisīdati, bhikkhu dutiyau hauti, ubhau nikkhantā haunti, ubhau vītarāgā, nasayanigharai, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sabhaujanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. rahaupaṭicchan‍nasikkhāpadaṃ

284. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau sahāyakassa gharaṃ gantvā tassa pajāpatiyā saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai  nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi| atha khau sau purisau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyau upanandau mayhaṃ pajāpatiyā saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaissatī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa purisassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaissatīti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

285. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

286. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

mātugāmau nāma manussitthī, na yakkhī na paitī na tiracchānagatā, antamasau tadahujātāpi dārikā, pagaiva mahattarī|

saddhinti aikatau|

pāci॰ 200,291; pārā॰ 445,454 rahau nāma cakkhussa rahau sautassa rahau| cakkhussa rahau nāma na sak‍kā hauti akkhiṃ vā nikhaṇīyamānai bhamukaṃ vā ukkhipīyamānai sīsaṃ vā ukkhipīyamānai passituṃ| sautassa rahau nāma na sak‍kā hauti pakatikathā sautuṃ|

pārā॰ 445 paṭicchan‍naṃ nāma āsanaṃ kuṭṭaina kuḍḍaina (sī॰ syā॰) vā kavāṭaina vā kilañ‍jaina vā sāṇipākāraina vā rukkhaina vā thambhaina vā kautthaḷiyā kauṭṭhaḷiyā (sī॰ syā॰) kautthaḷikāya (ka॰) vā, yaina kainaci paṭicchan‍naṃ hauti|

nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyyāti mātugāmai nisin‍nai bhikkhu upanisin‍nau vā hauti upanipan‍nau vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| bhikkhu nisin‍nai mātugāmau upanisin‍nau vā hauti upanipan‍nau vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| ubhau vā nisin‍nā haunti ubhau vā nipan‍nā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

287. mātugāmai mātugāmasañ‍ñī rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| mātugāmai vaimatikau rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| mātugāmai amātugāmasañ‍ñī rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

yakkhiyā vā paitiyā vā paṇḍakaina vā  tiracchānagatāya vā manussaviggahitthiyā vā saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| amātugāmai mātugāmasañ‍ñī, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa| amātugāmai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| amātugāmai amātugāmasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

288. anāpatti  yau kauci viñ‍ñū purisau dutiyau hauti, tiṭṭhati na nisīdati, arahaupaikkhau, añ‍ñavihitau nisīdati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

rahaupaṭicchan‍nasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. rahaunisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

289. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau sahāyakassa gharaṃ gantvā tassa pajāpatiyā saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi| atha khau sau purisau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyau upanandau mayhaṃ pajāpatiyā saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaissatī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa purisassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pāsādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

290. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

291. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ  imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

mātugāmau nāma manussitthī, na yakkhī na paitī na tiracchānagatā, viñ‍ñū paṭibalā subhāsitadubbhāsitaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ ājānituṃ|

saddhinti aikatau|

aikau  aikāyāti bhikkhu caiva hauti mātugāmau ca|

pāci॰ 200,286; pārā॰ 445,454 rahau  nāma cakkhussa rahau, sautassa rahau| cakkhussa rahau nāma na sak‍kā hauti akkhiṃ vā nikhaṇīyamānai bhamukaṃ vā ukkhipīyamānai sīsaṃ vā ukkhipīyamānai passituṃ| sautassa rahau nāma na sak‍kā hauti pakatikathā sautuṃ|

nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiyyāti mātugāmai nisin‍nai bhikkhu upanisin‍nau vā hauti upanipan‍nau vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| bhikkhu nisin‍nai mātugāmau upanisin‍nau vā hauti upanipan‍nau vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| ubhau vā nisin‍nā haunti ubhau vā nipan‍nā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

292. mātugāmai mātugāmasañ‍ñī aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| mātugāmai vaimatikau aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| mātugāmai amātugāmasañ‍ñī aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

yakkhiyā vā paitiyā vā paṇḍakaina vā tiracchānagatamanussaviggahitthiyā vā tiracchānagatāya vā manussaviggahitthiyā vā (ka॰) saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| amātugāmai mātugāmasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| amātugāmai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| amātugāmai  amātugāmasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

293. anāpatti yau kauci viñ‍ñū purisau dutiyau hauti, tiṭṭhati na nisīdati, arahaupaikkhau añ‍ñavihitau nisīdati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

rahaunisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. cārittasikkhāpadaṃ

294. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa  upaṭṭhākakulaṃ āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ bhattaina nimantaisi| añ‍ñaipi bhikkhū bhattaina nimantaisi| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau  sakyaputtau puraibhattaṃ, kulāni payirupāsati| atha khau tai bhikkhū tai manussai aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘daithāvusau bhatta’’nti| ‘‘āgamaitha, bhantai, yāvāyyau upanandau āgacchatī’’ti| dutiyampi khau tai bhikkhū…pai॰… tatiyampi khau tai bhikkhū tai manussai aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘daithāvusau, bhattaṃ; purai kālau atik‍kamatī’’ti| ‘‘yampi mayaṃ, bhantai, bhattaṃ karimhā ayyassa upanandassa kāraṇā| āgamaitha, bhantai, yāvāyyau upanandau āgacchatī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau puraibhattaṃ kulāni payirupāsitvā divā āgacchati| bhikkhū na cittarūpaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau nimantitau sabhattau samānau puraibhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, nimantitau sabhattau samānau puraibhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ  āpaj‍jasīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, nimantitau sabhattau samānau puraibhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu nimantitau sabhattau samānau puraibhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

295. mahāva॰ 277 taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa upaṭṭhākakulaṃ saṅghassatthāya khādanīyaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘ayyassa upanandassa dassaitvā saṅghassa dātabba’’nti| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhau hauti| atha khau tai manussā ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhū pucchiṃsu – ‘‘kahaṃ, bhantai, ayyau upanandau’’ti? ‘‘aisāvusau, āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhau’’ti| ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, khādanīyaṃ ayyassa upanandassa dassaitvā saṅghassa dātabba’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ  pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, paṭiggahaitvā nikkhipatha yāva upanandau āgacchatī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ puraibhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ  āpaj‍jitu’’nti pacchābhattaṃ kulāni  payirupāsitvā divā paṭik‍kami, khādanīyaṃ ussāriyittha| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau pacchābhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, pacchābhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jasīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, pacchābhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana bhikkhavai imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu nimantitau sabhattau samānau puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

296. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū cīvaradānasamayai kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā kulāni na payirupāsanti| cīvaraṃ parittaṃ uppaj‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cīvaradānasamayai kulāni payirupāsituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –
