‘‘yau pana bhikkhu nimantitau sabhattau samānau puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| cīvaradānasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

297. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū cīvarakammaṃ karaunti, atthau ca hauti sūciyāpi suttainapi satthakainapi| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā kulāni na payirupāsanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cīvarakārasamayai kulāni payirupāsituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu nimantitau sabhattau samānau puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

298. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū gilānā haunti, atthau ca hauti bhaisaj‍jaihi| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā kulāni na payirupāsanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, santaṃ bhikkhuṃ āpucchā kulāni payirupāsituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

299. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu nimantitau sabhattau samānau santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| cīvaradānasamayau, cīvarakārasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

300. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

nimantitau nāma pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaujanānaṃ añ‍ñataraina bhaujanaina nimantitau|

sabhattau nāma yaina nimantitau  taina sabhattau|

santaṃ nāma bhikkhuṃ sak‍kā hauti āpucchā pavisituṃ|

asantaṃ nāma bhikkhuṃ na sak‍kā hauti āpucchā pavisituṃ|

puraibhattaṃ nāma yaina nimantitau taṃ abhuttāvī|

pacchābhattaṃ nāma yaina nimantitau taṃ antamasau kusaggainapi aruṇuggamanaipi (sī॰) bhuttaṃ hauti|

kulaṃ nāma cattāri kulāni – khattiyakulaṃ, brāhmaṇakulaṃ, vaissakulaṃ, suddakulaṃ|

kulaisu  cārittaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti añ‍ñassa gharūpacāraṃ auk‍kamantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ ummāraṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñatra  samayāti ṭhapaitvā samayaṃ|

cīvaradānasamayau nāma anatthatai kathinai vassānassa pacchimau māsau, atthatai kathinai pañ‍ca māsā|

cīvarakārasamayau nāma cīvarai kayiramānai|

301. nimantitai nimantitasañ‍ñī santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñatra samayā, āpatti pācittiyassa| nimantitai vaimatikau santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñatra samayā, āpatti pācittiyassa| nimantitai animantitasañ‍ñī santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñatra samayā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

animantitai nimantitasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| animantitai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| animantitai animantitasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

302. anāpatti  samayai, santaṃ bhikkhuṃ āpucchā pavisati, asantaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā pavisati, añ‍ñassa gharaina maggau hauti, gharūpacāraina maggau hauti, antarārāmaṃ gacchati, bhikkhunupassayaṃ gacchati, titthiyasaiyyaṃ gacchati, paṭik‍kamanaṃ gacchati, bhattiyagharaṃ gacchati, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

cārittasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mahānāmasikkhāpadaṃ

303. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina mahānāmassa sak‍kassa bhaisaj‍jaṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ catumāsaṃ catummāsaṃ (sī॰) cātumāsaṃ (syā॰) bhaisaj‍jaina pavāraitu’’nti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, mahānāma! taina hi tvaṃ, mahānāma, saṅghaṃ catumāsaṃ bhaisaj‍jaina pavāraihī’’ti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, catumāsaṃ bhaisaj‍jappac‍cayapavāraṇaṃ sāditunti|

304. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ parittaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ viñ‍ñāpainti| tathaiva mahānāmassa sak‍kassa bhaisaj‍jaṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| dutiyampi khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ aparampi catumāsaṃ bhaisaj‍jaina pavāraitu’’nti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, mahānāma! taina hi tvaṃ, mahānāma, saṅghaṃ aparampi catumāsaṃ bhaisaj‍jaina pavāraihī’’ti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, puna pavāraṇampi sāditunti|

305. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ parittaṃyaiva bhaisaj‍jaṃ viñ‍ñāpainti| tathaiva mahānāmassa sak‍kassa bhaisaj‍jaṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| tatiyampi khau mahānāmau  sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ yāvajīvaṃ bhaisaj‍jaina pavāraitu’’nti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, mahānāma! taina hi tvaṃ, mahānāma, saṅghaṃ yāvajīvaṃ bhaisaj‍jaina pavāraihī’’ti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, nic‍capavāraṇampi sāditunti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū dun‍nivatthā haunti duppārutā anākappasampan‍nā| mahānāmau sak‍kau vattā hauti – ‘‘kissa tumhai, bhantai, dun‍nivatthā duppārutā anākappasampan‍nā? nanu nāma pabbajitaina sunivatthaina bhavitabbaṃ supārutaina ākappasampan‍nainā’’ti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mahānāmai sak‍kai upanandhiṃsu| atha khau chabbaggiyānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau mayaṃ upāyaina mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ maṅku karaiyyāmā’’ti? atha khau chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘mahānāmaina khau, āvusau, sak‍kaina saṅghau bhaisaj‍jaina pavāritau| handa mayaṃ, āvusau, mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ  sappiṃ  viñ‍ñāpaimā’’ti| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū yaina mahānāmau sak‍kau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘dauṇaina, āvusau, sappinā atthau’’ti| ‘‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaitha| manussā vajaṃ gatā sappiṃ āharituṃ| kālaṃ āharissathā’’ti kālai harissathāti (syā॰)|

dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘dauṇaina, āvusau, sappinā atthau’’ti| ‘‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaitha| manussā vajaṃ gatā sappiṃ āharituṃ| kālaṃ āharissathā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tayā, āvusau, adātukāmaina pavāritaina, yaṃ tvaṃ pavāraitvā na daisī’’ti! atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā – ‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaithā’ti vuc‍camānā nāgamaissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū mahānāmassa sak‍kassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mahānāmaina sak‍kaina – ‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaithā’ti vuc‍camānā nāgamaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, mahānāmaina sak‍kaina – ‘‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaithā’’ti vuc‍camānā nāgamaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, mahānāmaina sak‍kaina – ‘‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai āgamaithā’’ti vuc‍camānā nāgamaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ  uddisaiyyātha –

306. ‘‘agilānaina bhikkhunā catumāsappac‍cayapavāraṇā sāditabbā, añ‍ñatra punapavāraṇāya, añ‍ñatra nic‍capavāraṇāya; tatau cai uttari sādiyaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

307. agilānaina bhikkhunā catumāsappac‍cayapavāraṇā sāditabbāti gilānappac‍cayapavāraṇā sāditabbā|

punapavāraṇāpi sāditabbāti yadā gilānau bhavissāmi tadā viñ‍ñāpaissāmīti|

nic‍capavāraṇāpi sāditabbāti yadā gilānau bhavissāmi tadā viñ‍ñāpaissāmīti|

tatau  cai uttari sādiyaiyyāti atthi pavāraṇā bhaisaj‍japariyantā na rattipariyantā, atthi pavāraṇā rattipariyantā na bhaisaj‍japariyantā, atthi pavāraṇā bhaisaj‍japariyantā ca rattipariyantā ca, atthi pavāraṇā naiva bhaisaj‍japariyantā na rattipariyantā|

bhaisaj‍japariyantā nāma bhaisaj‍jāni pariggahitāni haunti – ‘‘aittakaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi pavāraimī’’ti| rattipariyantā nāma rattiyau pariggahitāyau haunti – ‘‘aittakāsu rattīsu pavāraimī’’ti| bhaisaj‍japariyantā ca rattipariyantā ca nāma bhaisaj‍jāni ca pariggahitāni haunti rattiyau ca pariggahitāyau haunti – ‘‘aittakaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi aittakāsu rattīsu pavāraimī’’ti| naiva bhaisaj‍japariyantā na rattipariyantā nāma bhaisaj‍jāni ca apariggahitāni haunti rattiyau ca apariggahitāyau haunti|

308. bhaisaj‍japariyantai  – yaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi pavāritau hauti tāni bhaisaj‍jāni ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñāni bhaisaj‍jāni viñ‍ñāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| rattipariyantai – yāsu rattīsu pavāritau hauti, tā rattiyau ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñāsu rattīsu viñ‍ñāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| bhaisaj‍japariyantai ca rattipariyantai ca – yaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi pavāritau hauti, tāni bhaisaj‍jāni ṭhapaitvā yāsu rattīsu pavāritau hauti, tā rattiyau ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñāni bhaisaj‍jāni añ‍ñāsu rattīsu viñ‍ñāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| naiva bhaisaj‍japariyantai na rattipariyantai, anāpatti|

309. na bhaisaj‍jaina karaṇīyaina karaṇīyai (sī॰ syā॰) bhaisaj‍jaṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| añ‍ñaina bhaisaj‍jaina karaṇīyaina añ‍ñaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| tatuttari tatuttarisañ‍ñī bhaisaj‍jaṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| tatuttari vaimatikau bhaisaj‍jaṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| tatuttari natatuttarisañ‍ñī bhaisaj‍jaṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

natatuttari tatuttarisañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| natatuttari vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| natatuttari natatuttarisañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

310. anāpatti  yaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi pavāritau hauti tāni bhaisaj‍jāni viñ‍ñāpaiti, yāsu rattīsu pavāritau hauti tāsu rattīsu viñ‍ñāpaiti, ‘‘imaihi tayā  bhaisaj‍jaihi pavāritāmha, amhākañ‍ca iminā ca iminā ca bhaisaj‍jaina atthau’’ti ācikkhitvā viñ‍ñāpaiti, ‘‘yāsu tayā rattīsu pavāritāmha tāyau ca rattiyau vītivattā amhākañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jaina atthau’’ti ācikkhitvā viñ‍ñāpaiti, ñātakānaṃ pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

mahānāmasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. uyyuttasaināsikkhāpadaṃ

311. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina rājā pasainadi kausalau saināya abbhuyyātau hauti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya agamaṃsu| addasā khau rājā pasainadi kausalau chabbaggiyai bhikkhū dūratauva āgacchantai| disvāna pak‍kausāpaitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tumhai, bhantai, āgatatthā’’ti? ‘‘mahārājānaṃ mayaṃ daṭṭhukāmā’’ mahārāja mahārājānaṃ mayaṃ daṭṭhukāmā (ka॰) ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, maṃ diṭṭhaina yuddhābhinandinaṃ yuddhābhinandinā (ka॰); nanu bhagavā passitabbau’’ti? manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya āgacchissanti! amhākampi alābhā, amhākampi dul‍laddhaṃ, yai mayaṃ ājīvassa haitu puttadārassa kāraṇā saināya āgacchāmā’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya gacchissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya gacchathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya gacchissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰…  aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya gacchaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

312. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau mātulau saināya gilānau hauti| sau tassa bhikkhunau santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘ahañhi saināya gilānau| āgacchatu bhadantau| icchāmi bhadantassa āgata’’nti| atha khau tassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – ‘na uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya gantabba’nti| ayañ‍ca mai mātulau saināya gilānau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tathārūpappac‍cayā saināya gantuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

313. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya gacchaiyya, añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, pācittiya’’nti|

314. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

uyyuttā nāma sainā gāmatau nikkhamitvā niviṭṭhā vā hauti payātā vā|

sainā nāma hatthī assā rathā pattī| dvādasapurisau hatthī, tipurisau assau, catupurisau rathau, cattārau purisā sarahatthā patti| dassanāya gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yattha ṭhitau passati, āpatti  pācittiyassa| dassanūpacāraṃ vijahitvā punappunaṃ passati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayāti ṭhapaitvā tathārūpappac‍cayaṃ|

315. uyyuttai uyyuttasañ‍ñī dassanāya gacchati, añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, āpatti pācittiyassa| uyyuttai vaimatikau dassanāya gacchati, añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, āpatti pācittiyassa| uyyuttai anuyyuttasañ‍ñī dassanāya gacchati, añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aikamaikaṃ  dassanāya gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yattha ṭhitau passati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dassanūpacāraṃ vijahitvā punappunaṃ passati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anuyyuttai uyyuttasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anuyyuttai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anuyyuttai anuyyuttasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

316. anāpatti ārāmai ṭhitau passati, bhikkhussa ṭhitaukāsaṃ vā nisin‍naukāsaṃ vā nipan‍naukāsaṃ vā āgacchati, paṭipathaṃ gacchantau passati, tathārūpappac‍cayā, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

uyyuttasaināsikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. saināvāsasikkhāpadaṃ

317. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sati karaṇīyai sainaṃ gantvā atiraikatirattaṃ saināya vasanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā saināya vasissanti! amhākampi alābhā, amhākampi dul‍laddhaṃ, yai mayaṃ ājīvassa haitu puttadārassa kāraṇā saināya paṭivasāmā’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atiraikatirattaṃ saināya vasissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, atiraikatirattaṃ saināya vasathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, atiraikatirattaṃ saināya vasissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

318. ‘‘siyā ca tassa bhikkhunau kaucidaiva pac‍cayau sainaṃ gamanāya, dirattatirattaṃ taina bhikkhunā saināya vasitabbaṃ| tatau cai uttariṃ vasaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

319. siyā ca tassa bhikkhunau kaucidaiva pac‍cayau sainaṃ gamanāyāti  siyā pac‍cayau siyā karaṇīyaṃ|

dirattatirattaṃ  taina bhikkhunā saināya vasitabbanti dvaitissau rattiyau vasitabbaṃ|

tatau cai uttari vasaiyyāti catutthai divasai atthaṅgatai sūriyai saināya vasati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

320. atiraikatirattai atiraikasañ‍ñī saināya vasati, āpatti pācittiyassa| atiraikatirattai vaimatikau saināya vasati, āpatti pācittiyassa| atiraikatirattai ūnakasañ‍ñī saināya vasati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

ūnakatirattai atiraikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakatirattai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakatirattai ūnakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

321. anāpatti dvaitissau rattiyau vasati, ūnakadvaitissau rattiyau vasati, dvai rattiyau vasitvā tatiyāya rattiyā purāruṇā nikkhamitvā puna vasati, gilānau vasati, gilānassa karaṇīyaina vasati, sainā  vā paṭisaināya ruddhā hauti, kainaci palibuddhau hauti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

saināvāsasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. uyyaudhikasikkhāpadaṃ

322. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū dirattatirattaṃ saināya vasamānā uyyaudhikampi balaggampi sainābyūhampi anīkadassanampi gacchanti| añ‍ñataraupi chabbaggiyau bhikkhu uyyaudhikaṃ gantvā kaṇḍaina paṭividdhau hauti| manussā taṃ bhikkhuṃ uppaṇḍaisuṃ – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhantai, suyuddhaṃ ahausi, kati tai lakkhāni laddhānī’’ti? sau bhikkhu taihi manussaihi uppaṇḍīyamānau maṅku ahausi| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā uyyaudhikaṃ dassanāya āgacchissanti! amhākampi alābhā, amhākampi dul‍laddhaṃ, yai mayaṃ ājīvassa haitu  puttadārassa kāraṇā uyyaudhikaṃ āgacchāmā’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uyyaudhikaṃ dassanāya gacchissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, uyyaudhikaṃ dassanāya gacchathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, uyyaudhikaṃ dassanāya gacchissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

323. ‘‘dirattatirattaṃ cai bhikkhu saināya vasamānau uyyaudhikaṃ vā balaggaṃ vā sainābyūhaṃ vā anīkadassanaṃ vā gacchaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

324. dirattatirattaṃ cai bhikkhu saināya vasamānauti dvaitissau rattiyau vasamānau|

uyyaudhikaṃ nāma yattha sampahārau dissati|

balaggaṃ nāma aittakā hatthī, aittakā assā, aittakā rathā, aittakā pattī|

sainābyūhaṃ nāma itau hatthī hauntu, itau assā hauntu, itau rathā hauntu, itau pattikā hauntu|

anīkaṃ nāma hatthānīkaṃ, assānīkaṃ, rathānīkaṃ, pattānīkaṃ| tayau hatthī pacchimaṃ hatthānīkaṃ, tayau assā pacchimaṃ assānīkaṃ, tayau rathā pacchimaṃ rathānīkaṃ, cattārau purisā sarahatthā pattī pacchimaṃ pattānīkaṃ| dassanāya gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yattha ṭhitau passati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dassanūpacāraṃ vijahitvā punappunaṃ passati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aikamaikaṃ dassanāya gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yattha ṭhitau passati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dassanūpacāraṃ vijahitvā punappunaṃ passati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

325. anāpatti  ārāmai ṭhitau passati, bhikkhussa ṭhitaukāsaṃ vā nisin‍naukāsaṃ vā  nipan‍naukāsaṃ vā āgantvā sampahārau dissati, paṭipathaṃ gacchantau passati, sati karaṇīyai gantvā passati, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

uyyaudhikasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

acailakavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

pūvaṃ kathaupanandassa, tayaṃpaṭṭhākamaiva ca|

mahānāmau pasainadi, saināviddhau imai dasāti acailakaṃ uyyaujañ‍ca, sabhaujanaṃ duvai rahau| sabhattakañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jaṃ, uyyuttaṃ sainuyyaudhikaṃ||

6. surāpānavaggau

1. surāpānasikkhāpadaṃ

326. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā caitiyaisu cārikaṃ caramānau yaina bhaddavatikā taina pāyāsi| addasaṃsu khau gaupālakā pasupālakā kassakā pathāvinau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mā khau, bhantai, bhagavā ambatitthaṃ agamāsi| ambatitthai, bhantai, jaṭilassa assamai nāgau paṭivasati iddhimā āsivisau āsīvisau (sī॰ syā॰) ghauravisau| sau bhagavantaṃ mā vihaiṭhaisī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau gaupālakā pasupālakā kassakā pathāvinau bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mā khau, bhantai, bhagavā ambatitthaṃ agamāsi| ambatitthai, bhantai, jaṭilassa assamai nāgau paṭivasati iddhimā āsivisau ghauravisau| sau bhagavantaṃ mā vihaiṭhaisī’’ti| tatiyampi khau bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi|

atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina bhaddavatikā tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā bhaddavatikāyaṃ viharati| atha khau āyasmā sāgatau yaina ambatitthassa ambatitthakassa (sī॰), ambatitthaṃ (syā॰) jaṭilassa assamau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā agyāgāraṃ pavisitvā tiṇasanthārakaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā nisīdi pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| addasā khau sau nāgau āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ paviṭṭhaṃ| disvāna dummanau dukkhī dummanau (sī॰ syā॰) padhūpāyi padhūpāsi (syā॰ ka॰)| āyasmāpi sāgatau padhūpāyi padhūpāsi (syā॰ ka॰)| atha khau sau nāgau makkhaṃ asahamānau paj‍jali| āyasmāpi sāgatau taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā paj‍jali| atha khau āyasmā sāgatau tassa nāgassa taijasā taijaṃ pariyādiyitvā yaina bhaddavatikā tainupasaṅkami| atha khau bhagavā bhaddavatikāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina kausambī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| assausuṃ khau kausambikā upāsakā – ‘‘ayyau kira sāgatau ambatitthikaina nāgaina saddhiṃ saṅgāmaisī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina kausambī tadavasari| atha khau kausambikā upāsakā bhagavatau pac‍cuggamanaṃ karitvā yaināyasmā sāgatau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau kausambikā  upāsakā āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, ayyānaṃ dul‍labhañ‍ca manāpañ‍ca, kiṃ paṭiyādaimā’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kausambikai upāsakai aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘atthāvusau, kāpautikā nāma pasan‍nā bhikkhūnaṃ dul‍labhā ca manāpā ca, taṃ paṭiyādaithā’’ti| atha khau kausambikā upāsakā gharai gharai kāpautikaṃ pasan‍naṃ paṭiyādaitvā āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhaṃ disvāna āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pivatu, bhantai, ayyau sāgatau kāpautikaṃ pasan‍naṃ, pivatu, bhantai, ayyau sāgatau kāpautikaṃ pasan‍na’’nti| atha khau āyasmā sāgatau gharai gharai kāpautikaṃ pasan‍naṃ pivitvā nagaramhā nikkhamantau nagaradvārai paripati|

atha khau bhagavā sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ nagaramhā nikkhamantau addasa āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ nagaradvārai paripatantaṃ| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘gaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, sāgata’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ ārāmaṃ naitvā yaina bhagavā taina sīsaṃ katvā nipātaisuṃ| atha khau āyasmā sāgatau parivattitvā yaina bhagavā taina pādai karitvā saiyyaṃ kappaisi| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘nanu, bhikkhavai, pubbai sāgatau tathāgatai sagāravau ahausi sappatissau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘api nu khau, bhikkhavai, sāgatau aitarahi tathāgatai sagāravau sappatissau’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘nanu, bhikkhavai, sāgatau ambatitthikaina nāgaina ḍaiḍḍubhaināpi (sī॰ syā॰) saddhiṃ saṅgāmaisī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘api nu khau, bhikkhavai, sāgatau aitarahi pahauti nāgaina saddhiṃ saṅgāmaitu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘api nu khau, bhikkhavai, taṃ pātabbaṃ yaṃ pivitvā visañ‍ñī assā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāgatassa ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, sāgatau maj‍jaṃ pivissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

327. ‘‘surāmairayapānai pācittiya’’nti|

328. surā nāma piṭṭhasurā pūvasurā audanasurā kiṇṇapakkhittā sambhārasaṃyuttā|

mairayau nāma pupphāsavau phalāsavau madhvāsavau guḷāsavau sambhārasaṃyuttau|

pivaiyyāti  antamasau kusaggainapi pivati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

maj‍jai maj‍jasañ‍ñī pivati, āpatti pācittiyassa| maj‍jai vaimatikau pivati, āpatti pācittiyassa| maj‍jai amaj‍jasañ‍ñī pivati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

amaj‍jai maj‍jasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| amaj‍jai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| amaj‍jai amaj‍jasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

329. anāpatti amaj‍jañ‍ca hauti maj‍javaṇṇaṃ maj‍jagandhaṃ maj‍jarasaṃ taṃ pivati, sūpasampākai, maṃsasampākai, tailasampākai, āmalakaphāṇitai, amaj‍jaṃ ariṭṭhaṃ pivati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

surāpānasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. aṅgulipataudakasikkhāpadaṃ

330. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sattarasavaggiyaṃ bhikkhuṃ aṅgulipataudakaina hāsaisuṃ| sau bhikkhu uttantau anassāsakau kālamakāsi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ aṅgulipataudakaina  hāsaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ aṅgulipataudakaina hāsaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhuṃ aṅgulipataudakaina hāsaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

331. ‘‘aṅgulipataudakai pācittiya’’nti|

332. aṅgulipataudakau nāma aṅgulipataudakau nāma aṅguliyāpi tudanti (syā॰) upasampan‍nau upasampan‍naṃ hasādhippāyau hassādhippāyau (sī॰ syā॰) kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī aṅgulipataudakaina hāsaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau aṅgulipataudakaina hāsaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī aṅgulipataudakaina  hāsaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

333. anupasampan‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

334. anāpatti na hasādhippāyau, sati karaṇīyai āmasati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

aṅgulipataudakasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. hasadhammasikkhāpadaṃ

335. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū aciravatiyā nadiyā  udakai kīḷanti| taina khau pana samayaina rājā pasainadi kausalau mal‍likāya daiviyā saddhiṃ uparipāsādavaragatau hauti| addasā khau rājā pasainadi kausalau sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū aciravatiyā nadiyā udakai kīḷantai| disvāna mal‍likaṃ daiviṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitai tai, mal‍likai, arahantau udakai kīḷantī’’ti| ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau, mahārāja, bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ apañ‍ñattaṃ| tai vā bhikkhū appakatañ‍ñunau’’ti| atha khau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau ahaṃ upāyaina bhagavatau ca na āraucaiyyaṃ, bhagavā ca jānaiyya imai  bhikkhū udakai kīḷitā’’ti? atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū pak‍kausāpaitvā mahantaṃ guḷapiṇḍaṃ adāsi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhantai, guḷapiṇḍaṃ bhagavatau daithā’’ti| sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū taṃ guḷapiṇḍaṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘imaṃ, bhantai, guḷapiṇḍaṃ rājā pasainadi kausalau bhagavatau daitī’’ti| ‘‘kahaṃ pana tumhai, bhikkhavai, rājā addasā’’ti| ‘‘aciravatiyā nadiyā, bhagavā, udakai kīḷantai’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, udakai kīḷissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

336. ‘‘udakai  hasadhammai hassadhammai (sī॰ syā॰) pācittiya’’nti|

337. udakai hasadhammau nāma uparigaupphakai udakai hasādhippāyau nimuj‍jati vā ummuj‍jati vā palavati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

338. udakai hasadhammai hasadhammasañ‍ñī, āpatti pācittiyassa| udakai hasadhammai vaimatikau, āpatti pācittiyassa| udakai hasadhammai ahasadhammasañ‍ñī, āpatti pācittiyassa|

haiṭṭhāgaupphakai udakai kīḷati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| udakai nāvāya kīḷati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| hatthaina vā pādaina vā kaṭṭhaina vā kaṭhalāya vā udakaṃ paharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhājanagataṃ udakaṃ vā kañ‍jikaṃ vā khīraṃ vā tak‍kaṃ vā rajanaṃ vā passāvaṃ vā cikkhal‍laṃ vā kīḷati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

udakai ahasadhammai hasadhammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| udakai ahasadhammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| udakai ahasadhammai ahasadhammasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

339. anāpatti na hasādhippāyau, sati karaṇīyai udakaṃ autaritvā nimuj‍jati  vā ummuj‍jati vā palavati vā, pāraṃ gacchantau nimuj‍jati  vā ummuj‍jati vā palavati vā, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

hasadhammasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. anādariyasikkhāpadaṃ

340. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā chan‍nau anācāraṃ ācarati| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘māvusau chan‍na, aivarūpaṃ akāsi| naitaṃ kappatī’’ti| sau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca karautiyaiva| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā chan‍nau anādariyaṃ karissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, chan‍na, anādariyaṃ karausīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, anādariyaṃ karissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

341. ‘‘anādariyai pācittiya’’nti|

342. anādariyaṃ nāma dvai anādariyāni – puggalānādariyañ‍ca dhammānādariyañ‍ca|

puggalānādariyaṃ nāma upasampan‍naina pañ‍ñattaina vuc‍camānau – ‘‘ayaṃ ukkhittakau vā vambhitau vā garahitau vā, imassa vacanaṃ akataṃ bhavissatī’’ti anādariyaṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

dhammānādariyaṃ nāma upasampan‍naina pañ‍ñattaina vuc‍camānau ‘‘kathāyaṃ nassaiyya vā vinassaiyya vā antaradhāyaiyya vā’’, taṃ vā na sikkhitukāmau anādariyaṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

343. upasampan‍nai  upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī anādariyaṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau anādariyaṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī anādariyaṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

apañ‍ñattaina vuc‍camānau – ‘‘idaṃ na sal‍laikhāya na dhutatthāya na pāsādikatāya na apacayāya na vīriyārambhāya saṃvattatī’’ti anādariyaṃ karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍naina pañ‍ñattaina vā apañ‍ñattaina vā vuc‍camānau – ‘‘idaṃ na sal‍laikhāya na dhutatthāya na  pāsādikatāya na apacayāya na vīriyārambhāya saṃvattatī’’ti anādariyaṃ karauti, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa|

anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

344. anāpatti – ‘‘aivaṃ amhākaṃ ācariyānaṃ uggahau paripucchā’’ti bhaṇati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

anādariyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. bhiṃsāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

345. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū bhiṃsāpainti| tai bhiṃsāpīyamānā raudanti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tumhai, āvusau, raudathā’’ti? ‘‘imai, āvusau, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū amhai bhiṃsāpaintī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ bhiṃsāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ bhiṃsāpaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhuṃ bhiṃsāpaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

346. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ bhiṃsāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

347. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunti añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ|

bhiṃsāpaiyyāti  upasampan‍nau upasampan‍naṃ bhiṃsāpaitukāmau rūpaṃ vā saddaṃ vā gandhaṃ vā rasaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbaṃ vā upasaṃharati| bhāyaiyya vā sau na vā bhāyaiyya, āpatti pācittiyassa| caurakantāraṃ vā vāḷakantāraṃ vā pisācakantāraṃ vā ācikkhati| bhāyaiyya vā sau  na vā bhāyaiyya, āpatti pācittiyassa|

348. upasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī bhiṃsāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau bhiṃsāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| ‘upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī bhiṃsāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍naṃ bhiṃsāpaitukāmau rūpaṃ vā saddaṃ vā gandhaṃ vā rasaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbaṃ vā upasaṃharati| bhāyaiyya vā sau na vā bhāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| caurakantāraṃ vā vāḷakantāraṃ vā pisācakantāraṃ vā ācikkhati| bhāyaiyya vā sau na vā bhāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai  vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

349. anāpatti na bhiṃsāpaitukāmau rūpaṃ vā saddaṃ vā gandhaṃ vā rasaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbaṃ vā upasaṃharati, caurakantāraṃ vā vāḷakantāraṃ vā pisācakantāraṃ vā ācikkhati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

bhiṃsāpanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. jautikasikkhāpadaṃ

350. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati suṃsumāragirai suṃsumāragirai (sī॰ syā॰) saṃsumāragirai (ka॰) bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū haimantikai kālai añ‍ñataraṃ mahantaṃ susirakaṭṭhaṃ jautiṃ samādahitvā visibbaisuṃ| tasmiñ‍ca susirai kaṇhasappau agginā santattau nikkhamitvā bhikkhū paripātaisi| bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ upadhāviṃsu| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū jautiṃ samādahitvā visibbaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū jautiṃ samādahitvā visibbaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā jautiṃ samādahitvā visibbaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu visibbanāpaikkhau jautiṃ samādahaiyya vā samādahāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

351. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū gilānā haunti| gilānapucchakā bhikkhū gilānai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kac‍cāvusau, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīya’’nti? ‘‘pubbai mayaṃ, āvusau, jautiṃ samādahitvā visibbaima; taina  nau phāsu hauti| idāni pana ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhitta’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā  na visibbaima, taina nau na phāsu hautī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā jautiṃ samādahitvā vā samādahāpaitvā vā visibbaituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu agilānau visibbanāpaikkhau jautiṃ samādahaiyya  vā samādahāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

352. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū padīpaipi jautikaipi jantāgharaipi kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tathārūpappac‍cayā jautiṃ samādahituṃ samādahāpaituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

353. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu agilānau visibbanāpaikkhau jautiṃ samādahaiyya vā samādahāpaiyya vā, añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, pācittiya’’nti|

354. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

agilānau  nāma yassa vinā agginā phāsu hauti|

gilānau nāma yassa vinā agginā na phāsu hauti|

visibbanāpaikkhauti tappitukāmau|

jauti nāma aggi vuc‍cati|

samādahaiyyāti sayaṃ samādahati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

samādahāpaiyyāti  añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| sakiṃ āṇattau bahukampi samādahati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñatra tathā rūpappac‍cayāti ṭhapaitvā tathārūpappac‍cayaṃ|

355. agilānau agilānasañ‍ñī visibbanāpaikkhau jautiṃ samādahati vā samādahāpaiti vā, añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, āpatti pācittiyassa| agilānau vaimatikau visibbanāpaikkhau jautiṃ samādahati  vā samādahāpaiti vā, añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, āpatti pācittiyassa| agilānau gilānasañ‍ñī visibbanāpaikkhau jautiṃ samādahati vā samādahāpaiti vā, añ‍ñatra tathārūpappac‍cayā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

paṭilātaṃ ukkhipati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānau agilānasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānau vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānau gilānasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

356. anāpatti gilānassa, añ‍ñaina kataṃ visibbaiti, vītac‍citaṅgāraṃ visibbaiti, padīpai jautikai jantāgharai tathārūpappac‍cayā, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

jautikasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. nahānasikkhāpadaṃ

357. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū tapaudai nahāyanti| taina khau pana samayaina atha khau (sī॰ syā॰) rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau ‘‘sīsaṃ nahāyissāmī’’ti tapaudaṃ  gantvā – ‘‘yāvāyyā nahāyantī’’ti aikamantaṃ paṭimānaisi| bhikkhū yāva samandhakārā nahāyiṃsu| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau vikālai sīsaṃ nahāyitvā, nagaradvārai thakitai bahinagarai vasitvā, kālassaiva asambhin‍naina vilaipanaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, mahārāja, kālassaiva āgatau asambhin‍naina vilaipanainā’’ti? atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau bhagavā rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū rājānampi passitvā na mattaṃ  jānitvā nahāyantī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā rājānampi passitvā na mattaṃ jānitvā nahāyissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

358. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū uṇhasamayai pariḷāhasamayai kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na nahāyanti, saidagataina gattaina sayanti| cīvarampi saināsanampi dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uṇhasamayai pariḷāhasamayai aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyaiyya, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| diyaḍḍhau māsau saisau gimhānanti gimhānaṃ (itipi) vassānassa paṭhamau māsau ic‍caitai aḍḍhataiyyamāsā uṇhasamayau pariḷāhasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ  bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

359. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū gilānā haunti| gilānapucchakā bhikkhū gilānai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kac‍cāvusau, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci  yāpanīya’’nti? ‘‘pubbai mayaṃ, āvusau, aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyāma, taina nau phāsu hauti; idāni pana ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhitta’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na nahāyāma, taina nau na phāsu hautī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyaiyya, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| diyaḍḍhau māsau saisau gimhānanti gimhānaṃ (itipi) vassānassa  paṭhamau māsau ic‍caitai aḍḍhataiyyamāsā uṇhasamayau, pariḷāhasamayau, gilānasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

360. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū navakammaṃ katvā kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na nahāyanti| tai saidagataina gattaina sayanti| cīvarampi saināsanampi dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kammasamayai aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyaiyya, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| diyaḍḍhau māsau saisau gimhānanti vassānassa paṭhamau māsau ic‍caitai aḍḍhataiyyamāsā uṇhasamayau, pariḷāhasamayau, gilānasamayau, kammasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

361. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū addhānaṃ gantvā kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na nahāyanti| tai saidagataina gattaina sayanti| cīvarampi saināsanampi dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, addhānagamanasamayai aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyaiyya, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| diyaḍḍhau māsau saisau gimhānanti vassānassa paṭhamau māsau ic‍caitai aḍḍhataiyyamāsā uṇhasamayau, pariḷāhasamayau, gilānasamayau, kammasamayau, addhānagamanasamayau – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

362. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū ajjhaukāsai cīvarakammaṃ karauntā sarajaina vātaina aukiṇṇā haunti| daivau ca thaukaṃ thaukaṃ phusāyati| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na nahāyanti, kilin‍naina  gattaina sayanti| cīvarampi saināsanampi  dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vātavuṭṭhisamayai aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

363. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyaiyya, añ‍ñatra samayā, pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau| diyaḍḍhau māsau saisau gimhānanti vassānassa paṭhamau māsau ic‍caitai aḍḍhataiyyamāsā uṇhasamayau, pariḷāhasamayau, gilānasamayau, kammasamayau, addhānagamanasamayau, vātavuṭṭhisamayau  – ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

364. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

aurainaddhamāsanti ūnakaddhamāsaṃ|

nahāyaiyyāti cuṇṇaina vā mattikāya vā nahāyati, payaugai payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| nahānapariyausānai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñatra  samayāti ṭhapaitvā samayaṃ|

uṇhasamayau nāma diyaḍḍhau māsau saisau gimhānaṃ| pariḷāhasamayau nāma vassānassa paṭhamau māsau ‘‘ic‍caitai aḍḍhataiyyamāsā uṇhasamayau pariḷāhasamayau’’ti nahāyitabbaṃ|

gilānasamayau nāma yassa vinā nahānaina na phāsu hauti| gilānasamayauti nahāyitabbaṃ|

kammasamayau nāma antamasau parivaiṇampi sammaṭṭhaṃ hauti| ‘‘kammasamayau’’ti nahāyitabbaṃ|

addhānagamanasamayau nāma ‘‘addhayaujanaṃ gacchissāmī’’ti nahāyitabbaṃ, gacchantaina nahāyitabbaṃ, gataina nahāyitabbaṃ|

vātavuṭṭhisamayau nāma bhikkhū sarajaina vātaina aukiṇṇā haunti, dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni kāyai patitāni haunti| ‘‘vātavuṭṭhisamayau’’ti nahāyitabbaṃ|

365. ūnakaddhamāsai ūnakasañ‍ñī, añ‍ñatra samayā, nahāyati, āpatti pācittiyassa| ūnakaddhamāsai vaimatikau, añ‍ñatra samayā, nahāyati, āpatti  pācittiyassa| ūnakaddhamāsai atiraikasañ‍ñī, añ‍ñatra samayā, nahāyati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

atiraikaddhamāsai ūnakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| atiraikaddhamāsai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| atiraikaddhamāsai atiraikasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

366. anāpatti samayai, addhamāsaṃ nahāyati, atiraikaddhamāsaṃ nahāyati, pāraṃ gacchantau nahāyati, sabbapac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

nahānasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. dubbaṇṇakaraṇasikkhāpadaṃ

367. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū ca paribbājakā ca sākaitā sāvatthiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| antarāmaggai caurā nikkhamitvā tai acchindiṃsu| sāvatthiyā rājabhaṭā nikkhamitvā tai caurai sabhaṇḍai gahaitvā bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisuṃ – ‘‘āgacchantu, bhadantā, sakaṃ sakaṃ cīvaraṃ sañ‍jānitvā gaṇhantū’’ti| bhikkhū na sañ‍jānanti| tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā attanau attanau cīvaraṃ na sañ‍jānissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhūnaṃ tadanucchavikaṃ tadanulaumikaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya…pai॰… saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, vinayānuggahāya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

368. ‘‘navaṃ pana bhikkhunā cīvaralābhaina tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ ādātabbaṃ – nīlaṃ vā kaddamaṃ vā kāḷasāmaṃ vā| anādā  cai bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ  navaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

369. navaṃ nāma akatakappaṃ vuc‍cati|

cīvaraṃ nāma chan‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ|

tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ ādātabbanti antamasau kusaggainapi ādātabbaṃ|

nīlaṃ nāma dvai nīlāni – kaṃsanīlaṃ, palāsanīlaṃ|

kaddamau nāma audakau vuc‍cati|

kāḷasāmaṃ  nāma yaṃkiñ‍ci kāḷasāmakaṃ kāḷakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

anādā cai bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇanti antamasau kusaggainapi anādiyitvā tiṇṇaṃ  dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ navaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

370. anādin‍nai anādin‍nasañ‍ñī paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| anādin‍nai vaimatikau paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| anādin‍nai ādin‍nasañ‍ñī paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

ādin‍nai anādin‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ādin‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ādin‍nai ādin‍nasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

371. anāpattiādiyitvā paribhuñ‍jati, kappau naṭṭhau hauti, kappakataukāsau jiṇṇau hauti, kappakataina akappakataṃ saṃsibbitaṃ hauti, aggaḷai anuvātai paribhaṇḍai, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dubbaṇṇakaraṇasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vikappanasikkhāpadaṃ

372. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau  sakyaputtau bhātunau saddhivihārikassa bhikkhunau sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappaitvā appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ apac‍cuddhārakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) paribhuñ‍jati| atha khau sau bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ayaṃ, āvusau, āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau mayhaṃ cīvaraṃ sāmaṃ vikappaitvā appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ paribhuñ‍jatī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappaitvā appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ paribhuñ‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappaitvā appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ paribhuñ‍jasīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ  vikappaitvā appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ paribhuñ‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

373. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā sikkhamānāya vā sāmaṇairassa vā sāmaṇairiyā vā sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappaitvā appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ apac‍cuddhārakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

374. yau panāti  yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhussāti  añ‍ñassa bhikkhussa|

bhikkhunī nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

sikkhamānā nāma dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā|

sāmaṇairau nāma dasasikkhāpadikau|

sāmaṇairī nāma dasasikkhāpadikā|

sāmanti sayaṃ vikappaitvā|

cīvaraṃ nāma chan‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ vikappanupagaṃ pacchimaṃ|

vikappanā nāma dvai vikappanā – sammukhāvikappanā ca parammukhāvikappanā ca|

sammukhāvikappanā nāma ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ tuyhaṃ vikappaimi itthan‍nāmassa vā’’ti|

parammukhāvikappanā  nāma ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappanatthāya tuyhaṃ dammī’’ti| taina vattabbau – ‘‘kau tai mittau vā sandiṭṭhau vā’’ti? ‘‘itthan‍nāmau ca itthan‍nāmau cā’’ti| taina vattabbau – ‘‘ahaṃ taisaṃ dammi, taisaṃ santakaṃ paribhuñ‍ja vā vissaj‍jaihi vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karauhī’’ti|

appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ  nāma tassa vā adin‍naṃ, tassa vā avissasantau paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

375. appac‍cuddhāraṇai appac‍cuddhāraṇasañ‍ñī paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| appac‍cuddhāraṇai vaimatikau paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| appac‍cuddhāraṇai appac‍cuddhāraṇasañ‍ñī paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhiṭṭhaiti vā vissaj‍jaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pac‍cuddhāraṇai appac‍cuddhāraṇasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pac‍cuddhāraṇai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pac‍cuddhāraṇai pac‍cuddhāraṇasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

376. anāpatti sau vā daiti, tassa vā vissasantau paribhuñ‍jati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

vikappanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. cīvaraapanidhānasikkhāpadaṃ

377. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū asan‍nihitaparikkhārā haunti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sattarasavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pattampi cīvarampi apanidhainti| sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘daithāvusau, amhākaṃ  pattampi cīvarampī’’ti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū hasanti, tai raudanti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tumhai, āvusau, raudathā’’ti? ‘‘imai, āvusau, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū amhākaṃ pattampi cīvarampi apanidhaintī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ pattampi  cīvarampi apanidhaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pattampi cīvarampi apanidhaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhūnaṃ pattampi cīvarampi apanidhaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

378. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā nisīdanaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ vā kāyabandhanaṃ vā apanidhaiyya vā apanidhāpaiyya vā, antamasau hasāpaikkhaupi, pācittiya’’nti|

379. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhussāti añ‍ñassa bhikkhussa|

pattau nāma dvai pattā – ayaupattau, mattikāpattau|

cīvaraṃ nāma chan‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ, vikappanupagaṃ pacchimaṃ|

nisīdanaṃ nāma sadasaṃ vuc‍cati|

sūcigharaṃ nāma sasūcikaṃ vā asūcikaṃ vā|

kāyabandhanaṃ nāma dvai kāyabandhanāni – paṭṭikā, sūkarantakaṃ|

apanidhaiyya vāti sayaṃ apanidhaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

apanidhāpaiyya vāti añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaisi, āpatti pācittiyassa| apanidhāpaiyya vā ti ayyaṃ āṇāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| sakiṃ āṇattau bahukampi apanidhaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

antamasau hasāpaikkhaupīti kīḷādhippāyau|

380. upasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā nisīdanaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ vā kāyabandhanaṃ vā apanidhaiti vā apanidhāpaiti vā, antamasau hasāpaikkhaupi, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau…pai॰… upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā nisīdanaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ vā kāyabandhanaṃ vā apanidhaiti vā apanidhāpaiti vā, antamasau hasāpaikkhaupi, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñaṃ  parikkhāraṃ apanidhaiti vā apanidhāpaiti vā, antamasau hasāpaikkhaupi, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nassa pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ  vā añ‍ñaṃ vā parikkhāraṃ apanidhaiti vā apanidhāpaiti vā, antamasau hasāpaikkhaupi, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

381. anāpatti nahasādhippāyau, dun‍nikkhittaṃ paṭisāmaiti, ‘‘dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā dassāmī’’ti paṭisāmaiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

cīvaraapanidhānasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

surāpānavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

surā aṅguli hāsau ca tauyañ‍ca (itipi), anādariyañ‍ca bhiṃsanaṃ|

jautinahānadubbaṇṇaṃ, sāmaṃ apanidhaina cāti||

7. sappāṇakavaggau

1. sañ‍cic‍casikkhāpadaṃ

382. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī issāsau hauti, kākā cassa amanāpā haunti| sau kākai vijjhitvā vijjhitvā sīsaṃ chinditvā sūlai paṭipāṭiyā ṭhapaisi| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kainimai, āvusau, kākā jīvitā vauraupitā’’ti? ‘‘mayā, āvusau| amanāpā mai kākā’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā udāyī sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, udāyi, sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

383. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

384. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

sañ‍cic‍cāti jānantau sañ‍jānantau caic‍ca abhivitaritvā vītik‍kamau|

pāṇau nāma tiracchānagatapāṇau vuc‍cati|

jīvitā vauraupaiyyāti jīvitindriyaṃ upacchindati uparaudhaiti  santatiṃ vikaupaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

385. pāṇai  pāṇasañ‍ñī jīvitā vauraupaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| pāṇai vaimatikau jīvitā vauraupaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pāṇai appāṇasañ‍ñī jīvitā vauraupaiti, anāpatti| appāṇai pāṇasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| appāṇai vaimitakau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| appāṇai appāṇasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

386. anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, namaraṇādhippāyassa, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sañ‍cic‍casikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. sappāṇakasikkhāpadaṃ

387. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍janti| yai  tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

388. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

389. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

jānāti nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti, añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti| ‘‘sappāṇaka’’nti jānantau, ‘‘paribhaugaina marissantī’’ti jānantau paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

390. sappāṇakai sappāṇakasañ‍ñī paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| sappāṇakai vaimatikau paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sappāṇakai appāṇakasañ‍ñī paribhuñ‍jati, anāpatti| appāṇakai sappāṇakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| appāṇakai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| appāṇakai appāṇakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

391. anāpatti ‘‘sappāṇaka’’nti ajānantau, ‘‘appāṇaka’’nti jānantau, ‘‘paribhaugaina na marissantī’’ti jānantau paribhuñ‍jati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sappāṇakasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. uk‍kauṭanasikkhāpadaṃ

392. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ yathādhammaṃ nihatādhikaraṇaṃ punakammāya uk‍kauṭainti – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kammaṃ anihataṃ dun‍nihataṃ  puna nihanitabba’’nti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ yathādhammaṃ nihatādhikaraṇaṃ punakammāya uk‍kauṭaissantī’’ti  …pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ yathādhammaṃ nihatādhikaraṇaṃ punakammāya uk‍kauṭaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, jānaṃ yathādhammaṃ nihatādhikaraṇaṃ punakammāya uk‍kauṭaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

393. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ yathādhammaṃ nihatādhikaraṇaṃ punakammāya uk‍kauṭaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

394. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

jānāti nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti, añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti, sau vā āraucaiti|

yathādhammaṃ nāma dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina kataṃ, aitaṃ yathādhammaṃ nāma|

adhikaraṇaṃ  nāma cattāri adhikaraṇāni – vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ|

punakammāya uk‍kauṭaiyyāti ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ punakātabbaṃ kammaṃ anihataṃ dun‍nihataṃ puna nihanitabbaṃ’’ti uk‍kauṭaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

395. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī uk‍kauṭaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikau uk‍kauṭaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī uk‍kauṭaiti, anāpatti| adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

396. anāpatti  ‘‘adhammaina vā vaggaina vā na kammārahassa vā kammaṃ kata’’nti jānantau uk‍kauṭaiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

uk‍kauṭanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. duṭṭhul‍lasikkhāpadaṃ

397. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā bhātunau saddhivihārikassa bhikkhunau āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau| mā kassaci āraucaihī’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā saṅghaṃ tassā āpattiyā parivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau tassā āpattiyā parivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivasantau taṃ bhikkhuṃ passitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā saṅghaṃ tassā āpattiyā parivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tassā āpattiyā parivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ vaidayāmahaṃ (syā॰), āvusau, vaidiyatī’’ti maṃ āyasmā dhāraitū’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, yau añ‍ñaupi imaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati saupi aivaṃ karautī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘ayaṃ, āvusau, āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā āpaj‍ji (?) sau mai āraucaiti mā kassaci āraucaihī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana  tvaṃ, āvusau, paṭicchādaisī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu bhikkhussa jānaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ paṭicchādaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhussa jānaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ paṭicchādaisīti| ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, bhikkhussa jānaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ paṭicchādaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

398. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa jānaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ paṭicchādaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

399. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhussāti añ‍ñassa bhikkhussa|

jānāti nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti, sau vā āraucaiti|

duṭṭhul‍lā  nāma āpatti cattāri ca pārājikāni tairasa ca saṅghādisaisā|

paṭicchādaiyyāti ‘‘imaṃ jānitvā caudaissanti, sāraissanti khuṃsaissanti, vambhaissanti, maṅkuṃ karissanti nāraucaissāmī’’ti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

400. duṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā duṭṭhul‍lāpattisañ‍ñī paṭicchādaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| duṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā vaimatikau paṭicchādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| duṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā aduṭṭhul‍lāpattisañ‍ñī paṭicchādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nassa duṭṭhul‍laṃ vā aduṭṭhul‍laṃ vā ajjhācāraṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aduṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā duṭṭhul‍lāpattisañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aduṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aduṭṭhul‍lāya āpattiyā aduṭṭhul‍lāpattisañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

401. anāpatti – ‘‘saṅghassa bhaṇḍanaṃ vā kalahau vā viggahau vā vivādau vā bhavissatī’’ti nāraucaiti, ‘‘saṅghabhaidau vā saṅgharāji vā bhavissatī’’ti nāraucaiti, ‘‘ayaṃ kakkhaḷau pharusau jīvitantarāyaṃ vā brahmacariyantarāyaṃ vā karissatī’’ti nāraucaiti, añ‍ñai patirūpai bhikkhū apassantau nāraucaiti, nachādaitukāmau nāraucaiti, ‘‘pañ‍ñāyissati sakaina kammainā’’ti nāraucaiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

duṭṭhul‍lasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. ūnavīsativassasikkhāpadaṃ

402. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| idaṃ vatthu mahāva॰ 99 taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai sattarasavaggiyā dārakā sahāyakā haunti| upālidārakau taisaṃ pāmaukkhau hauti| atha khau upālissa mātāpitūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau upāyaina upāli amhākaṃ ac‍cayaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyya na ca kilamaiyyā’’ti? atha khau upālissa mātāpitūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau upāli laikhaṃ sikkhaiyya, aivaṃ khau upāli amhākaṃ ac‍cayaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyya, na ca kilamaiyyā’’ti| atha khau upālissa mātāpitūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau upāli laikhaṃ sikkhissati, aṅguliyau  dukkhā bhavissanti| sacai khau upāli gaṇanaṃ sikkhaiyya, aivaṃ khau upāli amhākaṃ ac‍cayaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyya na ca kilamaiyyā’’ti| atha khau upālissa mātāpitūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau upāli gaṇanaṃ sikkhissati, urassa dukkhau bhavissati| sacai khau upāli rūpaṃ sikkhaiyya, aivaṃ khau upāli amhākaṃ ac‍cayaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyya na ca kilamaiyyā’’ti| atha khau upālissa mātāpitūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau upāli rūpaṃ sikkhissati, akkhīni dukkhā bhavissanti| imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā sukhasīlā sukhasamācārā subhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā nivātaisu sayanaisu  sayanti| sacai khau upāli samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajaiyya, aivaṃ khau upāli amhākaṃ ac‍cayaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyya, na ca kilamaiyyā’’ti|

assausi khau upālidārakau mātāpitūnaṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau upālidārakau yaina tai dārakā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai dārakai aitadavauca – ‘‘aitha mayaṃ, ayyā, samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajissāmā’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, ayya, pabbajissasi, aivaṃ mayampi pabbajissāmā’’ti| atha khau tai dārakā aikamaikassa mātāpitarau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘anujānātha maṃ agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| atha khau taisaṃ dārakānaṃ mātāpitarau – ‘‘sabbaipimai dārakā samānacchandā kalyāṇādhippāyā’’ti anujāniṃsu| tai bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāciṃsu| tai bhikkhū pabbājaisuṃ upasampādaisuṃ| tai rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya raudanti – ‘‘yāguṃ daitha, bhattaṃ daitha, khādanīyaṃ daithā’’ti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘āgamaitha, āvusau, yāva ratti vibhāyati| sacai yāgu bhavissati, pivissatha| sacai bhattaṃ bhavissati, bhuñ‍jissatha| sacai khādanīyaṃ bhavissati, khādissatha| nau cai bhavissati yāgu vā bhattaṃ vā khādanīyaṃ vā, piṇḍāya caritvā bhuñ‍jissathā’’ti| aivampi khau  tai bhikkhū bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānā raudantiyaiva – ‘‘yāguṃ daitha, bhattaṃ daitha, khādanīyaṃ daithā’’ti| saināsanaṃ ūhadantipi ummihantipi|

assausi  khau bhagavā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya dārakasaddaṃ| sutvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau sau, ānanda, dārakasaddau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ  kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū jānaṃ ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā jānaṃ ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaissanti! ūnakavīsativassau, bhikkhavai, puggalau akkhamau hauti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ anadhivāsakajātikau hauti| vīsativassauva khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau khamau hauti sītassa uṇhassa…pai॰… pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau hauti| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

403. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaiyya, sau ca puggalau anupasampan‍nau, tai ca bhikkhū gārayhā, idaṃ tasmiṃ pācittiya’’nti|

404. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

jānāti nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti, añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti, sau vā āraucaiti|

ūnavīsativassau  nāma appattavīsativassau|

‘‘upasampādaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariyaṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati sammanati (ka॰), āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyassa āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariyassa ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

405. ūnavīsativassai  ūnavīsativassasañ‍ñī upasampādaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| ūnavīsativassai vaimatikau upasampādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnavīsativassai paripuṇṇavīsativassasañ‍ñī upasampādaiti, anāpatti| paripuṇṇavīsativassai ūnavīsativassasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paripuṇṇavīsativassai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paripuṇṇavīsativassai paripuṇṇavīsativassasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

406. anāpatti ūnavīsativassaṃ paripuṇṇavīsativassasañ‍ñī upasampādaiti, paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ paripuṇṇavīsativassasañ‍ñī upasampādaiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

ūnavīsativassasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. thaiyyasatthasikkhāpadaṃ

407. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau satthau rājagahā paṭiyālaukaṃ gantukāmau hauti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tai manussai aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampāyasmantaihi saddhiṃ gamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘mayaṃ khau, bhantai, suṅkaṃ pariharissāmā’’ti| ‘‘pajānāthāvusau’’ti| assausuṃ khau kammiyā kammikā (sī॰ syā॰) – ‘‘satthau kira suṅkaṃ pariharissatī’’ti| tai maggai pariyuṭṭhiṃsu| atha khau tai kammikā taṃ satthaṃ gahaitvā acchinditvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, bhantai, jānaṃ thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ gacchasī’’ti? palibundhaitvā muñ‍ciṃsu| atha khau sau bhikkhu sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu jānaṃ thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, jānaṃ thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jasīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, jānaṃ thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

408. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu jānaṃ thaiyyasatthaina  saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya, antamasau gāmantarampi, pācittiya’’nti|

409. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

jānāti nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti, añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti, sau vā āraucaiti|

thaiyyasatthau nāma caurā katakammā vā haunti akatakammā vā, rājānaṃ vā thaiyyaṃ gacchanti suṅkaṃ vā pariharanti|

saddhinti aikatau|

saṃvidhāyāti – ‘‘gacchāmāvusau, gacchāma bhantai; gacchāma bhantai, gacchāmāvusau, aj‍ja vā hiyyau vā parai vā gacchāmā’’ti saṃvidahati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

antamasau gāmantarampīti kuk‍kuṭasampātai gāmai gāmantarai gāmantarai āpatti pācittiyassa| agāmakai arañ‍ñai addhayaujanai addhayaujanai āpatti pācittiyassa|

410. thaiyyasatthai  thaiyyasatthasañ‍ñī saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, antamasau gāmantarampi, āpatti pācittiyassa| thaiyyasatthai vaimatikau saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, antamasau gāmantarampi, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| thaiyyasatthai athaiyyasatthasañ‍ñī saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, antamasau gāmantarampi, anāpatti| bhikkhu saṃvidahati, manussā na saṃvidahanti, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa| athaiyyasatthai thaiyyasatthasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| athaiyyasatthai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| athaiyyasatthai athaiyyasatthasañ‍ñī  anāpatti|

411. anāpatti asaṃvidahitvā gacchati, manussā saṃvidahanti bhikkhu na saṃvidahati, visaṅkaitaina gacchati, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

thaiyyasatthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. saṃvidhānasikkhāpadaṃ

412. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ gacchantau añ‍ñataraina gāmadvāraina atik‍kamati| añ‍ñatarā itthī sāmikaina saha bhaṇḍitvā gāmatau nikkhamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ passitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ, bhantai, ayyau gamissatī’’ti? ‘‘sāvatthiṃ khau ahaṃ, bhagini, gamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘ahaṃ ayyaina saddhiṃ gamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aiyyāsi, bhaginī’’ti| atha khau tassā itthiyā sāmikau gāmatau nikkhamitvā manussai pucchi – ‘‘apāyyau apayyā (sī॰ syā॰) aivarūpiṃ itthiṃ passaiyyāthā’’ti? ‘‘aisāyyau, pabbajitaina saha gacchatī’’ti| atha khau sau purisau anubandhitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ gahaitvā ākauṭaitvā muñ‍ci| atha khau sau bhikkhu añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai padhūpaintau nisīdi| atha khau sā itthī taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nāyyau sau bhikkhu maṃ nippātaisi; apica, ahamaiva taina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ gacchāmi; akārakau sau bhikkhu; gaccha, naṃ khamāpaihī’’ti| atha khau sau purisau taṃ bhikkhuṃ khamāpaisi| atha khau sau bhikkhu sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu mātugāmaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mātugāmaina  saddhiṃ  saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jasīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mātugāmaina  saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ, uddisaiyyātha –

413. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu mātugāmaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya, antamasau gāmantarampi, pācittiya’’nti|

414. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

mātugāmau nāma manussitthī, na yakkhī na paitī na tiracchānagatā viñ‍ñū paṭibalā subhāsitadubbhāsitaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ ājānituṃ|

saddhinti  aikatau|

saṃvidhāyāti – ‘‘gacchāma bhagini, gacchāmāyya, gacchāmāyya, gacchāma bhagini, aj‍ja vā hiyyau vā parai vā gacchāmā’’ti saṃvidahati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

antamasau gāmantarampīti kuk‍kuṭasampātai gāmai gāmantarai gāmantarai āpatti pācittiyassa| agāmakai arañ‍ñai addhayaujanai addhayaujanai āpatti pācittiyassa|

415. mātugāmai mātugāmasañ‍ñī saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, antamasau gāmantarampi, āpatti pācittiyassa| mātugāmai vaimatikau saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, antamasau gāmantarampi, āpatti pācittiyassa| mātugāmai amātugāmasañ‍ñī saṃvidhāya  aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, antamasau gāmantarampi, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhu saṃvidahati mātugāmau na saṃvidahati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yakkhiyā vā paitiyā paṇḍakaina vā tiracchānagatamanussaviggahitthiyā vā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, antamasau gāmantarampi, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| amātugāmai mātugāmasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| amātugāmai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| amātugāmai amātugāmasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

416. anāpatti  asaṃvidahitvā gacchati, mātugāmau saṃvidahati bhikkhu na saṃvidahati, visaṅkaitaina gacchati, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

saṃvidhānasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. ariṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

417. idaṃ vatthu cūḷava॰ 65; ma॰ ni॰ 1.234 taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina ariṭṭhassa nāma bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa  gandhabādhipubbassa (syā॰ ka॰) aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti| assausuṃ khau sambahulā bhikkhū  – ‘‘ariṭṭhassa kira nāma bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, āvusau ariṭṭha, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃbyākhau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti|

‘‘mā, āvusau ariṭṭha, aivaṃ avaca| mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi| na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ abbhācikkhanaṃ (itipi)| na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| anaikapariyāyaināvusau ariṭṭha, antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā| alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya| appassādā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau  aittha bhiyyau| maṃsapaisūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā…pai॰… tiṇuk‍kūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… supinakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… yācitakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… asisūnūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… sattisūlūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’’ti|

aivampi khau ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau taihi bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva taṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘‘aivaṃbyākhau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, – ‘yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti| yatau ca khau tai bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaituṃ, atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha  khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai  aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, ariṭṭha, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃbyākhau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti|

kassa nu khau nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mayā aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsi? nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā| alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya| appassādā kāmā vuttā mayā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā…pai॰… maṃsapaisūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… tiṇuk‍kūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… supinakūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… yācitakūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… asisūnūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… sattisūlūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… sappasirūpamā kāmā  vuttā mayā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| atha ca pana tvaṃ, maughapurisa, attanā duggahitaina amhai  caiva abbhācikkhasi, attānañ‍ca khaṇasi, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavasi| tañhi tai, maughapurisa, bhavissati dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰…| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

418. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya, anaikapariyāyaināvusau, antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā, alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāyā’ti| aivañ‍ca aivañ‍ca pana (ka॰) sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbau tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānau taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

419. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

aivaṃ vadaiyyāti – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti|

sau  bhikkhūti yau sau aivaṃvādī bhikkhu|

bhikkhūhīti añ‍ñaihi  bhikkhūhi| yai passanti yai suṇanti taihi vattabbau – ‘‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya, anaikapariyāyaināvusau antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā| alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāyā’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbau| tatiyampi vattabbau| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sutvā na vadanti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhampi  ākaḍḍhitvā vattabbau – ‘‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya, anaikapariyāyaināvusau antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā| alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāyā’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbau| tatiyampi vattabbau| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau bhikkhu samanubhāsitabbau| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samanubhāsitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

420. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti| sau taṃ diṭṭhiṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| yadi  saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ samanubhāsaiyya – tassā diṭṭhiyā paṭinissaggāya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti| sau taṃ diṭṭhiṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ samanubhāsati tassā diṭṭhiyā paṭinissaggāya| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau samanubhāsanā, tassā diṭṭhiyā paṭinissaggāya, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti| sau taṃ diṭṭhiṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ samanubhāsati tassā diṭṭhiyā paṭinissaggāya| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau samanubhāsanā, tassā diṭṭhiyā paṭinissaggāya, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘samanubhaṭṭhau  saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, tassā diṭṭhiyā paṭinissaggāya| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

ñattiyā  duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa|

421. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikau na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

422. anāpatti asamanubhāsantassa, paṭinissaj‍jantassa, ummattakassāti|

ariṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ukkhittasambhaugasikkhāpadaṃ

423. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ tathāvādinā ariṭṭhaina bhikkhunā bhikkhunā gaddhabādhipubbaina (?) akaṭānudhammaina taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhiṃ sambhuñ‍jantipi saṃvasantipi sahāpi saiyyaṃ kappainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ tathāvādinā ariṭṭhaina bhikkhunā bhikkhunā gaddhabādhipubbaina (?) akaṭānudhammaina taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhiṃ sambhuñ‍jissantipi saṃvasissantipi sahāpi saiyyaṃ kappaissantīti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ tathāvādinā ariṭṭhaina bhikkhunā bhikkhunā gaddhabādhipubbaina (?) akaṭānudhammaina  taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhiṃ sambhuñ‍jathāpi saṃvasathāpi sahāpi saiyyaṃ kappaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, jānaṃ tathāvādinā ariṭṭhaina bhikkhunā bhikkhunā gaddhabādhipubbanai (?) akaṭānudhammaina taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhiṃ sambhuñ‍jissathāpi saṃvasissathāpi sahāpi saiyyaṃ kappaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –
