424. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu jānaṃ tathāvādinā bhikkhunā akaṭānudhammaina taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhiṃ sambhuñ‍jaiyya vā saṃvasaiyya vā saha vā saiyyaṃ  kappaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

425. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

jānāti nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti, añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti, sau vā āraucaiti|

tathāvādināti – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti aivaṃ vādinā|

akaṭānudhammau nāma ukkhittau anausāritau|

taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhinti aitaṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhiṃ|

sambhuñ‍jaiyya vāti sambhaugau nāma dvai sambhaugā – āmisasambhaugau ca dhammasambhaugau ca| āmisasambhaugau nāma āmisaṃ daiti vā paṭiggaṇhāti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammasambhaugau nāma uddisati vā uddisāpaiti vā, padaina uddisati vā uddisāpaiti vā, padai padai āpatti pācittiyassa| akkharāya uddisati vā uddisāpaiti vā, akkharakkharāya āpatti pācittiyassa|

saṃvasaiyya  vāti ukkhittakaina saddhiṃ upausathaṃ vā pavāraṇaṃ vā saṅghakammaṃ vā karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

saha  vā saiyyaṃ kappaiyyāti aikacchan‍nai ukkhittakai nipan‍nai bhikkhu nipaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| bhikkhu nipan‍nai ukkhittakau nipaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| ubhau vā nipaj‍janti, āpatti pācittiyassa| uṭṭhahitvā punappunaṃ nipaj‍janti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

426. ukkhittakai  ukkhittakasañ‍ñī sambhuñ‍jati vā saṃvasati vā saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| ukkhittakai vaimatikau sambhuñ‍jati vā saṃvasati vā saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ukkhittakai anukkhittakasañ‍ñī sambhuñ‍jati vā saṃvasati vā saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiti, anāpatti| anukkhittakai ukkhittakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anukkhittakai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anukkhittakai anukkhittakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

427. anāpatti anukkhittauti jānāti, ukkhittau ausāritauti jānāti, ukkhittau taṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭinissaṭṭhauti jānāti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

ukkhittasambhaugasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. kaṇṭakasikkhāpadaṃ

428. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina kaṇṭakassa kaṇḍakassa (syā॰ ka॰) nāma samaṇuddaisassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti| assausuṃ khau sambahulā bhikkhū kaṇṭakassa nāma kira samaṇuddaisassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina kaṇṭakau samaṇuddaisau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā kaṇṭakaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, āvusau kaṇṭaka, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yai’mai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃbyākhau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti|

mā, āvusau kaṇṭaka, aivaṃ avaca| mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi| na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ| na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| anaikapariyāyaina, āvusau kaṇṭaka, antarāyikā  dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā| alañ‍ca  pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya| appassādā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau…pai॰… aivampi khau kaṇṭakau samaṇuddaisau taihi bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva taṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘‘aivaṃbyākhau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti|

yatau ca khau tai bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu kaṇṭakaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaituṃ, atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā kaṇṭakaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, kaṇṭaka, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā  dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃbyākhau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti|

‘‘kassa nu khau nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mayā aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsi? nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā, alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya? appassādā kāmā vuttā mayā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā…pai॰… sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| atha ca pana tvaṃ, maughapurisa, attanā duggahitaina amhai caiva abbhācikkhasi attānañ‍ca khaṇasi bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavasi| tañhi tai, maughapurisa, bhavissati dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰…  pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti| vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau kaṇṭakaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ nāsaitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nāsaitabbau – aj‍jataggai tai, āvusau kaṇṭaka, na  caiva sau bhagavā satthā apadisitabbau| yampi cañ‍ñai samaṇuddaisā labhanti bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ dirattatirattaṃ sahasaiyyaṃ sāpi  tai natthi| cara pirai vinassā’’ti| atha khau saṅghau kaṇṭakaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ nāsaisi|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ tathānāsitaṃ kaṇṭakaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ upalāpaintipi upaṭṭhāpaintipi sambhuñ‍jantipi sahāpi saiyyaṃ kappainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ tathānāsitaṃ kaṇṭakaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ upalāpaissantipi upaṭṭhāpaissantipi sambhuñ‍jissantipi sahāpi saiyyaṃ kappaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ tathānāsitaṃ kaṇṭakaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ upalāpaithāpi upaṭṭhāpaithāpi sambhuñ‍jathāpi sahāpi saiyyaṃ kappaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, jānaṃ tathānāsitaṃ kaṇṭakaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ upalāpaissathāpi upaṭṭhāpaissathāpi sambhuñ‍jissathāpi sahāpi saiyyaṃ kappaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

429. ‘‘samaṇuddaisaupi cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti, sau samaṇuddaisau bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘māvusau samaṇuddaisa, aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| anaikapariyāyaināvusau samaṇuddaisa, antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā| alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāyā’ti| aivañ‍ca aivañ‍ca pana (ka॰) sau samaṇuddaisau bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sau samaṇuddaisau bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘aj‍jataggai tai, āvusau samaṇuddaisa, na caiva sau bhagavā satthā apadisitabbau| yampi cañ‍ñai samaṇuddaisā labhanti bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ dirattatirattaṃ sahasaiyyaṃ sāpi tai natthi| cara pirai vinassā’ti| yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ tathānāsitaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ upalāpaiyya vā upaṭṭhāpaiyya vā sambhuñ‍jaiyya vā saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

430. samaṇuddaisau nāma sāmaṇairau vuc‍cati|

aivaṃ  vadaiyyāti – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti|

sau  samaṇuddaisauti  yau sau aivaṃvādī samaṇuddaisau|

bhikkhūhīti  añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi, yai passanti yai suṇanti taihi vattabbau – ‘‘mā, āvusau samaṇuddaisa, aivaṃ avaca| mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi| na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ| na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| anaikapariyāyaināvusau samaṇuddaisa, antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā| alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāyā’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbau… tatiyampi vattabbau…pai॰… sacai paṭinissaj‍jati ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati sau samaṇuddaisau bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘aj‍jataggai tai, āvusau samaṇuddaisa, na caiva sau bhagavā satthā apadisitabbau| yampi cañ‍ñai samaṇuddaisā labhanti bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ dirattatirattaṃ sahasaiyyaṃ sāpi tai natthi| cara pirai vinassā’’ti|

yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

jānāti nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti, añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti, sau vā āraucaiti|

tathānāsitanti aivaṃ nāsitaṃ|

samaṇuddaisau nāma sāmaṇairau vuc‍cati|

upalāpaiyya vāti tassa pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā uddaisaṃ vā paripucchaṃ vā dassāmīti upalāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

upaṭṭhāpaiyya vāti tassa cuṇṇaṃ vā mattikaṃ vā dantakaṭṭhaṃ vā mukhaudakaṃ vā sādiyati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

sambhuñ‍jaiyya vāti  sambhaugau nāma dvai sambhaugā – āmisasambhaugau ca dhammasambhaugau ca| āmisasambhaugau nāma āmisaṃ daiti vā paṭiggaṇhāti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammasambhaugau nāma uddisati vā uddisāpaiti vā, padaina uddisati vā uddisāpaiti vā, padai padai āpatti  pācittiyassa| akkharāya uddisati vā uddisāpaiti vā, akkharakkharāya āpatti pācittiyassa|

saha  vā saiyyaṃ kappaiyyāti aikacchan‍nai nāsitakai samaṇuddaisai nipan‍nai bhikkhu nipaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| bhikkhu nipan‍nai nāsitakau samaṇuddaisau nipaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| ubhau vā nipaj‍janti, āpatti pācittiyassa| uṭṭhahitvā punappunaṃ nipaj‍janti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

431. nāsitakai nāsitakasañ‍ñī upalāpaiti vā upaṭṭhāpaiti vā sambhuñ‍jati vā saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| nāsitakai vaimatikau upalāpaiti vā upaṭṭhāpaiti vā sambhuñ‍jati vā saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nāsitakai anāsitakasañ‍ñī upalāpaiti vā upaṭṭhāpaiti vā sambhuñ‍jati vā saha vā saiyyaṃ kappaiti, anāpatti| anāsitakai nāsitakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anāsitakai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anāsitakai anāsitakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

432. anāpatti  anāsitakauti jānāti, taṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭinissaṭṭhauti jānāti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

kaṇṭakasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

sappāṇakavaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sañ‍cic‍cavadhasappāṇaṃ, uk‍kauṭaṃ duṭṭhul‍lachādanaṃ|

ūnavīsati satthañ‍ca, saṃvidhānaṃ ariṭṭhakaṃ|

ukkhittaṃ kaṇṭakañ‍caiva, dasa sikkhāpadā imaiti||

8. sahadhammikavaggau

1. sahadhammikasikkhāpadaṃ

433. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā chan‍nau anācāraṃ  ācarati| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘māvusau chan‍na, aivarūpaṃ akāsi| naitaṃ kappatī’’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchāmī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā chan‍nau bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau aivaṃ vakkhati – na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchāmī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, chan‍na, bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchāmī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau aivaṃ vakkhasi – ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchāmī’’ti| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

434. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau aivaṃ vadaiyya  – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchāmī’ti, pācittiyaṃ| sikkhamānaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā añ‍ñātabbaṃ paripucchitabbaṃ paripañhitabbaṃ| ayaṃ tattha sāmīcī’’ti|

435. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhūhīti añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi|

sahadhammikaṃ  nāma yaṃ bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ aitaṃ sahadhammikaṃ nāma| taina vuc‍camānau aivaṃ vadaiti vadaiyya (ka॰) – ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchāmī’’ti imāni padāni syā॰ pautthakai na dissanti| paṇḍitaṃ byattaṃ imāni padāni syā॰ pautthakai na dissanti maidhāviṃ bahussutaṃ dhammakathikaṃ paripucchāmīti bhaṇati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

436. upasampan‍nai  upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī aivaṃ vadaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau aivaṃ vadaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī  aivaṃ vadaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

apañ‍ñattaina vuc‍camānau – ‘‘idaṃ na sal‍laikhāya na dhutatthāya na pāsādikatāya na apacayāya na vīriyārambhāya saṃvattatī’’ti aivaṃ vadaiti, ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paṇḍitaṃ maidhāviṃ bahussutaṃ dhammakathikaṃ paripucchāmī’’ti bhaṇati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anupasampan‍naina pañ‍ñattaina vā apañ‍ñattaina vā vuc‍camānau  – ‘‘idaṃ na sal‍laikhāya na dhutatthāya na pāsādikatāya na apacayāya na vīriyārambhāya saṃvattatī’’ti aivaṃ vadaiti, ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paṇḍitaṃ maidhāviṃ bahussutaṃ dhammakathikaṃ paripucchāmī’’ti bhaṇati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

sikkhamānaināti sikkhitukāmaina|

añ‍ñātabbanti jānitabbaṃ|

paripucchitabbanti ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ; imassa vā k‍vatthau’’ti?

paripañhitabbanti cintaitabbaṃ tulayitabbaṃ|

ayaṃ tattha sāmīcīti ayaṃ tattha anudhammatā|

437. anāpatti  ‘‘jānissāmi sikkhissāmī’’ti bhaṇati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sahadhammikasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. vilaikhanasikkhāpadaṃ

438. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| idaṃ vatthu cūḷava॰ 320 taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vinayakathaṃ kathaiti, vinayassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, vinayapariyattiyā  vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ādissa ādissa āyasmatau upālissa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi bhikkhū bhagavā (syā॰ ka॰) – ‘‘bhagavā khau anaikapariyāyaina vinayakathaṃ kathaiti, vinayassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, vinayapariyattiyā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ādissa ādissa āyasmatau upālissa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| handa mayaṃ, āvusau, āyasmatau upālissa santikai vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇāmā’’ti, tai ca bahū bhikkhū thairā ca navā ca majjhimā ca āyasmatau upālissa santikai vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇanti|

atha khau chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi  – ‘‘aitarahi khau, āvusau, bahū bhikkhū thairā ca navā ca majjhimā ca āyasmatau upālissa santikai vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇanti| sacai imai vinayai pakatañ‍ñunau bhavissanti amhai yainicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvadicchakaṃ ākaḍḍhissanti parikaḍḍhissanti| handa mayaṃ, āvusau, vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaimā’’ti| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘kiṃ panimaihi khuddānukhuddakaihi sikkhāpadaihi uddiṭṭhaihi, yāvadaiva kuk‍kuc‍cāya vihaisāya vilaikhāya saṃvattantī’’ti! yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – kathañhi  nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaissantīti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

439. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kiṃ panimaihi khuddānukhuddakaihi  sikkhāpadaihi uddiṭṭhaihi, yāvadaiva kuk‍kuc‍cāya vihaisāya vilaikhāya saṃvattantī’ti, sikkhāpadavivaṇṇakai pācittiya’’nti|

440. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

pātimaukkhai uddissamānaiti uddisantai vā uddisāpaintai vā sajjhāyaṃ vā karauntai|

aivaṃ  vadaiyyāti – ‘‘kiṃ panimaihi khuddānukhuddakaihi sikkhāpadaihi uddiṭṭhaihi, yāvadaiva kuk‍kuc‍cāya vihaisāya vilaikhāya saṃvattantīti| ‘‘yai imaṃ pariyāpuṇanti taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ hauti, vihaisā hauti, vilaikhā hauti, yai imaṃ na pariyāpuṇanti taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ na hauti vihaisā na hauti vilaikhā na hauti| anuddiṭṭhaṃ idaṃ varaṃ, anuggahitaṃ idaṃ varaṃ, apariyāpuṭaṃ idaṃ varaṃ, adhāritaṃ idaṃ varaṃ, vinayau vā antaradhāyatu, imai vā bhikkhū apakatañ‍ñunau hauntū’’ti upasampan‍nassa vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

441. upasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñaṃ dhammaṃ vivaṇṇaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nassa vinayaṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā dhammaṃ vivaṇṇaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai  vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

442. anāpatti na vivaṇṇaitukāmau, ‘‘iṅgha tvaṃ suttantai vā gāthāyau vā abhidhammaṃ vā pariyāpuṇassu, pacchā vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇissasī’’ti bhaṇati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

vilaikhanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. mauhanasikkhāpadaṃ

443. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū anācāraṃ ācaritvā ‘‘añ‍ñāṇakaina āpan‍nāti jānantū’’ti pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘idānaiva khau mayaṃ jānāma, ayampi kira dhammau suttāgatau suttapariyāpan‍nau anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchatī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vakkhanti – idānaiva khau mayaṃ jānāma, ayampi kira dhammau suttāgatau suttapariyāpan‍nau anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vadaitha – ‘‘idānaiva khau mayaṃ jānāma, ayampi kira dhammau suttāgatau suttapariyāpan‍nau anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchatī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vakkhatha – ‘‘idānaiva khau mayaṃ jānāma, ayampi kira dhammau suttāgatau suttapariyāpan‍nau anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchatī’’ti! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

444. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu anvaddhamāsaṃ  pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘idānaiva khau ahaṃ jānāmi, ayampi kira dhammau suttāgatau suttapariyāpan‍nau anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchatī’ti| tañ‍cai bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñai bhikkhū jānaiyyuṃ nisin‍napubbaṃ iminā bhikkhunā dvattikkhattuṃ pātimaukkhai uddissamānai, kau pana vādau bhiyyau bhiyyauti (syā॰), na ca tassa bhikkhunau añ‍ñāṇakaina mutti atthi, yañ‍ca tattha āpattiṃ āpan‍nau tañ‍ca yathādhammau kāraitabbau, uttari cassa mauhau āraupaitabbau – ‘tassa tai, āvusau, alābhā, tassa tai dul‍laddhaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ pātimaukkhai uddissamānai na sādhukaṃ aṭṭhiṃ katvā aṭṭhikatvā (syā॰ ka॰) manasi karausī’ti| idaṃ tasmiṃ mauhanakai pācittiya’’nti|

445. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

anvaddhamāsanti anupausathikaṃ|

pātimaukkhai uddissamānaiti uddisantai|

aivaṃ  vadaiyyāti anācāraṃ ācaritvā – ‘‘añ‍ñāṇakaina āpan‍nauti jānantū’’ti pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘idānaiva khau ahaṃ jānāmi, ayampi kira dhammau suttāgatau suttapariyāpan‍nau anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchatī’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

tañ‍cai  mauhaitukāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñai bhikkhū jānaiyyuṃ nisin‍napubbaṃ iminā bhikkhunā dvattikkhattuṃ pātimaukkhai uddissamānai, kau  pana vādau bhiyyau, na ca tassa bhikkhunau añ‍ñāṇakaina mutti atthi, yañ‍ca tattha āpattiṃ āpan‍nau, tañ‍ca yathādhammau kāraitabbau, uttari cassa mauhau āraupaitabbau| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, āraupaitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

446. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu pātimaukkhai uddissamānai na sādhukaṃ aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi karauti| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau mauhaṃ āraupaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu pātimaukkhai uddissamānai na sādhukaṃ aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi karauti| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau mauhaṃ āraupaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau mauhassa āraupanā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘āraupitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau mauhau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

anāraupitai mauhai mauhaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āraupitai mauhai mauhaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

447. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī mauhaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

448. anāpatti  na vitthāraina sutaṃ hauti, ūnakadvattikkhattuṃ vitthāraina sutaṃ hauti, na mauhaitukāmassa, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

mauhanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. pahārasikkhāpadaṃ

449. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina  chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā sattarasavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pahāraṃ dainti| tai raudanti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tumhai, āvusau, raudathā’’ti? ‘‘imai, āvusau, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā amhākaṃ pahāraṃ daintī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā bhikkhūnaṃ pahāraṃ dassantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, kupitā anattamanā bhikkhūnaṃ pahāraṃ daithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, kupitā anattamanā bhikkhūnaṃ pahāraṃ dassatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

450. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa kupitau anattamanau pahāraṃ dadaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

451. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhussāti añ‍ñassa bhikkhussa|

kupitau anattamanauti anabhiraddhau āhatacittau khilajātau|

pahāraṃ dadaiyyāti kāyaina vā kāyapaṭibaddhaina vā nissaggiyaina vā antamasau uppalapattainapi  pahāraṃ daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

452. upasampan‍nai  upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī kupitau anattamanau pahāraṃ daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau kupitau anattamanau pahāraṃ daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī kupitau anattamanau pahāraṃ daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍nassa kupitau anattamanau pahāraṃ daiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

453. anāpatti kainaci vihaiṭhīyamānau maukkhādhippāyau pahāraṃ daiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

pahārasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. talasattikasikkhāpadaṃ

454. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā sattarasavaggiyānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ talasattikaṃ uggiranti| tai pahārasamuc‍citā raudanti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tumhai, āvusau, raudathā’’ti? ‘‘imai, āvusau, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā amhākaṃ talasattikaṃ uggirantī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā sattarasavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ talasattikaṃ uggirissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, kupitā anattamanā sattarasavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ talasattikaṃ uggirathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, kupitā anattamanā sattarasavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ talasattikaṃ uggirissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

455. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa kupitau anattamanau talasattikaṃ uggiraiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

456. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhussāti añ‍ñassa bhikkhussa|

kupitau anattamanauti anabhiraddhau āhatacittau khilajātau|

talasattikaṃ  uggiraiyyāti  kāyaṃ vā kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ vā antamasau uppalapattampi uc‍cāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

457. upasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī kupitau anattamanau talasattikaṃ uggirati, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau kupitau anattamanau talasattikaṃ uggirati, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī kupitau anattamanau talasattikaṃ uggirati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍nassa kupitau anattamanau talasattikaṃ uggirati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

458. anāpatti kainaci vihaiṭhīyamānau maukkhādhippāyau talasattikaṃ uggirati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

talasattikasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. amūlakasikkhāpadaṃ

459. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi  buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

460. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

461. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunti añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ|

amūlakaṃ nāma adiṭṭhaṃ assutaṃ aparisaṅkitaṃ|

saṅghādisaisaināti tairasan‍naṃ añ‍ñataraina|

anuddhaṃsaiyyāti caudaiti vā caudāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

462. upasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

ācāravipattiyā vā diṭṭhivipattiyā vā anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍naṃ anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

463. anāpatti tathāsañ‍ñī caudaiti, vā caudāpaiti vā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

amūlakasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sañ‍cic‍casikkhāpadaṃ

464. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sattarasavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahanti – ‘‘bhagavatā, āvusau, sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – ‘na ūnavīsativassau puggalau upasampādaitabbau’ti| tumhai ca ūnavīsativassā upasampan‍nā| kac‍ci nau tumhai anupasampan‍nā’’ti? tai raudanti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tumhai, āvusau, raudathā’’ti? ‘‘imai, āvusau, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū amhākaṃ sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahantī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhūnaṃ sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

465. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahaiyya uppādaiyya (itipi) – ‘itissa muhuttampi aphāsu bhavissatī’ti aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiya’’nti|

466. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ  atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhussāti añ‍ñassa bhikkhussa|

sañ‍cic‍cāti jānantau sañ‍jānantau caic‍ca abhivitaritvā vītik‍kamau|

kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahaiyyāti ‘‘ūnavīsativassau mañ‍ñai tvaṃ upasampan‍nau, vikālai mañ‍ñai tayā bhuttaṃ, maj‍jaṃ mañ‍ñai tayā pītaṃ, mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau mañ‍ñai tayā nisin‍na’’nti kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aitadaiva  pac‍cayaṃ karitvā, anañ‍ñanti na añ‍ñau kauci pac‍cayau hauti kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahituṃ|

467. upasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahati, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahati, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍nassa sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

468. anāpatti  na kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahitukāmau ‘‘ūnavīsativassau mañ‍ñai tvaṃ upasampan‍nau, vikālai mañ‍ñai tayā bhuttaṃ, maj‍jaṃ mañ‍ñai tayā pītaṃ, mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau mañ‍ñai tayā nisin‍naṃ, iṅgha jānāhi, mā tai pacchā kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausī’’ti bhaṇati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sañ‍cic‍casikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. upassutisikkhāpadaṃ

469. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū paisalaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍanti| paisalā bhikkhū aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘alaj‍jinau imai, āvusau, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū| na sak‍kā imaihi saha bhaṇḍitu’’nti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘kissa tumhai, āvusau, amhai alaj‍jivādaina pāpaithā’’ti? ‘‘kahaṃ pana tumhai, āvusau, assutthā’’ti? ‘‘mayaṃ āyasmantānaṃ upassutiṃ upassuti (?) tiṭṭhamhā’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ upassutiṃ upassuti (?) tiṭṭhissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ upassutiṃ upassuti (?) tiṭṭhathāti? ‘‘sac‍ca, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ  kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ upassutiṃ upassuti (?) tiṭṭhissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

470. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ upassutiṃ tiṭṭhaiyya – ‘yaṃ imai bhaṇissanti taṃ saussāmī’ti aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñaṃ, pācittiya’’nti|

471. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhūnanti  añ‍ñaisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ|

bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānanti adhikaraṇajātānaṃ|

upassutiṃ tiṭṭhaiyyāti ‘‘imaisaṃ sutvā caudaissāmi sāraissāmi paṭicaudaissāmi paṭisāraissāmi maṅkū karissāmī’’ti gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yattha ṭhitau suṇāti, āpatti pācittiyassa| pacchatau gacchantau turitau gacchati saussāmīti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yattha ṭhitau suṇāti, āpatti pācittiyassa| puratau gacchantau auhiyyati saussāmīti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yattha ṭhitau suṇāti, āpatti pācittiyassa| bhikkhussa ṭhitaukāsaṃ vā nisin‍naukāsaṃ vā nipan‍naukāsaṃ vā āgantvā mantaintaṃ uk‍kāsitabbaṃ, vijānāpaitabbaṃ, nau cai uk‍kāsaiyya vā vijānāpaiyya vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aitadaiva pac‍cayaṃ karitvā anañ‍ñanti na añ‍ñau kauci pac‍cayau hauti upassutiṃ tiṭṭhituṃ|

472. upasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī upassutiṃ tiṭṭhati, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau upassutiṃ tiṭṭhati, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī upassutiṃ tiṭṭhati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍nassa upassutiṃ tiṭṭhati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai  anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

473. anāpatti – ‘‘imaisaṃ sutvā auramissāmi viramissāmi vūpasamissāmi vūpasamaissāmi (sī॰) attānaṃ parimaucaissāmī’’ti gacchati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

upassutisikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kammapaṭibāhanasikkhāpadaṃ

474. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū anācāraṃ ācaritvā aikamaikassa kammai kayiramānai paṭik‍kausanti| taina khau pana samayaina saṅghau san‍nipatitau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū cīvarakammaṃ karauntā aikassa chandaṃ adaṃsu| atha khau saṅghau – ‘‘ayaṃ, āvusau, chabbaggiyau bhikkhu aikakau āgatau, handassa mayaṃ kammaṃ karaumā’’ti tassa kammaṃ akāsi| atha khau sau bhikkhu yaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kiṃ, āvusau, saṅghau akāsī’’ti? ‘‘saṅghau mai, āvusau, kammaṃ akāsī’’ti| ‘‘na mayaṃ, āvusau, aitadatthāya chandaṃ adamhā – ‘‘tuyhaṃ kammaṃ karissatī’’ti| sacai ca mayaṃ jānaiyyāma ‘‘tuyhaṃ kammaṃ karissatī’’ti, na mayaṃ chandaṃ dadaiyyāmā’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ khiyyadhammaṃ (itipi) āpaj‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ  chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ khiyyadhammaṃ (itipi) āpaj‍jathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ  khiyyadhammaṃ (itipi) āpaj‍jissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

475. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

476. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

dhammikaṃ nāma kammaṃ apalaukanakammaṃ ñattikammaṃ ñattidutiyakammaṃ ñatticatutthakammaṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina kataṃ, aitaṃ dhammikaṃ nāma kammaṃ| chandaṃ datvā khiyyati āpatti pācittiyassa|

477. dhammakammai  dhammakammasañ‍ñī chandaṃ datvā khiyyati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikau chandaṃ datvā khiyyati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī chandaṃ datvā khiyyati, anāpatti| adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

478. anāpatti – ‘‘adhammaina vā vaggaina vā na kammārahassa vā kammaṃ kata’’nti jānantau khiyyati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

kammapaṭibāhanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. chandaṃadatvāgamanasikkhāpadaṃ

479. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina saṅghau san‍nipatitau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū cīvarakammaṃ karauntā aikassa chandaṃ adaṃsu| atha khau saṅghau ‘‘yassatthāya san‍nipatitau taṃ kammaṃ karissāmī’’ti ñattiṃ ṭhapaisi| atha khau sau bhikkhu – ‘‘aivamaivimai aikamaikassa kammaṃ karaunti, kassa tumhai kammaṃ karissathā’’ti chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu saṅghai vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya  chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, saṅghai vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamasīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, saṅghai vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

480. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu saṅghai vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

481. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

saṅghai vinicchayakathā nāma vatthu vā āraucitaṃ hauti avinicchitaṃ, ñatti vā ṭhapitā hauti, kammavācā vā vippakatā hauti|

chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamaiyyāti – ‘‘kathaṃ idaṃ kammaṃ kuppaṃ assa vaggaṃ assa na karaiyyā’’ti gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| parisāya hatthapāsaṃ vijahantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| vijahitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

482. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikau chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamati, anāpatti| adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

483. anāpatti – ‘‘saṅghassa bhaṇḍanaṃ vā kalahau vā viggahau vā vivādau vā bhavissatī’’ti gacchati, ‘‘saṅghabhaidau vā saṅgharāji vā bhavissatī’’ti gacchati, ‘‘adhammaina vā vaggaina vā na kammārahassa vā kammaṃ karissatī’’ti gacchati, gilānau gacchati, gilānassa karaṇīyaina gacchati, uc‍cāraina vā passāvaina vā pīḷitau gacchati, ‘‘na kammaṃ  kaupaitukāmau puna pac‍cāgamissāmī’’ti gacchati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

chandaṃ adatvā gamanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

11. dubbalasikkhāpadaṃ

484. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau saṅghassa saināsanañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaiti bhattāni ca uddisati| sau cāyasmā dubbalacīvarau hauti| taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa aikaṃ cīvaraṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| atha khau saṅghau taṃ cīvaraṃ āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa adāsi| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘yathāsanthutaṃ bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇāmaintī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū samaggaina saṅghaina cīvaraṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, samaggaina saṅghaina cīvaraṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, samaggaina saṅghaina cīvaraṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ, vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

485. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu samaggaina saṅghaina cīvaraṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya ‘yathāsanthutaṃ bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇāmaintī’ti, pācittiya’’nti|

486. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

samaggau nāma saṅghau samānasaṃvāsakau samānasīmāyaṃ ṭhitau|

cīvaraṃ nāma chan‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ vikappanupagaṃ pacchimaṃ|

datvāti sayaṃ datvā|

yathāsanthutaṃ nāma yathāmittatā yathāsandiṭṭhatā yathāsambhattatā yathāsamānupajjhāyakatā yathāsamānācariyakatā|

saṅghikaṃ  nāma saṅghassa din‍naṃ hauti paric‍cattaṃ|

lābhau nāma cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā, antamasau cuṇṇapiṇḍaupi, dantakaṭṭhampi, dasikasuttampi|

pacchā  khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti upasampan‍nassa saṅghaina sammatassa saināsanapañ‍ñāpakassa vā bhattuddaisakassa vā yāgubhājakassa vā phalabhājakassa vā khaj‍jabhājakassa vā appamattakavissaj‍jakassa vā cīvaraṃ din‍nai khiyyati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

487. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī cīvaraṃ din‍nai khiyyati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikau cīvaraṃ din‍nai khiyyati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī cīvaraṃ din‍nai khiyyati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñaṃ  parikkhāraṃ din‍nai khiyyati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| upasampan‍nassa saṅghaina asammatassa saināsanapañ‍ñāpakassa vā bhattuddaisakassa vā yāgubhājakassa vā phalabhājakassa vā khaj‍jabhājakassa vā appamattakavissaj‍jakassa vā cīvaraṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā parikkhāraṃ din‍nai khiyyati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nassa saṅghaina  sammatassa vā asammatassa vā saināsanapañ‍ñāpakassa vā bhattuddaisakassa vā yāgubhājakassa vā phalabhājakassa vā khaj‍jabhājakassa vā appamattakavissaj‍jakassa vā cīvaraṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā parikkhāraṃ din‍nai khiyyati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

488. anāpatti – pakatiyā chandā dausā mauhā bhayā karauntaṃ ‘‘k‍vatthau tassa din‍naina laddhāpi vinipātaissati na sammā upanaissatī’’ti khiyyati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dubbalasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aikādasamaṃ|

12. pariṇāmanasikkhāpadaṃ

489. idaṃ vatthu pārā॰ 657 taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñatarassa pūgassa saṅghassa sacīvarabhattaṃ paṭiyattaṃ hauti – ‘‘bhaujaitvā cīvaraina acchādaissāmā’’ti| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū yaina sau pūgau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ pūgaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘daithāvusau, imāni cīvarāni imaisaṃ bhikkhūna’’nti| ‘‘na mayaṃ, bhantai, dassāma| amhākaṃ saṅghassa anuvassaṃ sacīvarabhikkhā pañ‍ñattā’’ti| ‘‘bahū, āvusau, saṅghassa dāyakā, bahū saṅghassa bhattā bhaddā (ka॰)| imai tumhai nissāya tumhai sampassantā idha viharanti| tumhai cai imaisaṃ na dassatha, atha kau  carahi imaisaṃ dassati? daithāvusau, imāni cīvarāni imaisaṃ bhikkhūna’’nti| atha khau sau pūgau chabbaggiyaihi bhikkhūhi nippīḷiyamānau yathāpaṭiyattaṃ cīvaraṃ chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ datvā saṅghaṃ bhattaina parivisi| yai tai bhikkhū jānanti  saṅghassa sacīvarabhattaṃ paṭiyattaṃ ‘‘na ca jānanti chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ din‍na’’nti tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘auṇaujaithāvusau, saṅghassa cīvara’’nti| ‘‘natthi, bhantai| yathāpaṭiyattaṃ cīvaraṃ ayyā chabbaggiyā ayyānaṃ chabbaggiyānaṃ pariṇāmaisu’’nti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaissantī’’ti…pai॰…  sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

490. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

491. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

jānāti nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti, añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti, sau vā āraucaiti|

saṅghikaṃ  nāma saṅghassa din‍naṃ hauti paric‍cattaṃ|

lābhau nāma cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā, antamasau cuṇṇapiṇḍaupi, dantakaṭṭhampi, dasikasuttampi|

pariṇataṃ nāma ‘‘dassāma karissāmā’’ti vācā bhin‍nā hauti, taṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

492. pariṇatai pariṇatasañ‍ñī puggalassa pariṇāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| pariṇatai vaimatikau puggalassa pariṇāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pariṇatai apariṇatasañ‍ñī puggalassa pariṇāmaiti, anāpatti| saṅghassa pariṇataṃ añ‍ñasaṅghassa vā caitiyassa vā pariṇāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| caitiyassa pariṇataṃ añ‍ñacaitiyassa  vā saṅghassa vā puggalassa vā pariṇāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalassa pariṇataṃ añ‍ñapuggalassa vā saṅghassa vā caitiyassa vā pariṇāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apariṇatai pariṇatasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apariṇatai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apariṇatai apariṇatasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

493. anāpatti – ‘‘kattha daimā’’ti pucchīyamānau – ‘‘yattha tumhākaṃ daiyyadhammau paribhaugaṃ vā labhaiyya paṭisaṅkhāraṃ vā labhaiyya ciraṭṭhitikau vā assa yattha vā pana tumhākaṃ cittaṃ pasīdati tattha daithā’’ti bhaṇati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

pariṇāmanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dvādasamaṃ|

sahadhammikavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sahadhamma-vivaṇṇañ‍ca, mauhāpanaṃ pahārakaṃ|

talasatti amūlañ‍ca, sañ‍cic‍ca ca upassuti|

paṭibāhanachandañ‍ca, dabbañ‍ca pariṇāmananti||

9. ratanavaggau

1. antaipurasikkhāpadaṃ

494. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina rājā pasainadi kausalau uyyānapālaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai, uyyānaṃ saudhaihi| uyyānaṃ gamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau sau uyyānapālau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa paṭissutvā uyyānaṃ saudhaintau addasa bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ| disvāna yaina rājā pasainadi kausalau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘suddhaṃ, daiva, uyyānaṃ| apica, bhagavā tattha nisin‍nau’’ti| ‘‘hautu, bhaṇai! mayaṃ bhagavantaṃ payirupāsissāmā’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau uyyānaṃ gantvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau upāsakau bhagavantaṃ payirupāsantau nisin‍nau hauti| addasā khau rājā pasainadi kausalau taṃ upāsakaṃ bhagavantaṃ payirupāsantaṃ nisin‍naṃ| disvāna bhītau aṭṭhāsi| atha khau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa aitadahausi – ‘‘nārahatāyaṃ purisau pāpau hautuṃ, yathā bhagavantaṃ payirupāsatī’’ti| yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| atha khau sau upāsakau bhagavatau gāravaina rājānaṃ  pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ naiva abhivādaisi na pac‍cuṭṭhāsi| atha khau rājā pasainadi  kausalau anattamanau ahausi – ‘‘kathañhi nāmāyaṃ purisau mayi āgatai naiva abhivādaissati na pac‍cuṭṭhaissatī’’ti! atha khau bhagavā rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ anattamanaṃ viditvā rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aisau khau, mahārāja, upāsakau bahussutau āgatāgamau kāmaisu vītarāgau’’ti| atha khau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa aitadahausi – ‘‘nārahatāyaṃ upāsakau aurakau hautuṃ, bhagavāpi imassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsatī’’ti| taṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vadaiyyāsi, upāsaka, yaina atthau’’ti| ‘‘suṭṭhu, daivā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

495. taina  khau pana samayaina rājā pasainadi kausalau uparipāsādavaragatau hauti| addasā khau rājā pasainadi kausalau taṃ upāsakaṃ rathikāya rathiyāya (itipi) chattapāṇiṃ gacchantaṃ| disvāna pak‍kausāpaitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘tvaṃ kira, upāsaka, bahussutau āgatāgamau| sādhu, upāsaka, amhākaṃ itthāgāraṃ dhammaṃ vācaihī’’ti| ‘‘yamahaṃ yampāhaṃ (sī॰), daiva, jānāmi ayyānaṃ vāhasā, ayyāva daivassa itthāgāraṃ dhammaṃ vācaissantī’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau – ‘‘sac‍caṃ khau upāsakau āhā’’ti yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaitu yau amhākaṃ itthāgāraṃ dhammaṃ vācaissatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi…pai॰… padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘tainahānanda, rañ‍ñau itthāgāraṃ dhammaṃ vācaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau  paṭissutvā kālaina kālaṃ pavisitvā rañ‍ñau itthāgāraṃ dhammaṃ vācaiti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami|

496. taina khau pana samayaina rājā pasainadi kausalau mal‍likāya daiviyā saddhiṃ sayanagatau hauti| addasā khau mal‍likā daivī āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna sahasā vuṭṭhāsi; pītakamaṭṭhaṃ  dussaṃ pabhassittha| atha khau āyasmā ānandau tatauva paṭinivattitvā ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā ānandau pubbai appaṭisaṃviditau rañ‍ñau antaipuraṃ pavisissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ  kira tvaṃ, ānanda, pubbai appaṭisaṃviditau rañ‍ñau antaipuraṃ pavisasīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, ānanda, pubbai appaṭisaṃviditau rañ‍ñau antaipuraṃ pavisissasi! naitaṃ, ānanda, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

497. a॰ ni॰ 10.45 ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā rājantaipurappavaisanai| katamai dasa? idha, bhikkhavai, rājā mahaisiyā saddhiṃ nisin‍nau hauti, tattha bhikkhu pavisati| mahaisī vā bhikkhuṃ disvā sitaṃ pātukarauti| bhikkhu vā mahaisiṃ disvā sitaṃ pātukarauti| tattha rañ‍ñau aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘addhā imaisaṃ kataṃ vā karissanti vā’’ti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā bahukic‍cau bahukaraṇīyau| añ‍ñataraṃ itthiṃ gantvā nassarati| sā taina gabbhaṃ gaṇhi| tattha rañ‍ñau aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘na khau idha añ‍ñau kauci pavisati añ‍ñatra pabbajitaina| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau antaipurai añ‍ñataraṃ ratanaṃ nassati| tattha rañ‍ñau aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘na khau idha añ‍ñau kauci pavisati añ‍ñatra pabbajitaina| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau antaipurai abbhantarā guyhamantā bahiddhā sambhaidaṃ gacchanti| tattha rañ‍ñau aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘na khau idha añ‍ñau kauci pavisati añ‍ñatra pabbajitaina| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau antaipurai puttau vā pitaraṃ patthaiti pitā vā puttaṃ patthaiti| taisaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘na khau idha añ‍ñau kauci pavisati añ‍ñatra pabbajitaina| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā nīcaṭṭhāniyaṃ uc‍cai ṭhānai ṭhapaiti| yaisaṃ taṃ amanāpaṃ taisaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘rājā khau pabbajitaina saṃsaṭṭhau| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaṭṭhau ādīnavau, rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā uc‍caṭṭhāniyaṃ nīcai ṭhānai ṭhapaiti| yaisaṃ taṃ amanāpaṃ taisaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘rājā khau pabbajitaina saṃsaṭṭhau| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā akālai sainaṃ uyyaujaiti| yaisaṃ taṃ amanāpaṃ taisaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘rājā khau pabbajitaina saṃsaṭṭhau| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhamau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā kālai sainaṃ uyyaujaitvā antarāmaggatau nivattāpaiti| yaisaṃ taṃ amanāpaṃ taisaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘‘rājā khau pabbajitaina saṃsaṭṭhau| siyā nu khau pabbajitassa kamma’’nti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, navamau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau rājantaipuraṃ hatthisammaddaṃ assasammaddaṃ rathasammaddaṃ raj‍janīyāni raj‍janīyāni (ka॰) rūpasaddagandharasaphauṭṭhabbāni, yāni na pabbajitassa sāruppāni| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dasamau ādīnavau rājantaipurappavaisanai| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa ādīnavā rājantaipurappavaisanai’’ti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ  anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

498. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa muddhābhisittassa (syā॰) anikkhantarājakai aniggataratanakai pubbai appaṭisaṃviditau indakhīlaṃ atik‍kāmaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

499. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

khattiyau nāma ubhatau sujātau hauti, mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupakuṭṭhau jātivādaina|

muddhāvasittau nāma  khattiyābhisaikaina abhisittau hauti|

anikkhantarājakaiti rājā sayanigharā anikkhantau hauti|

aniggataratanakaiti mahaisī sayanigharā anikkhantā hauti, ubhau vā anikkhantā haunti|

pubbai appaṭisaṃviditauti pubbai anāmantaitvā|

indakhīlau nāma sayanigharassa ummārau vuc‍cati|

sayanigharaṃ  nāma yattha katthaci rañ‍ñau sayanaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti, antamasau sāṇipākāraparikkhittampi|

indakhīlaṃ atik‍kāmaiyyāti paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ ummāraṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

500. appaṭisaṃviditai appaṭisaṃviditasañ‍ñī indakhīlaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| appaṭisaṃviditai vaimatikau indakhīlaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| appaṭisaṃviditai paṭisaṃviditasañ‍ñī indakhīlaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

paṭisaṃviditai appaṭisaṃviditasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paṭisaṃviditai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paṭisaṃviditai paṭisaṃviditasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

501. anāpatti  paṭisaṃviditai, na khattiyau hauti, na khattiyābhisaikaina abhisittau hauti, rājā sayanigharā nikkhantau hauti, mahaisī sayanigharā nikkhantā hauti, ubhau vā nikkhantā haunti, na sayanigharai, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

antaipurasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. ratanasikkhāpadaṃ

502. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu aciravatiyā nadiyā nahāyati| añ‍ñataraupi brāhmaṇau pañ‍casatānaṃ thavikaṃ thalai nikkhipitvā aciravatiyā nadiyā nahāyantau vissaritvā agamāsi| atha khau sau bhikkhu – ‘‘tassāyaṃ brāhmaṇassa thavikā, mā idha nassī’’ti aggahaisi| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau saritvā turitau ādhāvitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘api mai, bhau, thavikaṃ passaiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘handa, brāhmaṇā’’ti adāsi| atha khau tassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau ahaṃ upāyaina imassa bhikkhunau puṇṇapattaṃ na dadaiyya’’nti! ‘‘na mai, bhau, pañ‍casatāni, sahassaṃ mai’’ti palibundhaitvā muñ‍ci| atha khau sau bhikkhu ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai  ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu ratanaṃ uggahaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, ratanaṃ uggahaisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, ratanaṃ uggahaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ  sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu ratanaṃ vā ratanasammataṃ vā uggaṇhaiyya vā uggaṇhāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

503. taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyā ussavau hauti| manussā alaṅkatappaṭiyattā uyyānaṃ gacchanti| visākhāpi migāramātā alaṅkatappaṭiyattā  ‘‘uyyānaṃ gamissāmī’’ti gāmatau  nikkhamitvā – ‘‘kyāhaṃ karissāmi uyyānaṃ gantvā, yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ payirupāsaiyya’’nti ābharaṇaṃ aumuñ‍citvā uttarāsaṅgaina bhaṇḍikaṃ bandhitvā dāsiyā adāsi – ‘‘handa, jai, imaṃ bhaṇḍikaṃ gaṇhāhī’’ti| atha khau visākhā migāramātā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau visākhā migāramātā bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sā dāsī taṃ bhaṇḍikaṃ vissaritvā agamāsi| bhikkhū passitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, uggahaitvā nikkhipathā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ratanaṃ vā ratanasammataṃ vā ajjhārāmai uggahaitvā vā uggahāpaitvā vā nikkhipituṃ – ‘‘yassa bhavissati  sau harissatī’’ti| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu ratanaṃ vā ratanasammataṃ vā, añ‍ñatra ajjhārāmā, uggaṇhaiyya vā uggaṇhāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

504. taina  khau pana samayaina kāsīsu janapadai anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa kammantagāmau hauti| taina ca gahapatinā antaivāsī āṇattau hauti – ‘‘sacai bhadantā āgacchanti bhattaṃ karaiyyāsī’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū kāsīsu janapadai cārikaṃ caramānā yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa kammantagāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| addasā khau sau purisau tai bhikkhū dūratauva āgacchantai| disvāna yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaintu, bhantai, ayyā svātanāya gahapatinau bhatta’’nti| adhivāsaisuṃ khau tai bhikkhū tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau sau purisau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā kālaṃ āraucāpaitvā aṅgulimuddikaṃ aumuñ‍citvā tai bhikkhū bhattaina parivisitvā – ‘‘ayyā bhuñ‍jitvā gacchantu, ahampi kammantaṃ gamissāmī’’ti aṅgulimuddikaṃ  vissaritvā agamāsi| bhikkhū passitvā  – ‘‘sacai mayaṃ gamissāma nassissatāyaṃ aṅgulimuddikā’’ti tatthaiva acchiṃsu| atha khau sau purisau kammantā āgacchantau tai bhikkhū passitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa, bhantai, ayyā idhaiva  acchantī’’ti? atha khau tai bhikkhū tassa purisassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaitvā sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ratanaṃ vā ratanasammataṃ vā ajjhārāmai vā ajjhāvasathai vā uggahaitvā vā uggahāpaitvā vā nikkhipituṃ – yassa bhavissati sau harissatī’’ti| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

505. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu ratanaṃ vā ratanasammataṃ vā, añ‍ñatra ajjhārāmā vā ajjhāvasathā vā, uggaṇhaiyya vā uggaṇhāpaiyya vā, pācittiyaṃ| ratanaṃ vā pana bhikkhunā ratanasammataṃ vā ajjhārāmai vā ajjhāvasathai vā uggahaitvā vā uggahāpaitvā vā nikkhipitabbaṃ – ‘yassa bhavissati sau harissatī’ti| ayaṃ tattha sāmīcī’’ti|

506. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

ratanaṃ nāma muttā maṇi vaiḷuriyau saṅkhau silā pavālaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ lauhitaṅkau lauhitakau (?) masāragal‍laṃ|

ratanasammataṃ  nāma yaṃ manussānaṃ upabhaugaparibhaugaṃ, aitaṃ ratanasammataṃ nāma|

añ‍ñatra ajjhārāmā vā ajjhāvasathā vāti ṭhapaitvā ajjhārāmaṃ ajjhāvasathaṃ|

ajjhārāmau nāma parikkhittassa ārāmassa antau ārāmau, aparikkhittassa upacārau|

ajjhāvasathau nāma parikkhittassa āvasathassa antau  āvasathau, aparikkhittassa upacārau|

uggaṇhaiyyāti  sayaṃ gaṇhāti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

uggaṇhāpaiyyāti añ‍ñaṃ gāhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

ratanaṃ vā pana bhikkhunā ratanasammataṃ vā ajjhārāmai vā ajjhāvasathai vā uggahaitvā vā uggahāpaitvā vā nikkhipitabbanti rūpaina vā nimittaina vā sañ‍ñāṇaṃ katvā nikkhipitvā ācikkhitabbaṃ – ‘‘yassa bhaṇḍaṃ naṭṭhaṃ sau āgacchatū’’ti| sacai tattha āgacchati sau vattabbau – ‘‘āvusau, kīdisaṃ tai bhaṇḍa’’nti? sacai rūpaina vā nimittaina vā sampādaiti dātabbaṃ, nau cai sampādaiti ‘‘vicināhi āvusau’’ti vattabbau| tamhā āvāsā pak‍kamantaina yai tattha haunti bhikkhū patirūpā, taisaṃ hatthai nikkhipitvā  pak‍kamitabbaṃ| nau cai haunti bhikkhū patirūpā, yai tattha haunti gahapatikā patirūpā, taisaṃ hatthai nikkhipitvā pak‍kamitabbaṃ|

ayaṃ tattha sāmīcīti ayaṃ tattha anudhammatā|

507. anāpatti ratanaṃ vā ratanasammataṃ vā ajjhārāmai vā ajjhāvasathai vā uggahaitvā vā uggahāpaitvā vā nikkhipati – ‘‘yassa bhavissati sau harissatī’’ti, ratanasammataṃ vissāsaṃ gaṇhāti, tāvakālikaṃ gaṇhāti, paṃsukūlasañ‍ñissa, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

ratanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. vikālagāmappavisanasikkhāpadaṃ

508. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū vikālai gāmaṃ pavisitvā sabhāyaṃ nisīditvā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathainti, saiyyathidaṃ – imā tiracchānakathāyau dī॰ 1.17; ma॰ ni॰ 2.223; saṃ॰ ni॰; a॰ ni॰ 10.69 rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ itthikathaṃ purisakathaṃ (ka॰) majjhimapaṇṇāsaṭṭhakathā 156 piṭṭhai aulaukaitabbā sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ  laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisitvā sabhāyaṃ nisīditvā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaissanti, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā, saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti!

assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū vikālai  gāmaṃ pavisitvā sabhāyaṃ nisīditvā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaissanti, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, vikālai gāmaṃ pavisitvā sabhāyaṃ nisīditvā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaitha, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, vikālai gāmaṃ pavisitvā sabhāyaṃ nisīditvā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaissatha, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu vikālai gāmaṃ pavisaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

509. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ gacchantā sāyaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ upagacchiṃsu| manussā tai bhikkhū passitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pavisatha, bhantai’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ vikālai gāmaṃ pavisitu’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na pavisiṃsu| caurā tai bhikkhū acchindiṃsu| atha khau tai bhikkhū sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āpucchā  vikālai gāmaṃ pavisituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu anāpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

510. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ gacchantau sāyaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ upagacchi| manussā taṃ bhikkhuṃ passitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pavisatha, bhantai’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ anāpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisitu’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau na pāvisi| caurā taṃ bhikkhuṃ acchindiṃsu| atha khau sau bhikkhu sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, santaṃ bhikkhuṃ āpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau pana bhikkhu santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

511. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ahinā daṭṭhau hauti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ‘‘aggiṃ āharissāmī’’ti gāmaṃ gacchati| atha khau sau bhikkhu – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisitu’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau na pāvisi…pai॰… bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tathārūpai ac‍cāyikai  karaṇīyai santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

512. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisaiyya, añ‍ñatra tathārūpā ac‍cāyikā karaṇīyā, pācittiya’’nti|

513. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

santau  nāma bhikkhu sak‍kā hauti āpucchā pavisituṃ|

asantau nāma bhikkhu na sak‍kā hauti āpucchā pavisituṃ|

vikālau nāma majjhanhikai vītivattai yāva aruṇuggamanā|

gāmaṃ pavisaiyyāti parikkhittassa gāmassa parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kamantassa āpatti pācittiyassa| aparikkhittassa gāmassa upacāraṃ auk‍kamantassa āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñatra tathārūpā ac‍cāyikā karaṇīyāti ṭhapaitvā tathārūpaṃ ac‍cāyikaṃ karaṇīyaṃ|

514. vikālai vikālasañ‍ñī santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā gāmaṃ pavisati, añ‍ñatra tathārūpā ac‍cāyikā karaṇīyā, āpatti pācittiyassa| vikālai vaimatikau santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā gāmaṃ pavisati, añ‍ñatra tathārūpā ac‍cāyikā karaṇīyā, āpatti pācittiyassa| vikālai kālasañ‍ñī santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā gāmaṃ pavisati, añ‍ñatra tathārūpā ac‍cāyikā karaṇīyā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

kālai vikālasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kālai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kālai kālasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

515. anāpatti tathārūpai ac‍cāyikai karaṇīyai, santaṃ bhikkhuṃ āpucchā pavisati, asantaṃ  bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā pavisati, antarārāmaṃ gacchati, bhikkhunupassayaṃ gacchati, titthiyasaiyyaṃ gacchati, paṭik‍kamanaṃ gacchati, gāmaina maggau hauti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

vikālagāmappavisanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. sūcigharasikkhāpadaṃ

516. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraina dantakāraina bhikkhū pavāritā haunti – ‘‘yaisaṃ ayyānaṃ sūcigharaina atthau  ahaṃ sūcigharainā’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū bahū sūcigharai viñ‍ñāpainti| yaisaṃ khuddakā sūcigharā tai mahantai sūcigharai viñ‍ñāpainti| yaisaṃ mahantā sūcigharā tai khuddakai sūcigharai viñ‍ñāpainti| atha khau sau dantakārau bhikkhūnaṃ bahū sūcigharai karauntau na sak‍kauti añ‍ñaṃ vik‍kāyikaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ kātuṃ, attanāpi na yāpaiti, puttadāraupissa kilamati| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā na mattaṃ jānitvā bahū sūcigharai viñ‍ñāpaissanti! ayaṃ imaisaṃ bahū sūcigharai karauntau na sak‍kauti añ‍ñaṃ vik‍kāyikaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ kātuṃ, attanāpi na yāpaiti, puttadāraupissa kilamatī’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā bahū sūcigharai viñ‍ñāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā bahū sūcigharai viñ‍ñāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā na mattaṃ  jānitvā bahū sūcigharai viñ‍ñāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

517. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu aṭṭhimayaṃ vā dantamayaṃ vā visāṇamayaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ kārāpaiyya bhaidanakaṃ, pācittiya’’nti|

518. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

aṭṭhi  nāma yaṃ kiñ‍ci aṭṭhi|

dantau nāma hatthidantau vuc‍cati|

visāṇaṃ nāma yaṃ kiñ‍ci visāṇaṃ|

kārāpaiyyāti karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina bhinditvā pācittiyaṃ daisaitabbaṃ|

519. attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti pariyausāvāpaiti (ka॰), āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi  vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti pariyausāvāpaiti (ka॰), āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñassatthāya karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

520. anāpatti gaṇṭhikāya gaṇḍikāya (syā॰), araṇikai, vidhai vīṭhai (sī॰), vīthai (syā॰), añ‍janiyā, añ‍janisalākāya, vāsijaṭai, udakapuñchaniyā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sūcigharasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. mañ‍capīṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

521. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau uc‍cai mañ‍cai sayati| atha khau bhagavā sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau yaināyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa vihārau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgacchatu mai, bhantai, bhagavā sayanaṃ passatū’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tatauva paṭinivattitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āsayatau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau  vaiditabbau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

522. ‘‘navaṃ pana bhikkhunā mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā kārayamānaina aṭṭhaṅgulapādakaṃ kāraitabbaṃ sugataṅgulaina, añ‍ñatra haiṭṭhimāya aṭaniyā; taṃ atik‍kāmayatau chaidanakaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

523. navaṃ nāma karaṇaṃ upādāya vuc‍cati|

mañ‍cau  nāma cattārau mañ‍cā – masārakau, bundikābaddhau, kuḷīrapādakau, āhac‍capādakau|

pīṭhaṃ nāma cattāri pīṭhāni – masārakaṃ, bundikābaddhaṃ, kuḷīrapādakaṃ, āhac‍capādakaṃ|

kārayamānaināti karauntau vā kārāpaintau vā|

aṭṭhaṅgulapādakaṃ  kāraitabbaṃ sugataṅgulaina, añ‍ñatra haiṭṭhimāya  aṭaniyāti ṭhapaitvā haiṭṭhimaṃ aṭaniṃ; taṃ atik‍kāmaitvā karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, paṭilābhaina chinditvā pācittiyaṃ daisaitabbaṃ|

524. attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñassatthāya karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

525. anāpatti pamāṇikaṃ karauti, ūnakaṃ karauti, añ‍ñaina kataṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ paṭilabhitvā chinditvā paribhuñ‍jati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

mañ‍capīṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. tūlaunaddhasikkhāpadaṃ

526. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mañ‍campi pīṭhampi tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpainti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā mañ‍campi pīṭhampi tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpaissanti, saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mañ‍campi pīṭhampi tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, mañ‍campi pīṭhampi tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, mañ‍campi pīṭhampi tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

527. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpaiyya, uddālanakaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

528. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

mañ‍cau nāma cattārau mañ‍cā – masārakau, bundikābaddhau, kuḷīrapādakau, āhac‍capādakau|

pīṭhaṃ  nāma cattāri pīṭhāni – masārakaṃ, bundikābaddhaṃ, kuḷīrapādakaṃ, āhac‍capādakaṃ|

tūlaṃ  nāma tīṇi tūlāni – rukkhatūlaṃ, latātūlaṃ, pauṭakitūlaṃ|

kārāpaiyyāti karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina uddālaitvā pācittiyaṃ daisaitabbaṃ|

529. attanā  vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñassatthāya karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

530. anāpatti āyaugai, kāyabandhanai, aṃsabaddhakai, pattathavikāya, parissāvanai, bibbauhanaṃ karauti, añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā uddālaitvā paribhuñ‍jati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

tūlaunaddhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. nisīdanasikkhāpadaṃ

531. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ nisīdanaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ hauti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā nisīdanaṃ anuñ‍ñāta’’nti appamāṇikāni nisīdanāni dhārainti| mañ‍cassapi pīṭhassapi purataupi pacchataupi aulambainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū appamāṇikāni nisīdanāni dhāraissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, appamāṇikāni nisīdanāni dhāraithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, appamāṇikāni nisīdanāni dhāraissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘nisīdanaṃ pana bhikkhunā kārayamānaina pamāṇikaṃ kāraitabbaṃ| tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ – dīghasau dvai vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā; tiriyaṃ diyaḍḍhaṃ| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau chaidanakaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

532. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī mahākāyau hauti| sau bhagavatau puratau nisīdanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā samantatau samañchamānau nisīdati| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa  tvaṃ, udāyi, nisīdanaṃ samantatau samañchasi; saiyyathāpi purāṇāsikauṭṭhau’’ti? ‘‘tathā hi pana, bhantai, bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ atikhuddakaṃ nisīdanaṃ anuñ‍ñāta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, nisīdanassa dasaṃ vidatthiṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

533. ‘‘nisīdanaṃ pana bhikkhunā kārayamānaina pamāṇikaṃ kāraitabbaṃ| tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ – dīghasau dvai vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā; tiriyaṃ diyaḍḍhaṃ| dasā vidatthi| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau chaidanakaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

534. nisīdanaṃ nāma sadasaṃ vuc‍cati|

kārayamānaināti  karauntau vā kārāpaintau vā pamāṇikaṃ kāraitabbaṃ| tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ – dīghasau dvai vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā; tiriyaṃ diyaḍḍhaṃ| dasā vidatthi| taṃ atik‍kāmaitvā karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina chinditvā pācittiyaṃ daisaitabbaṃ|

535. attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñassatthāya karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

536. anāpatti  pamāṇikaṃ karauti, ūnakaṃ karauti, añ‍ñaina kataṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ paṭilabhitvā chinditvā paribhuñ‍jati, vitānaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā sāṇipākāraṃ vā bhisiṃ vā bibbauhanaṃ vā karauti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

nisīdanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. kaṇḍuppaṭicchādisikkhāpadaṃ

537. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ kaṇḍuppaṭicchādi anuñ‍ñātā hauti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā kaṇḍuppaṭicchādi anuñ‍ñātā’’ti appamāṇikāyau kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiyau dhārainti; purataupi pacchataupi ākaḍḍhantā āhiṇḍanti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū appamāṇikāyau kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiyau dhāraissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, appamāṇikāyau kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiyau dhāraithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, appamāṇikāyau kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiyau dhāraissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ  vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

538. ‘‘kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiṃ pana bhikkhunā kārayamānaina pamāṇikā kāraitabbā| tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ – dīghasau catassau vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā; tiriyaṃ dvai vidatthiyau| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau chaidanakaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

539. kaṇḍuppaṭicchādi nāma yassa adhaunābhi ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ kaṇḍu vā pīḷakā vā assāvau vā thul‍lakacchu vā ābādhau, tassa paṭicchādanatthāya|

kārayamānaināti karauntau vā kārāpaintau vā| pamāṇikā kāraitabbā| tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ – dīghasau catassau vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā; tiriyaṃ dvai vidatthiyau| taṃ atik‍kāmaitvā karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina chinditvā pācittiyaṃ daisaitabbaṃ|

540. attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñassatthāya  karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

541. anāpatti pamāṇikaṃ karauti, ūnakaṃ karauti, añ‍ñaina kataṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ paṭilabhitvā chinditvā paribhuñ‍jati, vitānaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā sāṇipākāraṃ vā bhisiṃ vā bibbauhanaṃ vā karauti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

kaṇḍuppaṭicchādisikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vassikasāṭikāsikkhāpadaṃ

542. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ vassikasāṭikā anuñ‍ñātā hauti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā vassikasāṭikā anuñ‍ñātā’’ti appamāṇikāyau vassikasāṭikāyau dhārainti| purataupi pacchataupi ākaḍḍhantā āhiṇḍanti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū appamāṇikāyau vassikasāṭikāyau dhāraissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, appamāṇikāyau vassikasāṭikāyau dhāraithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, appamāṇikāyau vassikasāṭikāyau dhāraissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

543. ‘‘vassikasāṭikaṃ pana bhikkhunā kārayamānaina pamāṇikā kāraitabbā| tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ – dīghasau cha vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā; tiriyaṃ aḍḍhataiyyā| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau chaidanakaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

544. vassikasāṭikā  nāma vassānassa catumāsatthāya|

kārayamānaināti karauntau vā kārāpaintau vā| pamāṇikā kāraitabbā| tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ – dīghasau  cha vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā; tiriyaṃ aḍḍhataiyyā| taṃ atik‍kāmaitvā karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina chinditvā pācittiyaṃ daisaitabbaṃ|

545. attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñassatthāya karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

546. anāpatti  pamāṇikaṃ karauti, ūnakaṃ karauti, añ‍ñaina kataṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ paṭilabhitvā chinditvā paribhuñ‍jati, vitānaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā sāṇipākāraṃ vā bhisiṃ vā bibbauhanaṃ vā karauti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

vassikasāṭikāsikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. nandasikkhāpadaṃ

547. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā nandau bhagavatau mātucchāputtau abhirūpau hauti dassanīyau pāsādikau caturaṅgulaumakau bhagavatā bhagavatau (ka॰)| sau sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ cīvaraṃ dhāraiti| addasaṃsu khau thairā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ nandaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna – ‘‘bhagavā āgacchatī’’ti āsanā vuṭṭhahanti| tai upagatai jānitvā upagataṃ sañ‍jānitvā (syā॰), upagatai sañ‍jānitvā (sī॰) ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā nandau sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ cīvaraṃ dhāraissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, nanda, sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ cīvaraṃ dhāraisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, nanda, sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ cīvaraṃ dhāraissasi! naitaṃ, nanda, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

548. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ cīvaraṃ kārāpaiyya atiraikaṃ vā, chaidanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ| tatridaṃ sugatassa sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ – dīghasau nava vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā; tiriyaṃ cha vidatthiyau| idaṃ sugatassa sugatacīvarappamāṇa’’nti|
