549. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ  sugatacīvaraṃ (ka॰) nāma dīghasau nava vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā; tiriyaṃ cha vidatthiyau|

kārāpaiyyāti karauti  vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina chinditvā pācittiyaṃ daisaitabbaṃ|

550. attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñassatthāya karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

551. anāpatti ūnakaṃ karauti, añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā chinditvā  paribhuñ‍jati, vitānaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā sāṇipākāraṃ vā bhisiṃ vā bibbauhanaṃ vā karauti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

nandasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

ratanavaggau rājavaggau (sī॰) navamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

rañ‍ñau ca ratanaṃ santaṃ, sūci mañ‍cañ‍ca tūlikaṃ|

nisīdanañ‍ca kaṇḍuñ‍ca, vassikā sugataina cāti||

uddiṭṭhā  khau, āyasmantau, dvainavuti pācittiyā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

khuddakaṃ samattaṃ|

pācittiyakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



6. pāṭidaisanīyakaṇḍaṃ

1. paṭhamapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ

imai  khau panāyasmantau cattārau pāṭidaisanīyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

552. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā paṭik‍kamanakālai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ passitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘handāyya, bhikkhaṃ paṭiggaṇhā’’ti| ‘‘suṭṭhu, bhaginī’’ti sabbaiva aggahaisi| sā upakaṭṭhai kālai nāsakkhi piṇḍāya carituṃ, chin‍nabhattā ahausi| atha khau sā bhikkhunī dutiyampi divasaṃ…pai॰… tatiyampi divasaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā paṭik‍kamanakālai taṃ bhikkhuṃ passitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘handāyya, bhikkhaṃ paṭiggaṇhā’’ti| ‘‘suṭṭhu, bhaginī’’ti sabbaiva aggahaisi| sā upakaṭṭhai kālai nāsakkhi piṇḍāya carituṃ, chin‍nabhattā ahausi| atha khau sā bhikkhunī catutthai divasai rathikāya pavaidhaintī gacchati| saiṭṭhi gahapati rathaina paṭipathaṃ āgacchantau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘apaihāyyai’’ti| sā vauk‍kamantī tatthaiva paripati| saiṭṭhi gahapati taṃ bhikkhuniṃ khamāpaisi – ‘‘khamāhāyyai, mayāsi pātitā’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, gahapati, tayā pātitā| apica, ahamaiva dubbalā’’ti| ‘‘kissa pana tvaṃ, ayyai, dubbalā’’ti? atha khau sā  bhikkhunī saiṭṭhissa gahapatissa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| saiṭṭhi gahapati taṃ bhikkhuniṃ gharaṃ naitvā bhaujaitvā ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā bhikkhuniyā hatthatau āmisaṃ paṭiggahaissanti! kicchalābhau mātugāmau’’ti!

assausuṃ  khau bhikkhū tassa saiṭṭhissa gahapatissa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā hatthatau āmisaṃ paṭiggahaissatī’’ti  …pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhuniyā hatthatau āmisaṃ paṭiggahaisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘ñātikā tai, bhikkhu, añ‍ñātikā’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñātikā, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātakau, maughapurisa, añ‍ñātikāya na jānāti patirūpaṃ vā appatirūpaṃ vā santaṃ vā asantaṃ vā| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā hatthatau āmisaṃ paṭiggahaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

553. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ taina bhikkhunā – ‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’’ti|

554. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

añ‍ñātikā nāma mātitau vā| pititau vā yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā asambaddhā|

bhikkhunī nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

antaragharaṃ nāma rathikā byūhaṃ siṅghāṭakaṃ gharaṃ|

khādanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ| ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

555. añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ  vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| añ‍ñātikāya vaimatikau antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| añ‍ñātikāya  ñātikasañ‍ñī antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ āhāratthāya paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aikatauupasampan‍nāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā – ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya  ñātikasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

556. anāpatti ñātikāya, dāpaiti na daiti, upanikkhipitvā daiti antarārāmai, bhikkhunupassayai, titthiyasaiyyāya, paṭik‍kamanai, gāmatau nīharitvā daiti, yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ ‘‘sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jā’’ti daiti, sikkhamānāya, sāmaṇairiyā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

paṭhamapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ

557. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū kulaisu nimantitā bhuñ‍janti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vausāsantiyau ṭhitā haunti – ‘‘idha sūpaṃ daitha, idha audanaṃ daithā’’ti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍janti| añ‍ñai bhikkhū na cittarūpaṃ bhuñ‍janti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau vausāsantiyau na nivāraissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau vausāsantiyau na nivāraithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā …pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, bhikkhuniyau vausāsantiyau na nivāraissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

558. ‘‘bhikkhū  panaiva kulaisu nimantitā bhuñ‍janti, tatra cai sā tatra cai (syā॰) bhikkhunī vausāsamānarūpā ṭhitā hauti – ‘idha sūpaṃ daitha, idha audanaṃ daithā’ti, taihi bhikkhūhi sā bhikkhunī apasādaitabbā – ‘apasak‍ka tāva, bhagini, yāva bhikkhū bhuñ‍jantī’ti| aikassa caipi aikassapi cai (sī॰ syā॰) bhikkhunau na paṭibhāsaiyya taṃ bhikkhuniṃ apasādaituṃ – ‘apasak‍ka tāva, bhagini, yāva bhikkhū bhuñ‍jantī’ti paṭidaisaitabbaṃ  taihi bhikkhūhi – ‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jimhā asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimā’’’ti|

559. bhikkhū panaiva kulaisu nimantitā bhuñ‍jantīti kulaṃ nāma cattāri kulāni – khattiyakulaṃ, brāhmaṇakulaṃ, vaissakulaṃ, suddakulaṃ|

nimantitā bhuñ‍jantīti pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaujanānaṃ añ‍ñataraina bhaujanaina nimantitā bhuñ‍janti|

bhikkhunī nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

vausāsantī  nāma yathāmittatā yathāsandiṭṭhatā yathāsambhattatā yathāsamānupajjhāyakatā yathāsamānācariyakatā – ‘‘idha sūpaṃ daitha, idha audanaṃ daithā’’ti| aisā vausāsantī nāma|

taihi bhikkhūhīti bhuñ‍jamānaihi bhikkhūhi|

sā bhikkhunīti yā sā vausāsantī bhikkhunī|

taihi bhikkhūhi sā bhikkhunī apasādaitabbā – ‘‘apasak‍ka tāva, bhagini, yāva bhikkhū bhuñ‍jantī’’ti| aikassa caipi aikassapi cai (sī॰ syā॰) bhikkhunau anapasāditau anapasāditai (sī॰ syā॰) – ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

560. upasampan‍nāya upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī vausāsantiyā na nivāraiti, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| upasampan‍nāya vaimatikau vausāsantiyā na nivāraiti, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| upasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī vausāsantiyā na nivāraiti, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

aikatauupasampan‍nāya  vausāsantiyā na nivāraiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya  vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

561. anāpatti attanau bhattaṃ dāpaiti na daiti, añ‍ñaisaṃ bhattaṃ daiti na dāpaiti, yaṃ na din‍naṃ taṃ dāpaiti, yattha na din‍naṃ tattha dāpaiti, sabbaisaṃ samakaṃ dāpaiti, sikkhamānā vausāsati, sāmaṇairī vausāsati, pañ‍ca bhaujanāni ṭhapaitvā sabbattha, anāpatti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dutiyapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. tatiyapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ

562. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ kulaṃ ubhataupasan‍naṃ hauti| saddhāya vaḍḍhati, bhaugaina hāyati, yaṃ tasmiṃ kulai uppaj‍jati puraibhattaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā taṃ sabbaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā appaikadā anasitā acchanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā na mattaṃ jānitvā paṭiggahaissanti! imai imaisaṃ datvā appaikadā anasitā acchantī’’ti!! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai  aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kulaṃ saddhāya vaḍḍhati, bhaugaina hāyati aivarūpassa kulassa ñattidutiyaina kammaina saikkhasammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

563. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmaṃ kulaṃ saddhāya vaḍḍhati, bhaugaina hāyati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa kulassa saikkhasammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmaṃ kulaṃ saddhāya vaḍḍhati, bhaugaina  hāyati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa kulassa saikkhasammutiṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa kulassa saikkhasammutiyā dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa kulassa saikkhasammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yāni khau pana tāni saikkhasammatāni kulāni, yau pana bhikkhu tathārūpaisu saikkhasammataisu kulaisu khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ taina bhikkhunā – ‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

564. taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ ussavau hauti| manussā bhikkhū nimantaitvā bhaujainti| tampi khau kulaṃ bhikkhū nimantaisi| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nādhivāsainti – ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā saikkhasammataisu kulaisu khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādituṃ bhuñ‍jitu’’nti| tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau nāma amhākaṃ jīvitaina yaṃ ayyā amhākaṃ na paṭiggaṇhantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, nimantitaina saikkhasammataisu kulaisu khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ  vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādituṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yāni khau pana tāni saikkhasammatāni kulāni, yau pana bhikkhu tathārūpaisu saikkhasammataisu kulaisu pubbai animantitau  khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādaiyya  vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ taina bhikkhunā – ‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

565. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tassa kulassa kulūpakau hauti| atha khau sau bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina taṃ kulaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| taina khau pana samayaina sau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| atha khau tai manussā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘bhuñ‍jatha, bhantai’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ animantitaina saikkhasammataisu kulaisu khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādituṃ bhuñ‍jitu’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau na paṭiggahaisi; nāsakkhi piṇḍāya carituṃ; chin‍nabhattau ahausi| atha khau sau bhikkhu ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā saikkhasammataisu kulaisu khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādituṃ  bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

566. ‘‘yāni khau pana tāni saikkhasammatāni kulāni, yau pana bhikkhu tathārūpaisu saikkhasammataisu kulaisu pubbai animantitau agilānau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ taina bhikkhunā – ‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’ti|

567. yāni khau pana tāni saikkhasammatāni kulānīti saikkhasammataṃ nāma kulaṃ yaṃ kulaṃ saddhāya vaḍḍhati, bhaugaina hāyati| aivarūpassa kulassa ñattidutiyaina kammaina saikkhasammuti din‍nā hauti|

yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

tathārūpaisu saikkhasammataisu kulaisūti aivarūpaisu saikkhasammataisu kulaisu|

animantitau  nāma aj‍jatanāya vā svātanāya vā animantitau, gharūpacāraṃ auk‍kamantai nimantaiti, aisau animantitau nāma|

nimantitau  nāma aj‍jatanāya vā svātanāya vā nimantitau, gharūpacāraṃ anauk‍kamantai nimantaiti, aisau nimantitau nāma|

agilānau nāma sak‍kauti piṇḍāya carituṃ|

gilānau nāma na sak‍kauti piṇḍāya carituṃ|

khādanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ|

animantitau agilānau ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

568. saikkhasammatai saikkhasammatasañ‍ñī animantitau agilānau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃva sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| saikkhasammatai vaimatikau…pai॰… saikkhasammatai asaikkhasammatasañ‍ñī animantitau agilānau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ āhāratthāya paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asaikkhasammatai saikkhasammatasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asaikkhasammatai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| asaikkhasammatai asaikkhasammatasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

569. anāpatti nimantitassa, gilānassa, nimantitassa vā gilānassa vā saisakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, añ‍ñaisaṃ bhikkhā tattha pañ‍ñattā hauti, gharatau nīharitvā dainti, nic‍cabhattai, salākabhattai, pakkhikai, upausathikai, pāṭipadikai, yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ – ‘‘sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jā’’ti daiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

tatiyapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. catutthapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ

570. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sākiyadāsakā avaruddhā haunti| sākiyāniyau icchanti ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu bhattaṃ kātuṃ| assausuṃ khau sākiyadāsakā – ‘‘sākiyāniyau kira ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu bhattaṃ kattukāmā’’ti| tai maggai pariyuṭṭhiṃsu| sākiyāniyau  paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ ādāya ārañ‍ñakaṃ saināsanaṃ agamaṃsu| sākiyadāsakā nikkhamitvā sākiyāniyau acchindiṃsu ca dūsaisuñ‍ca| sākiyā nikkhamitvā tai caurai sabhaṇḍai saha bhaṇḍaina (ka॰) gahaitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā ārāmai caurai paṭivasantai nāraucaissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū sākiyānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yāni khau pana tāni ārañ‍ñakāni saināsanāni sāsaṅkasammatāni sappaṭibhayāni, yau pana bhikkhu tathārūpaisu saināsanaisu saināsanaisu viharantau (sī॰ syā॰) pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaṃ khādanīyaṃ  vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā ajjhārāmai sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ taina bhikkhunā – ‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

571. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu gilānau hauti| manussā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā ādāya ārañ‍ñakaṃ saināsanaṃ agamaṃsu| atha khau tai manussā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘bhuñ‍jatha, bhantai’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā  paṭiggahaitvā khādituṃ bhuñ‍jitu’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau na paṭiggahaisi, nāsakkhi piṇḍāya carituṃ pavisituṃ (ka॰), chin‍nabhattau ahausi| atha khau sau bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādituṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

572. ‘‘yāni khau pana tāni ārañ‍ñakāni saināsanāni sāsaṅkasammatāni sappaṭibhayāni, yau pana bhikkhu tathārūpaisu saināsanaisu  pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā ajjhārāmai sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā agilānau khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ taina bhikkhunā – ‘gārayhaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ  āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’’ti|

573. yāni khau pana tāni ārañ‍ñakāni saināsanānīti ārañ‍ñakaṃ nāma saināsanaṃ pañ‍cadhanusatikaṃ pacchimaṃ|

sāsaṅkaṃ nāma ārāmai ārāmūpacārai caurānaṃ niviṭṭhaukāsau dissati, bhuttaukāsau dissati, ṭhitaukāsau dissati, nisin‍naukāsau dissati, nipan‍naukāsau dissati|

sappaṭibhayaṃ nāma ārāmai ārāmūpacārai cauraihi manussā hatā dissanti, viluttā dissanti, ākauṭitā dissanti|

yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

tathārūpaisu saināsanaisūti aivarūpaisu saināsanaisu|

appaṭisaṃviditaṃ  nāma pañ‍can‍naṃ paṭisaṃviditaṃ, aitaṃ appaṭisaṃviditaṃ nāma| ārāmaṃ ārāmūpacāraṃ ṭhapaitvā paṭisaṃviditaṃ, aitaṃ aitampi (sī॰) appaṭisaṃviditaṃ nāma|

paṭisaṃviditaṃ  nāma yau kauci itthī vā purisau vā ārāmaṃ ārāmūpacāraṃ āgantvā āraucaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmassa, bhantai, khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā āharissantī’’ti| sacai sāsaṅkaṃ hauti, sāsaṅkanti ācikkhitabbaṃ; sacai sappaṭibhayaṃ hauti, sappaṭibhayanti ācikkhitabbaṃ; sacai – ‘‘hautu, bhantai, āhariyissatī’’ti bhaṇati, caurā vattabbā – ‘‘manussā idhūpacaranti apasak‍kathā’’ti| yāguyā paṭisaṃviditai tassā parivārau āhariyyati, aitaṃ paṭisaṃviditaṃ nāma| bhattaina paṭisaṃviditai tassa parivārau āhariyyati, aitaṃ paṭisaṃviditaṃ nāma| khādanīyaina paṭisaṃviditai tassa parivārau āhariyyati, aitaṃ paṭisaṃviditaṃ nāma| kulaina paṭisaṃviditai yau tasmiṃ kulai manussau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā āharati, aitaṃ paṭisaṃviditaṃ nāma| gāmaina paṭisaṃviditai yau tasmiṃ gāmai manussau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā āharati, aitaṃ paṭisaṃviditaṃ nāma| pūgaina paṭisaṃviditai yau tasmiṃ pūgai manussau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā āharati, aitaṃ paṭisaṃviditaṃ nāma|

khādanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ|

ajjhārāmau nāma parikkhittassa ārāmassa antauārāmau| aparikkhittassa upacārau|

agilānau nāma sak‍kauti piṇḍāya carituṃ gantuṃ (ka॰)|

gilānau nāma na sak‍kauti piṇḍāya carituṃ gantuṃ (ka॰)|

appaṭisaṃviditaṃ  agilānau ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

574. appaṭisaṃviditai appaṭisaṃviditasañ‍ñī khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā ajjhārāmai sahatthā  paṭiggahaitvā agilānau khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| appaṭisaṃviditai vaimatikau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ  vā ajjhārāmai sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā agilānau  khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| appaṭisaṃviditai paṭisaṃviditasañ‍ñī khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā ajjhārāmai sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā agilānau khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ āhāratthāya paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paṭisaṃviditai appaṭisaṃviditasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paṭisaṃviditai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paṭisaṃviditai paṭisaṃviditasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

575. anāpatti paṭisaṃviditai, gilānassa, paṭisaṃviditai vā gilānassa vā saisakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, bahārāmai paṭiggahaitvā antauārāmai bhuñ‍jati, tattha jātakaṃ mūlaṃ vā tacaṃ vā pattaṃ vā pupphaṃ vā phalaṃ vā bhuñ‍jati, yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

catutthapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

uddiṭṭhā khau, āyasmantau, cattārau pāṭidaisanīyā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

pāṭidaisanīyakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



7. saikhiyakaṇḍaṃ

1. parimaṇḍalavaggau

imai khau panāyasmantau saikhiyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

576. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintā nivāsainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintā nivāsaissanti, saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintā nivāsaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintā nivāsaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintā nivāsaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰…  aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘parimaṇḍalaṃ nivāsaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

parimaṇḍalaṃ  nivāsaitabbaṃ nābhimaṇḍalaṃ jāṇumaṇḍalaṃ paṭicchādaintaina| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca puratau vā pacchatau vā aulambaintau nivāsaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa,

ādikammikassāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

577. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintā pārupanti…pai॰…|

‘‘parimaṇḍalaṃ pārupissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

parimaṇḍalaṃ pārupitabbaṃ ubhau kaṇṇai samaṃ katvā| yau  anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca puratau vā pacchatau vā aulambaintau pārupati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa,

ādikammikassāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

578. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaragharai gacchanti…pai॰…|

‘‘suppaṭicchan‍nau antaragharai  gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

suppaṭicchan‍naina antaragharai gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaragharai gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa,

ādikammikassāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

579. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaragharai nisīdanti…pai॰…|

‘‘suppaṭicchan‍nau antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

suppaṭicchan‍naina antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaragharai nisīdati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, vāsūpagatassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

580. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū hatthampi pādampi kīḷāpaintā antaragharai gacchanti…pai॰…|

‘‘susaṃvutau antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

susaṃvutaina antaragharai gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā kīḷāpaintau antaragharai gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

581. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū  hatthampi pādampi kīḷāpaintā antaragharai nisīdanti…pai॰…|

‘‘susaṃvutau antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

susaṃvutaina antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā kīḷāpaintau antaragharai nisīdati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

582. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintā antaragharai gacchanti…pai॰…|

‘‘aukkhittacakkhu antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

aukkhittacakkhunā antaragharai gantabbaṃ yugamattaṃ paikkhantaina| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintau antaragharai gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa,

ādikammikassāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

583. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintā antaragharai nisīdanti…pai॰…|

‘‘aukkhittacakkhu  antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

aukkhittacakkhunā antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ yugamattaṃ paikkhantaina| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintau antaragharai nisīdati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa,

ādikammikassāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

584. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ukkhittakāya antaragharai gacchanti…pai॰…|

‘‘na ukkhittakāya antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na ukkhittakāya antaragharai gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatau vā ubhatau vā ukkhipitvā antaragharai gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa,

ādikammikassāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

585. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ukkhittakāya antaragharai nisīdanti…pai॰…|

‘‘na ukkhittakāya antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na ukkhittakāya antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatau vā ubhatau vā ukkhipitvā antaragharai nisīdati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, vāsūpagatassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

parimaṇḍalavaggau paṭhamau|

2. uj‍jagghikavaggau

586. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mahāhasitaṃ hasantā antaragharai gacchanti…pai॰…|

‘‘na uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca mahāhasitaṃ hasantau antaragharai gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, hasanīyasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ mihitamattaṃ karauti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

587. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mahāhasitaṃ hasantā antaragharai nisīdanti…pai॰…|

‘‘na uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na  uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca mahāhasitaṃ hasantau antaragharai nisīdati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, hasanīyasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ mihitamattaṃ karauti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

588. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntā antaragharai gacchanti…pai॰…|

‘‘appasaddau antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

appasaddaina antaragharai gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntau antaragharai gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

589. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntā antaragharai nisīdanti…pai॰…|

‘‘appasaddau antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

appasaddaina antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntau antaragharai nisīdati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

590. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau  pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchanti kāyaṃ aulambaintā…pai॰…|

‘‘na kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai gantabbaṃ| kāyaṃ paggahaitvā gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchati kāyaṃ aulambaintau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

591. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdanti, kāyaṃ aulambaintā…pai॰…|

‘‘na kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ| kāyaṃ paggahaitvā nisīditabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdati kāyaṃ aulambaintau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, vāsūpagatassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

592. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchanti bāhuṃ aulambaintā…pai॰…|

‘‘na bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai gantabbaṃ| bāhuṃ paggahaitvā gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchati bāhuṃ aulambaintau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

593. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdanti bāhuṃ aulambaintā…pai॰…|

‘‘na bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ| bāhuṃ paggahaitvā nisīditabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdati bāhuṃ aulambaintau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, vāsūpagatassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

594. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍakassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchanti sīsaṃ aulambaintā…pai॰…|

‘‘na sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai gantabbaṃ| sīsaṃ paggahaitvā gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchati sīsaṃ aulambaintau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

595. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdanti sīsaṃ aulambaintā…pai॰…|

‘‘na sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ| sīsaṃ paggahaitvā nisīditabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdati sīsaṃ aulambaintau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, vāsūpagatassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

uj‍jagghikavaggau dutiyau|

3. khambhakatavaggau

596. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū khambhakatā antaragharai gacchanti…pai॰…|

‘‘na khambhakatau antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na  khambhakataina antaragharai gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatau vā ubhatau vā khambhaṃ katvā antaragharai gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

597. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū khambhakatā antaragharai nisīdanti…pai॰…|

na khambhakataina antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatau vā ubhatau vā khambhaṃ katvā antaragharai nisīdati, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, vāsūpagatassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

598. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sasīsaṃ pārupitvā antaragharai gacchanti…pai॰…|

‘‘na auguṇṭhitau antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na auguṇṭhitaina antaragharai gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sasīsaṃ pārupitvā antaragharai gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

599. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sasīsaṃ pārupitvā antaragharai nisīdanti…pai॰…|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, vāsūpagatassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

600. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uk‍kuṭikāya antaragharai gacchanti…pai॰…|

‘‘na  uk‍kuṭikāya antaragharai gamissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na  uk‍kuṭikāya antaragharai gantabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uk‍kuṭikāya antaragharai gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

601. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū pal‍latthikāya pal‍lattikāya (ka॰) antaragharai nisīdanti…pai॰…|

‘‘na pal‍latthikāya antaragharai nisīdissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na pal‍latthikāya antaragharai nisīditabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthapal‍latthikāya vā dussapal‍latthikāya vā antaragharai nisīdati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, vāsūpagatassa, āpadāsu,

ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

602. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau  pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhanti chaḍḍaitukāmā viya…pai॰…|

‘‘sak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

sak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātau paṭiggahaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti chaḍḍaitukāmau viya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

603. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintā piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhanti, ākirantaipi atik‍kantaipi atik‍kamantaipi (sī॰) na jānanti…pai॰…|

‘‘pattasañ‍ñī piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

pattasañ‍ñinā piṇḍapātau paṭiggahaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintau piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

604. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantā sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ paṭiggaṇhanti…pai॰…|

‘‘samasūpakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

sūpau nāma dvai sūpā – muggasūpau, māsasūpau| hatthahāriyau samasūpakau piṇḍapātau paṭiggahaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, rasarasai, ñātakānaṃ pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

605. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū thūpīkataṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhanti…pai॰…|

‘‘samatittikaṃ samatitthikaṃ (ka॰) piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

samatittikau  samatitthikaṃ (ka॰) piṇḍapātau paṭiggahaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca thūpīkataṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

khambhakatavaggau tatiyau|

4. sak‍kac‍cavaggau

606. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍janti abhuñ‍jitukāmā viya…pai॰…|

‘‘sak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

sak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātau bhuñ‍jitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa,

ādikammikassāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

607. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍janti, ākirantaipi atik‍kantaipi na jānanti…pai॰…|

‘‘pattasañ‍ñī  piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

pattasañ‍ñinā piṇḍapātau bhuñ‍jitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintau piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

608. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ aumasitvā aumadditvā (ka॰) piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘sapadānaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

sapadānaṃ piṇḍapātau bhuñ‍jitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aumasitvā aumadditvā (ka॰) piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, añ‍ñaisaṃ daintau aumasati, añ‍ñassa bhājanai ākirantau aumasati, uttaribhaṅgai, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

609. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantā sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘samasūpakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

sūpau nāma dvai sūpā – muggasūpau, māsasūpau hatthahāriyau| samasūpakau piṇḍapātau bhuñ‍jitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, rasarasai, ñātakānaṃ pavāritānaṃ, attanau dhanaina, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

610. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū thūpakatau aumadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘na thūpakatau aumadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na thūpakatau aumadditvā piṇḍapātau bhuñ‍jitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca thūpakatau aumadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jati āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, parittakai saisai aikatau saṃkaḍḍhitvā aumadditvā bhuñ‍jati, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

611. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sūpampi byañ‍janampi audanaina paṭicchādainti bhiyyaukamyataṃ upādāya…pai॰…|

‘‘na  sūpaṃ vā byañ‍janaṃ vā audanaina paṭicchādaissāmi bhiyyaukamyataṃ upādāyāti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na sūpaṃ vā byañ‍janaṃ vā audanaina paṭicchādaitabbaṃ bhiyyaukamyataṃ upādāya| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpaṃ vā byañ‍janaṃ vā audanaina paṭicchādaiti bhiyyaukamyataṃ upādāya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, sāmikā paṭicchādaitvā dainti, na bhiyyaukamyataṃ upādāya, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

612. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sūpampi audanampi attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā sūpampi audanampi attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissanti! kassa sampan‍naṃ na manāpaṃ! kassa sāduṃ na ruc‍catī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sūpampi audanampi attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, sūpampi audanampi attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jathāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, sūpampi audanampi attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘na sūpaṃ vā audanaṃ vā attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

613. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū gilānā haunti| gilānapucchakā bhikkhū gilānai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kac‍cāvusau, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīya’’nti? ‘‘pubbai mayaṃ, āvusau, sūpampi audanampi attanau atthāya  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jāma, taina nau phāsu hauti| idāni pana – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhitta’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na viñ‍ñāpaima, taina nau na phāsu hautī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā sūpampi audanampi attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘na sūpaṃ vā audanaṃ vā agilānau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na sūpaṃ vā audanaṃ vā agilānaina attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpaṃ vā audanaṃ vā agilānau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, ñātakānaṃ pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

614. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ujjhānasañ‍ñī paraisaṃ pattaṃ aulaukainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na ujjhānasañ‍ñī paraisaṃ pattaṃ aulaukaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na ujjhānasañ‍ñinā paraisaṃ pattau aulaukaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ujjhānasañ‍ñī paraisaṃ pattaṃ aulaukaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, ‘‘dassāmī’’ti vā  ‘‘dāpaissāmī’’ti vā aulaukaiti, na ujjhānasañ‍ñissa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

615. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ karaunti…pai॰…|

‘‘nātimahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

nātimahantau kabaḷau kātabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca mahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, khaj‍jakai, phalāphalai, uttaribhaṅgai, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

616. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū dīghaṃ ālaupaṃ karaunti…pai॰…|

‘‘parimaṇḍalaṃ ālaupaṃ karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

parimaṇḍalau ālaupau kātabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca dīghaṃ ālaupaṃ karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, khaj‍jakai, phalāphalai, uttaribhaṅgai, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

sak‍kac‍cavaggau catutthau|

5. kabaḷavaggau

‘‘na  anāhaṭai kabaḷai mukhadvāraṃ vivarissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na anāhaṭai kabaḷai mukhadvāraṃ vivaritabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca anāhaṭai kabaḷai mukhadvāraṃ vivarati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

‘‘na bhuñ‍jamānau sabbaṃ hatthaṃ mukhai pakkhipissāmīti sikkhākaraṇīyā’’ti|

na bhuñ‍jamānaina sabbau hatthau mukhai pakkhipitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca bhuñ‍jamānau sabbaṃ hatthaṃ mukhai pakkhipati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

619. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sakabaḷaina mukhaina byāharanti…pai॰…|

‘‘na  sakabaḷaina mukhaina byāharissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na sakabaḷaina mukhaina byāharitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sakabaḷaina  mukhaina byāharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

620. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū piṇḍukkhaipakaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

na piṇḍukkhaipakaṃ bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca piṇḍukkhaipakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, khaj‍jakai, phalāphalai, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

621. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kabaḷāvacchaidakaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘na  kabaḷāvacchaidakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na  kabaḷāvacchaidakaṃ bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kabaḷāvacchaidakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, khaj‍jakai phalāphalai, uttaribhaṅgai, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

622. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū avagaṇḍakārakaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

na avagaṇḍakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatau vā ubhatau vā gaṇḍaṃ katvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, phalāphalai, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

623. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū hatthaniddhunakaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, kacavaraṃ chaḍḍaintau hatthaṃ niddhunāti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

624. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sitthāvakārakaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

na sitthāvakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sitthāvakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, kacavaraṃ  chaḍḍaintau sitthaṃ chaḍḍayati, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

625. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jivhānicchārakaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘na  jivhānicchārakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na jivhānicchārakaṃ bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca jivhānicchārakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

626. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū capucapukārakaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘na capucapukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na capucapukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca capucapukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

kabaḷavaggau pañ‍camau|

6. surusuruvaggau

627. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraina brāhmaṇaina saṅghassa payaupānaṃ  paṭiyattaṃ hauti| bhikkhū surusurukārakaṃ khīraṃ pivanti| añ‍ñatarau naṭapubbakau bhikkhu aivamāha – ‘‘sabbauyaṃ mañ‍ñai saṅghau sītīkatau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu saṅghaṃ ārabbha davaṃ karissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, saṅghaṃ ārabbha davaṃ akāsīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, saṅghaṃ ārabbha davaṃ karissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, buddhaṃ vā dhammaṃ vā  saṅghaṃ vā ārabbha davau kātabbau| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘na  surusurukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na surusurukārakaṃ  bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca surusurukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

628. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū hatthanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘na hatthanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na  hatthanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

629. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū pattanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘na pattanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na pattanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca pattanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, parittakai saisai aikatau saṅkaḍḍhitvā nil‍laihitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

630. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘na auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

631. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai suṃsumāragirai (sī॰ syā॰), saṃsumāragirai (ka॰) bhaisakaḷāvanai  migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū kaukanadai kaukanudai (ka॰) pāsādai sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggaṇhanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggahaissanti, saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggahaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggaṇhantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggahaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘na sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggahaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na  sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakau paṭiggahaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, ‘‘dhauvissāmī’’ti vā ‘‘dhauvāpaissāmī’’ti vā paṭiggaṇhāti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

632. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū kaukanadai pāsādai sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaissanti, saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘na sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaitabbaṃ| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, uddharitvā vā bhinditvā vā paṭiggahai vā nīharitvā vā chaḍḍaiti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

633. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū chattapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū chattapāṇissa  dhammaṃ daisaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, chattapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, chattapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘na chattapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

634. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū chattapāṇissa gilānassa dhammaṃ  daisaituṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā chattapāṇissa gilānassa dhammaṃ na daisaissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, chattapāṇissa gilānassa dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘na chattapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

chattaṃ nāma tīṇi chattāni – saitacchattaṃ, kilañ‍jacchattaṃ, paṇṇacchattaṃ maṇḍalabaddhaṃ salākabaddhaṃ|

dhammau nāma buddhabhāsitau sāvakabhāsitau isibhāsitau daivatābhāsitau atthūpasañhitau dhammūpasañhitau|

daisaiyyāti padaina daisaiti, padai padai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| akkharāya daisaiti, akkharakkharāya  āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na chattapāṇissa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca chattapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

635. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū daṇḍapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na daṇḍapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

daṇḍau nāma majjhimassa purisassa catuhatthau daṇḍau| tatau uk‍kaṭṭhau adaṇḍau, aumakau adaṇḍau|

na daṇḍapāṇissa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca daṇḍapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

636. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū satthapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na satthapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

satthaṃ  nāma aikataudhāraṃ ubhataudhāraṃ paharaṇaṃ|

na  satthapāṇissa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca satthapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

637. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū āvudhapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na āvudhapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

āvudhaṃ nāma cāpau kaudaṇḍau|

na  āvudhapāṇissa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca āvudhapāṇissa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

surusuruvaggau chaṭṭhau|

7. pādukavaggau

638. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū pādukāruḷhassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na  pādukāruḷhassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na pādukāruḷhassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ak‍kantassa vā paṭimuk‍kassa vā aumuk‍kassa vā agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

639. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū upāhanāruḷhassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na upāhanāruḷhassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhākaraṇīyā’’ti|

na upāhanāruḷhassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ak‍kantassa vā paṭimuk‍kassa vā aumuk‍kassa vā agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

640. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū yānagatassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na yānagatassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

yānaṃ  nāma vayhaṃ rathau sakaṭaṃ sandamānikā sivikā pāṭaṅkī|

na yānagatassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca yānagatassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

641. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau  pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sayanagatassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na sayanagatassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na sayanagatassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca antamasau chamāyampi nipan‍nassa sayanagatassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

642. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū pal‍latthikāya nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na pal‍latthikāya nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na  pal‍latthikāya nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthapal‍latthikāya vā dussapal‍latthikāya vā nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

643. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū vaiṭhitasīsassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na vaiṭhitasīsassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

vaiṭhitasīsau nāma kaisantaṃ na dassāpaitvā vaiṭhitau hauti|

na vaiṭhitasīsassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca vaiṭhitasīsassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, kaisantaṃ vivarāpaitvā daisaiti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

644. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū auguṇṭhitasīsassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na auguṇṭhitasīsassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

auguṇṭhitasīsau  nāma sasīsaṃ pārutau vuc‍cati|

na auguṇṭhitasīsassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca auguṇṭhitasīsassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, sīsaṃ vivarāpaitvā daisaiti, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

645. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū  chamāya nisīditvā āsanai nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na chamāyaṃ nisīditvā nisin‍naina (aṭṭhakathā) āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

646. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaithāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

647. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, bārāṇasiyaṃ añ‍ñatarassa chapakassa chavakassa (syā॰) pajāpati gabbhinī ahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sā chapakī taṃ chapakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘gabbhinīmhi, ayyaputta! icchāmi ambaṃ khāditu’nti| ‘natthi ambaṃ ambau (syā॰), akālau ambassā’ti| ‘sacai na labhissāmi marissāmī’ti| taina khau pana samayaina, rañ‍ñau ambau dhuvaphalau hauti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sau chapakau yaina sau ambau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ ambaṃ abhiruhitvā nilīnau acchi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā purauhitaina brāhmaṇaina saddhiṃ yaina sau ambau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā uc‍cai āsanai nisīditvā mantaṃ pariyāpuṇāti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, tassa chapakassa aitadahausi – ‘yāva adhammikau ayaṃ rājā, yatra hi nāma uc‍cai āsanai nisīditvā mantaṃ pariyāpuṇissati| ayañ‍ca brāhmaṇau adhammikau, yatra hi nāma nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa mantaṃ vācaissati| ahañ‍camhi adhammikau, yauhaṃ itthiyā kāraṇā rañ‍ñau ambaṃ avaharāmi| sabbamidaṃ carimaṃ kata’nti tatthaiva paripati|

imā gāthāyau jā॰ 1.4.33-35 jātakai añ‍ñathā dissanti ‘‘ubhau atthaṃ na jānanti, ubhau dhammaṃ na passarai|

yau cāyaṃ mantaṃ vācaiti, yau cādhammainadhīyati||

‘‘sālīnaṃ audanau bhuttau, sucimaṃsūpasaicanau|

tasmā dhammai na vattāmi, dhammau ariyaibhi vaṇṇitau||

‘‘dhiratthu  taṃ dhanalābhaṃ, yasalābhañ‍ca brāhmaṇa|

yā vutti vinipātaina, adhammacaraṇaina vā||

‘‘paribbaja mahābrahmai, pacantañ‍ñaipi pāṇinau|

mā tvaṃ adhammau ācaritau, asmā kumbhamivābhidā’’ti||

‘‘tadāpi mai, bhikkhavai, amanāpā nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa mantaṃ vācaituṃ, kimaṅga pana aitarahi na amanāpā bhavissati nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘na nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

648. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ṭhitā nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na  ṭhitau nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na  ṭhitaina nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ṭhitau nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

649. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū pacchatau gacchantā puratau gacchantassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na pacchatau gacchantau puratau gacchantassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na pacchatau gacchantaina puratau gacchantassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca pacchatau gacchantau puratau gacchantassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

aikādasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

650. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uppathaina gacchantā pathaina gacchantassa dhammaṃ daisainti…pai॰…|

‘‘na uppathaina gacchantau pathaina gacchantassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na uppathaina gacchantaina pathaina gacchantassa agilānassa dhammau daisaitabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uppathaina gacchantau pathaina gacchantassa  agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

dvādasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

651. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ṭhitā uc‍cārampi passāvampi karaunti…pai॰…|

‘‘na ṭhitau agilānau uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na ṭhitaina agilānaina uc‍cārau vā passāvau vā kātabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ṭhitau agilānau uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca…pai॰… ādikammikassāti|

tairasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

652. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū haritai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi karaunti…pai॰…|

‘‘na  haritai agilānau uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na haritai agilānaina uc‍cārau vā passāvau vā khaiḷau vā kātabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca haritai agilānau uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, appaharitai katau haritaṃ auttharati, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

cuddasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

653. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi karaunti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti  – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi karissanti, saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti! assausuṃ  khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi karissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi karauthāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi karissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘na udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

654. taina khau pana samayaina gilānā bhikkhū udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi  khaiḷampi kātuṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā  udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi kātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘na udakai agilānau uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na udakai agilānaina uc‍cārau vā passāvau vā khaiḷau vā kātabbau| yau anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai agilānau uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantassa, gilānassa, thalai katau udakaṃ auttharati, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, khittacittassa, vaidanāṭṭassa, ādikammikassāti|

pan‍narasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

pādukavaggau sattamau|

uddiṭṭhā  khau, āyasmantau, saikhiyā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

saikhiyā niṭṭhitā|

saikhiyakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



8. adhikaraṇasamathā

imai khau panāyasmantau satta adhikaraṇasamathā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

655. uppan‍nuppan‍nānaṃ  adhikaraṇānaṃ samathāya vūpasamāya sammukhāvinayau dātabbau, sativinayau dātabbau, amūḷhavinayau dātabbau, paṭiñ‍ñāya kāraitabbaṃ, yaibhuyyasikā, tassapāpiyasikā, tiṇavatthārakauti|

uddiṭṭhā khau, āyasmantau, satta adhikaraṇasamathā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

adhikaraṇasamathā niṭṭhitā|


uddiṭṭhaṃ khau, āyasmantau, nidānaṃ; uddiṭṭhā cattārau pārājikā dhammā; uddiṭṭhā tairasa saṅghādisaisā dhammā; uddiṭṭhā dvai aniyatā dhammā; uddiṭṭhā tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā dhammā; uddiṭṭhā dvainavuti pācittiyā dhammā; uddiṭṭhā cattārau pāṭidaisanīyā dhammā; uddiṭṭhā saikhiyā dhammā; uddiṭṭhā satta adhikaraṇasamathā dhammā| aittakaṃ tassa bhagavatau suttāgataṃ suttapariyāpan‍naṃ anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchati| tattha sabbaihaiva samaggaihi sammaudamānaihi avivadamānaihi sikkhitabbanti|

mahāvibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|


|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||


bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau


1. pārājikakaṇḍaṃ (bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau)

1. paṭhamapārājikaṃ

656. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sāḷhau migāranattā bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihāraṃ kattukāmau hauti| atha khau sāḷhau migāranattā bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, ayyai, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihāraṃ kātuṃ| daitha mai navakammikaṃ bhikkhuni’’nti| taina khau pana samayaina catassau bhaginiyau bhikkhunīsu pabbajitā haunti – nandā, nandavatī, sundarīnandā, thul‍lanandāti| tāsu sundarīnandā bhikkhunī taruṇapabbajitā abhirūpā hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā paṇḍitā byattā maidhāvinī dakkhā analasā, tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatā, alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| atha khau bhikkhunisaṅghau sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samman‍nitvā sāḷhassa migāranattunau navakammikaṃ adāsi| taina khau pana samayaina sundarīnandā bhikkhunī sāḷhassa  migāranattunau nivaisanaṃ abhikkhaṇaṃ gacchati – ‘‘vāsiṃ daitha, parasuṃ pharasuṃ (syā॰ ka॰) daitha, kuṭhāriṃ kudhāriṃ (ka॰) daitha, kuddālaṃ daitha, nikhādanaṃ daithā’’ti| sāḷhaupi migāranattā bhikkhunupassayaṃ abhikkhaṇaṃ  gacchati katākataṃ jānituṃ| tai abhiṇhadassanaina paṭibaddhacittā ahaisuṃ|

atha khau sāḷhau migāranattā sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dūsaituṃ aukāsaṃ alabhamānau aitadaivatthāya bhikkhunisaṅghassa bhattaṃ akāsi| atha khau sāḷhau migāranattā bhattaggai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaintau – ‘‘aittakā bhikkhuniyau ayyāya sundarīnandāya vuḍḍhatarā’’ti aikamantaṃ āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi ‘‘aittakā navakatarā’’ti – aikamantaṃ āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi| paṭicchan‍nai aukāsai  nikūṭai sundarīnandāya bhikkhuniyā  āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi, yathā thairā bhikkhuniyau jānaiyyuṃ – ‘‘navakānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ santikai nisin‍nā’’ti; navakāpi bhikkhuniyau jānaiyyuṃ – ‘‘thairānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ santikai nisin‍nā’’ti| atha khau sāḷhau migāranattā bhikkhunisaṅghassa kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, ayyai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| sundarīnandā bhikkhunī sal‍lakkhaitvā – ‘‘na bahukatau sāḷhau migāranattā bhikkhunisaṅghassa bhattaṃ akāsi; maṃ sau dūsaitukāmau| sacāhaṃ gamissāmi vissarau mai bhavissatī’’ti, antaivāsiniṃ bhikkhuniṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha mai piṇḍapātaṃ nīhara| yau cai maṃ pucchati, ‘gilānā’ti paṭivaidaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti khau sā bhikkhunī sundarīnandāya bhikkhuniyā pac‍cassausi|

taina khau pana samayaina sāḷhau migāranattā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitau hauti sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ paṭipucchantau – ‘‘kahaṃ, ayyai, ayyā sundarīnandā? kahaṃ, ayyai, ayyā sundarīnandā’’ti? aivaṃ  vuttai sundarīnandāya bhikkhuniyā antaivāsinī bhikkhunī sāḷhaṃ migāranattāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘gilānāvusau; piṇḍapātaṃ nīharissāmī’’ti| atha khau sāḷhau migāranattā – ‘‘yampāhaṃ atthāya yaṃpāhaṃ (syā॰) bhikkhunisaṅghassa bhattaṃ akāsiṃ ayyāya sundarīnandāya kāraṇā’’ti manussai āṇāpaitvā – ‘‘bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ bhattaina parivisathā’’ti vatvā yaina bhikkhunupassayau tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina sundarīnandā bhikkhunī bahārāmakauṭṭhakai ṭhitā hauti sāḷhaṃ migāranattāraṃ patimānaintī| addasā khau sundarīnandā bhikkhunī sāḷhaṃ migāranattāraṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna upassayaṃ pavisitvā sasīsaṃ pārupitvā mañ‍cakai nipaj‍ji| atha khau sāḷhau migāranattā yaina sundarīnandā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aphāsu, kissa nipan‍nāsī’’ti? ‘‘aivañhaitaṃ, āvusau, hauti yā anicchantaṃ icchatī’’ti| ‘‘kyāhaṃ taṃ, ayyai, na icchissāmi? api cāhaṃ aukāsaṃ na labhāmi taṃ dūsaitu’’nti| avassutau avassutāya sundarīnandāya bhikkhuniyā kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī jarādubbalā caraṇagilānā sundarīnandāya bhikkhuniyā avidūrai nipan‍nā hauti| addasā khau sā bhikkhunī  sāḷhaṃ migāranattāraṃ avassutaṃ avassutāya sundarīnandāya bhikkhuniyā kāyasaṃsaggaṃ  samāpaj‍jantaṃ| disvāna ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā sundarīnandā avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyissatī’’ti! atha khau sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā santuṭṭhā laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā sundarīnandā avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyissatī’’ti! atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā santuṭṭhā laj‍jinau kuk‍kuc‍cakā sikkhākāmā tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma sundarīnandā bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyissatī’’ti!

atha khau tai bhikkhū sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, sundarīnandā bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyatī’’ti sādiyīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, bhikkhavai, sundarīnandāya bhikkhuniyā ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, sundarīnandā bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya pasan‍nānaṃ vā bhiyyaubhāvāya| atha khvaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānañ‍caiva appasādāya pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya duppausatāya mahicchatāya asantuṭṭhitāya asantuṭṭhatāya (syā॰), asantuṭṭhiyā (ka॰) saṅgaṇikāya kausaj‍jassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitvā, anaikapariyāyaina subharatāya supausatāya appicchassa santuṭṭhassa santuṭṭhiyā (ka॰) sal‍laikhassa dhutassa pāsādikassa apacayassa vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitvā, bhikkhūnaṃ tadanucchavikaṃ tadanulaumikaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi dasa atthavasai  paṭic‍ca – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya, dummaṅkūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya, appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya, saddhammaṭṭhitiyā vinayānuggahāya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

657. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ āmasanaṃ vā parāmasanaṃ vā gahaṇaṃ vā chupanaṃ vā paṭipīḷanaṃ vā sādiyaiyya, ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalikā’’ti|

658. yā  panāti yā yādisā yathāyuttā yathājac‍cā yathānāmā yathāgauttā yathāsīlā yathāvihārinī yathāgaucarā thairā vā navā vā majjhimā vā, aisā vuc‍cati yā panāti|
