bhikkhunīti bhikkhikāti bhikkhunī; bhikkhācariyaṃ ajjhupagatāti bhikkhunī; bhin‍napaṭadharāti bhikkhunī; samañ‍ñāya bhikkhunī; paṭiñ‍ñāya bhikkhunī; aihi bhikkhunīti bhikkhunī; tīhi saraṇagamanaihi upasampan‍nāti bhikkhunī; bhadrā bhikkhunī; sārā bhikkhunī; saikhā bhikkhunī; asaikhā bhikkhunī; samaggaina ubhatausaṅghaina ñatticatutthaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina upasampan‍nāti bhikkhunī| tatra yāyaṃ bhikkhunī samaggaina ubhatausaṅghaina ñatticatutthaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina upasampan‍nā, ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

avassutā nāma sārattā apaikkhavatī paṭibaddhacittā|

avassutau nāma sārattau apaikkhavā paṭibaddhacittau|

purisapuggalau nāma manussapurisau na yakkhau na paitau na tiracchānagatau viñ‍ñū paṭibalau kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jituṃ|

adhakkhakanti haiṭṭhakkhakaṃ|

ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalanti uparijāṇumaṇḍalaṃ|

āmasanaṃ  nāma āmaṭṭhamattaṃ|

parāmasanaṃ nāma itaucitau ca sañ‍caupanaṃ|

gahaṇaṃ nāma gahitamattaṃ|

chupanaṃ nāma phuṭṭhamattaṃ|

paṭipīḷanaṃ  vā sādiyaiyyāti aṅgaṃ gahaitvā nippīḷanaṃ sādiyati|

ayampīti purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

pārājikā hautīti saiyyathāpi nāma purisau sīsacchin‍nau abhabbau taina sarīrabandhanaina jīvituṃ, aivamaiva bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ  āmasanaṃ vā parāmasanaṃ vā gahaṇaṃ vā chupanaṃ vā paṭipīḷanaṃ vā sādiyantī assamaṇī hauti asakyadhītā| taina vuc‍cati pārājikā hautīti|

asaṃvāsāti saṃvāsau nāma aikakammaṃ aikuddaisau samasikkhatā, aisau saṃvāsau nāma| sau tāya saddhiṃ natthi, taina vuc‍cati asaṃvāsāti|

659. ubhatauavassutai adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti pārājikassa| kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

ubbhakkhakaṃ adhaujāṇumaṇḍalaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ  āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

660. aikatauavassutai adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

ubbhakkhakaṃ adhaujāṇumaṇḍalaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

661. ubhatauavassutai yakkhassa vā paitassa vā paṇḍakassa vā tiracchānagatamanussaviggahassa vā adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina  nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

ubbhakkhakaṃ  adhaujāṇumaṇḍalaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

662. aikatauavassutai  adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

ubbhakkhakaṃ adhaujāṇumaṇḍalaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ  āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

663. anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantiyā, asādiyantiyā, ummattikāya, khittacittāya, vaidanāṭṭāya, ādikammikāyāti|

paṭhamapārājikaṃ samattaṃ bhikkhunivibhaṅgai sikkhāpadagaṇanā bhikkhūhi§asādhāraṇavasaina pakāsitāti vaiditabbā|

2. dutiyapārājikaṃ

664. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sundarīnandā bhikkhunī sāḷhaina migāranattunā gabbhinī hauti| yāva gabbhau taruṇau ahausi tāva chādaisi| paripak‍kai gabbhai vibbhamitvā vijāyi| bhikkhuniyau thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sundarīnandā khau, ayyai, aciravibbhantā vijātā| kac‍ci nau sā bhikkhunīyaiva samānā gabbhinī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti| ‘‘kissa pana tvaṃ, ayyai, jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ naivattanā paṭicaudaisi na gaṇassa āraucaisī’’ti? ‘‘yau aitissā avaṇṇau mayhaisau avaṇṇau, yā aitissā akitti mayhaisā akitti, yau aitissā ayasau mayhaisau ayasau, yau aitissā alābhau mayhaisau  alābhau| kyāhaṃ, ayyai, attanau avaṇṇaṃ attanau akittiṃ attanau ayasaṃ attanau alābhaṃ paraisaṃ āraucaissāmī’’ti? yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ naivattanā paṭicaudaissati na gaṇassa āraucaissatī’’ti! atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū yai tai bhikkhū…pai॰… (?) bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā  bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ naivattanā paṭicaudaiti paṭicaudaisi… āraucaisīti (ka॰) na gaṇassa āraucaitī’’ti paṭicaudaisi… āraucaisīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ naivattanā paṭicaudaissati na gaṇassa āraucaissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

665. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ naivattanā paṭicaudaiyya na gaṇassa āraucaiyya, yadā ca sā ṭhitā vā assa cutā vā nāsitā vā avassaṭā vā, sā pacchā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘pubbaivāhaṃ, ayyai, añ‍ñāsiṃ aitaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aivarūpā ca aivarūpā ca sā bhaginīti, nau ca khau attanā paṭicaudaissaṃ na gaṇassa āraucaissa’nti, ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā’’ti|

666. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

jānāti nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti, añ‍ñai  vā tassā āraucainti, sā vā āraucaiti|

pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nanti aṭṭhan‍naṃ pārājikānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ|

naivattanā  paṭicaudaiyyāti na sayaṃ caudaiyya|

na gaṇassa āraucaiyyāti na añ‍ñāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ āraucaiyya|

yadā  ca sā ṭhitā vā assa cutā vāti ṭhitā nāma saliṅgai ṭhitā vuc‍cati| cutā nāma kālaṅkatā vuc‍cati| nāsitā nāma sayaṃ vā vibbhantā hauti añ‍ñaihi vā nāsitā| avassaṭā nāma titthāyatanaṃ saṅkantā vuc‍cati| sā pacchā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘pubbaivāhaṃ, ayyai, añ‍ñāsiṃ aitaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aivarūpā ca aivarūpā ca sā bhaginī’’ti|

nau ca khau attanā paṭicaudaissanti sayaṃ vā na caudaissaṃ|

na gaṇassa āraucaissanti na añ‍ñāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ āraucaissaṃ|

ayampīti purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

pārājikā hautīti saiyyathāpi nāma paṇḍupalāsau bandhanā pamuttau abhabbau haritatthāya, aivamaiva bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ naivattanā paṭicaudaissāmi na gaṇassa āraucaissāmīti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai assamaṇī hauti asakyadhītā| taina vuc‍cati pārājikā  hautīti|

asaṃvāsāti saṃvāsau nāma aikakammaṃ aikuddaisau samasikkhatā| aisau saṃvāsau nāma| sau tāya saddhiṃ natthi| taina vuc‍cati asaṃvāsāti|

667. anāpatti ‘‘saṅghassa bhaṇḍanaṃ vā kalahau vā viggahau vā vivādau vā bhavissatī’’ti nāraucaiti, ‘‘saṅghabhaidau vā saṅgharāji vā bhavissatī’’ti nāraucaiti, ‘‘ayaṃ kakkhaḷā pharusā jīvitantarāyaṃ vā brahmacariyantarāyaṃ vā karissatī’’ti nāraucaiti, añ‍ñā patirūpā bhikkhuniyau apassantī nāraucaiti, nacchādaitukāmā nāraucaiti, pañ‍ñāyissati sakaina kammaināti nāraucaiti, ummattikāya…pai॰… ādikammikāyāti|

dutiyapārājikaṃ samattaṃ|

3. tatiyapārājikaṃ

668. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ anuvattati| yā  tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ anuvattissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ anuvattatīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ anuvattissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

669. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anādaraṃ appaṭikāraṃ akatasahāyaṃ tamanuvattaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā – ‘aisau khau, ayyai, bhikkhu samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittau dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anādarau appaṭikārau akatasahāyau, māyyai, aitaṃ bhikkhuṃ anuvattī’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ  samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyaṃ cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā ukkhittānuvattikā’’ti|

670. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

samaggau nāma saṅghau samānasaṃvāsakau samānasīmāyaṃ ṭhitau|

ukkhittau nāma āpattiyā adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā appaṭinissaggai vā adassanaina vā appaṭikammaina vā appaṭinissaggaina vā (ka॰), adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai vā (?) ukkhittau|

dhammaina vinayaināti yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina|

satthusāsanaināti  jinasāsanaina buddhasāsanaina|

anādarau nāma saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā puggalaṃ vā kammaṃ vā nādiyati|

appaṭikārau nāma ukkhittau anausāritau|

akatasahāyau  nāma samānasaṃvāsakā bhikkhū vuc‍canti sahāyā| sau taihi saddhiṃ natthi, taina vuc‍cati akatasahāyauti|

tamanuvattaiyyāti  yaṃdiṭṭhikau sau hauti yaṃkhantikau yaṃrucikau, sāpi taṃdiṭṭhikā hauti taṃkhantikā taṃrucikā|

sā bhikkhunīti yā sā ukkhittānuvattikā bhikkhunī|

bhikkhunīhīti añ‍ñāhi bhikkhunīhi| yā passanti yā suṇanti tāhi vattabbā – ‘‘aisau khau, ayyai, bhikkhu samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittau dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anādarau appaṭikārau akatasahāyau| māyyai, aitaṃ bhikkhuṃ  anuvattī’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sutvā na vadanti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sā bhikkhunī saṅghamajjhampi ākaḍḍhitvā vattabbā – ‘‘aisau khau, ayyai, bhikkhu samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittau dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anādarau appaṭikārau akatasahāyau| māyyai, aitaṃ bhikkhuṃ anuvattī’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sā bhikkhunī samanubhāsitabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samanubhāsitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

671. ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anādaraṃ appaṭikāraṃ akatasahāyaṃ tamanuvattati, sā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samanubhāsaiyya tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anādaraṃ appaṭikāraṃ akatasahāyaṃ tamanuvattati| sā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samanubhāsati tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā samanubhāsanā tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya, sā tuṇhassa; yassā  nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘samanubhaṭṭhā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

ñattiyā  duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā, kammavācā pariyausānai āpatti pārājikassa|

ayampīti purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

pārājikā hautīti saiyyathāpi nāma puthusilā dvaidhā bhin‍nā appaṭisandhikā hauti, aivamaiva bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya  na paṭinissaj‍jantī assamaṇī hauti asakyadhītā| taina vuc‍cati pārājikā hautīti|

asaṃvāsāti saṃvāsau nāma aikakammaṃ aikuddaisau samasikkhatā| aisau saṃvāsau nāma| sau tāya saddhiṃ natthi| taina vuc‍cati asaṃvāsāti|

672. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pārājikassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

673. anāpatti  asamanubhāsantiyā, paṭinissaj‍jantiyā, ummattikāya…pai॰… ādikammikāyāti|

tatiyapārājikaṃ samattaṃ|

4. catutthapārājikaṃ

674. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthaggahaṇampi sādiyanti, saṅghāṭikaṇṇaggahaṇampi sādiyanti, santiṭṭhantipi, sal‍lapantipi, saṅkaitampi gacchanti, purisassapi abbhāgamanaṃ sādiyanti, chan‍nampi anupavisanti, kāyampi tadatthāya upasaṃharanti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi  nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthaggahaṇampi sādiyissanti, saṅghāṭikaṇṇaggahaṇampi sādiyissanti, santiṭṭhissantipi, sal‍lapissantipi, saṅkaitampi gacchissanti, purisassapi abbhāgamanaṃ sādiyissanti, chan‍nampi anupavisissanti, kāyampi tadatthāya upasaṃharissanti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāyā’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthaggahaṇampi sādiyanti, saṅghāṭikaṇṇaggahaṇampi sādiyanti, santiṭṭhantipi, sal‍lapantipi, saṅkaitampi gacchanti, purisassapi abbhāgamanaṃ sādiyanti, chan‍nampi anupavisanti, kāyampi tadatthāya upasaṃharanti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāyāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi  buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthaggahaṇampi sādiyissanti, saṅghāṭikaṇṇaggahaṇampi sādiyissanti, santiṭṭhissantipi, sal‍lapissantipi, saṅkaitampi gacchissanti, purisassapi abbhāgamanaṃ sādiyissanti, chan‍nampi anupavisissanti, kāyampi tadatthāya upasaṃharissanti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

675. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthaggahaṇaṃ vā sādiyaiyya, saṅghāṭikaṇṇaggahaṇaṃ vā sādiyaiyya, santiṭṭhaiyya vā, sal‍lapaiyya vā, saṅkaitaṃ vā  gacchaiyya, purisassa vā abbhāgamanaṃ sādiyaiyya, chan‍naṃ vā anupavisaiyya, kāyaṃ vā tadatthāya upasaṃharaiyya aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya, ayampi pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā aṭṭhavatthukā’’ti|

676. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

avassutā nāma sārattā apaikkhavatī paṭibaddhacittā|

avassutau nāma sārattau apaikkhavā paṭibaddhacittau|

purisapuggalau nāma manussapurisau, na yakkhau na paitau na tiracchānagatau viñ‍ñū paṭibalau kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jituṃ|

hatthaggahaṇaṃ  vā sādiyaiyyāti hatthau nāma kapparaṃ upādāya yāva agganakhā| aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya ubbhakkhakaṃ adhaujāṇumaṇḍalaṃ gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

saṅghāṭikaṇṇaggahaṇaṃ vā sādiyaiyyāti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya nivatthaṃ vā pārutaṃ vā gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

santiṭṭhaiyya vāti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya purisassa hatthapāsai tiṭṭhati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

sal‍lapaiyya vāti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya purisassa hatthapāsai ṭhitā sal‍lapati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

saṅkaitaṃ vā gacchaiyyāti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya purisaina – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ aukāsaṃ idaṃ padaṃ aṭṭhakathāyaṃ na dissati āgacchā’’ti – vuttā gacchati| padai padai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| purisassa hatthapāsaṃ auk‍kantamattai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

purisassa  vā abbhāgamanaṃ sādiyaiyyāti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya purisassa abbhāgamanaṃ sādiyati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| hatthapāsaṃ auk‍kantamattai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

chan‍naṃ vā anupavisaiyyāti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya yaina kainaci paṭicchan‍naṃ aukāsaṃ paviṭṭhamattai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

kāyaṃ vā tadatthāya upasaṃharaiyyāti aitassa asaddhammassa paṭisaivanatthāya purisassa hatthapāsai ṭhitā kāyaṃ upasaṃharati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

ayampīti purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

pārājikā hautīti saiyyathāpi  nāma tālau matthakacchin‍nau abhabbau puna viruḷhiyā aivamaiva bhikkhunī aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ  paripūraintī assamaṇī hauti asakyadhītā| taina vuc‍cati pārājikā hautīti|

asaṃvāsāti  saṃvāsau nāma aikakammaṃ aikuddaisau samasikkhatā| aisau saṃvāsau nāma| sau tāya saddhiṃ natthi| taina vuc‍cati asaṃvāsāti|

677. anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantiyā, asādiyantiyā, ummattikāya, khittacittāya, vaidanāṭṭāya, ādikammikāyāti|

catutthapārājikaṃ samattaṃ|

uddiṭṭhā khau, ayyāyau, aṭṭha pārājikā dhammā| yaisaṃ bhikkhunī añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā āpaj‍jitvā na labhati bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ, yathā purai tathā pacchā, pārājikā hauti asaṃvāsā| tatthāyyāyau pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

bhikkhunivibhaṅgai pārājikakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



2. saṅghādisaisakaṇḍaṃ (bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau)

1. paṭhamasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ

imai khau panāyyāyau sattarasa saṅghādisaisā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

678. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau upāsakau bhikkhunisaṅghassa udausitaṃ uddausitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) datvā kālaṅkatau hauti| tassa dvai puttā haunti – aikau assaddhau appasan‍nau, aikau saddhau pasan‍nau| tai paittikaṃ sāpataiyyaṃ vibhajiṃsu| atha khau sau assaddhau appasan‍nau taṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘amhākaṃ udausitau, taṃ bhājaimā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai sau saddhau pasan‍nau taṃ assaddhaṃ appasan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘māyyau, aivaṃ avaca| amhākaṃ pitunā bhikkhunisaṅghassa din‍nau’’ti| dutiyampi khau sau assaddhau appasan‍nau taṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘amhākaṃ udausitau, taṃ bhājaimā’’ti| atha khau sau saddhau pasan‍nau taṃ assaddhaṃ appasan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘māyyau, aivaṃ avaca| amhākaṃ pitunā bhikkhunisaṅghassa din‍nau’’ti| tatiyampi khau sau assaddhau appasan‍nau taṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘amhākaṃ udausitau, taṃ bhājaimā’’ti| atha khau sau saddhau pasan‍nau – ‘‘sacai mayhaṃ  bhavissati, ahampi bhikkhunisaṅghassa dassāmī’’ti – taṃ assaddhaṃ appasan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhājaimā’’ti| atha khau sau udausitau taihi bhājīyamānau tassa assaddhassa appasan‍nassa pāpuṇāti pāpuṇi (syā॰)| atha khau sau assaddhau appasan‍nau bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘nikkhamathāyyai, amhākaṃ udausitau’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘māyyau, aivaṃ avaca, tumhākaṃ pitunā bhikkhunisaṅghassa din‍nau’’ti| ‘‘din‍nau na din‍nau’’ti vauhārikai mahāmattai pucchiṃsu| mahāmattā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kau, ayyai, jānāti bhikkhunisaṅghassa din‍nau’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī tai mahāmattai aitadavauca  – ‘‘api nāyyau api nvayyā (syā॰), api nāyyau (ka॰) tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā sakkhiṃ ṭhapayitvā dānaṃ diyyamāna’’nti? atha khau tai mahāmattā – ‘‘sac‍caṃ khau ayyā āhā’’ti taṃ udausitaṃ bhikkhunisaṅghassa akaṃsu| atha khau sau purisau parājitau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘assamaṇiyau imā muṇḍā bandhakiniyau| kathañhi nāma amhākaṃ udausitaṃ acchindāpaissantī’’ti! thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī mahāmattānaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| mahāmattā taṃ purisaṃ daṇḍāpaisuṃ| atha khau sau purisau daṇḍitau bhikkhunūpassayassa avidūrai ājīvakasaiyyaṃ kārāpaitvā ājīvakai uyyaujaisi – ‘‘aitā bhikkhuniyau ac‍cāvadathā’’ti|

thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī mahāmattānaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| mahāmattā taṃ purisaṃ bandhāpaisuṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘paṭhamaṃ bhikkhuniyau udausitaṃ acchindāpaisuṃ, dutiyaṃ daṇḍāpaisuṃ, tatiyaṃ bandhāpaisuṃ| idāni ghātāpaissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā ussayavādikā viharissatī’’ti! atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī ussayavādikā viharatīti| ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī ussayavādikā viharissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

679. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī ussayavādikā viharaiyya gahapatinā vā gahapatiputtaina vā dāsaina vā kammakāraina kammakaraina (sī॰ syā॰) vā antamasau samaṇaparibbājakaināpi, ayaṃ bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

680. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

ussayavādikā nāma aḍḍakārikā vuc‍cati|

gahapati  nāma yau kauci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

gahapatiputtau  nāma yai kaici puttabhātarau|

dāsau nāma antaujātau dhanak‍kītau karamarānītau|

kammakārau nāma bhaṭakau āhatakau|

samaṇaparibbājakau nāma bhikkhuñ‍ca bhikkhuniñ‍ca sikkhamānañ‍ca sāmaṇairañ‍ca sāmaṇairiñ‍ca ṭhapaitvā yau kauci paribbājakasamāpan‍nau|

aḍḍaṃ  karissāmīti dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aikassa āraucaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dutiyassa āraucaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| aḍḍapariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

paṭhamāpattikanti saha vatthujjhācārā āpaj‍jati asamanubhāsanāya|

nissāraṇīyanti saṅghamhā nissārīyati|

saṅghādisaisanti saṅghauva tassā āpattiyā mānattaṃ daiti mūlāya paṭikassati abbhaiti, na sambahulā na aikā bhikkhunī| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅghādisaisau’’ti| tassaiva āpattinikāyassa nāmakammaṃ adhivacanaṃ| tainapi vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

681. anāpatti manussaihi ākaḍḍhīyamānā gacchati, ārakkhaṃ yācati, anaudissa ācikkhati, ummattikāya…pai॰… ādikammikāyāti|

paṭhamasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ

682. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina vaisāliyaṃ añ‍ñatarassa licchavissa pajāpati aticārinī hauti| atha khau sau licchavi  taṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu viramāhi, anatthaṃ khau tai karissāmī’’ti| aivampi sā vuc‍camānā nādiyi| taina khau pana samayaina vaisāliyaṃ licchavigaṇau san‍nipatitau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau sau licchavi tai licchavayau aitadavauca – ‘‘aikaṃ mai, ayyau, itthiṃ anujānāthā’’ti| ‘‘kā nāma sā’’ti? ‘‘mayhaṃ pajāpati aticarati, taṃ ghātaissāmī’’ti| ‘‘jānāhī’’ti| assausi khau sā itthī – ‘‘sāmikau kira maṃ ghātaitukāmau’’ti| varabhaṇḍaṃ ādāya sāvatthiṃ gantvā titthiyai upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| titthiyā na icchiṃsu pabbājaituṃ| bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| bhikkhuniyaupi na icchiṃsu pabbājaituṃ| thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhaṇḍakaṃ dassaitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhaṇḍakaṃ gahaitvā pabbājaisi|

atha khau sau licchavi taṃ itthiṃ gavaisantau sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhunīsu pabbajitaṃ disvāna yaina rājā pasainadi kausalau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pajāpati mai, daiva, varabhaṇḍaṃ ādāya sāvatthiṃ anuppattā| taṃ daivau anujānātū’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai, vicinitvā ācikkhā’’ti| ‘‘diṭṭhā, daiva, bhikkhunīsu pabbajitā’’ti| ‘‘sacai, bhaṇai, bhikkhunīsu pabbajitā, na sā labbhā kiñ‍ci kātuṃ| svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, caratu brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| atha khau sau licchavi ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti  – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau cauriṃ pabbājaissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau tassa licchavissa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā cauriṃ pabbājaissatī’’ti! atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī cauriṃ pabbājaitīti pabbājaisīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī cauriṃ pabbājaissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

683. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ cauriṃ vajjhaṃ viditaṃ anapalaukaitvā rājānaṃ vā saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā pūgaṃ vā saiṇiṃ vā añ‍ñatra kappā vuṭṭhāpaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

684. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

jānāti  nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti añ‍ñai vā tassā āraucainti, sā vā āraucaiti|

caurī nāma yā pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati, aisā caurī nāma|

vajjhā nāma yaṃ katvā vajjhappattā hauti|

viditā nāma añ‍ñaihi manussaihi ñātā hauti  ‘‘vajjhā aisā’’ti|

anapalaukaitvāti anāpucchā|

rājā nāma yattha rājā anusāsati, rājā apalaukaitabbau|

saṅghau nāma bhikkhunisaṅghau vuc‍cati, bhikkhunisaṅghau apalaukaitabbau|

gaṇau nāma yattha gaṇau anusāsati, gaṇau apalaukaitabbau|

pūgau nāma yattha pūgau anusāsati, pūgau apalaukaitabbau|

saiṇi nāma yattha saiṇi anusāsati, saiṇi apalaukaitabbau|

añ‍ñatra  kappāti ṭhapaitvā kappaṃ| kappaṃ nāma dvai kappāni – titthiyaisu vā pabbajitā hauti añ‍ñāsu vā bhikkhunīsu pabbajitā| añ‍ñatra kappā ‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ  vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

ayampīti purimaṃ upādāya vuc‍cati|

paṭhamāpattikanti saha vatthujjhācārā āpaj‍jati asamanubhāsanāya|

nissāraṇīyanti saṅghamhā nissārīyati|

saṅghādisaisanti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

685. cauriyā caurisañ‍ñā añ‍ñatra kappā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| cauriyā vaimatikā añ‍ñatra kappā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa| cauriyā acaurisañ‍ñā añ‍ñatra kappā  vuṭṭhāpaiti, anāpatti| acauriyā caurisañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| acauriyā vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| acauriyā acaurisañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

686. anāpatti ajānantī vuṭṭhāpaiti, apalaukaitvā vuṭṭhāpaiti, kappakataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dutiyasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. tatiyasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ

687. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhaddāya kāpilāniyā antaivāsinī bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍitvā gāmakaṃ gāmakai (syā॰) ñātikulaṃ agamāsi| bhaddā kāpilānī taṃ bhikkhuniṃ apassantī bhikkhuniyau pucchi – ‘‘kahaṃ itthan‍nāmā, na dissatī’’ti! ‘‘bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ, ayyai, bhaṇḍitvā na dissatī’’ti| ‘‘ammā, amukasmiṃ gāmakai aitissā ñātikulaṃ| tattha gantvā vicinathā’’ti| bhikkhuniyau tattha gantvā taṃ bhikkhuniṃ passitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, ayyai, aikikā āgatā, kac‍cisi appadhaṃsitā’’ti? ‘‘appadhaṃsitāmhi, ayyai’’ti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī aikā gāmantaraṃ gacchissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aikā gāmantaraṃ gacchatīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi  buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aikā gāmantaraṃ gacchissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī aikā gāmantaraṃ gacchaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

688. taina  khau pana samayaina dvai bhikkhuniyau sākaitā sāvatthiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| antarāmaggai nadī taritabbā hauti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau nāvikai upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sādhu nau, āvusau, tāraithā’’ti| ‘‘nāyyai, sak‍kā ubhau sakiṃ tāraitu’’nti| aikau aikaṃ uttāraisi| uttiṇṇau uttiṇṇaṃ dūsaisi| anuttiṇṇau anuttiṇṇaṃ dūsaisi| tā pacchā samāgantvā pucchiṃsu – ‘‘kac‍cisi, ayyai, appadhaṃsitā’’ti? ‘‘padhaṃsitāmhi, ayyai! tvaṃ pana, ayyai, appadhaṃsitā’’ti? ‘‘padhaṃsitāmhi, ayyai’’ti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī aikā nadīpāraṃ gacchissatī’’ti! atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aikā nadīpāraṃ gacchatī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aikā nadīpāraṃ gacchissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī aikā vā gāmantaraṃ gacchaiyya, aikā vā nadīpāraṃ gacchaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

689. taina  khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhuniyau kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ gacchantā gantvā (ka॰) sāyaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ upagacchiṃsu| tattha añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī abhirūpā hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā| añ‍ñatarau purisau tassā bhikkhuniyā saha dassanaina paṭibaddhacittau hauti| atha khau sau purisau tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ saiyyaṃ pañ‍ñapaintau tassā bhikkhuniyā saiyyaṃ  aikamantaṃ pañ‍ñāpaisi| atha khau sā bhikkhunī sal‍lakkhaitvā – ‘‘pariyuṭṭhitau ayaṃ purisau; sacai rattiṃ āgacchissati, vissarau mai bhavissatī’’ti, bhikkhuniyau anāpucchā añ‍ñataraṃ kulaṃ gantvā saiyyaṃ kappaisi| atha khau sau purisau rattiṃ  āgantvā taṃ bhikkhuniṃ gavaisantau bhikkhuniyau ghaṭṭaisi| bhikkhuniyau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ apassantiyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau sā bhikkhunī purisaina saddhiṃ nikkhantā’’ti|

atha khau sā bhikkhunī tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina tā bhikkhuniyau tainupasaṅkami| bhikkhuniyau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, ayyai, purisaina saddhiṃ nikkhantā’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, ayyai, purisaina saddhiṃ nikkhantā’’ti| bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī aikā rattiṃ vippavasissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aikā rattiṃ vippavasīti vippavasīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aikā rattiṃ vippavasissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī aikā vā gāmantaraṃ gacchaiyya, aikā vā nadīpāraṃ gacchaiyya, aikā vā rattiṃ vippavasaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

690. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhuniyau kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| tattha añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī vac‍caina pīḷitā aikikā auhīyitvā auhiyitvā (ka॰) pacchā agamāsi| manussā taṃ bhikkhuniṃ passitvā dūsaisuṃ| atha khau sā bhikkhunī yaina tā bhikkhuniyau tainupasaṅkami| bhikkhuniyau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, ayyai, aikikā auhīnā, kac‍cisi appadhaṃsitā’’ti? ‘‘padhaṃsitāmhi, ayyai’’ti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – kathañhi  nāma bhikkhunī aikā gaṇamhā auhīyissatīti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aikā gaṇamhā auhīyatīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aikā gaṇamhā auhīyissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

691. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī aikā vā gāmantaraṃ gacchaiyya, aikā vā nadīpāraṃ gacchaiyya, aikā vā rattiṃ vippavasaiyya, aikā vā gaṇamhā auhīyaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ  dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

692. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

aikā vā gāmantaraṃ gacchaiyyāti parikkhittassa gāmassa parikkhaipaṃ paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa, dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aparikkhittassa gāmassa upacāraṃ paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikā vā nadīpāraṃ gacchaiyyāti nadī nāma timaṇḍalaṃ paṭicchādaitvā yattha katthaci uttarantiyā bhikkhuniyā antaravāsakau taimiyati| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ uttarantiyā āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| dutiyaṃ pādaṃ uttarantiyā āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikā vā rattiṃ vippavasaiyyāti saha aruṇuggamanā dutiyikāya bhikkhuniyā hatthapāsaṃ vijahantiyā āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| vijahitai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikā vā gaṇamhā auhīyaiyyāti agāmakai arañ‍ñai dutiyikāya bhikkhuniyā dassanūpacāraṃ vā  savanūpacāraṃ vā vijahantiyā āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| vijahitai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

ayampīti  purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

paṭhamāpattikanti saha vatthujjhācārā āpaj‍jati asamanubhāsanāya|

nissāraṇīyanti saṅghamhā nissārīyati|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

693. anāpatti dutiyikā bhikkhunī pak‍kantā vā hauti vibbhantā vā kālaṅkatā vā pakkhasaṅkantā vā, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

tatiyasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. catutthasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ

694. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī bhaṇḍanakārikā hauti kalahakārikā vivādakārikā bhassakārikā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārikā| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī tassā kammai karīyamānai paṭik‍kausati| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī gāmakaṃ agamāsi kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau bhikkhunisaṅghau – ‘‘thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī pak‍kantā’’ti, caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ āpattiyā adassanai adassanaina (ka॰) ukkhipi| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī gāmakai taṃ karaṇīyaṃ tīraitvā punadaiva sāvatthiṃ pac‍cāgacchi| caṇḍakāḷī  bhikkhunī thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā āgacchantiyā naiva āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi na pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ pādakaṭhalikaṃ upanikkhipi na pac‍cuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisi na pānīyaina āpucchi| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, ayyai, mayi āgacchantiyā naiva āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi na pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ pādakaṭhalikaṃ upanikkhipi na pac‍cuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisi na pānīyaina āpucchī’’ti? ‘‘aivañhaitaṃ, ayyai, hauti yathā taṃ anāthāyā’’ti| ‘‘kissa pana tvaṃ, ayyai, anāthā’’ti? ‘‘imā maṃ, ayyai, bhikkhuniyau – ‘‘ayaṃ anāthā appañ‍ñātā, natthi imissā kāci paṭivattā’’ti, āpattiyā adassanai adassanaina (ka॰) ukkhipiṃsū’’ti|

thul‍lanandā  bhikkhunī – ‘‘bālā aitā abyattā aitā, naiva jānanti kammaṃ vā kammadausaṃ vā kammavipattiṃ vā kammasampattiṃ vā| mayaṃ khau jānāma kammampi kammadausampi kammavipattimpi kammasampattimpi| mayaṃ khau akataṃ vā kammaṃ kāraiyyāma kataṃ vā kammaṃ kaupaiyyāmā’’ti, lahuṃ lahuṃ bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ san‍nipātaitvā caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ausāraisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā kārakasaṅghaṃ anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ ausāraissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā  kārakasaṅghaṃ anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ ausāraitīti ausāraisīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā kārakasaṅghaṃ anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ ausāraissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

695. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā kārakasaṅghaṃ anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ ausāraiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

696. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

samaggau nāma saṅghau samānasaṃvāsakau samānasīmāyaṃ ṭhitau|

ukkhittā  nāma āpattiyā adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā appaṭinissaggai vā adassanaina vā appaṭikammaina vā appaṭinissaggaina vā (ka॰), adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai vā (?) ukkhittā|

dhammaina vinayaināti yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina|

satthusāsanaināti jinasāsanaina buddhasāsanaina|

anapalaukaitvā  kārakasaṅghanti kammakārakasaṅghaṃ anāpucchā|

anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandanti gaṇassa chandaṃ ajānitvā|

‘‘ausāraissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā, kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

ayampīti purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

paṭhamāpattikanti saha vatthujjhācārā āpaj‍jati asamanubhāsanāya|

nissāraṇīyanti saṅghamhā nissārīyati|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

697. dhammakammai  dhammakammasañ‍ñā ausāraiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā ausāraiti āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā ausāraiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

698. anāpatti kammakārakasaṅghaṃ apalaukaitvā ausāraiti, gaṇassa chandaṃ jānitvā ausāraiti, vattai vattantiṃ ausāraiti, asantai kammakārakasaṅghai ausāraiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

catutthasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. pañ‍camasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ

699. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sundarīnandā bhikkhunī abhirūpā hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā| manussā bhattaggai sundarīnandaṃ  bhikkhuniṃ passitvā avassutā avassutāya sundarīnandāya bhikkhuniyā aggamaggāni bhaujanāni dainti| sundarīnandā bhikkhunī yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jati; añ‍ñā bhikkhuniyau na cittarūpaṃ labhanti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā sundarīnandā avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ  vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādissati bhuñ‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, sundarīnandā bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādati bhuñ‍jatīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, sundarīnandā bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādissati bhuñ‍jissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

700. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

701. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

avassutā nāma sārattā apaikkhavatī paṭibaddhacittā|

avassutau nāma sārattau apaikkhavā paṭibaddhacittau|

purisapuggalau  nāma manussapurisau, na yakkhau na paitau na tiracchānagatau, viñ‍ñū paṭibalau sāraj‍jituṃ|

khādanīyaṃ  nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – udakadantapaunaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ|

‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

ayampīti purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

paṭhamāpattikanti saha vatthujjhācārā āpaj‍jati asamanubhāsanāya|

nissāraṇīyanti saṅghamhā nissārīyati|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

udakadantapaunaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aikatauavassutai ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

702. ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| udakadantapaunaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ubhatauavassutai yakkhassa vā paitassa vā paṇḍakassa vā tiracchānagatamanussaviggahassa vā hatthatau ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai  āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| udakadantapaunaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aikatauavassutai ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| udakadantapaunaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

703. anāpatti  ubhatauanavassutā haunti, ‘‘anavassutau’’ti jānantī paṭiggaṇhāti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

pañ‍camasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. chaṭṭhasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ

704. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sundarīnandā bhikkhunī abhirūpā hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā| manussā bhattaggai sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ passitvā avassutā sundarīnandāya bhikkhuniyā aggamaggāni bhaujanāni dainti| sundarīnandā bhikkhunī kuk‍kuc‍cāyantī na paṭiggaṇhāti| anantarikā bhikkhunī sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, ayyai, na paṭiggaṇhāsī’’ti? ‘‘avassutā, ayyai’’ti| ‘‘tvaṃ pana, ayyai, avassutā’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, ayyai, avassutā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ anavassutā| iṅghaṃ, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā  bhaujanīyaṃ vā taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā, bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti|

yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī aivaṃ vakkhati – ‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ anavassutā| iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā yatau tvaṃ anavassutā! iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi  buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aivaṃ vakkhati – ‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā yatau tvaṃ anavassutā; iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

705. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ anavassutā| iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’ti, ayampi bhikkhunī paṭhamāpattikaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

706. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

aivaṃ vadaiyyāti – ‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ  anavassutā| iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti uyyaujaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tassā vacanaina ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai  āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| bhaujanapariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

ayampīti purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

paṭhamāpattikanti saha vatthujjhācārā āpaj‍jati asamanubhāsanāya|

nissāraṇīyanti saṅghamhā nissārīyati|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

udakadantapaunaṃ ‘‘paṭiggaṇhā’’ti uyyaujaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tassā vacanaina khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

707. aikatauavassutai  yakkhassa vā paitassa vā paṇḍakassa vā tiracchānagatamanussaviggahassa vā hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā ‘‘khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti uyyaujaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tassā vacanaina ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhaujanapariyausānai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| udakadantapaunaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti uyyaujaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tassā vacanaina ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

708. anāpatti ‘‘anavassutau’’ti jānantī uyyaujaiti, ‘‘kupitā na  paṭiggaṇhātī’’ti  uyyaujaiti, ‘‘kulānuddayatāya na paṭiggaṇhātī’’ti uyyaujaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

chaṭṭhasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. sattamasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ

709. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍitvā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, dhammaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, saṅghaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau, santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’’ti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā caṇḍakāḷī caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī (ka॰) kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vakkhati – buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau, santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau, santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā  sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ  vakkhati – ‘‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau, santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’’ti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

710. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, dhammaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, saṅghaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau! santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ santikai  brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’ti, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā – ‘māyyai, kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca – buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, dhammaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, saṅghaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau! santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmīti, abhiramāyyai, svākkhātau dhammau; cara brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsīyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

711. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

kupitā anattamanāti anabhiraddhā āhatacittā khilajātā|

aivaṃ vadaiyyāti – ‘‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau! santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’’ti|

sā  bhikkhunīti yā sā aivaṃvādinī bhikkhunī| bhikkhunīhīti añ‍ñāhi bhikkhunīhi|

yā passanti yā suṇanti tāhi vattabbā – ‘‘māyyai, kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca – ‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau! santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’ti| abhiramāyyai, svākkhātau dhammau; cara brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sutvā na vadanti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sā bhikkhunī saṅghamajjhampi ākaḍḍhitvā vattabbā – ‘‘māyyai, kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca – ‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva  samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau! santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’ti| abhiramāyyai, svākkhātau dhammau; cara brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sā bhikkhunī samanubhāsitabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samanubhāsitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

712. ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, dhammaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, saṅghaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau! santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’ti| sā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ  samanubhāsaiyya tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi| kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau! santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ  santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmī’ti| sā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samanubhāsati tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā samanubhāsanā tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati sā, bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘samanubhaṭṭhā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā| kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantiyā ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā paṭippassambhanti|

ayampīti  purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

yāvatatiyakanti yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya āpaj‍jati, na saha vatthujjhācārā|

nissāraṇīyanti saṅghamhā nissārīyati|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

713. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

714. anāpatti asamanubhāsantiyā, paṭinissaj‍jantiyā, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

sattamasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. aṭṭhamasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ

715. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘chandagāminiyau ca  bhikkhuniyau, dausagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, mauhagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’’ti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā caṇḍakāḷī kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vakkhati – chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā  aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī kismiñ‍cidaiva  adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vakkhati – ‘‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’’ti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

716. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, dausagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, mauhagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’ti, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā – ‘māyyai, kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca – chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau dausagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau mauhagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyauti| ayyā khau chandāpi gacchaiyya, dausāpi gacchaiyya, mauhāpi gacchaiyya, bhayāpi gacchaiyyā’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsīyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

717. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇaiti adhikaraṇaṃ nāma cattāri adhikaraṇāni – vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ|

pac‍cākatā nāma parājitā vuc‍cati|

kupitā anattamanāti  anabhiraddhā āhatacittā khilajātā|

aivaṃ vadaiyyāti – ‘‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’’ti|

sā  bhikkhunīti yā sā aivaṃvādinī bhikkhunī|

bhikkhunīhīti añ‍ñāhi bhikkhunīhi|

yā passanti yā suṇanti tāhi vattabbā – ‘‘māyyai, kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca – ‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’ti| ayyā khau chandāpi gacchaiyya…pai॰… bhayāpi gacchaiyyā’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sutvā na vadanti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sā bhikkhunī saṅghamajjhampi ākaḍḍhitvā vattabbā – ‘‘māyyai, kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca – ‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’ti| ayyā khau chandāpi gacchaiyya…pai॰… bhayāpi gacchaiyyā’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sā bhikkhunī samanubhāsitabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samanubhāsitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

718. ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘chandagāminiyau  ca bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’ti| sā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samanubhāsaiyya tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’ti| sā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samanubhāsati tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā samanubhāsanā tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘samanubhaṭṭhā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā| kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantiyā ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā paṭippassambhanti|

ayampīti purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

yāvatatiyakanti yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya āpaj‍jati, na saha vatthujjhācārā|

nissāraṇīyanti saṅghamhā nissārīyati|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati  saṅghādisaisauti|

719. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaissa| dhammakammai vaimatikā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

720. anāpatti asamanubhāsantiyā, paṭinissaj‍jantiyā, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aṭṭhamasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. navamasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ

721. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā antaivāsikā bhikkhuniyau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau saṃsaṭṭhā viharissanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā  añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikāti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau saṃsaṭṭhā viharissanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

722. ‘‘bhikkhuniyau panaiva saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīhi aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’ti| aivañ‍ca tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyyuṃ, tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsīyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyyuṃ, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyyuṃ, imāpi bhikkhuniyau yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

723. bhikkhuniyau panaivāti upasampan‍nāyau vuc‍canti|

saṃsaṭṭhā viharantīti saṃsaṭṭhā nāma ananulaumikaina kāyikavācasikaina saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti|

pāpācārāti  pāpakaina ācāraina saman‍nāgatā|

pāpasaddāti  pāpakaina kittisaddaina abbhuggatā|

pāpasilaukāti pāpakaina micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti|

bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikāti añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā kammai  karīyamānai paṭik‍kausanti|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikāti añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vaj‍jaṃ paṭicchādainti|

tā bhikkhuniyauti yā tā saṃsaṭṭhā bhikkhuniyau|

bhikkhunīhīti añ‍ñāhi bhikkhunīhi|

yā passanti yā suṇanti tāhi vattabbā – ‘‘bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍janti, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍janti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sutvā na vadanti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tā bhikkhuniyau saṅghamajjhampi ākaḍḍhitvā vattabbā – ‘‘bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍janti, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍janti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tā bhikkhuniyau samanubhāsitabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samanubhāsitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

724. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmā ca itthan‍nāmā ca bhikkhuniyau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| tā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍janti| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmañ‍ca itthan‍nāmañ‍ca bhikkhuniyau samanubhāsaiyya tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmā ca itthan‍nāmā ca bhikkhuniyau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| tā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍janti| saṅghau  itthan‍nāmañ‍ca itthan‍nāmañ‍ca bhikkhuniyau samanubhāsati tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya ca itthan‍nāmāya ca bhikkhunīnaṃ samanubhāsanā tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘samanubhaṭṭhā saṅghaina, itthan‍nāmā ca itthan‍nāmā ca bhikkhuniyau tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā, kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantīnaṃ ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā paṭippassambhanti| dvai tissau aikatau samanubhāsitabbā| tatuttari na  samanubhāsitabbā|

imāpi bhikkhuniyauti purimāyau upādāya vuc‍canti|

yāvatatiyakanti yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya āpaj‍janti, na saha vatthujjhācārā|

nissāraṇīyanti saṅghamhā nissārīyati|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

725. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍janti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā na paṭinissaj‍janti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍janti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

726. anāpatti  asamanubhāsantīnaṃ, paṭinissaj‍jantīnaṃ, ummattikānaṃ, ādikammikānanti|

navamasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. dasamasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ

727. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunīsaṅghaina samanubhaṭṭhā bhikkhuniyau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau aivācārā aivaṃsaddā aivaṃsilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa  vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| tā saṅghau na kiñ‍ci āha| tumhañ‍ñaiva tumhaiyaiva (syā॰) saṅghau uñ‍ñāya paribhavaina akkhantiyā vaibhassiyā vaibhassā (sī॰ syā॰) dubbalyā aivamāha – ‘bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’’ti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī saṅghaina samanubhaṭṭhā bhikkhuniyau aivaṃ vakkhati – saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitīti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī saṅghaina samanubhaṭṭhā bhikkhuniyau aivaṃ vadaiti – saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau aivācārā aivaṃsaddā aivaṃsilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| tā saṅghau na kiñ‍ci āha| tumhañ‍ñaiva saṅghau uñ‍ñāya paribhavaina akkhantiyā vaibhassiyā dubbalyā aivamāha – bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī saṅghaina samanubhaṭṭhā bhikkhuniyau aivaṃ vakkhati – saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitīti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

728. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī aivaṃ vadaiyya  – ‘saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau aivācārā aivaṃsaddā aivaṃsilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| tā saṅghau na kiñ‍ci āha| tumhañ‍ñaiva saṅghau uñ‍ñāya paribhavaina akkhantiyā vaibhassiyā dubbalyā aivamāha – bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’ti| sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā – ‘mā, ayyai, aivaṃ avaca – saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau aivācārā aivaṃsaddā aivaṃsilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| tā saṅghau na kiñ‍ci āha| tumhañ‍ñaiva saṅghau uñ‍ñāya paribhavaina akkhantiyā vaibhassiyā dubbalyā aivamāha – bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’ti| aivañ‍ca sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsīyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ayampi bhikkhunī yāvatatiyakaṃ dhammaṃ āpan‍nā nissāraṇīyaṃ saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

729. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

aivaṃ vadaiyyāti – ‘‘saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau aivācārā aivaṃsaddā aivaṃsilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| tā saṅghau na kiñ‍ci āha’’|

tumhañ‍ñaiva saṅghau uñ‍ñāyāti avañ‍ñāya|

paribhavaināti pāribhabyatā|

akkhantiyāti kaupaina|

vaibhassiyāti  vibhassīkatā vibhassikatāya (sī॰)|

dubbalyāti  apakkhatā|

aivamāha – ‘‘bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| vicic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’ti|

sā bhikkhunīti yā sā aivaṃvādinī bhikkhunī|

bhikkhunīhīti añ‍ñāhi bhikkhunīhi|

yā passanti yā suṇanti tāhi vattabbā – ‘‘māyyai, aivaṃ avaca – ‘saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’ti| dutiyampi  vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sutvā na vadanti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sā bhikkhunī saṅghamajjhampi ākaḍḍhitvā vattabbā – ‘‘māyyai, aivaṃ avaca – ‘saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau…pai॰… vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sā bhikkhunī samanubhāsitabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samanubhāsitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

730. ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī saṅghaina samanubhaṭṭhā bhikkhuniyau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau aivācārā aivaṃsaddā aivaṃsilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| tā saṅghau na kiñ‍ci āha| tumhañ‍ñaiva saṅghau uñ‍ñāya paribhavaina akkhantiyā vaibhassiyā dubbalyā aivamāha – bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā  pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’ti| sā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samanubhāsaiyya tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī saṅghaina samanubhaṭṭhā bhikkhuniyau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharittha| santi saṅghai añ‍ñāpi bhikkhuniyau aivācārā aivaṃsaddā aivaṃsilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| tā saṅghau na kiñ‍ci āha| tumhañ‍ñaiva saṅghau uñ‍ñāya paribhavaina akkhantiyā vaibhassiyā dubbalyā aivamāha – bhaginiyau khau saṃsaṭṭhā viharanti pāpācārā pāpasaddā pāpasilaukā, bhikkhunisaṅghassa vihaisikā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñissā vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā| vivic‍cathāyyai| vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginīnaṃ saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’ti| sā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samanubhāsati tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā samanubhāsanā tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘samanubhaṭṭhā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā, kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantiyā ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā paṭippassambhanti|

ayampīti purimāyau upādāya vuc‍cati|

yāvatatiyakanti yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya āpaj‍jati, na sahavatthujjhācārā|

nissāraṇīyanti saṅghamhā nissārīyati|

saṅghādisaisauti  saṅghauva tassā āpattiyā mānattaṃ daiti, mūlāya paṭikassati, abbhaiti, na sambahulā na aikā bhikkhunī| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅghādisaisau’’ti| tassaiva āpattinikāyassa nāmakammaṃ adhivacanaṃ, tainapi vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

731. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

adhammakammai  dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

732. anāpatti asamanubhāsantiyā, paṭinissaj‍jantiyā, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dasamasaṅghādisaisasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

uddiṭṭhā khau, ayyāyau, sattarasa saṅghādisaisā dhammā – nava paṭhamāpattikā, aṭṭha yāvatatiyakā| yaisaṃ bhikkhunī añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā āpaj‍jati, tāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai pakkhamānattaṃ caritabbaṃ| ciṇṇamānattā bhikkhunī yattha siyā vīsatigaṇau bhikkhunisaṅghau tattha (sā bhikkhunī) ( ) (katthapi natthi) abbhaitabbā| aikāyapi cai ūnau vīsatigaṇau bhikkhunisaṅghau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ abbhaiyya| sā ca bhikkhunī anabbhitā, tā ca bhikkhuniyau gārayhā, ayaṃ tattha sāmīci|

tatthāyyāyau pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

sattarasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

bhikkhunivibhaṅgai saṅghādisaisakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



3. nissaggiyakaṇḍaṃ (bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau)

1. pattavaggau

1. paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

imai khau panāyyāyau tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

733. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau bahū pattai san‍nicayaṃ karaunti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau bahū pattai san‍nicayaṃ karissanti, pattavāṇij‍jaṃ vā bhikkhuniyau karissanti, āmattikāpaṇaṃ vā pasāraissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau pattasan‍nicayaṃ karissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau pattasan‍nicayaṃ karauntīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau pattasan‍nicayaṃ karissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

734. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī pattasan‍nicayaṃ karaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

735. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

pattau nāma dvai pattā – ayaupattau, mattikāpattau| tayau pattassa vaṇṇā – uk‍kaṭṭhau pattau, majjhimau pattau, aumakau pattau| uk‍kaṭṭhau nāma pattau aḍḍhāḷhakaudanaṃ gaṇhāti catubhāgaṃ khādanaṃ tadupiyaṃ byañ‍janaṃ| majjhimau nāma pattau nāḷikaudanaṃ gaṇhāti catubhāgaṃ khādanaṃ tadupiyaṃ byañ‍janaṃ| aumakau  nāma pattau patthaudanaṃ gaṇhāti catubhāgaṃ khādanaṃ tadupiyaṃ byañ‍janaṃ| tatau uk‍kaṭṭhau apattau aumakau apattau|

san‍nicayaṃ karaiyyāti anadhiṭṭhitau avikappitau|

nissaggiyau  hautīti saha aruṇuggamanā nissaggiyau hauti| nissaj‍jitabbau saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā aikabhikkhuniyā vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbau| tāya bhikkhuniyā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ayaṃ mai, ayyai, pattau rattātik‍kantau nissaggiyau, imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmī’’ti| nissaj‍jitvā  āpatti daisaitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā| nissaṭṭhapattau dātabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ pattau itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā nissaggiyau saṅghassa nissaṭṭhau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau imaṃ pattaṃ itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā dadaiyyā’’ti|

tāya bhikkhuniyā sambahulā bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘ayaṃ mai, ayyāyau, pattau rattātik‍kantau nissaggiyau, imāhaṃ ayyānaṃ nissaj‍jāmī’’ti| nissaj‍jitvā āpatti daisaitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā| nissaṭṭhapattau dātabbau –

‘‘suṇantu mai ayyāyau| ayaṃ pattau itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā nissaggiyau ayyānaṃ nissaṭṭhau| yadi ayyānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, ayyāyau imaṃ pattaṃ itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā dadaiyyu’’nti|

tāya  bhikkhuniyā aikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyā – ‘‘ayaṃ mai, ayyai, pattau rattātik‍kantau nissaggiyau| imāhaṃ ayyāya nissaj‍jāmī’’ti| nissaj‍jitvā āpatti daisaitabbā| tāya bhikkhuniyā āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā| nissaṭṭhapattau dātabbau – ‘‘imaṃ pattaṃ ayyāya dammī’’ti|

736. rattātik‍kantai  atik‍kantasañ‍ñā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| rattātik‍kantai vaimatikā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| rattātik‍kantai anatik‍kantasañ‍ñā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| anadhiṭṭhitai adhiṭṭhitasañ‍ñā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| avikappitai vikappitasañ‍ñā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| avissaj‍jitai  vissaj‍jitasañ‍ñā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| anaṭṭhai naṭṭhasañ‍ñā… avinaṭṭhai vinaṭṭhasañ‍ñā… abhin‍nai bhin‍nasañ‍ñā… aviluttai viluttasañ‍ñā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nissaggiyaṃ pattaṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| rattānatik‍kantai atik‍kantasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| rattānatik‍kantai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| rattānatik‍kantai anatik‍kantasañ‍ñā anāpatti|

737. anāpatti antauaruṇai adhiṭṭhaiti, vikappaiti, vissaj‍jaiti, nassati, vinassati, bhij‍jati, acchinditvā gaṇhanti, vissāsaṃ gaṇhanti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau nissaṭṭhapattaṃ na dainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, nissaṭṭhapattau na dātabbau| yā na dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ

738. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhuniyau gāmakāvāsai vassaṃvuṭṭhā vassaṃvutthā (sī॰ syā॰) sāvatthiṃ agamaṃsu vattasampan‍nā iriyāpathasampan‍nā duc‍cauḷā lūkhacīvarā| upāsakā tā bhikkhuniyau passitvā – ‘‘imā bhikkhuniyau vattasampan‍nā iriyāpathasampan‍nā duc‍cauḷā lūkhacīvarā, imā bhikkhuniyau acchin‍nā bhavissantī’’ti bhikkhunisaṅghassa akālacīvaraṃ adaṃsu| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī – ‘‘amhākaṃ kathinaṃ atthataṃ kālacīvara’’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaisi| upāsakā tā bhikkhuniyau passitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘apayyāhi cīvaraṃ laddha’’nti? ‘‘na mayaṃ, āvusau, cīvaraṃ labhāma| ayyā thul‍lanandā – ‘amhākaṃ kathinaṃ atthataṃ kālacīvara’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaisī’’ti| upāsakā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā akālacīvaraṃ ‘kālacīvara’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaissatī’’ti! assausuṃ  khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ upāsakānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā akālacīvaraṃ ‘kālacīvara’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaissatī’’ti! atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ  …pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī akālacīvaraṃ ‘‘kālacīvara’’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaitīti bhājāpaisīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī akālacīvaraṃ ‘‘kālacīvara’’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

739. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī akālacīvaraṃ ‘kālacīvara’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

740. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

akālacīvaraṃ nāma anatthatai kathinai aikādasamāsai uppan‍naṃ, atthatai kathinai sattamāsai uppan‍naṃ, kālaipi ādissa din‍naṃ, aitaṃ akālacīvaraṃ nāma|
