tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. catutthasikkhāpadaṃ

897. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīnaṃ hatthai cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā santaruttaraina janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamanti| tāni cīvarāni ciraṃ nikkhittāni kaṇṇakitāni haunti| tāni bhikkhuniyau autāpainti| bhikkhuniyau tā bhikkhuniyau aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kassimāni, ayyai, cīvarāni kaṇṇakitānī’’ti? atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīnaṃ hatthai cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā santaruttaraina janapadacārikaṃ  pak‍kamissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīnaṃ hatthai cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā santaruttaraina janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīnaṃ hatthai cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā santaruttaraina janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

898. ‘‘yā pana  bhikkhunī pañ‍cāhikaṃ saṅghāṭicāraṃ saṅghāṭivāraṃ (syā॰) atik‍kāmaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

899. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

pañ‍cāhikaṃ saṅghāṭicāraṃ atik‍kāmaiyyāti pañ‍camaṃ divasaṃ pañ‍ca cīvarāni naiva nivāsaiti na pārupati na autāpaiti, pañ‍camaṃ divasaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

900. pañ‍cāhātik‍kantai atik‍kantasañ‍ñā, āpatti pācittiyassa| pañ‍cāhātik‍kantai vaimatikā, āpatti pācittiyassa| pañ‍cāhātik‍kantai anatik‍kantasañ‍ñā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

pañ‍cāhānatik‍kantai atik‍kantasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pañ‍cāhānatik‍kantai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pañ‍cāhānatik‍kantai anatik‍kantasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

901. anāpatti pañ‍camaṃ divasaṃ pañ‍ca cīvarāni nivāsaiti vā pārupati vā autāpaiti vā, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ

902. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī piṇḍāya caritvā al‍lacīvaraṃ pattharitvā vihāraṃ pāvisi| añ‍ñatarā  bhikkhunī taṃ  cīvaraṃ pārupitvā gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sā nikkhamitvā bhikkhuniyau pucchi – ‘‘apāyyai, mayhaṃ cīvaraṃ passaiyyāthā’’ti? bhikkhuniyau tassā bhikkhuniyā aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau sā bhikkhunī ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī mayhaṃ cīvaraṃ anāpucchā pārupissatī’’ti! atha khau sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ anāpucchā pārupissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ anāpucchā pārupatīti pārupīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ anāpucchā pārupissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

903. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī cīvarasaṅkamanīyaṃ dhāraiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

904. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

cīvarasaṅkamanīyaṃ nāma upasampan‍nāya pañ‍can‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ tassā vā adin‍naṃ taṃ vā anāpucchā nivāsaiti vā pārupati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

905. upasampan‍nāya upasampan‍nasañ‍ñā cīvarasaṅkamanīyaṃ dhāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nāya vaimatikā cīvarasaṅkamanīyaṃ dhāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñā cīvarasaṅkamanīyaṃ dhāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍nāya cīvarasaṅkamanīyaṃ dhāraiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya upasampan‍nasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

906. anāpatti sā vā daiti, taṃ vā āpucchā nivāsaiti vā pārupati vā, acchin‍nacīvarikāya, naṭṭhacīvarikāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

907. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā upaṭṭhākakulaṃ thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhikkhunisaṅghassa, ayyai, cīvaraṃ dassāmā’’ti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī – ‘‘tumhai bahukic‍cā bahukaraṇīyā’’ti antarāyaṃ akāsi| taina khau pana samayaina tassa kulassa gharaṃ ḍayhati| tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā amhākaṃ daiyyadhammaṃ antarāyaṃ karissati! ubhayaināmha paribāhirā parihīnā (sī॰), bhaugaihi ca puñ‍ñaina cā’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā gaṇassa cīvaralābhaṃ antarāyaṃ karissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī gaṇassa cīvaralābhaṃ antarāyaṃ akāsīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī gaṇassa cīvaralābhaṃ antarāyaṃ karissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

908. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī gaṇassa cīvaralābhaṃ antarāyaṃ karaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

909. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

gaṇau nāma bhikkhunisaṅghau vuc‍cati|

cīvaraṃ  nāma chan‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ vikappanupagaṃ pacchimaṃ|

antarāyaṃ  karaiyyāti ‘‘kathaṃ imai cīvaraṃ na imaṃ cīvaraṃ (ka॰) dadaiyyu’’nti antarāyaṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa| añ‍ñaṃ parikkhāraṃ antarāyaṃ karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sambahulānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ vā aikabhikkhuniyā vā anupasampan‍nāya vā cīvaraṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā parikkhāraṃ antarāyaṃ karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

910. anāpatti  ānisaṃsaṃ dassaitvā nivāraiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. sattamasikkhāpadaṃ

911. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhunisaṅghassa akālacīvaraṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| atha khau bhikkhunisaṅghau taṃ cīvaraṃ bhājaitukāmau san‍nipati| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā antaivāsiniyau bhikkhuniyau pak‍kantā haunti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī tā bhikkhuniyau aitadavauca – ‘‘ayyai, bhikkhuniyau pak‍kantā, na tāva cīvaraṃ bhājīyissatī’’ti| cīvaravibhaṅgaṃ paṭibāhi| bhikkhuniyau na tāva cīvaraṃ bhājīyissatīti pak‍kamiṃsu vipak‍kamiṃsu (syā॰)| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī antaivāsinīsu bhikkhunīsu āgatāsu taṃ cīvaraṃ bhājāpaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā dhammikaṃ cīvaravibhaṅgaṃ paṭibāhissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dhammikaṃ cīvaravibhaṅgaṃ paṭibāhatīti paṭibāhīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dhammikaṃ cīvaravibhaṅgaṃ paṭibāhissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

912. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī dhammikaṃ cīvaravibhaṅgaṃ  paṭibāhaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

913. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

dhammikaunāma cīvaravibhaṅgau samaggau bhikkhunisaṅghau san‍nipatitvā bhājaiti|

paṭibāhaiyyāti  kathaṃ imaṃ cīvaraṃ na bhājaiyyāti cīvaraṃ bhājaiyyāti (ka॰) paṭibāhati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

914. dhammikai  dhammikasañ‍ñā paṭibāhati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammikai vaimatikā paṭibāhati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dhammikai adhammikasañ‍ñā paṭibāhati, anāpatti| adhammikai dhammikasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammikai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammikai adhammikasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

915. anāpatti ānisaṃsaṃ dassaitvā paṭibāhati, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

916. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī naṭānampi naṭakānampi laṅghakānampi saukajjhāyikānampi saukasāyikānampi (sī॰) kumbhathūṇikānampi kumbhathuṇikānampi (ka॰) samaṇacīvaraṃ daiti – ‘‘mayhaṃ parisati parisatiṃ (sī॰) vaṇṇaṃ bhāsathā’’ti| naṭāpi naṭakāpi laṅghakāpi saukajjhāyikāpi kumbhathūṇikāpi thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā parisati vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti – ‘‘ayyā thul‍lanandā bahussutā bhāṇikā  visāradā paṭṭā dhammiṃ kathaṃ kātuṃ; daitha ayyāya, karautha ayyāyā’’ti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā, tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā agārikassa samaṇacīvaraṃ dassatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī agārikassa samaṇacīvaraṃ daitīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī agārikassa samaṇacīvaraṃ dassati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

917. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī agārikassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā samaṇacīvaraṃ dadaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

918. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

agārikau  nāma yau kauci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

paribbājakau nāma bhikkhuñ‍ca sāmaṇairañ‍ca ṭhapaitvā yau kauci paribbājakasamāpan‍nau|

paribbājikā nāma bhikkhuniñ‍ca sikkhamānañ‍ca sāmaṇairiñ‍ca ṭhapaitvā yā kāci paribbājikasamāpan‍nā|

samaṇacīvaraṃ  nāma kappakataṃ vuc‍cati| daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

919. anāpatti mātāpitūnaṃ daiti, tāvakālikaṃ daiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. navamasikkhāpadaṃ

920. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā upaṭṭhākakulaṃ thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai mayaṃ, ayyai, sak‍kauma, bhikkhunisaṅghassa cīvaraṃ dassāmā’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina vassaṃvuṭṭhā bhikkhuniyau cīvaraṃ bhājaitukāmā san‍nipatiṃsu| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī tā bhikkhuniyau aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamaitha, ayyai, atthi bhikkhunisaṅghassa cīvarapac‍cāsā’’ti| bhikkhuniyau thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘gacchāyyai, taṃ cīvaraṃ jānāhī’’ti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī yaina taṃ kulaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai manussai aitadavauca – ‘‘daithāvusau, bhikkhunisaṅghassa cīvara’’nti| ‘‘na mayaṃ, ayyai, sak‍kauma bhikkhunisaṅghassa cīvaraṃ dātu’’nti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā dubbalacīvarapac‍cāsāya cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dubbalacīvarapac‍cāsāya cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaitīti atik‍kāmaisīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī  dubbalacīvarapac‍cāsāya cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

921. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī dubbalacīvarapac‍cāsāya cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

922. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

dubbalacīvarapac‍cāsā nāma ‘‘sacai mayaṃ sak‍kauma, dassāma karissāmā’’ti vācā bhin‍nā hauti|

cīvarakālasamayau nāma anatthatai kathinai vassānassa pacchimau  māsau, atthatai kathinai pañ‍ca māsā|

cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaiyyāti anatthatai kathinai vassānassa pacchimaṃ divasaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| atthatai kathinai kathinuddhāradivasaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

923. dubbalacīvarai dubbalacīvarasañ‍ñā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dubbalacīvarai vaimatikā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dubbalacīvarai adubbalacīvarasañ‍ñā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, anāpatti|

adubbalacīvarai dubbalacīvarasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adubbalacīvarai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adubbalacīvarai adubbalacīvarasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

924. anāpatti  ānisaṃsaṃ dassaitvā nivāraiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. dasamasikkhāpadaṃ

925. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraina upāsakaina saṅghaṃ uddissa vihārau kārāpitau hauti| sau tassa vihārassa mahai ubhatausaṅghassa akālacīvaraṃ dātukāmau hauti| taina khau pana samayaina ubhatausaṅghassa kathinaṃ atthataṃ hauti| atha khau sau upāsakau saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā kathinuddhāraṃ yāci| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kathinaṃ uddharituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana bhikkhavai kathinaṃ uddharitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

926. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau kathinaṃ uddharaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau kathinaṃ uddharati| yassāyasmatau khamati kathinassa uddhārau, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘ubbhataṃ saṅghaina kathinaṃ, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

927. atha khau sau upāsakau bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā kathinuddhāraṃ yāci| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī – ‘‘cīvaraṃ amhākaṃ  bhavissatī’’ti kathinuddhāraṃ paṭibāhi| atha khau sau upāsakau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau amhākaṃ kathinuddhāraṃ na dassantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau  bhikkhuniyau tassa upāsakassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā dhammikaṃ kathinuddhāraṃ paṭibāhissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dhammikaṃ kathinuddhāraṃ paṭibāhatīti paṭibāhīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai,  thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dhammikaṃ kathinuddhāraṃ paṭibāhissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

928. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī dhammikaṃ kathinuddhāraṃ paṭibāhaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

929. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

dhammikau nāma kathinuddhārau samaggau bhikkhunisaṅghau san‍nipatitvā uddharati|

paṭibāhaiyyāti ‘‘kathaṃ idaṃ kathinaṃ na uddharaiyyā’’ti kathinaṃ uddharaiyyāti (ka॰) paṭibāhati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

930. dhammikai dhammikasañ‍ñā paṭibāhati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammikai vaimatikā paṭibāhati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dhammikai adhammikasañ‍ñā paṭibāhati, anāpatti| adhammikai dhammikasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammikai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammikai adhammikasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

931. anāpatti anisaṃsaṃ dassaitvā paṭibāhati, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

naggavaggau tatiyau|

4. tuvaṭṭavaggau

1. paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

932. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau dvai aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭainti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau dvai aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau dvai aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau dvai aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau dvai aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

933. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhuniyau dvai aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaiyyuṃ, pācittiya’’nti|

934. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhuniyauti upasampan‍nāyau vuc‍canti|

dvai aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaiyyunti aikāya nipan‍nāya aparā nipaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| ubhau vā nipaj‍janti, āpatti pācittiyassa| uṭṭhahitvā punappunaṃ nipaj‍janti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

935. anāpatti aikāya nipan‍nāya aparā nisīdati, ubhau vā nisīdanti, ummattikānaṃ, ādikammikānanti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ

936. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau dvai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā tuvaṭṭainti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau dvai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā tuvaṭṭaissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ  khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau  appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau dvai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā tuvaṭṭaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau dvai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā tuvaṭṭaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau dvai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā tuvaṭṭaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

937. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhuniyau dvai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā tuvaṭṭaiyyuṃ, pācittiya’’nti|

938. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhuniyauti upasampan‍nāyau vuc‍canti|

dvai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā tuvaṭṭaiyyunti tañ‍ñaiva attharitvā tañ‍ñaiva pārupanti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

939. aikattharaṇapāvuraṇai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇasañ‍ñā tuvaṭṭainti, āpatti pācittiyassa| aikattharaṇapāvuraṇai vaimatikā tuvaṭṭainti, āpatti pācittiyassa| aikattharaṇapāvuraṇai nānattharaṇapāvuraṇasañ‍ñā tuvaṭṭainti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aikattharaṇai nānāpāvuraṇai nānāpāvuraṇasañ‍ñā (ka॰), āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nānattharaṇai aikapāvuraṇai aikapāvuraṇasañ‍ñā (ka॰), āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nānattharaṇapāvuraṇai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nānattharaṇapāvuraṇai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nānattharaṇapāvuraṇai nānattharaṇapāvuraṇasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

940. anāpatti vavatthānaṃ dassaitvā nipaj‍janti, ummattikānaṃ, ādikammikānanti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ

941. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bahussutā hauti bhāṇikā visāradā paṭṭā dhammiṃ kathaṃ kātuṃ| bhaddāpi kāpilānī bahussutā hauti bhāṇikā visāradā paṭṭā  dhammiṃ kathaṃ kātuṃ uḷārasambhāvitā| manussā – ‘‘ayyā bhaddā kāpilānī bahussutā bhāṇikā visāradā paṭṭā dhammiṃ kathaṃ kātuṃ uḷārasambhāvitā’’ti bhaddaṃ kāpilāniṃ paṭhamaṃ payirupāsitvā, pacchā thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ payirupāsanti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī issāpakatā – ‘‘imā kira appicchā santuṭṭhā pavivittā asaṃsaṭṭhā yā imā sañ‍ñattibahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharantī’’ti bhaddāya kāpilāniyā puratau caṅkamatipi tiṭṭhatipi nisīdatipi saiyyampi kappaiti uddisatipi uddisāpaitipi sajjhāyampi karauti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā ayyāya bhaddāya kāpilāniyā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhaddāya kāpilāniyā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karautīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhaddāya kāpilāniyā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰…  aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

942. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

943. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

bhikkhuniyāti añ‍ñāya bhikkhuniyā|

sañ‍cic‍cāti jānantī sañ‍jānantī caic‍ca abhivitaritvā vītik‍kamau|

aphāsuṃ karaiyyāti – ‘‘iminā imissā aphāsu bhavissatī’’ti anāpucchā puratau caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti uddisati vā uddisāpaiti vā sajjhāyaṃ vā karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

944. upasampan‍nāya  upasampan‍nasañ‍ñā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nāya vaimatikā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍nāya  sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya upasampan‍nasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

945. anāpatti na aphāsuṃ kattukāmā āpucchā puratau caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti uddisati vā uddisāpaiti vā sajjhāyaṃ vā karauti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. catutthasikkhāpadaṃ

946. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ naiva upaṭṭhaiti na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ naiva upaṭṭhaissati na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ naiva upaṭṭhaiti na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karautīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ naiva upaṭṭhaissati na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

947. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ naiva upaṭṭhaiyya na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

948. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

dukkhitā nāma gilānā vuc‍cati|

sahajīvinī  nāma saddhivihārinī vuc‍cati|

naiva upaṭṭhaiyyāti na sayaṃ upaṭṭhaiyya|

na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyyāti na añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiyya|

‘‘naiva upaṭṭhaissāmi, na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissāmī’’ti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai āpatti pācittiyassa| antaivāsiniṃ vā anupasampan‍naṃ vā naiva upaṭṭhaiti, na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

949. anāpatti  sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ

950. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhaddā kāpilānī sākaitai vassaṃ upagatā hauti| sā kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina ubbāḷhā thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘sacai mai ayyā thul‍lanandā upassayaṃ dadaiyya āgacchaiyyāmahaṃ sāvatthi’’nti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī aivamāha – ‘‘āgacchatu, dassāmī’’ti| atha khau bhaddā kāpilānī sākaitā sāvatthiṃ agamāsi| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhaddāya kāpilāniyā upassayaṃ adāsi| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bahussutā hauti bhāṇikā visāradā paṭṭā dhammiṃ kathaṃ kātuṃ| bhaddāpi kāpilānī bahussutā bahussutāyaiva (ka॰) hauti bhāṇikā visāradā paṭṭā dhammiṃ kathaṃ kātuṃ uḷārasambhāvitā| manussā – ‘‘ayyā bhaddā kāpilānī bahussutā bhāṇikā visāradā paṭṭā dhammiṃ kathaṃ kātuṃ uḷārasambhāvitā’’ti bhaddaṃ kāpilāniṃ  paṭhamaṃ payirupāsitvā pacchā thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ payirupāsanti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī issāpakatā – ‘‘imā kira  appicchā santuṭṭhā pavivittā asaṃsaṭṭhā yā imā sañ‍ñattibahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharantī’’ti kupitā anattamanā bhaddaṃ kāpilāniṃ upassayā nik‍kaḍḍhi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā  ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā ayyāya bhaddāya kāpilāniyā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhaddāya kāpilāniyā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhatīti nik‍kaḍḍhīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhaddāya kāpilāniyā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

951. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhaiyya vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

952. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

bhikkhuniyāti añ‍ñāya bhikkhuniyā| upassayau nāma kavāṭabaddhau vuc‍cati| datvāti sayaṃ datvā| kupitā anattamanāti anabhiraddhā āhatacittā khilajātā|

nik‍kaḍḍhaiyyāti gabbhai  gahaitvā pamukhaṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati, āpatti pācittiyassa| pamukhai gahaitvā bahi nik‍kaḍḍhati, āpatti  pācittiyassa| aikaina payaugaina bahukaipi dvārai atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiyyāti añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa aittha ‘‘āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti sabbattha dissati, bhikkhuvibhaṅgai pana idisaisu āṇattisahagatasikkhāpadaisu ‘‘añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiti āpatti pācittiyassā’’ti āgatattā tamanuvattitvā visaudhitamidaṃ| sakiṃ āṇattā bahukaipi dvārai atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

953. upasampan‍nāya upasampan‍nasañ‍ñā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nāya vaimatikā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhati vā  nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

tassā  parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| akavāṭabaddhā nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tassā parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍naṃ kavāṭabaddhā vā akavāṭabaddhā vā nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tassā parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya upasampan‍nasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

954. anāpatti  alaj‍jiniṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, tassā parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, ummattikaṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, tassā parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, bhaṇḍanakārikaṃ kalahakārikaṃ vivādakārikaṃ bhassakārikaṃ saṅghai adhikaraṇakārikaṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, tassā parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, antaivāsiniṃ vā saddhivihāriniṃ vā na sammā vattantiṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, tassā parikkhāraṃ nik‍kaḍḍhati vā nik‍kaḍḍhāpaiti vā, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

955. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī saṃsaṭṭhā viharati gahapatināpi gahapatiputtainapi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā caṇḍakāḷī saṃsaṭṭhā viharissati gahapatināpi gahapatiputtainapī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī saṃsaṭṭhā viharati gahapatināpi gahapatiputtainapīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī saṃsaṭṭhā viharissati gahapatināpi gahapatiputtainapi! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ  vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

956. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī saṃsaṭṭhā viharaiyya gahapatinā vā gahapatiputtaina vā sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi aivamassa vacanīyā – ‘māyyai, saṃsaṭṭhā vihari gahapatināpi gahapatiputtainapi| vivic‍cāyyai; vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginiyā saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’ti| aivañ‍ca aivañ‍ca pana (ka॰) sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya, sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsīyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

957. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

saṃsaṭṭhā nāma ananulaumikaina kāyikavācasikaina saṃsaṭṭhā|

gahapati nāma yau kauci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

gahapatiputtau nāma yai kaici yau kauci (ka॰) puttabhātarau|

sā bhikkhunīti yā sā saṃsaṭṭhā bhikkhunī|

bhikkhunīhīti añ‍ñāhi bhikkhunīhi| yā passanti yā suṇanti tāhi vattabbā – ‘‘māyyai, saṃsaṭṭhā vihari gahapatināpi gahapatiputtainapi| vivic‍cāyyai; vivaikañ‍ñaiva bhaginiyā saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sutvā na vadanti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sā bhikkhunī saṅghamajjhampi ākaḍḍhitvā vattabbā – ‘‘māyyai, saṃsaṭṭhā vihari gahapatināpi gahapatiputtainapi| vivic‍cāyyai; vivaikañ‍ñaivabhaginiyā saṅghau vaṇṇaitī’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sā bhikkhunī samanubhāsitabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samanubhāsitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

958. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī saṃsaṭṭhā viharati gahapatināpi  gahapatiputtainapi| sā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samanubhāsaiyya tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī saṃsaṭṭhā viharati gahapatināpi gahapatiputtainapi| sā taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samanubhāsati tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā samanubhāsanā tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘samanubhaṭṭhā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya; khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

ñattiyā  duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa|

959. dhammakammai  dhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

960. anāpatti asamanubhāsantiyā, paṭinissaj‍jantiyā, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. sattamasikkhāpadaṃ

961. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau antauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ caranti| dhuttā  dūsainti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau antauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ carissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau antauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ carantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau antauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ carissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

962. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī antauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ caraiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

963. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

antauraṭṭhaiti yassa vijitai viharati, tassa raṭṭhai|

sāsaṅkaṃ nāma tasmiṃ maggai caurānaṃ niviṭṭhaukāsau dissati, bhuttaukāsau dissati, ṭhitaukāsau dissati, nisin‍naukāsau dissati, nipan‍naukāsau dissati|

sappaṭibhayaṃ nāma tasmiṃ maggai cauraihi manussā hatā dissanti, viluttā dissanti, ākauṭitā dissanti|

asatthikā nāma vinā satthaina|

cārikaṃ caraiyyāti kuk‍kuṭasampātai gāmai gāmantarai gāmantarai āpatti pācittiyassa| agāmakai arañ‍ñai addhayaujanai addhayaujanai āpatti pācittiyassa|

964. anāpatti  satthaina saha gacchati, khaimai appaṭibhayai gacchati, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

965. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau tirauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ caranti| dhuttā dūsainti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti  vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau tirauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ carissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau tirauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ carantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau tirauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ carissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

966. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī tirauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ caraiyya, pācattiya’’nti|

967. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

tirauraṭṭhaiti yassa vijitai viharati, taṃ ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñassa raṭṭhai|

sāsaṅkaṃ nāma tasmiṃ maggai caurānaṃ niviṭṭhaukāsau dissati, bhuttaukāsau dissati, ṭhitaukāsau dissati, nisin‍naukāsau dissati, nipan‍naukāsau dissati|

sappaṭibhayaṃ nāma tasmiṃ maggai cauraihi manussā hatā dissanti, viluttā dissanti, ākauṭitā dissanti|

asatthikā  nāma vinā satthaina|

cārikaṃ caraiyyāti kuk‍kuṭasampātai gāmai gāmantarai gāmantarai, āpatti pācittiyassa| agāmakai arañ‍ñai addhayaujanai addhayaujanai āpatti pācittiyassa|

968. anāpatti satthaina saha gacchati, khaimai appaṭibhayai gacchati, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. navamasikkhāpadaṃ

969. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ caranti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ carissanti haritāni tiṇāni ca sammaddantā, aikindriyaṃ jīvaṃ vihaiṭhaintā, bahū khuddakai pāṇai saṅghātaṃ āpādaintā’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ carissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ carantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ carissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

970. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ caraiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

971. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

antauvassanti purimaṃ vā taimāsaṃ pacchimaṃ vā taimāsaṃ avasitvā|

cārikaṃ  caraiyyāti kuk‍kuṭasampātai gāmai  gāmantarai gāmantarai āpatti pācittiyassa| agāmakai arañ‍ñai addhayaujanai addhayaujanai āpatti pācittiyassa|

972. anāpatti sattāhakaraṇīyaina gacchati, kainaci ubbāḷhā gacchati, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. dasamasikkhāpadaṃ

973. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau tatthaiva rājagahai  vassaṃ vasanti, tattha haimantaṃ, tattha gimhaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘āhundarikā bhikkhunīnaṃ disā andhakārā, na imāsaṃ disā pakkhāyantī’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya…pai॰… saddhammaṭṭhitiyā vinayānuggahāya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

974. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī vassaṃvuṭṭhā cārikaṃ na pak‍kamaiyya antamasau chappañ‍cayaujanānipi, pācittiya’’nti|

975. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ  atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

vassaṃvuṭṭhā nāma purimaṃ vā taimāsaṃ pacchimaṃ vā taimāsaṃ vuṭṭhā|

‘‘cārikaṃ na pak‍kamissāmi antamasau chappañ‍cayaujanānipī’’ti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai āpatti pācittiyassa|

976. anāpatti  sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā dutiyikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tuvaṭṭavaggau catutthau|

5. cittāgāravaggau

1. paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

977. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa uyyānai cittāgārai paṭibhānacittaṃ kataṃ  hauti| bahū manussā cittāgāraṃ dassanāya gacchanti| chabbaggiyāpi bhikkhuniyau cittāgāraṃ dassanāya agamaṃsu| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau cittāgāraṃ dassanāya gacchissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau cittāgāraṃ dassanāya gacchissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau  cittāgāraṃ dassanāya gacchantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau cittāgāraṃ dassanāya gacchissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

978. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī rājāgāraṃ vā cittāgāraṃ vā ārāmaṃ vā uyyānaṃ vā paukkharaṇiṃ vā dassanāya gacchaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

979. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

rājāgāraṃ nāma yattha katthaci rañ‍ñau kīḷituṃ ramituṃ kataṃ hauti|

cittāgāraṃ nāma yattha katthaci manussānaṃ kīḷituṃ ramituṃ kataṃ hauti|

ārāmau nāma yattha katthaci manussānaṃ kīḷituṃ ramituṃ katau hauti|

uyyānaṃ nāma yattha katthaci manussānaṃ kīḷituṃ ramituṃ kataṃ hauti|

paukkharaṇī nāma yattha katthaci manussānaṃ kīḷituṃ ramituṃ katā hauti|

980. dassanāya gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yattha ṭhitā passati, āpatti pācittiyassa| dassanūpacāraṃ vijahitvā punappunaṃ passati, āpatti pācittiyassa| aikamaikaṃ dassanāya gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yattha ṭhitā passati, āpatti pacittiyassa| dassanūpacāraṃ vijahitvā punappunaṃ passati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

981. anāpatti  ārāmai ṭhitā passati, gacchantī vā āgacchantī vā  passati, sati karaṇīyai gantvā passati, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ

982. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau āsandimpi pal‍laṅkampi paribhuñ‍janti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau āsandimpi pal‍laṅkampi paribhuñ‍jissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau āsandimpi pal‍laṅkampi paribhuñ‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau āsandimpi pal‍laṅkampi paribhuñ‍jantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau āsandimpi pal‍laṅkampi paribhuñ‍jissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

983. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī āsandiṃ vā pal‍laṅkaṃ vā paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

984. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ  atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

āsandī nāma atik‍kantappamāṇā vuc‍cati|

pal‍laṅkau nāma āharimaihi ‘‘asaṃhārimaihi’’ iti kaṅkhāvitaraṇiyā samaiti vāḷaihi katau hauti|

paribhuñ‍jaiyyāti tasmiṃ abhinisīdati vā abhinipaj‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

985. anāpatti  āsandiyā pādai chinditvā paribhuñ‍jati, pal‍laṅkassa vāḷai bhinditvā paribhuñ‍jati, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ

986. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau suttaṃ kantanti| manussā  vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau suttaṃ kantissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā  …pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau suttaṃ kantissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau suttaṃ kantantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau suttaṃ kantissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā  pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

987. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī suttaṃ kantaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

988. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

suttaṃ nāma cha suttāni – khaumaṃ, kappāsikaṃ, kausaiyyaṃ, kambalaṃ, sāṇaṃ, bhaṅgaṃ|

kantaiyyāti sayaṃ kantati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| uj‍javuj‍javai āpatti pācittiyassa|

989. anāpatti kantitasuttaṃ kantati, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. catutthasikkhāpadaṃ

990. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ karaunti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ karissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ karauntīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ karissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

991. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ karaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

992. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ nāma agārikassa yāguṃ vā bhattaṃ vā khādanīyaṃ  vā pacati, sāṭakaṃ vā vaiṭhanaṃ vā dhauvati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

993. anāpatti yāgupānai, saṅghabhattai, caitiyapūjāya, attanau vaiyyāvac‍cakarassa yāguṃ vā bhattaṃ vā khādanīyaṃ vā pacati, sāṭakaṃ vā vaiṭhanaṃ vā dhauvati, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ

994. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘aihāyyai, imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaihī’’ti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī ‘sādhū’ti paṭissuṇitvā naiva vūpasamaiti na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauti| atha khau sā bhikkhunī  bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā bhikkhuniyā – ‘aihāyyai’, imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaihī’ti vuc‍camānā – ‘sādhū’ti paṭissuṇitvā, naiva vūpasamaissati na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā – ‘‘aihāyyai, imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaihī’’ti vuc‍camānā – ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā, naiva  vūpasamaiti na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karautīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā – ‘‘aihāyyai, imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaihī’’ti vuc‍camānā – ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā, naiva vūpasamaissati na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

995. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā – ‘aihāyyai, imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaihī’ti vuc‍camānā  – ‘sādhū’ti paṭissuṇitvā sā pacchā anantarāyikinī naiva vūpasamaiyya na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

996. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

bhikkhuniyāti añ‍ñāya bhikkhuniyā|

adhikaraṇaṃ nāma cattāri adhikaraṇāni – vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ|

aihāyyai imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaihīti aihāyyai imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vinicchaihi|

sā  pacchā anantarāyikinīti asati antarāyai|

naiva vūpasamaiyyāti na sayaṃ vūpasamaiyya|

na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyyāti na añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiyya| ‘‘naiva vūpasamaissāmi na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissāmī’’ti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

997. upasampan‍nāya  upasampan‍nasañ‍ñā adhikaraṇaṃ naiva vūpasamaiti na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nāya  vaimatikā adhikaraṇaṃ naiva vūpasamaiti, na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñā adhikaraṇaṃ naiva vūpasamaiti, na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍nāya adhikaraṇaṃ naiva vūpasamaiti, na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya upasampan‍nasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nāya anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

998. anāpatti  sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

999. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī naṭānampi naṭakānampi laṅghakānampi saukajjhāyikānampi kumbhathūṇikānampi sahatthā khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ daiti – ‘‘mayhaṃ parisati vaṇṇaṃ bhāsathā’’ti| naṭāpi naṭakāpi laṅghakāpi saukajjhāyikāpi kumbhathūṇikāpi thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā parisati vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti – ‘‘ayyā thul‍lanandā bahussutā bhāṇikā visāradā paṭṭā dhammiṃ kathaṃ kātuṃ; daitha ayyāya, karautha ayyāyā’’ti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā  ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā agārikassa sahatthā khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ dassatīti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī agārikassa sahatthā khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ daitīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī agārikassa sahatthā khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ dassati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1000. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī agārikassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā sahatthā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā dadaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1001. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

agārikau nāma yau kauci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

paribbājakau nāma bhikkhuñ‍ca sāmaṇairañ‍ca ṭhapaitvā yau kauci paribbājakasamāpan‍nau|

paribbājikā  nāma bhikkhuniñ‍ca sikkhamānañ‍ca sāmaṇairiñ‍ca ṭhapaitvā yā kāci paribbājikasamāpan‍nā|

khādanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – udakadantapaunaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ|

dadaiyyāti kāyaina vā kāyapaṭibaddhaina vā nissaggiyaina vā  daiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| udakadantapaunaṃ daiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1002. anāpatti dāpaiti na daiti, upanikkhipitvā daiti, bāhirālaipaṃ  daiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. sattamasikkhāpadaṃ

1003. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī āvasathacīvaraṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jati| añ‍ñā utuniyau bhikkhuniyau na labhanti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā āvasathacīvaraṃ anissaj‍jitvā  paribhuñ‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī āvasathacīvaraṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jatīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī āvasathacīvaraṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1004. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī āvasathacīvaraṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1005. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

āvasathacīvaraṃ nāma ‘‘utuniyau bhikkhuniyau paribhuñ‍jantū’’ti din‍naṃ hauti|

anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jaiyyāti dvaitissau rattiyau paribhuñ‍jitvā catutthadivasai dhauvitvā bhikkhuniyā vā sikkhamānāya vā sāmaṇairiyā vā anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1006. anissaj‍jitai anissaj‍jitasañ‍ñā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| anissaj‍jitai vaimatikā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| anissaj‍jitai nissaj‍jitasañ‍ñā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

nissaj‍jitai anissaj‍jitasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaj‍jitai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaj‍jitai nissaj‍jitasañ‍ñā anāpatti|

1007. anāpatti nissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jati, puna pariyāyaina paribhuñ‍jati, añ‍ñā utuniyau bhikkhuniyau na haunti, acchin‍nacīvarikāya, naṭṭhacīvarikāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

1008. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī āvasathaṃ anissaj‍jitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā āvasathau ḍayhati| bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘handāyyai, bhaṇḍakaṃ nīharāmā’’ti| aikac‍cā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ, ayyai, nīharissāma| yaṃ kiñ‍ci naṭṭhaṃ sabbaṃ amhai abhiyuñ‍jissatī’’ti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī punadaiva taṃ āvasathaṃ pac‍cāgantvā bhikkhuniyau pucchi – ‘‘apāyyai, bhaṇḍakaṃ nīharitthā’’ti? ‘‘na  mayaṃ, ayyai, nīharimhā’’ti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau āvasathai ḍayhamānai bhaṇḍakaṃ na nīharissantī’’ti! yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā āvasathaṃ anissaj‍jitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī āvasathaṃ anissaj‍jitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kamatīti pak‍kamīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī āvasathaṃ anissaj‍jitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kamissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1009. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī āvasathaṃ anissaj‍jitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1010. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

āvasathau nāma kavāṭabaddhau vuc‍cati|

anissaj‍jitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyyāti bhikkhuniyā vā sikkhamānāya vā sāmaṇairiyā vā anissaj‍jitvā parikkhittassa āvasathassa parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā āpatti pācittiyassa| aparikkhittassa āvasathassa upacāraṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā āpatti pācittiyassa|

1011. anissaj‍jitai anissaj‍jitasañ‍ñā pak‍kamati, āpatti pācittiyassa| anissaj‍jitai vaimatikā pak‍kamati, āpatti pācittiyassa| anissaj‍jitai  nissaj‍jitasañ‍ñā pak‍kamati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

akavāṭabaddhaṃ  anissaj‍jitvā pak‍kamati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaj‍jitai anissaj‍jitasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaj‍jitai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nissaj‍jitai nissaj‍jitasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1012. anāpatti  nissaj‍jitvā pak‍kamati, sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. navamasikkhāpadaṃ

1013. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi  nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1014. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1015. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

tiracchānavij‍jā tiracchānavij‍jaṃ (ka॰) nāma yaṃ kiñ‍ci bāhirakaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ|

pariyāpuṇaiyyāti  padaina pariyāpuṇāti, padai padai āpatti pācittiyassa| akkharāya pariyāpuṇāti, akkharakkharāya āpatti pācittiyassa|

1016. anāpatti  laikhaṃ pariyāpuṇāti, dhāraṇaṃ pariyāpuṇāti, guttatthāya parittaṃ pariyāpuṇāti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. dasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1017. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina  chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācaissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā  bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1018. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1019. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

tiracchānavij‍jā tiracchānavij‍jaṃ (ka॰) nāma yaṃ kiñ‍ci bāhirakaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ|

vācaiyyāti padaina vācaiti, padai padai āpatti pācittiyassa| akkharāya vācaiti, akkharakkharāya āpatti pācittiyassa|

1020. anāpatti  laikhaṃ vācaiti, dhāraṇaṃ vācaiti, guttatthāya parittaṃ vācaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

cittāgāravaggau pañ‍camau|

6. ārāmavaggau

1. paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

1021. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū gāmakāvāsai aikacīvarā cīvarakammaṃ karaunti| bhikkhuniyau anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisitvā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ  bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

1022. taina khau pana samayaina tai bhikkhū tamhā āvāsā pak‍kamiṃsu| bhikkhuniyau – ‘‘ayyā pak‍kantā’’ti, ārāmaṃ nāgamaṃsu| atha khau tai bhikkhū punadaiva taṃ āvāsaṃ pac‍cāgacchiṃsu| bhikkhuniyau – ‘‘ayyā āgatā’’ti, āpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisitvā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā  tai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tā bhikkhuniyau tai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tumhai, bhaginiyau, ārāmaṃ  naiva sammaj‍jittha na pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpitthāti? bhagavatā, ayyā, sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti (ka॰) hauti – ‘‘na anāpucchā ārāmau pavisitabbau’’ti| taina mayaṃ na āgamimhā’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, santaṃ bhikkhuṃ āpucchā ārāmaṃ  pavisituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

1023. taina khau pana samayaina tai bhikkhū tamhā āvāsā pak‍kamitvā punadaiva taṃ āvāsaṃ pac‍cāgacchiṃsu| bhikkhuniyau – ‘‘ayyā pak‍kantā’’ti anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisiṃsu| tāsaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – ‘na santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā ārāmau pavisitabbau’ti| mayañ‍camhā santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisimhā| kac‍ci nu khau mayaṃ pācittiyaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nā’’ti…pai॰… bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1024. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī jānaṃ sabhikkhukaṃ ārāmaṃ anāpucchā pavisaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1025. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

jānāti nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti, añ‍ñai vā tassā āraucainti, tai vā āraucainti|

sabhikkhukau nāma ārāmau yattha bhikkhū rukkhamūlaipi vasanti|

anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisaiyyāti bhikkhuṃ vā sāmaṇairaṃ vā ārāmikaṃ vā anāpucchā parikkhittassa  ārāmassa parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā āpatti pācittiyassa| aparikkhittassa ārāmassa upacāraṃ auk‍kamantiyā āpatti pācittiyassa|

1026. sabhikkhukai sabhikkhukasañ‍ñā santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisati, āpatti pācittiyassa| sabhikkhukai vaimatikā santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā  ārāmaṃ pavisati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sabhikkhukai abhikkhukasañ‍ñā santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā ārāmaṃ pavisati, anāpatti|

abhikkhukai sabhikkhukasañ‍ñā, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa| abhikkhukai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| abhikkhukai abhikkhukasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1027. anāpatti santaṃ bhikkhuṃ āpucchā pavisati, asantaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā pavisati, sīsānulaukikā gacchati, yattha bhikkhuniyau san‍nipatitā haunti tattha gacchati, ārāmaina maggau hauti, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ

1028. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau upālissa upajjhāyau āyasmā kappitakau susānai viharati| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ mahattarā mahantatarā (sī॰) bhikkhunī kālaṅkatā hauti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ nīharitvā āyasmatau kappitakassa vihārassa avidūrai jhāpaitvā thūpaṃ katvā gantvā tasmiṃ thūpai raudanti| atha khau āyasmā kappitakau taina saddaina ubbāḷhau taṃ thūpaṃ bhinditvā pakiraisi| chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau – ‘‘iminā kappitakaina amhākaṃ ayyāya thūpau bhin‍nau, handa naṃ ghātaimā’’ti, mantaisuṃ| añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī āyasmatau upālissa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| āyasmā upāli āyasmatau kappitakassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau āyasmā kappitakau vihārā nikkhamitvā nilīnau acchi| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau yaināyasmatau kappitakassa vihārau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatau kappitakassa vihāraṃ pāsāṇaihi ca laiḍḍūhi ca auttharāpaitvā, ‘‘matau kappitakau’’ti pak‍kamiṃsu|

atha  khau āyasmā kappitakau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| addasaṃsu khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau āyasmantaṃ kappitakaṃ piṇḍāya carantaṃ| disvāna aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘ayaṃ kappitakau jīvati, kau nu khau amhākaṃ mantaṃ saṃharī’’ti? assausuṃ khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau – ‘‘ayyaina kira upālinā amhākaṃ mantau saṃhaṭau’’ti| tā āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ ak‍kausiṃsu – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayaṃ kāsāvaṭau malamaj‍janau nihīnajac‍cau amhākaṃ mantaṃ saṃharissatī’’ti! yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti  – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ayyaṃ upāliṃ ak‍kausissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau upāliṃ ak‍kausantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau upāliṃ ak‍kausissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1029. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuṃ ak‍kausaiyya vā paribhāsaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

1030. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

bhikkhunti upasampan‍naṃ| ak‍kausaiyya vāti dasahi vā ak‍kausavatthūhi ak‍kausati aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina, āpatti pācittiyassa|

paribhāsaiyya vāti bhayaṃ upadaṃsaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1031. upasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñā ak‍kausati vā paribhāsati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai vaimatikā ak‍kausati vā paribhāsati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñā ak‍kausati vā paribhāsati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍naṃ  ak‍kausati vā paribhāsati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1032. anāpatti  atthapuraikkhārāya, dhammapuraikkhārāya, anusāsanipuraikkhārāya, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ

1033. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī bhaṇḍanakārikā hauti kalahakārikā vivādakārikā bhassakārikā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārikā| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī tassā kammai karīyamānai paṭik‍kausati| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī gāmakaṃ agamāsi kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau bhikkhunisaṅghau – ‘‘thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī pak‍kantā’’ti caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipi| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī gāmakai taṃ karaṇīyaṃ tīraitvā punadaiva sāvatthiṃ  pac‍cāgacchi| caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā āgacchantiyā naiva āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi na pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ pādakaṭhalikaṃ upanikkhipi; na pac‍cuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisi na pānīyaina āpucchi| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, ayyai, mayi āgacchantiyā naiva āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi na pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ pādakaṭhalikaṃ upanikkhipi; na pac‍cuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisi na pānīyaina āpucchī’’ti? ‘‘aivañhaitaṃ, ayyai, hauti yathā taṃ anāthāyā’’ti| ‘‘kissa pana tvaṃ, ayyai, anāthā’’ti? ‘‘imā maṃ, ayyai, bhikkhuniyau – ‘‘ayaṃ anāthā appañ‍ñātā, natthi  imissā kāci pativattā’’ti, āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipiṃsū’’ti| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī – ‘‘bālā aitā abyattā aitā naitā jānanti kammaṃ vā kammadausaṃ vā kammavipattiṃ vā kammasampattiṃ vā’’ti, caṇḍīkatā gaṇaṃ paribhāsi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā caṇḍīkatā gaṇaṃ paribhāsissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī caṇḍīkatā gaṇaṃ paribhāsatīti paribhāsīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā  …pai॰…kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī caṇḍīkatā gaṇaṃ  paribhāsissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1034. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī caṇḍīkatā gaṇaṃ paribhāsaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1035. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

caṇḍīkatā nāma kaudhanā vuc‍cati|

gaṇau nāma bhikkhunisaṅghau vuc‍cati|

paribhāsaiyyāti ‘‘bālā aitā abyattā aitā naitā jānanti kammaṃ vā kammadausaṃ vā kammavipattiṃ vā kammasampattiṃ vā’’ti paribhāsati, āpatti pācittiyassa| sambahulā bhikkhuniyau vā aikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ vā anupasampan‍naṃ vā paribhāsati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1036. anāpatti  atthapuraikkhārāya, dhammapuraikkhārāya, anusāsanipuraikkhārāya, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. catutthasikkhāpadaṃ

1037. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau bhikkhuniyau nimantaitvā bhaujaisi| bhikkhuniyau bhuttāvī bhuttāvinī (ka॰) pavāritā ñātikulāni gantvā aikac‍cā bhuñ‍jiṃsu aikac‍cā piṇḍapātaṃ ādāya agamaṃsu| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau paṭivissakai aitadavauca – ‘‘bhikkhuniyau mayā ayyā santappitā, aitha tumhaipi santappaissāmī’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kiṃ tvaṃ, ayyau, amhai santappaissasi! yāpi tayā nimantitā tāpi amhākaṃ gharāni āgantvā aikac‍cā bhuñ‍jiṃsu aikac‍cā  piṇḍapātaṃ  ādāya agamaṃsū’’ti| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau amhākaṃ gharai bhuñ‍jitvā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jissanti, na cāhaṃ paṭibalau yāvadatthaṃ dātu’’nti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau tassa brāhmaṇassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau bhuttāvī bhuttāvinī (ka॰) pavāritā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau bhuttāvī pavāritā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau bhuttāvī pavāritā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1038. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī nimantitā vā pavāritā vā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādaiyya vā bhuñ‍jaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

1039. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

nimantitā nāma pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaujanānaṃ añ‍ñataraina bhaujanaina nimantitā|

pavāritā nāma asanaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, bhaujanaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, hatthapāsai ṭhitā abhiharati, paṭikkhaipau pañ‍ñāyati|

khādanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – yāguṃ yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ khādanīyaṃ nāma|

bhaujanīyaṃ nāma pañ‍ca bhaujanāni – audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ|

‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1040. nimantitai  nimantitasañ‍ñā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| nimantitai vaimatikā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ  vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa| nimantitai animantitasañ‍ñā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā khādati vā bhuñ‍jati vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ  yāvajīvikaṃ āhāratthāya paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

1041. anāpatti nimantitā appavāritā, yāguṃ pivati, sāmikai apalaukaitvā bhuñ‍jati, yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jati, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ

1042. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñatarissā visikhāya piṇḍāya caramānā yaina añ‍ñataraṃ kulaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau tai manussā taṃ bhikkhuniṃ bhaujaitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘añ‍ñāpi, ayyai, bhikkhuniyau āgacchantū’’ti| atha khau sā bhikkhunī, ‘‘kathañhi nāma kathaṃ añ‍ñā (syā॰) bhikkhuniyau nāgacchaiyyu’’nti, bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘amukasmiṃ, ayyai, aukāsai vāḷā sunakhā caṇḍau balibaddau cikkhal‍lau aukāsau| mā khau tattha agamitthā’’ti| añ‍ñatarāpi bhikkhunī tassā visikhāya piṇḍāya caramānā yaina taṃ kulaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau tai manussā taṃ bhikkhuniṃ bhaujaitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa, ayyai, bhikkhuniyau na āgacchantī’’ti? atha khau sā bhikkhunī taisaṃ manussānaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| manussā  ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī kulaṃ maccharāyissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī kulaṃ  maccharāyatīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī kulaṃ  maccharāyissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1043. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī kulamaccharinī assa, pācittiya’’nti|

1044. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

kulaṃ nāma cattāri kulāni – khattiyakulaṃ, brāhmaṇakulaṃ, vaissakulaṃ, suddakulaṃ|

maccharinī assāti ‘‘kathaṃ bhikkhuniyau nāgacchaiyyu’’nti bhikkhunīnaṃ santikai kulassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti pācittiyassa| kulassa vā santikai bhikkhunīnaṃ avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1045. anāpatti kulaṃ na maccharāyantī santaṃyaiva ādīnavaṃ ācikkhati, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

1046. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhuniyau gāmakāvāsai vassaṃvuṭṭhā sāvatthiṃ agamaṃsu| bhikkhuniyau tā  bhikkhuniyau aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘katthāyyāyau vassaṃvuṭṭhā? kac‍ci auvādau iddhau ahausī’’ti? ‘‘natthayyai, tattha bhikkhū; kutau auvādau iddhau bhavissatī’’ti! yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ vasissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ vasantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ  vasissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1047. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ vasaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1048. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

abhikkhukau nāma āvāsau na sak‍kā hauti auvādāya vā saṃvāsāya vā gantuṃ| ‘‘vassaṃ vasissāmī’’ti saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti parivaiṇaṃ sammaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| saha aruṇuggamanā āpatti pācittiyassa|

1049. anāpatti vassupagatā bhikkhū pak‍kantā vā haunti vibbhantā vā kālaṅkatā vā pakkhasaṅkantā vā, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. sattamasikkhāpadaṃ

1050. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhuniyau gāmakāvāsai vassaṃvuṭṭhā sāvatthiṃ agamaṃsu| bhikkhuniyau tā bhikkhuniyau aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘katthāyyāyau vassaṃvuṭṭhā; kattha kac‍ci (syā॰) bhikkhusaṅghau pavāritau’’ti? ‘‘na mayaṃ, ayyai, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pavāraimā’’ti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau vassaṃvuṭṭhā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ na pavāraissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau vassaṃvuṭṭhā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ na pavāraintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau vassaṃvuṭṭhā  bhikkhusaṅghaṃ na pavāraissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1051. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī vassaṃvuṭṭhā ubhatausaṅghai tīhi ṭhānaihi na pavāraiyya diṭṭhaina vā sutaina vā parisaṅkāya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

1052. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

vassaṃvuṭṭhā nāma purimaṃ vā taimāsaṃ pacchimaṃ vā taimāsaṃ vuṭṭhā| ubhatausaṅghai tīhi ṭhānaihi na pavāraissāmi diṭṭhaina vā sutaina vā parisaṅkāya vā’’ti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai  āpatti pācittiyassa|

1053. anāpatti sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

1054. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau auvadanti| bhikkhuniyau chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aithāyyai auvādaṃ gamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘yampi mayaṃ, ayyai, gacchaiyyāma auvādassa kāraṇā, ayyā chabbaggiyā idhaiva āgantvā amhai auvadantī’’ti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau auvādaṃ na gacchissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau auvādaṃ na gacchantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau auvādaṃ  na gacchissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1055. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī auvādāya vā saṃvāsāya vā na gacchaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1056. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ  atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

auvādau nāma aṭṭha garudhammā|

saṃvāsau nāma aikakammaṃ aikuddaisau samasikkhatā| auvādāya vā saṃvāsāya vā na gacchissāmīti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1057. anāpatti sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā dutiyikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. navamasikkhāpadaṃ

1058. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau upausathampi na pucchanti auvādampi na yācanti| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau upausathampi na pucchissanti auvādampi na yācissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau ‘‘upausathampi na pucchanti auvādampi na yācantī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau upausathampi na pucchissanti auvādampi na yācissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1059. ‘‘anvaddhamāsaṃ bhikkhuniyā bhikkhusaṅghatau dvai dhammā pac‍cāsīsitabbā  – upausathapucchakañ‍ca auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca| taṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā pācittiya’’nti|

1060. anvaddhamāsanti  anupausathikaṃ| upausathau nāma dvai upausathā – cātuddasikau ca pan‍narasikau ca|

auvādau  nāma aṭṭha garudhammā| ‘‘upausathampi na pucchissāmi auvādampi na yācissāmī’’ti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1061. anāpatti sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā dutiyikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. dasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1062. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī pasākhai jātaṃ gaṇḍaṃ purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā bhaidāpaisi| atha khau sau purisau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ dūsaituṃ upak‍kami| sā vissaramakāsi| bhikkhuniyau upadhāvitvā taṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, ayyai, vissaramakāsī’’ti? atha khau sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī pasākhai jātaṃ gaṇḍaṃ purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā bhaidāpaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī pasākhai jātaṃ gaṇḍaṃ purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā bhaidāpaitīti  bhaidāpaisīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī pasākhai jātaṃ gaṇḍaṃ purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā bhaidāpaissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1063. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī pasākhai jātaṃ gaṇḍaṃ vā rudhitaṃ vā anapalaukaitvā saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā bhaidāpaiyya vā phālāpaiyya vā dhauvāpaiyya vā ālimpāpaiyya vā bandhāpaiyya vā maucāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

1064. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

pasākhaṃ nāma adhaunābhi ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ| jātanti tattha jātaṃ| gaṇḍau nāma yau kauci  gaṇḍau| rudhitaṃ nāma yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaṇaṃ| anapalaukaitvāti anāpucchā| saṅghau nāma bhikkhunisaṅghau vuc‍cati| gaṇau nāma sambahulā bhikkhuniyau vuc‍canti| purisau nāma manussapurisau, na yakkhau, na paitau, na tiracchānagatau, viñ‍ñū paṭibalau dūsaituṃ| saddhinti aikatau| aikainaikāti purisau caiva hauti bhikkhunī ca|

1065. ‘‘bhindā’’ti āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhin‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa| ‘‘phālaihī’’ti āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phālitai āpatti  pācittiyassa| ‘‘dhauvā’’ti āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dhauvitai dhautai (syā॰) āpatti pācittiyassa| ‘‘ālimpā’’ti āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| littai ālittai (syā॰) āpatti pācittiyassa| ‘‘bandhāhī’’ti āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| baddhai āpatti pācittiyassa| ‘‘maucaihī’’ti āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| muttai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1066. anāpatti apalaukaitvā bhaidāpaiti vā phālāpaiti vā dhauvāpaiti vā ālimpāpaiti vā bandhāpaiti vā maucāpaiti vā, yā kāci viñ‍ñū dutiyikā yā kāci viñ‍ñū dutiyā (syā॰), yau kauci viñ‍ñū dutiyau (ka॰) hauti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

ārāmavaggau chaṭṭhau|

7. gabbhinīvaggau

1. paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

1067. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau gabbhiniṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| sā piṇḍāya carati| manussā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘daithāyyāya bhikkhaṃ, garubhārā garugabbhā (syā॰) ayyā’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau gabbhiniṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau  appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau gabbhiniṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰…  sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau gabbhiniṃ vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau gabbhiniṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1068. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī gabbhiniṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1069. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

gabbhinī nāma āpan‍nasattā vuc‍cati| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1070. gabbhiniyā gabbhinisañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| gabbhiniyā vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gabbhiniyā agabbhinisañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, anāpatti| agabbhiniyā gabbhinisañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| agabbhiniyā vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| agabbhiniyā agabbhinisañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1071. anāpatti gabbhiniṃ agabbhinisañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, agabbhiniṃ agabbhinisañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ

1072. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau pāyantiṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| sā piṇḍāya carati| manussā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘daithāyyāya bhikkhaṃ, sadutiyikā ayyā’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau pāyantiṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau pāyantiṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau pāyantiṃ vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau pāyantiṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1073. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī pāyantiṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1074. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

pāyantī nāma mātā vā hauti hautu (ka॰) dhāti dhātī (sī॰ syā॰) vā|

vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|
