‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai  upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1075. pāyantiyā pāyantisañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| pāyantiyā vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pāyantiyā apāyantisañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, anāpatti| apāyantiyā  pāyantisañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apāyantiyā vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apāyantiyā apāyantisañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1076. anāpatti  pāyantiṃ apāyantisañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, apāyantiṃ apāyantisañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ

1077. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| tā bālā haunti abyattā na jānanti kappiyaṃ vā akappiyaṃ vā| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi  nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sikkhamānāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| tāya sikkhamānāya saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhikkhunīnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ahaṃ, ayyai, itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya sikkhamānā| saṅghaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

1078. ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya sikkhamānā saṅghaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ saṅghau  itthan‍nāmāya sikkhamānāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya sikkhamānā saṅghaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya sikkhamānāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ daiti| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya sikkhamānāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiyā dānaṃ, sā tuṇhassa; yassā  nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmāya sikkhamānāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

1079. sā sikkhamānā ‘‘aivaṃ vadaihī’’ti vattabbā – ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiṃ dvai vassāni avītik‍kamma samādānaṃ samādiyāmi| adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiṃ dvai vassāni avītik‍kamma samādānaṃ samādiyāmi| abrahmacariyā vairamaṇiṃ dvai vassāni avītik‍kamma samādānaṃ samādiyāmi| musāvādā vairamaṇiṃ dvai vassāni avītik‍kamma samādānaṃ samādiyāmi| surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇiṃ dvai vassāni avītik‍kamma samādānaṃ samādiyāmi| vikālabhaujanā vairamaṇiṃ dvai vassāni avītik‍kamma samādānaṃ samādiyāmī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā tā bhikkhuniyau anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1080. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1081. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

dvai  vassānīti dvai saṃvaccharāni| asikkhitasikkhā  nāma sikkhā vā na din‍nā hauti, din‍nā vā sikkhā kupitā| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi  duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1082. dhammakammai  dhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1083. anāpatti dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. catutthasikkhāpadaṃ

1084. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘aitha, sikkhamānā, imaṃ jānātha, imaṃ daitha, imaṃ āharatha, iminā atthau, imaṃ kappiyaṃ karauthā’’ti| tā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ, ayyai, sikkhamānā| bhikkhuniyau maya’’nti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu  sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ saṅghaina  asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| tāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhikkhunīnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ  nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ahaṃ, ayyai, itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā sikkhamānā saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

1085. ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā sikkhamānā saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā sikkhamānā saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ daiti| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya vuṭṭhānasammutiyā dānaṃ, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya vuṭṭhānasammuti; khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā tā bhikkhuniyau anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1086. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1087. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

dvai  vassānīti dvai saṃvaccharāni| sikkhitasikkhā nāma chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā| asammatā nāma ñattidutiyaina kammaina vuṭṭhānasammuti na din‍nāhauti| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya  āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1088. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1089. anāpatti dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ saṅghaina sammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ

1090. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau ūnadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| tā akkhamā haunti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ| uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ anadhivāsakajātikā haunti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau ūnadvādasavassaṃ  gihigataṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau ūnadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi  buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi  nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau ūnadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! ūnadvādasavassā ūnadvādasavassāhi (ka॰), bhikkhavai, gihigatā akkhamā hauti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ| uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ anadhivāsakajātikā hauti| dvādasavassāva dvādasavassā ca (syā॰ ka॰) khau, bhikkhavai, gihigatā khamā hauti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ| uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikā hauti| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1091. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī ūnadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1092. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

ūnadvādasavassā nāma appattadvādasavassā| gihigatā nāma purisantaragatā vuc‍cati| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ  vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1093. ūnadvādasavassāya  ūnadvādasavassasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| ūnadvādasavassāya vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnadvādasavassāya paripuṇṇasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, anāpatti|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya  ūnadvādasavassasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya paripuṇṇasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1094. anāpatti ūnadvādasavassaṃ paripuṇṇasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ paripuṇṇasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

1095. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| tā bālā haunti abyattā na jānanti kappiyaṃ vā akappiyaṃ vā| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ  kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| tāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhikkhunīnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ahaṃ, ayyai, itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassā gihigatā saṅghaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ  yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

1096. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassā gihigatā saṅghaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu  sikkhāsammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassā gihigatā saṅghaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ daiti| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiyā dānaṃ, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

sā paripuṇṇadvādasavassā gihigatā aivaṃ vadaihīti vattabbā – ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiṃ dvai vassāni avītik‍kamma samādānaṃ samādiyāmi…pai॰… vikālabhaujanā vairamaṇiṃ dvai vassāni avītik‍kamma samādānaṃ samādiyāmī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā tā bhikkhuniyau anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1097. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1098. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassā  nāma pattadvādasavassā| gihigatā nāma purisantaragatā vuc‍cati| dvai vassānīti  dvai saṃvaccharāni| asikkhitasikkhā nāma sikkhā vā na din‍nā hauti, din‍nā vā sikkhā kupitā| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti  gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1099. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1100. anāpatti paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. sattamasikkhāpadaṃ

1101. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ  gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘aitha sikkhamānā, imaṃ jānātha, imaṃ daitha, imaṃ āharatha, iminā atthau, imaṃ kappiyaṃ karauthā’’ti| tā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ, ayyai, sikkhamānā, bhikkhuniyau maya’’nti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ  vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammattaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma bhikkhavai bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina  asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| tāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya  gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhikkhunīnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ahaṃ, ayyai, itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya  ayyāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassā gihigatā dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā, saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau|

1102. ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassā gihigatā dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassā gihigatā dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ daiti| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya vuṭṭhānasammutiyā dānaṃ, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya gihigatāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya vuṭṭhānasammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā tā bhikkhuniyau anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau  imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1103. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1104. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassā nāma pattadvādasavassā| gihigatā nāma purisantaragatā vuc‍cati| dvai vassānīti dvai saṃvaccharāni| sikkhitasikkhā nāma chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā| asammatā nāma ñattidutiyaina kammaina vuṭṭhānasammuti na din‍nā hauti| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1105. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1106. anāpatti paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu  dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina sammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

1107. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā dvai vassāni naiva anuggaṇhāti na anuggaṇhāpaiti| tā  bālā haunti abyattā; na jānanti kappiyaṃ vā akappiyaṃ vā| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā dvai vassāni naiva anuggaṇhissati  na anuggaṇhāpaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā dvai vassāni naiva anuggaṇhāti na anuggaṇhāpaitīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā dvai vassāni naiva anuggaṇhissati na anuggaṇhāpaissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1108. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā dvai vassāni naiva anuggaṇhaiyya na anuggaṇhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1109. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ  atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

sahajīvinī nāma saddhivihārinī vuc‍cati| vuṭṭhāpaitvāti upasampādaitvā| dvai vassānīti dvai saṃvaccharāni|

naiva anuggaṇhaiyyāti na sayaṃ anuggaṇhaiyya uddaisaina paripucchāya auvādaina anusāsaniyā|

na anuggaṇhāpaiyyāti na añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiyya ‘‘dvai vassāni naiva anuggaṇhissāmi na anuggaṇhāpaissāmī’’ti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1110. anāpatti  sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. navamasikkhāpadaṃ

1111. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau vuṭṭhāpitaṃ  pavattiniṃ dvai vassāni nānubandhanti| tā bālā haunti abyattā; na jānanti kappiyaṃ vā akappiyaṃ vā| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau vuṭṭhāpitaṃ pavattiniṃ dvai vassāni nānubandhissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau vuṭṭhāpitaṃ pavattiniṃ dvai vassāni nānubandhantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau vuṭṭhāpitaṃ pavattiniṃ dvai vassāni nānubandhissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1112. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī vuṭṭhāpitaṃ pavattiniṃ dvai vassāni nānubandhaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1113. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

vuṭṭhāpitanti upasampāditaṃ| pavattinī nāma upajjhāyā vuc‍cati| dvai vassānīti dvai saṃvaccharāni| nānubandhaiyyāti na sayaṃ upaṭṭhahaiyya| dvai vassāni nānubandhissāmīti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1114. anāpatti upajjhāyā bālā vā hauti alaj‍jinī vā, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. dasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1115. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā naiva vūpakāsaiti na vūpakāsāpaiti| sāmikau aggahaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā naiva vūpakāsaissati na vūpakāsāpaissati, sāmikau aggahaisi! sacāyaṃ bhikkhunī pak‍kantā assa, na ca sāmikau  gaṇhaiyyā’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā naiva vūpakāsaiti na vūpakāsāpaiti …si (ka॰), sāmikau aggahaisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā naiva vūpakāsaissati na vūpakāsāpaissati, sāmikau aggahaisi! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1116. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā naiva vūpakāsaiyya na vūpakāsāpaiyya antamasau chappañ‍cayaujanānipi, pācittiya’’nti|

1117. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

sahajīvinī nāma saddhivihārinī vuc‍cati| vuṭṭhāpaitvāti upasampādaitvā| naiva  vūpakāsaiyyāti na sayaṃ vūpakāsaiyya| na vūpakāsāpaiyyāti na añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiyya| ‘‘naiva vūpakāsaissāmi na vūpakāsāpaissāmi antamasau chappañ‍cayaujanānipī’’ti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1118. anāpatti sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā dutiyikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

gabbhinivaggau sattamau|

8. kumārībhūtavaggau

1. paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

1119. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau ūnavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| tā akkhamā haunti sītassa uṇhassa  jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ| uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ anadhivāsakajātikā haunti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau ūnavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau ūnavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau ūnavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! ūnavīsativassā, bhikkhavai, kumāribhūtā akkhamā hauti sītassa uṇhassa…pai॰… pāṇaharānaṃ anadhivāsakajātikā hauti| vīsativassāva khau, bhikkhavai, kumāribhūtā khamā hauti sītassa uṇhassa…pai॰… pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikā hauti| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1120. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī ūnavīsativassaṃ  kumāribhūtaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1121. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

ūnavīsativassā nāma appattavīsativassā| kumāribhūtā nāma sāmaṇairī vuc‍cati| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai  upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1122. ūnavīsativassāya ūnavīsativassasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| ūnavīsativassāya vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnavīsativassāya paripuṇṇasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, anāpatti| paripuṇṇavīsativassāya ūnavīsativassasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paripuṇṇavīsativassāya  vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| paripuṇṇavīsativassāya paripuṇṇasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1123. anāpatti ūnavīsativassaṃ paripuṇṇasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ paripuṇṇasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ

1124. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| tā  bālā haunti abyattā; na jānanti kappiyaṃ vā akappiyaṃ vā| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhārasavassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana bhikkhavai dātabbā| tāya aṭṭhārasavassāya kumāribhūtāya saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhikkhunīnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ahaṃ, ayyai, itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya  aṭṭhārasavassā kumāribhūtā saṅghaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

1125. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya aṭṭhārasavassā kumāribhūtā saṅghaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya aṭṭhārasavassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya aṭṭhārasavassā kumāribhūtā saṅghaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya aṭṭhārasavassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiṃ daiti| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya aṭṭhārasavassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammutiyā dānaṃ, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmāya aṭṭhārasavassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhāsammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

sā aṭṭhārasavassā kumāribhūtā aivaṃ vadaihīti vattabbā – ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiṃ dvai vassāni avītik‍kamma samādānaṃ samādiyāmi…pai॰…  vikālabhaujanā vairamaṇiṃ dvai vassāni avītik‍kamma samādānaṃ samādiyāmī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā tā bhikkhuniyau anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1126. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1127. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

paripuṇṇavīsativassā  nāma pattavīsativassā| kumāribhūtā nāma sāmaṇairī vuc‍cati| dvai vassānīti dvai saṃvaccharāni| asikkhitasikkhā nāma sikkhā vā na din‍nā hauti, din‍nā vā sikkhā kupitā| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti  gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1128. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1129. anāpatti paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ

1130. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘aitha sikkhamānā, imaṃ jānātha, imaṃ daitha, imaṃ āharatha, iminā atthau, imaṃ kappiyaṃ karauthā’’ti| tā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ, ayyai, sikkhamānā; bhikkhuniyau maya’’nti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina  asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau  paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paripuṇṇavīsativassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| tāya paripuṇṇavīsativassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhikkhunīnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ahaṃ, ayyai, itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya paripuṇṇavīsativassā kumāribhūtā dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

1131. ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya paripuṇṇavīsativassā kumāribhūtā dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇavīsativassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu  sikkhitasikkhāya vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya paripuṇṇavīsativassā kumāribhūtā dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇavīsativassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya vuṭṭhānasammutiṃ daiti| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇavīsativassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya vuṭṭhānasammutiyā dānaṃ, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā  saṅghaina itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇavīsativassāya kumāribhūtāya dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya vuṭṭhānasammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā tā bhikkhuniyau anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1132. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1133. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

paripuṇṇavīsativassā nāma pattavīsativassā| kumāribhūtā nāma sāmaṇairī vuc‍cati| dvai vassānīti dvai saṃvaccharāni| sikkhitasikkhā nāma chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā| asammatā nāma ñattidutiyaina kammaina vuṭṭhānasammuti na din‍nā hauti| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1134. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1135. anāpatti  paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina sammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. catutthasikkhāpadaṃ

1136. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau ūnadvādasavassā vuṭṭhāpainti| tā bālā haunti abyattā na jānanti  kappiyaṃ  vā akappiyaṃ vā| saddhivihāriniyaupi bālā haunti abyattā; na jānanti kappiyaṃ vā akappiyaṃ vā| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau ūnadvādasavassā vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau ūnadvādasavassā vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau ūnadvādasavassā vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1137. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī ūnadvādasavassā vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1138. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

ūnadvādasavassā nāma appattadvādasavassā|

vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1139. anāpatti  paripuṇṇadvādasavassā vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ

1140. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassā saṅghaina asammatā vuṭṭhāpainti| tā bālā haunti abyattā; na jānanti kappiyaṃ vā akappiyaṃ vā| saddhivihāriniyaupi bālā haunti  abyattā; na jānanti kappiyaṃ vā akappiyaṃ vā| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassā saṅghaina asammatā vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassā saṅghaina asammatā vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassā saṅghaina asammatā vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya bhikkhuniyā vuṭṭhāpanasammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| tāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya bhikkhuniyā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ahaṃ, ayyai, itthan‍nāmā paripuṇṇadvādasavassā bhikkhunī saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhāpanasammutiṃ  yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| sā bhikkhunī saṅghaina paricchinditabbā – ‘‘byattāyaṃ bhikkhunī laj‍jinī’’ti| sacai bālā ca hauti alaj‍jinī ca, na dātabbā| sacai bālā hauti laj‍jinī, na dātabbā| sacai byattā hauti alaj‍jinī, na dātabbā| sacai byattā ca hauti laj‍jinī ca, dātabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

1141. ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā paripuṇṇadvādasavassā bhikkhunī saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhāpanasammutiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya bhikkhuniyā vuṭṭhāpanasammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā paripuṇṇadvādasavassā bhikkhunī saṅghaṃ vuṭṭhāpanasammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya bhikkhuniyā vuṭṭhāpanasammutiṃ daiti| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya bhikkhuniyā vuṭṭhāpanasammutiyā dānaṃ, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmāya paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya bhikkhuniyā vuṭṭhāpanasammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā tā bhikkhuniyau anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1142. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī paripuṇṇadvādasavassā saṅghaina asammatā vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1143. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassā nāma pattadvādasavassā|

asammatā nāma ñattidutiyaina kammaina vuṭṭhāpanasammuti na din‍nā hauti| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti  upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1144. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai vaimatikā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā vuṭṭhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

adhammakammai  dhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1145. anāpatti paripuṇṇadvādasavassā saṅghaina sammatā vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

1146. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa  ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī  bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā vuṭṭhāpanasammutiṃ yācati| atha khau bhikkhunisaṅghau caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ paricchinditvā – ‘‘alaṃ tāva tai, ayyai, vuṭṭhāpitainā’’ti, vuṭṭhāpanasammutiṃ na adāsi| caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī ‘sādhū’ti paṭissuṇi| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhunisaṅghau añ‍ñāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ vuṭṭhāpanasammutiṃ daiti| caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘ahamaiva nūna bālā, ahamaiva nūna alaj‍jinī; yaṃ saṅghau añ‍ñāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ vuṭṭhāpanasammutiṃ daiti, mayhamaiva na daitī’’ti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā caṇḍakāḷī – ‘alaṃ tāva tai, ayyai, vuṭṭhāpitainā’ti vuc‍camānā ‘sādhū’ti paṭissuṇitvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, ‘caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī alaṃ tāva tai ayyai vuṭṭhāpitainā’ti vuc‍camānā ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jatīti āpaj‍jīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī – ‘‘alaṃ tāva tai, ayyai, vuṭṭhāpitainā’’ti vuc‍camānā ‘sādhū’ti paṭissuṇitvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1147. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī – ‘alaṃ tāva tai, ayyai, vuṭṭhāpitainā’ti vuc‍camānā ‘sādhū’ti  paṭissuṇitvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1148. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

alaṃ tāva tai ayyai vuṭṭhāpitaināti alaṃ tāva tai, ayyai, upasampāditaina| ‘sādhū’ti paṭissuṇitvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1149. anāpatti pakatiyā chandā dausā mauhā bhayā karauntaṃ khiyyati, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. sattamasikkhāpadaṃ

1150. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā sikkhamānā thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ upasaṅkamitvā upasampadaṃ yāci| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī taṃ sikkhamānaṃ – ‘‘sacai mai tvaṃ, ayyai, cīvaraṃ dassasi aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā, naiva vuṭṭhāpaiti na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauti| atha khau sā sikkhamānā bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā sikkhamānaṃ – ‘sacai mai tvaṃ, ayyai, cīvaraṃ dassasi aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’ti vatvā, naiva vuṭṭhāpaissati na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sikkhamānaṃ – ‘‘sacai mai tvaṃ, ayyai, cīvaraṃ dassasi aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā, naiva vuṭṭhāpaiti na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karautīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sikkhamānaṃ – ‘‘sacai mai tvaṃ, ayyai, cīvaraṃ dassasi aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā, naiva vuṭṭhāpaissati na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1151. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī sikkhamānaṃ – ‘sacai mai tvaṃ, ayyai, cīvaraṃ dassasi, aivāhaṃ  taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’ti vatvā, sā pacchā anantarāyikinī naiva vuṭṭhāpaiyya na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1152. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

sikkhamānā nāma dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā|

sacai mai tvaṃ ayyai cīvaraṃ dassasi aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmīti aivāhaṃ taṃ upasampādaissāmi|

sā pacchā anantarāyikinīti asati antarāyai|

naiva vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti na sayaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya|

na  vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyyāti na añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiyya| ‘‘naiva vuṭṭhāpaissāmi na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissāmī’’ti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1153. anāpatti sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

1154. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā sikkhamānā thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ upasaṅkamitvā upasampadaṃ yāci| thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī taṃ sikkhamānaṃ ‘‘sacai maṃ tvaṃ, ayyai, dvai vassāni anubandhissasi aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā, naiva vuṭṭhāpaiti na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauti| atha khau sā sikkhamānā bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā sikkhamānaṃ  – sacai maṃ tvaṃ, ayyai, dvai vassāni anubandhissasi aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmīti vatvā, naiva vuṭṭhāpaissati na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sikkhamānaṃ – ‘‘sacai maṃ tvaṃ ayyai dvai vassāni anubandhissasi aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā, naiva vuṭṭhāpaiti na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karautīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sikkhamānaṃ – ‘‘sacai maṃ tvaṃ, ayyai, dvai vassāni anubandhissasi aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā, naiva vuṭṭhāpaissati na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ  sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1155. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī sikkhamānaṃ – ‘sacai maṃ tvaṃ ayyai dvai vassāni anubandhissasi aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’ti vatvā, sā pacchā anantarāyikinī  naiva vuṭṭhāpaiyya na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1156. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

sikkhamānā nāma dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā|

sacai maṃ tvaṃ ayyai dvai vassāni anubandhissasīti dvai saṃvaccharāni upaṭṭhahissasi|

aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmīti aivāhaṃ taṃ upasampādaissāmi|

sā pacchā anantarāyikinīti asati antarāyai|

naiva vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti na sayaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya|

na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyyāti na añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiyya| ‘‘naiva vuṭṭhāpaissāmi na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karissāmī’’ti dhuraṃ nikkhittamattai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1157. anāpatti  sati antarāyai, pariyaisitvā na labhati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aṭṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. navamasikkhāpadaṃ

1158. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ saukavassaṃ (sī॰) saukāvassaṃ (syā॰) caṇḍakāḷiṃ sikkhamānaṃ  vuṭṭhāpaiti| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ saukavassaṃ (sī॰) saukāvassaṃ (syā॰) caṇḍakāḷiṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ saukavassaṃ (sī॰) saukāvassaṃ (syā॰) caṇḍakāḷiṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitīti vuṭṭhāpaisīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau  bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ  caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ caṇḍakāḷiṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1159. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1160. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

purisau nāma pattavīsativassau| kumārakau nāma appattavīsativassau| saṃsaṭṭhā nāma ananulaumikaina kāyikavācasikaina saṃsaṭṭhā| caṇḍī nāma kaudhanā vuc‍cati|

saukāvāsā nāma paraisaṃ dukkhaṃ uppādaiti, saukaṃ āvisati| sikkhamānā nāma dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti  gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ  vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa; gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1161. anāpatti ajānantī vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

navamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. dasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1162. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī mātāpitūhipi sāmikainapi ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti| mātāpitaraupi sāmikaupi ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma  ayyā thul‍lanandā amhaihi ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissatī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau mātāpitūnampi sāmikassapi ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā mātāpitūhipi sāmikainapi ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī, mātāpitūhipi  sāmikainapi ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī mātāpitūhipi sāmikainapi ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1163. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī mātāpitūhi vā sāmikaina vā ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1164. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

mātāpitarau  nāma janakā vuc‍canti| sāmikau nāma yaina pariggahitā hauti| ananuñ‍ñātāti anāpucchā| sikkhamānā nāma dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

vuṭṭhāpaissāmīti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1165. anāpatti ajānantī vuṭṭhāpaiti, apalaukaitvā vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

11. aikādasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1166. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī – ‘‘sikkhamānaṃ  vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti  thairai bhikkhū san‍nipātaitvā pahūtaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ passitvā – ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, ayyā, sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti thairai bhikkhū uyyaujaitvā daivadattaṃ kaukālikaṃ kaṭamaudakatissakaṃ khaṇḍadaiviyā puttaṃ samuddadattaṃ san‍nipātaitvā sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā thul‍lanandā pārivāsikachandadānaina sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī pārivāsikachandadānaina sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitīti vuṭṭhāpaisīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī pārivāsikachandadānaina sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1167. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī pārivāsikachandadānaina sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1168. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

pārivāsikachandadānaināti  vuṭṭhitāya parisāya| sikkhamānā nāma dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1169. anāpatti avuṭṭhitāya parisāya vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aikādasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

12. dvādasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1170. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau anuvassaṃ  vuṭṭhāpainti, upassayau na sammati| manussā manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā (sī॰) ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau anuvassaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti, upassayau na sammatī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau anuvassaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau anuvassaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau anuvassaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1171. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī anuvassaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1172. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

anuvassanti anusaṃvaccharaṃ| vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti  upasampādaiyya|

‘‘vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1173. anāpatti aikantarikaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dvādasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

13. tairasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1174. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau aikaṃ vassaṃ dvai vuṭṭhāpainti| upassayau tathaiva na sammati| manussā manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā (sī॰) tathaiva ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti  – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau aikaṃ vassaṃ dvai vuṭṭhāpaissanti! upassayau tathaiva na sammatī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau aikaṃ vassaṃ dvai vuṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau aikaṃ vassaṃ dvai vuṭṭhāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi  nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau aikaṃ vassaṃ dvai vuṭṭhāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1175. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī aikaṃ vassaṃ dvai vuṭṭhāpaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1176. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

aikaṃ vassanti aikaṃ saṃvaccharaṃ| dvai vuṭṭhāpaiyyāti dvai upasampādaiyya|

‘‘dvai vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti gaṇaṃ vā ācariniṃ vā pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā pariyaisati, sīmaṃ vā samman‍nati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ| dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā| kammavācāpariyausānai upajjhāyāya āpatti pācittiyassa| gaṇassa ca ācariniyā ca āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

1177. anāpatti aikantarikaṃ aikaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

tairasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

kumārībhūtavaggau aṭṭhamau|

9. chattupāhanavaggau

1. paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ

1178. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau chattupāhanaṃ dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi  nāma bhikkhuniyau chattupāhanaṃ dhāraissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau chattupāhanaṃ dhāraissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau chattupāhanaṃ dhāraintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā  bhikkhuniyau chattupāhanaṃ dhāraissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī chattupāhanaṃ dhāraiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

1179. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī gilānā hauti| tassā  vinā chattupāhanaṃ na phāsu hauti…pai॰… bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānāya bhikkhuniyā chattupāhanaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1180. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā chattupāhanaṃ dhāraiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1181. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

agilānā nāma yassā vinā chattupāhanaṃ phāsu hauti|

gilānā nāma yassā vinā chattupāhanaṃ  na phāsu hauti|

chattaṃ nāma tīṇi chattāni – saitacchattaṃ, kilañ‍jacchattaṃ, paṇṇacchattaṃ maṇḍalabaddhaṃ salākabaddhaṃ| dhāraiyyāti sakimpi dhāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1182. agilānā agilānasañ‍ñā chattupāhanaṃ dhāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| agilānā vaimatikā chattupāhanaṃ dhāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| agilānā gilānasañ‍ñā chattupāhanaṃ dhāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

chattaṃ  dhāraiti na upāhanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| upāhanaṃ dhāraiti na chattaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānā agilānasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānā vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānā gilānasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1183. anāpatti gilānāya, ārāmai ārāmūpacārai dhāraiti, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

paṭhamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ

1184. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau yānaina yāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau yānaina yāyissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰ … tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau yānaina yāyissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau yānaina yāyantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau yānaina yāyissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī yānaina yāyaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

1185. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī gilānā hauti, na sak‍kauti padasā gantuṃ…pai॰… bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānāya bhikkhuniyā yānaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1186. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī agilānā yānaina yāyaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1187. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

agilānā nāma sak‍kauti padasā gantuṃ|

gilānā nāma na sak‍kauti padasā gantuṃ|

yānaṃ nāma vayhaṃ rathau sakaṭaṃ sandamānikā sivikā pāṭaṅkī|

yāyaiyyāti sakimpi yānaina yāyati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1188. agilānā agilānasañ‍ñā yānaina yāyati, āpatti pācittiyassa| agilānā vaimatikā yānaina yāyati, āpatti pācittiyassa| agilānā gilānasañ‍ñā yānaina yāyati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

gilānā agilānasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānā vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānā gilānasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1189. anāpatti gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dutiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ

1190. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī añ‍ñatarissā itthiyā kulūpikā hauti| atha khau sā itthī taṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handāyyai, imaṃ saṅghāṇiṃ amukāya nāma itthiyā daihī’’ti| atha khau sā bhikkhunī – ‘‘sacāhaṃ pattaina ādāya gacchāmi vissarau mai bhavissatī’’ti paṭimuñ‍citvā agamāsi| tassā rathikāya suttakai chin‍nai vippakiriyiṃsu| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi  nāma bhikkhuniyau saṅghāṇiṃ  dhāraissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰ … tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī saṅghāṇiṃ dhāraissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī  saṅghāṇiṃ dhāraitī’’ti dhāraisīti (ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī saṅghāṇiṃ dhāraissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1191. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī saṅghāṇiṃ dhāraiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1192. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

saṅghāṇi nāma yā kāci kaṭūpagā|

dhāraiyyāti sakimpi dhāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1193. anāpatti ābādhappac‍cayā, kaṭisuttakaṃ dhāraiti, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

tatiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. catutthasikkhāpadaṃ

1194. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau itthālaṅkāraṃ dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau itthālaṅkāraṃ dhāraissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau itthālaṅkāraṃ dhāraissantī’’ti  …pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau itthālaṅkāraṃ dhāraintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi  nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau itthālaṅkāraṃ  dhāraissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1195. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī itthālaṅkāraṃ dhāraiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1196. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

itthālaṅkārau nāma sīsūpagau gīvūpagau hatthūpagau pādūpagau kaṭūpagau| dhāraiyyāti sakimpi dhāraiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1197. anāpatti ābādhapac‍cayā, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

catutthasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ

1198. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau gandhavaṇṇakaina nahāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau gandhavaṇṇakaina nahāyissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ  ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau gandhavaṇṇakaina nahāyissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau gandhavaṇṇakaina nahāyantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau gandhavaṇṇakaina nahāyissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1199. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī gandhavaṇṇakaina nahāyaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1200. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

gandhau  nāma yau kauci gandhau| vaṇṇakaṃ nāma yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaṇṇakaṃ| nahāyaiyyāti nahāyati| payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, nahānapariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1201. anāpatti ābādhappac‍cayā, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

pañ‍camasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ

1202. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau vāsitakaina piñ‍ñākaina nahāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti  vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau vāsitakaina piñ‍ñākaina nahāyissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau vāsitakaina piñ‍ñākaina nahāyissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau vāsitakaina piñ‍ñākaina nahāyantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau vāsitakaina piñ‍ñākaina nahāyissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1203. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī vāsitakaina piñ‍ñākaina nahāyaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1204. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

vāsitakaṃ  nāma yaṃ kiñ‍ci gandhavāsitakaṃ| piñ‍ñākaṃ nāma tilapiṭṭhaṃ vuc‍cati| nahāyaiyyāti nahāyati| payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, nahānapariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa|

1205. anāpatti  ābādhappac‍cayā, pakatipiñ‍ñākaina nahāyati, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

chaṭṭhasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. sattamasikkhāpadaṃ

1206. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa  ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau bhikkhuniyā ummaddāpaintipi parimaddāpaintipi| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau bhikkhuniyā ummaddāpaissantipi parimaddāpaissantipi saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau bhikkhuniyā ummaddāpaissantipi parimaddāpaissantipī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau bhikkhuniyā ummaddāpaintipi parimaddāpaintipīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau bhikkhuniyā ummaddāpaissantipi parimaddāpaissantipi! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1207. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā ummaddāpaiyya vā parimaddāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

1208. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

bhikkhuniyāti  añ‍ñāya bhikkhuniyā| ummaddāpaiyya vāti ummaddāpaiti ubbaṭṭāpaiti (sī॰), āpatti pācittiyassa| parimaddāpaiyya vāti sambāhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1209. anāpatti  gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

sattamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8-9-10. aṭṭhama-navama-dasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1210. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau sikkhamānāya ummaddāpaintipi parimaddāpaintipi…pai॰… sāmaṇairiyā ummaddāpaintipi parimaddāpaintipi…pai॰… gihiniyā ummaddāpaintipi parimaddāpaintipi| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma  bhikkhuniyau gihiniyā ummaddāpaissantipi parimaddāpaissantipi, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau gihiniyā ummaddāpaissantipi parimaddāpaissantipīti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau gihiniyā ummaddāpaintipi parimaddāpaintipīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau gihiniyā ummaddāpaissantipi parimaddāpaissantipi! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1211. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī (sikkhamānāya…pai॰… sāmaṇairiyā…pai॰…) gihiniyā ummaddāpaiyya vā parimaddāpaiyya vā, pācittiya’’nti|

1212. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ  atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

sikkhamānā nāma dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhā|

sāmaṇairī  nāma dasasikkhāpadikā|

gihinī nāma agārinī vuc‍cati|

ummaddāpaiyya  vāti ummaddāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

parimaddāpaiyya vāti sambāhāpaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1213. anāpatti gilānāya ābādhappac‍cayā (ka॰), āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aṭṭhama navama dasamasikkhāpadāni niṭṭhitāni|

11. aikādasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1214. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau bhikkhussa puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdanti| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau bhikkhussa puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau bhikkhussa puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau bhikkhussa puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1215. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī bhikkhussa puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1216. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

bhikkhussa  puratauti upasampan‍nassa puratau| anāpucchāti anapalaukaitvā| āsanai nisīdaiyyāti antamasau chamāyapi nisīdati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1217. anāpucchitai anāpucchitasañ‍ñā āsanai nisīdati, āpatti pācittiyassa| anāpucchitai vaimatikā āsanai nisīdati, āpatti pācittiyassa| anāpucchitai āpucchitasañ‍ñā  āsanai nisīdati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

āpucchitai anāpucchitasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āpucchitai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āpucchitai āpucchitasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1218. anāpatti āpucchā āsanai nisīdati, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aikādasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

12. dvādasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1219. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchanti| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ  pucchantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1220. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1221. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

anaukāsakatanti  anāpucchā| bhikkhunti upasampan‍naṃ| pañhaṃ pucchaiyyāti suttantai aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā vinayaṃ vā abhidhammaṃ vā pucchati, āpatti pācittiyassa| vinayai aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā suttantaṃ vā abhidhammaṃ vā pucchati, āpatti pācittiyassa| abhidhammai aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā suttantaṃ vā vinayaṃ vā pucchati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

1222. anāpucchitai  anāpucchitasañ‍ñā pañhaṃ pucchati, āpatti pācittiyassa| anāpucchitai vaimatikā pañhaṃ pucchati, āpatti pācittiyassa| anāpucchitai āpucchitasañ‍ñā pañhaṃ pucchati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

āpucchitai anāpucchitasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āpucchitai vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āpucchitai āpucchitasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1223. anāpatti aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā pucchati, anaudissa aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā yattha katthaci pucchati, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

dvādasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

13. tairasamasikkhāpadaṃ

1224. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina  añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī asaṅkac‍cikā asaṅkacchikā (syā॰) gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| tassā rathikāya vātamaṇḍalikā saṅghāṭiyau ukkhipiṃsu| manussā uk‍kuṭṭhiṃ akaṃsu – ‘‘sundarā ayyāya thanudarā’’ti| sā bhikkhunī taihi manussaihi uppaṇḍiyamānā maṅku ahausi| atha khau sā bhikkhunī upassayaṃ gantvā bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī asaṅkac‍cikā gāmaṃ pavisissatī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī asaṅkac‍cikā gāmaṃ pāvisīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī asaṅkac‍cikā gāmaṃ pavisissati! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1225. ‘‘yā  pana bhikkhunī asaṅkac‍cikā asaṅkacchikā (syā॰) gāmaṃ pavisaiyya, pācittiya’’nti|

1226. yā  panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

asaṅkac‍cikāti vinā saṅkac‍cikaṃ|

saṅkac‍cikaṃ  nāma adhakkhakaṃ ubbhanābhi, tassa paṭicchādanatthāya|

gāmaṃ pavisaiyyāti parikkhittassa gāmassa parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā āpatti pācittiyassa| aparikkhittassa gāmassa upacāraṃ auk‍kamantiyā āpatti pācittiyassa|

1227. anāpatti acchin‍nacīvarikāya, naṭṭhacīvarikāya, gilānāya, assatiyā, ajānantiyā, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

tairasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

chattupāhanavaggau navamau|

uddiṭṭhā khau, ayyāyau, chasaṭṭhisatā pācittiyā dhammā| tatthāyyāyau pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

khuddakaṃ samattaṃ|

bhikkhunivibhaṅgai pācittiyakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



5. pāṭidaisanīyakaṇḍaṃ (bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau)

1. paṭhamapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ

imai khau panāyyāyau aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

1228. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissanti! kassa sampan‍naṃ na manāpaṃ, kassa sāduṃ na ruc‍catī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā, bhikkhuniyau sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ tāya bhikkhuniyā – ‘gārayhaṃ, ayyai, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā  bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

1229. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau gilānā haunti| gilānapucchikā bhikkhuniyau gilānā bhikkhuniyau aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kac‍ci, ayyai, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīya’’nti? ‘‘pubbai mayaṃ, ayyai, sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jāma,  taina nau phāsu hauti; idāni pana ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhitta’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na viñ‍ñāpaima, taina nau na phāsu hautī’’ti…pai॰… bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānāya bhikkhuniyā sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1230. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ tāya bhikkhuniyā – ‘gārayhaṃ, ayyai, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’’ti|

1231. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

agilānā nāma yassā vinā sappinā phāsu hauti|

gilānā nāma yassā vinā sappinā na phāsu hauti|

sappi nāma gausappi vā ajikāsappi vā mahiṃsasappi vā| yaisaṃ maṃsaṃ kappati taisaṃ sappi|

agilānā attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina ‘‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai  āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

1232. agilānā agilānasañ‍ñā sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| agilānā vaimatikā sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| agilānā gilānasañ‍ñā sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

gilānā  agilānasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānā vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānā gilānasañ‍ñā, anāpatti|

1233. anāpatti  gilānāya, gilānā hutvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā agilānā bhuñ‍jati, gilānāya saisakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, ñātakānaṃ pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

paṭhamapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyādipāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadāni

1234. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tailaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janti…pai॰… madhuṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janti…pai॰… phāṇitaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janti…pai॰… macchaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janti…pai॰… maṃsaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janti…pai॰… khīraṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janti…pai॰… dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissanti! kassa sampan‍naṃ na manāpaṃ, kassa sāduṃ  na ruc‍catī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya, paṭidaisaitabbaṃ tāya bhikkhuniyā – ‘gārayhaṃ, ayyai, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

1235. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau gilānā haunti| gilānapucchikā bhikkhuniyau gilānā bhikkhuniyau aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kac‍ci, ayyai, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīya’’nti? ‘‘pubbai mayaṃ, ayyai, dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jimhā,  taina nau phāsu hauti, idāni pana ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhitta’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na viñ‍ñāpaima, taina nau na phāsu hautī’’ti…pai॰… bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānāya bhikkhuniyā dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

1236. ‘‘yā pana bhikkhunī agilānā (tailaṃ…pai॰… madhuṃ…pai॰… phāṇitaṃ…pai॰… macchaṃ…pai॰… maṃsaṃ…pai॰… khīraṃ…pai॰…) dadhiṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya , paṭidaisaitabbaṃ tāya bhikkhuniyā – ‘gārayhaṃ, ayyai, dhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃ asappāyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’’’ti|

1237. yā panāti yā yādisā…pai॰… bhikkhunīti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā bhikkhunīti|

agilānā nāma yassā vinā dadhinā phāsu hauti|

gilānā nāma yassā vinā dadhinā na phāsu hauti|

tailaṃ nāma tilatailaṃ sāsapatailaṃ madhukatailaṃ airaṇḍatailaṃ vasātailaṃ|

madhu nāma makkhikāmadhu| phāṇitaṃ nāma ucchumhā nibbattaṃ| macchau nāma audakau vuc‍cati| maṃsaṃ nāma yaisaṃ maṃsaṃ kappati taisaṃ maṃsaṃ| khīraṃ nāma gaukhīraṃ vā ajikākhīraṃ vā mahiṃsakhīraṃ vā yaisaṃ maṃsaṃ kappati taisaṃ khīraṃ| dadhi nāma taisañ‍ñaiva dadhi|

agilānā attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

1238. agilānā agilānasañ‍ñā dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| agilānā vaimatikā dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| agilānā gilānasañ‍ñā dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

gilānā  agilānasañ‍ñā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānā vaimatikā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gilānā gilānasañ‍ñā anāpatti|

1239. anāpatti  gilānāya, gilānā hutvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā agilānā bhuñ‍jati, gilānāya saisakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, ñātakānaṃ pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti|

aṭṭhamapāṭidaisanīyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|
