uddiṭṭhā khau, ayyāyau, aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā dhammā| tatthāyyāyau pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

bhikkhunivibhaṅgai pāṭidaisanīyakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



6. saikhiyakaṇḍaṃ (bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau)

1. parimaṇḍalavaggau

imai khau panāyyāyau saikhiyā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

1240. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintī nivāsainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintī nivāsaissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰… tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintī nivāsaissantī’’ti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintī nivāsaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintī nivāsaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘parimaṇḍalaṃ nivāsaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

parimaṇḍalaṃ nivāsaitabbaṃ nābhimaṇḍalaṃ jāṇumaṇḍalaṃ paṭicchādaintiyā| yā anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca puratau vā pacchatau vā aulambaintī  nivāsaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti  asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantiyā, gilānāya, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, ādikammikāyāti…pai॰… (saṃkhittaṃ)|

7. pādukavaggau

1241. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi karaunti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau udakai uc‍cārampi  passāvampi khaiḷampi karissanti, saiyyathāpi gihiniyau kāmabhauginiyau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhuniyau taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā, tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau  udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi karissantī’’ti! atha khau bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū yai tai bhikkhū…pai॰… (?) bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā…pai॰… bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi karauntī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi karissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘na udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

taina khau pana samayaina gilānā bhikkhuniyau udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi kātuṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānāya bhikkhuniyā udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi kātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyau imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisantu –

‘‘na udakai agilānā uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti|

na  udakai agilānāya uc‍cārau vā passāvau vā khaiḷau vā kātabbau| yā anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai agilānā uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, assatiyā, ajānantiyā, gilānāya, thalai katau udakaṃ auttharati, āpadāsu, ummattikāya, khittacittāya, vaidanāṭṭāya, ādikammikāyāti|

pan‍narasamasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

pādukavaggau sattamau|

uddiṭṭhā  khau, ayyāyau, saikhiyā dhammā| tatthāyyāyau pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

saikhiyakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



7. adhikaraṇasamathā (bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau)

imai khau panāyyāyau satta adhikaraṇasamathā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

1242. uppan‍nuppan‍nānaṃ  adhikaraṇānaṃ samathāya vūpasamāya sammukhāvinayau dātabbau, sativinayau dātabbau, amūḷhavinayau dātabbau, paṭiñ‍ñāya kāraitabbaṃ, yaibhuyyasikā, tassapāpiyasikā, tiṇavatthārakauti|

uddiṭṭhā khau, ayyāyau, satta adhikaraṇasamathā dhammā| tatthāyyāyau pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyyāyau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

adhikaraṇasamathā niṭṭhitā|


uddiṭṭhaṃ khau, ayyāyau, nidānaṃ| uddiṭṭhā aṭṭha pārājikā dhammā| uddiṭṭhā sattarasa saṅghādisaisā dhammā| uddiṭṭhā tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā dhammā| uddiṭṭhā chasaṭṭhisatā pācittiyā dhammā| uddiṭṭhā aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā dhammā| uddiṭṭhā saikhiyā dhammā| uddiṭṭhā satta adhikaraṇasamathā dhammā| aittakaṃ tassa bhagavatau suttāgataṃ suttapariyāpan‍naṃ anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchati| tattha sabbāhaiva samaggāhi sammaudamānāhi avivadamānāhi sikkhitabbanti|

bhikkhunivibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|


pācittiyapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
